----------------------------DD4 Administrator Section-----------------------

=== Booting and Testing Areas

When the DD4 server starts, it reads a file named 'area.lst' in the current
directory.  This file contains a list of all the area files to read in.  To add
or delete areas, simply edit area.lst.

The server reads all of the area files into memory once at load time and then
closes them.  Thus you can edit area files while the server is running.
Changes will take effect the next time the server boots.  Because the server is
completely memory-based, zone resets are fast.

You can test areas by running a local install of the MUD, which can be obtained
from https://github.com/fromage-fraser/dd4

Instructions on how to do this can be obtained from that location.

The server reports syntax errors, including the area file name and a line
number.  Take the line number with a grain of salt; some kinds of errors cause
the server to run on for quite a few lines before ultimately detecting the
error.

The server also reports semantic errors, such as references to non-existent
mobiles, objects, or rooms.

The server exits after reporting any error.  DD4 takes only a few seconds
to load, however, so it's quite practical to use the whole server as a syntax
checker.



=== Compressing the Area Files

It is possible to run DD4 with a single combined, compressed area file. I mean,
don't?  But you COULD.

Here's how to do this on a *nix system:

(1) In 'area.lst', remove the last line (the '$' line).

(2) Execute the command:

	cat `cat area.lst` | compress > all_area.Z

(3) Edit 'area.lst' again.  Insert a '-' at the beginning of every line.
    Do not put any spaces between the '-' and the file name.  Put the last
    '$' line back at the end of the file.

(4) Edit 'start_dd4'.  Change the line:

	../src/dd4 $port >&! $logfile

    to:

	zcat all_area.Z | ../src/dd4 $port >&! $logfile

(5) Test the changes so far.  DD4 should start up normally, although it
    may take a few seconds longer to zcat everything.

Now you can remove all the original *.are files.

Notice that all of the compression and decompression takes place outside of the
DD4 server.  Thus, you can substitute any archiving program of your choice, as
long as it can write its output to standard output.

You can recover the original areas simply by running 'uncompress all_area.Z'
and dissecting them out of all_area.

From the server's point of view, when an area file name starts with '-', it
simply reads standard input for the area, terminating at '#$' as usual (but
without closing standard input).  Diagnostic messages are given with the full
name (e.g. '-arachnos.are'), but the line number will be reported as zero.

You can freely mix areas from standard input with ordinary area files.  Thus,
you could compress all the DD4 standard zones into a file such as dd4_area.Z,
prefixing them with '-' in 'area.lst'.  Then you could add your own areas
anywhere in the file (beginning, middle, end, wherever your areas need to go),
and omit the '-' on the lines for your areas.

The server will take a little longer to load with compressed area files,
because 'zcat' needs time to run.  This is offset by a reduction in time spent
opening disk files.  After loading, the server has all of the area database in
memory and never rereads the files.  Thus, there is zero performance impact
on server operation after loading.



=== Memory Usage

In order to simplify the code, the EnvyMud server has a fixed maximum size on
strings in the area database.  This size is defined at the beginning of 'db.c'
'db.c' with a '#define' statement.  As of the last update of this document, the
size is:

    #define  MAX_STRING 6000000

Right now we're using about 5400000 bytes, so you couldadd a few more areas
without touching the server at all.  The server will tell you when the string
table overflows, and you can simply increase the maximum limit and recompile.
The immortal 'memory' command will show you memory usage from within the game.

There is no other limit on area sizes or memory usage.

We decided to use a fixed size because it simplifies our job.  It also allows
significant performance improvements.

Passive creature additions for Dwarven Catacombs
----------------------------------------------
The new archetypes are bat, animated_mist, mysterious_being, shadow,
vampire_bat_form, prism_dragon and darkenbeast. Select them through the existing
archetype field. No individual trait fields or new area syntax are added.

The mysterious_being species has no anatomical parts, speech or corpse and
is inorganic. Negative body restrictions prevent the normal anatomy helpers
from assuming a head, eyes, arms, legs or heart. This says nothing about a
mind, soul or undead nature. Animated mist uses this body with flight,
sanctuary, sneak and pass-door. Bat and vampire bat forms use winged_mammal;
DarkenBeast uses winged_reptile. Both winged bodies have two hind legs,
wings and living organs, with no usable arms/hands or speech. Vampire forms
share one set of vampire defaults. The large-bat form adds flight, fangs and
bite, with length 91 cm, estimated weight 10 kg and medium size.

Echolocation is an internal trait for the two bat archetypes. It locates
same-room corporeal creatures despite darkness or ordinary visual blindness.
It does not reveal administrator-hidden or nonphysical creatures, distant
rooms or object details. Existing channel mute/silence settings are not
physical deafness. No player race or form gains this trait automatically.

Shadow is intelligent undead with no ordinary organs, blood or corpse. It
has cold/poison/sleep/charm/hold/drain/nonmagic immunity, dark resistance and
holy vulnerability. It keeps a shadowy humanoid outline, sneak, pass-door,
detection and muteness. It does not inherit wraith phase cycling or a silver
exception. Its strength_drain_touch trait has a 25% chance after actual
damaging natural melee to attempt -1 Strength through RES_DRAIN rules. The
new condition caps at -3 and lasts four ticks, refreshed by another success.
It grants no extra attack or permanent stat/level loss. Restoration and
Vitalize remove this condition as well as the existing Drained condition.
The conditions remain separate. Strength drain is an internal condition,
not a player-learnable skill or a castable spell.

Natural armour is an internal signed AC adjustment, included in GET_AC and
shown by MSTAT's trait display. Prism dragon and DarkenBeast receive -20 AC.
It is not a new area scalar or a change to stored armour. Their other scalar
HP/damage/critical/swiftness and separate XP adjustments stay neutral.

Prism dragon adds BODY_HUGE to the existing dragon body and keeps flight,
ordinary natural attacks and speech. Its shoulder height is 300 cm, length
1200 cm and estimated weight 3000 kg. Its special distribution is
75% spec_breath_any and 25% spec_prism_flash, with no boss slot. The flash casts existing Colour
Spray at its current opponent using the dragon's level. Only one special
runs per opportunity; breath opportunities therefore receive a smaller
share. Special XP uses the existing weighted calculation.

DarkenBeast is a living transformed animal with wings, claws and fangs,
charm immunity, medium metadata and descriptive length 120 cm. It retains
a corpse and uses the existing -1 natural-sunlight accuracy trait. This
named boss does not revert to an unspecified original animal in daylight.
Poison is an individual area special, not a racial default. Height and
weight remain unspecified. This is DD4's agreed adaptation of the AD&D
second-edition creature; prism dragon is this area's own creature.

Catacombs 2006 retains its blank descriptions and aggression without old
gold-derived HUGE/tail flags. Officer 2010 regains normal human anatomy.
Skeleton 2007 no longer uses legacy undead casting; wraith 2009 and vampire
2013 inherit their native specials. Zombie mage 2011 cancels AFF_MINDLESS
through its existing AFF XOR field and retains its mage special. Human
grand templar 2015 has an individual cleric combat special; no shop or
healer service is added. The other human roles keep existing behaviour.

Anatomically inappropriate equipment becomes carried loot on the mist,
shadow, vampire bat, prism dragon and DarkenBeast. All loot remains
obtainable, but worn-item bonuses no longer apply. Natural armour partly
replaces the two bosses' former equipment defence; it is not an exact
reproduction of random item bonuses. Every room, exit and room flag stays
unchanged, including the seven approved NO_UNDEAD rooms. No matching
Catacombs Scribe source exists in the repository.

Dwarven Day Care creatures
-------------------------
Use animated_soft_toy for simple enchanted cloth or stuffed toys, and
animated_toy_soldier for an animated soldier of unspecified material.
Both use the bipedal_construct body: a head, eyes, ears, two arms with
usable hands and fingers, two legs and feet, but no heart, brain or guts.
The inorganic flag describes their nonliving material in DD4. This body
alone does not make every future construct mindless or soulless.

These two toy archetypes add mindlessness and bloodless, analgic, soulless,
speechless physiology. They are immune to poison, sleep, charm and drain.
Soft toys are vulnerable to fire; the soldier has no material weakness.
Ordinary weapons and other magic still work. They have no natural attacks,
active special, armour bonus or HP/damage/critical/swiftness/XP adjustment.
Both have small size metadata and height 30 cm. Soft toys weigh 1 kg and
soldiers 2 kg. These dimensions are descriptive estimates.

The toys do not leave flesh corpses or severed body parts. Their carried
items and coins fall to the room through the existing corpseless rules.
Do not give them ACT_OBJECT: moving enchanted toys are ordinary mobiles.
Their individual area specials can still make them pick pockets. Theft
is not a racial default, and the toy gun remains a melee bayonet weapon.

Day Care reuses dwarf_untrained for youth 6600 and brat 6608, and dwarf
for nanny 6606 and guard 6607. The nanny keeps her combat-cleric special;
she is not an adept or healer service. The final room's dragon is a child
in a Halloween mask. The old doll keeps ring 6601, part of the Dwarven and
Goblin Alliance set. Rooms, flags, objects, resets and loot stay as they are.
No matching Day Care source exists in the repository.

These are DD4's modest animated toys, informed by the AD&D second-edition
doll/stuffed-animal construct precedent. They do not use the powerful
Ravenloft doll golem's magic protection. Use the existing archetype field;
there are no new area fields, Scribe commands or player abilities.

Miden'nir creatures and tail body weapons
----------------------------------------
The wyvern archetype uses winged_reptile, with flight, a tail, fangs and
anatomical talons. It is living, huge, speechless and armless, with large
size metadata and descriptive length 1,070 cm. Its natural_armour trait is
-20 AC. Height/weight are unspecified; HP/damage/critical/swiftness remain
neutral. The +5 XP adjustment replaces the former poison-special allowance.

The stinger is object 594: a poisoned ITEM_BODY_PART weapon that players
cannot pick up. ITEM_DONOT_RANDOMISE prevents random affixes; the engine's
usual level-based weapon damage still applies. Weapon value[0] is 6 (tail),
and value[3] is 16 (sting). Existing target tail, disarming and Regenerate
rules apply. No new body-part flag, target region or trauma is added.
Tail trauma makes an attached tail weapon unusable even if it stays worn.
Ordinary NPC combat then falls back to usable natural attacks; the wyvern
bites. Explicit weapon skills cannot use the injured part. Equipment stays
in place so healing the trauma can make an attached weapon usable again.
Curing trauma does not recreate a physically disarmed weapon.

The stinger follows existing weapon and poisoned-weapon damage rules:
NPC weapon damage gains its existing 50% base bonus, and eligible poisoned
contact can gain the existing 25% bonus. Poison uses its ordinary saves,
immunities, toxin defences and short, capped condition. Do not also assign
spec_poison; that would add a second poison route. No extra attack, separate
Venom trait or instant-death effect is supplied. The existing scorpion-form
stinger remains a piercing/backstab weapon with the same tail category.

Miden'nir reuses goblin for 3501/3506/3507 and human for the other people.
The lieutenant is rare, the leader elite, and the others common. The boy is
120 cm/25 kg. Legacy trainer/no-quest bits and the boy's unused bit are
removed. The bard still teaches teacher base 25 and musicianship 85; the
horsemen keep their coins and are described on foot. All room flags and
existing reset placements/caps are preserved. No matching source exists.

Graveyard creatures
-------------------

Graveyard uses human for Henry 3600, zombie for 3601/3605, ghoul for 3602,
and skeleton for 3603/3604. All stay common rank. The ordinary undead have
no individual casting special. Ghoul feeding and claw/bite paralysis come
from its archetype; do not repeat a legacy spec_cast_undead assignment,
which would replace the inherited special set. Deliberate caster roles,
such as the Catacombs zombie mage 2011, keep their individual special.

Skeletons and zombies are immune to cold, hold and paralysis as well as
poison, sleep and drain. Their other DD4 resistances and weaknesses stay
as they are. Ghouls and ghasts retain their different sleep/charm immunity
and gain the anatomical claw/fang bits their attacks already use.

These four archetypes have descriptive medium size and height 175 cm.
Skeleton weight is 12 kg; zombie, ghoul and ghast weight is 65 kg. These
weights are DD4 estimates. Individual dimensions replace the defaults:

    MobHeight 185
    MobWeight 95

Those are Henry's values. In a Scribe $mob block, write height 185 and
weight 95. The existing exporter writes the optional fields above; no new
source command or area keyword is needed. Omission or inherit keeps the
template value; explicit zero clears that dimension. These are descriptive
metadata and do not change combat, carrying capacity or corpse weight.

Keep individual infrared and encounter flags. Do not repeat inherited
body restrictions in XOR masks: the Graveyard skeletons use raw body 0,
and zombie 3601 no longer repeats no_speech. The tomb 3643 scenery reset
uses dusty-skeleton object 3610; the shed retains its wheelbarrow 3603.
Rooms, flags, doors, original mobile resets and other loot are preserved.
There is no matching Graveyard source file in the repository.

Creature dimension refinements
-----------------------------

The Dwarven Kingdom storekeeper 6503 keeps the normal dwarf height and
uses MobWeight 95 for his rotund build. Mine leader 6508 uses MobHeight 140
and MobWeight 100. These are individual replacements, not additions.

The vampire_bat_form archetype is the second-edition large-bat form:
length 91 cm, estimated weight 10 kg and medium size. Its height remains
unspecified. The sourcebook gives a three-foot body and a five-to-six-foot
wingspan but no weight; the 10 kg value is a DD4 estimate. Wingspan is only
a descriptive reference, with no new field. Existing vampire physiology,
defences, echolocation, natural bite and active special are preserved.

Prism Dragon defaults are shoulder height 300 cm, snout-to-tail length
1200 cm, estimated weight 3000 kg and large size. Those measurements fit
its large cavern but are not exact numbers supplied by the area author.
The archetype adds BODY_HUGE to its inherited dragon body. Ordinary huge
creature checks now apply, including restrictions on backstabbing,
stunning, grappling, headbutting and swallowing it. The flag is separate
from dimension metadata.

Height, length and weight remain whole-number descriptive values.
Fractional weights are not supported. No units were changed, and these
measurements do not introduce new weight-based combat or carrying rules.
Only the explicit HUGE body flag changes physical eligibility here.
Humanoid vampire and ordinary bat defaults stay as they were.

Abandoned Cathedral creatures
-----------------------------

Cathedral uses existing human, ghoul, dracolich, vampire, skeleton, zombie,
shadow, wight, wraith, banshee and ghost archetypes. The new giant_zombie
shares the ordinary zombie physiology, adds HUGE and giant-family matching,
and has descriptive height 450 cm, weight 700 kg and large size. Dwarf
defences against giants apply. Its combat scalars and special slots are neutral.

Erich 4306 keeps vampire anatomy and defences but has an individual
spec_mast_vampire assignment, replacing the inherited ordinary special.
He can speak and still leaves no corpse; his original equipment remains loot.
Esmerelda 4303 and priests 4304 have individual spec_cast_cleric assignments.
The priests wield ordinary obsidian maces 4318, with base 2d4/pound before
normal NPC level scaling and weapon bonuses. DONOT_RANDOMISE prevents new
affixes; it does not remove the existing level scaling. Existing evil
alignments, aggression and sanctuary are preserved.

Wyrenthoth 4305 and Erich are boss rank. Esmerelda, giant zombie 4307 and
Mother Superior 4313 are rare; the other creatures stay common. Rank affects
normal generated HP and existing loot generation: rare doubles base HP,
boss multiplies it by seven. These are individual encounter choices.
Isaac 4300 has individual height 180 cm and weight 100 kg. His deafness is
descriptive; the character channel-muting field is not a hearing mechanic.

Wights have anatomical/natural claws and immunity to cold, charm, hold,
paralysis and ordinary weapons, alongside their earlier defences. Silver
weapons bypass only their ordinary-weapon barrier and deal normal damage;
magical weapons also work normally. The internal silver_bypasses_nonmagic
trait supplies this rule. It lives only in species/archetype code and is
shown by MSTAT; there is no area field, individual override or new ACT bit.
It does not add silver vulnerability. Wraith silver-phase resistance and
vampire silver vulnerability continue to use their existing rules.

Wraiths add cold/charm/hold/paralysis immunity. Banshees add
cold/electricity/charm/hold immunity and keep the existing DD4 fear wail.
Wights use 175 cm/60 kg/medium; humanoid vampires use 175 cm/75 kg/medium.
Wraiths, ghosts and banshees have apparent height 175 cm and medium size,
with weight unspecified. Shadows have medium outlines and unknown numeric
dimensions. The large vampire bat keeps length 91 cm, weight 10 kg, medium
size and unspecified height. All weights are whole kilograms.

The dragon species now includes the claw/fang body parts its natural-attack
mask already named. This adds no extra attack or damage multiplier.
Dracoliches add flight, HUGE, large size and natural_armour -40. They select
spec_dracolich 70 percent or spec_breath_any 30 percent, one callback per
opportunity. Breath selection reduces gaze/spell opportunities; the separate
general fear aura still applies. Mixed breath is our DD4 choice where the
former dragon colour is unknown. This keeps the established DD4 gaze and
spell rules rather than adding a second special on the same opportunity.

Shared dracolich measurements remain unspecified because the original
dragon varies. Wyrenthoth has individual shoulder height 400 cm, body length
1600 cm and estimated skeletal weight 2000 kg. His wimpy flag is removed;
sentinel and stay-area remain. His gold key becomes carried loot under
dragon anatomy. Dimensions and armour scaling are DD4 estimates informed
by the area and second-edition creature entries.

Keep individual body masks deliberate. Cathedral skeleton/shadow/dracolich
records omit inherited restriction duplicates. The wraith stays mute and
inherits no_corpse; the singing Abbott can speak and inherits no_corpse.
The corporeal wight has ordinary remains. Preserve all original room flags,
doors, keys, reset placements and loot. Princess Evalynn's tomb 4348 stays
NO_UNDEAD, with its coffin/corpse/Holy Avenger chain intact. There is no
matching Cathedral Scribe source in the repository.

Mega-City One humans
--------------------

All thirteen Mega-City One definitions use humanoid/human. Ordinary defaults
are height 175 cm, weight 75 kg and medium size. Individual replacements
give juve 8000 height 180 cm/weight 60 kg, Gleep 8007 weight 100 kg and
Throt 8008 weight 60 kg. Gleep and Throt inherit the ordinary human height.
These dimensions remain descriptive whole-number values.

Gleep 8007 and Throt 8008 are rare; Senior Judge Eckersely 8010 is elite.
The other characters stay common. Rank applies to normal generated HP
and existing loot generation: rare doubles base HP, elite multiplies it
by five. These choices describe the individual encounters, not human races.
Their authored levels, alignments and combat scalars stay as they were.

Keep spec_cast_judge on 8003, 8006, 8010 and 8011, and spec_thief on 8004.
These are deliberate individual roles. The neutral human archetype does
not replace their science-fiction weapon behaviour or pickpocketing.
Keep individual detection, infrared, sanctuary and cowardice where authored.

Bite fighter 8012 has sharpened teeth. Its body XOR mask adds PART_FANGS,
decimal 17179869184, and MobAttackParts uses the same mask. In Scribe,
write bf fangs and atk fangs in the human mob block. Existing
natural-attack selection then uses a piercing bite at the ordinary attack
rate and damage scaling. Existing head anatomy checks apply. This uses
the existing per-mob fields; no new archetype, trait or command is needed.

Judge Beddows 8011 is female, matching her description and existing portrait
8011_judge_beddows.png. Her name and picture stay the same. She remains a
living human, as do the other characters; her clone origin and the Futsie's
mental-state description do not replace their ordinary human physiology.

Preserve all original mobile placements, equipment, guns, special functions,
doors, room flags and the safe/vault/Vibroblade chain. There is no matching
Mega-City One Scribe source in the repository. Keep future source records
consistent with these individual replacements and inherited human defaults.

Dangerous Neighborhood humans and dogs
--------------------------------------

The Ogres and Trolls are human street gangs. All seven human definitions
2101-2107 use humanoid/human, including the gang-associated woman 2105.
They inherit ordinary human anatomy, height 175 cm, weight 75 kg and
medium size. Bruiser 2104 replaces height with 198 cm and weight with
111 kg, matching the description's 6 ft 6 in and 245 lb. He stays medium.

The shared gang-leader definition 2103 is elite, so both hideout leaders
receive the usual five-times generated HP and existing rank loot effects.
The other definitions stay common, including the five recurring bruisers.
Keep authored levels, alignment, equipment and ordinary combat scalars.
The unused ACT value 16 is removed from 2101, 2102 and 2105; all their
other behaviour remains. The vandal keeps sneak and the bruiser keeps
detect-invisible. No gang or patrolman special is added.

The new quadrupedal_mammal body has a head, four legs and forelegs, ordinary
organs, feet, ears and eyes. It has no arms or speech and no default natural
attack. The dog archetype adds a tail, paws, claws and fangs, using only
the fangs for an ordinary piercing bite. Claws and tail are anatomy here,
not extra attacks. Normal head and trauma checks still apply. Dogs are
living organic creatures with blood, souls, pain and ordinary corpses;
they are not mindless. No special, extra resistance or combat bonus is added.
The provisional farm_mammal example remains. Goat now uses the clean
quadrupedal_mammal body described in Sentinel Pass below.

Dog defaults are shoulder height 50 cm, weight 25 kg, length 90 cm and
small size. Pitbull 2108 replaces weight with 30 kg and keeps its existing
hide, detect-invisible, aggression, sentinel and stay-area flags. Detection
is an individual advantage, not a magical sense shared by all dogs.
Rabid anticipation does not add a disease attack. The AD&D second-edition
Monstrous Manual Dog entry informs the ordinary bite and compact size;
the exact metric dimensions are DD4 estimates and remain descriptive.

In Scribe, use archetype dog and, for this pitbull, weight 30. Leave bf
and atk omitted to inherit the body and bite. In an area record this is
< dog~ common~ followed by MobWeight 30. Its raw body XOR mask is zero:
keeping the old value 36 would cancel inherited no-arms and no-speech.
The server and Scribe companion name list both recognise dog. A body
species name such as quadrupedal_mammal is not an archetype selection.

The eight corrected room descriptions follow the existing residents:
2102, 2116, 2124 and 2126 say Ogre; 2148, 2160, 2163 and 2166 say Troll.
The Dracolich idol, dragon-themed street names, troll-crest ring, all room
flags, doors, reset locations/caps and original equipment stay as they were.
There is no matching Dangerous Neighborhood Scribe source. Its existing
portraits are outside this conversion and remain unchanged.

The Foundry goblins and animals
------------------------------

The Foundry uses the existing goblin archetype for 102-110, dog for 101
and giant_lizard for the local Pit Beast 111. No new species or archetype
is needed. All original aggression, wimpy, scavenger, sentinel and movement
settings stay as authored. Treat these as deliberate encounter choices:
read how the flags work together before changing them during a conversion.
In particular, aggressive plus wimpy skips awake players when choosing an
aggression target; the fearful slaves and starving dogs keep that behaviour.

Goblins inherit infravision, anatomical fangs and the existing sunlight
accuracy trait. They can now see normally in the dark tunnels; this is
a real encounter improvement. The established inside/dark/weather checks
shield the lair from sunlight penalties. Dwarf training recognises them as
goblins. Keep ordinary living physiology and neutral racial combat/XP
scalars. Hoobuk's albinism does not remove infravision or add another penalty.

Ushog 102 is elite and Uburz 103 rare; the Pit Beast retains its existing
rare rank. The others stay common. Rank affects normal generated HP and
existing loot rules: elite multiplies HP by five, rare by two. Keep source
levels, alignment, equipment, the area XP modifier 125 and its ambience.

Whole-number individual height/weight replacements, in cm/kg, are:
101 weight 15; 102 height 110/weight 35; 103 height 140/weight 40;
104 weight 35; 107 weight 50; 109 height 100/weight 20. Other goblins
inherit height 120/weight 30; the dog inherits height 50 and length 90.
Goblins and dog remain small. These descriptive estimates reflect the
short captain, tall deputy, solid soldier, obese feeder, small slave and
explicitly starving dog. They add no damage or starvation penalty.

Shargook 108 has no teeth or external ears. Her body XOR toggles off
PART_FANGS and PART_EAR, decimal 17196646400, while retaining ordinary
speech, hearing and other anatomy. In Scribe use bf fangs ear. Goblin
defaults include those parts, so the individual mask removes them here.
Ushog still has one usable eye; keep sight. Lobuk remains a female humanoid
with hands and a wielded rock despite running on all fours. Her mental-state
description does not make her mindless or unable to speak.

Pit Beast 111 is a compact local lizard variant: height 35, weight 25,
length 150 and medium size. These replace the ordinary giant-lizard length
457 and large size. In Scribe use height 35, weight 25, length 150 and
size 1. It keeps the individual spec_poison, which is not supplied by the
giant_lizard archetype. This does not make other giant lizards venomous.

The shared giant_lizard body now has anatomical claws, alongside its fangs,
scales and tail. Its natural attack list remains fangs only: an ordinary
piercing bite. Claws add no extra attack or damage bonus. Dwarven Kingdom
lizard 6505 inherits that anatomy correction and keeps its existing
dimensions, rank, stats, equipment and behaviour.

The Foundry retains all original inhabitants, reset caps/locations, gear,
objects, room flags, exits and doors. The unreset crafting test objects
111/112 remain. Only the agreed spelling and room grammar corrections
change descriptive text. There is no matching Scribe source in the repo.
Keep future source records consistent with these inherited defaults and
individual replacements.

Fortress of Goblins, orcs, elves and preserved heads
-------------------------------------------------

The Fortress converts all 32 mobiles, 20300-20331. Most are goblins, with
human, dwarf, orc, elf, ghost and preserved_head exceptions. Every original
aggression, wimpy, sentinel, movement and NO_FIGHT/NOCHARM setting stays.
Keep the authored levels, alignments, equipment, two individual specials,
reset caps, rooms, doors and loot. The original caps produce 64 inhabitants,
not one mobile for each of the 68 reset rows. There is no matching source.

Rank comes from the encounter role. The king 20322 is boss; bath guards
20313/20314 and queen 20323 are elite; planner 20318 and executioner 20329
are rare. All others are common, including the deformity 20321. The old HP
letters are not rank markers. Rank HP multipliers apply once as usual.

The orc archetype now has infravision, anatomical fangs, height 175 cm,
weight 85 kg and medium size. Fangs do not add an automatic bite. It shares
the goblin's existing -1 sunlight accuracy trait and shelter/weather rules.
It adds no aggression, alignment, special or racial combat/XP multiplier.
The other target-family placeholders remain provisional.

The new elf archetype uses humanoid anatomy, infravision, height 150 cm,
weight 45 kg and medium size, with neutral combat/XP modifiers. Its internal
sleep_resistance_pct and charm_resistance_pct traits are each 90 percent.
A failed percentage roll still gets the normal saving throw or success
test. An effect carrying both types gets one roll at the higher percentage.
These are separate from ordinary RES_SLEEP/RES_CHARM flags; do not add those
flags to repeat the racial bonus. Charm, Sleep, vampire gaze and Transfix
use the shared protection. Domination remains disabled; its dormant save
path now uses the same categories if the spell is restored later.
Ordinary ghoul paralysis cannot
affect this elf; ghast touch still can. This is not general paralysis immunity.
MSTAT shows all three traits and their archetype origin. Player elf and orc
packages stay unchanged; NPC family elf does not assign a player race.

Elf sword/bow training is deliberately deferred until the first armed elf
review. Woodland stealth and construction/secret-door recognition wait for
the first relevant free-living elf review. Add these shared mechanics then
and retrofit this captive. Do not treat the current profile as final.

The new head_only species supplies head, eyes and ears, with no arms, legs,
heart or other working organs. preserved_head adds inorganic, bloodless,
soulless, analgic and mindless properties, no speech and no fresh corpse.
It is immune to poison, sleep, charm and drain, and can make one ordinary
fang bite through the existing natural-attack rules. It has no new special,
extra attack, ACT_OBJECT or NO_FIGHT. The mounted trophy 20312 keeps its
passive, sentinel, stay-area behaviour and can retaliate normally.
Its old body XOR 319 is cleared so it inherits its head and eyes correctly.
Use height 30, weight 5 and small size for this preserved head.

The willing executioner 20329 and the captive human 20326 are human. The
mutant 20321 also keeps its human origin and individual spec_breath_gas.
The dwarf captive 20327 inherits dwarf resistances and training. Good spirit
20324 uses the existing semi-material ghost and keeps alignment +500 and
its individual detection/faerie-fire effects, without new active powers.
Healer 20317 keeps NO_FIGHT and spec_cast_adept; no paid service is added.

Whole-number individual replacements (height cm, weight kg) are:
20300/20303 183/100, 20302 80/18, 20306 110/30, 20313/20314 200/120,
20319 100/20, 20322 120/50, 20325 175/65, 20326 175/60,
20327 130/55, 20328 150/40, 20329 183/136 and 20330 120/45.
Tall guards 20300/20303/20313/20314 are medium; other goblins stay small.
Other goblins inherit 120/30, ordinary humans 175/75 and the ghost 175/0.
Dimensions remain descriptive; a large goblin does not gain the HUGE flag.
Use Scribe height/weight/size for the corresponding individual replacements.

Infrared needs careful XOR handling. Most goblins now use raw AFF 4 instead
of 4|512, leaving resolved faerie fire plus inherited infrared unchanged.
Orc/dwarf/elf prisoners use 4|256 instead of 4|256|512 for the same reason.
The human captive keeps its individual infrared; the jailer and assassin
gain goblin infrared while retaining every existing stealth/detection bit.
The ghost's raw 772 is unchanged. Do not copy resolved flags into raw XORs.

Explicit male pronouns correct sex to male for 20300/20302/20314/20318/20324.
The portraits were checked; no artwork change is needed. All other sexes
stay. Five small mob spelling/grammar corrections and the executioner's
willing-service clarification change no behaviour or room descriptions.

Smugglers Ship humans and crew
-----------------------------

Smugglers Ship uses the existing humanoid/human profile for all twenty
mobiles, 17500-17519. Human defaults are height 175 cm, weight 75 kg and
medium size, with ordinary living anatomy and neutral racial combat/XP
modifiers. No new species, archetype, trait or authoring command is needed.
Leave all individual ACT, AFF and body masks as authored. Raw body zero
inherits the human's normal organs and limbs without cancelling any parts.

The captain 17502 keeps boss, Egor 17505 keeps rare and Punjak 17519 keeps
elite. Harvey 17506 and the senior officers Arnold 17513 and Arthur 17514
are rare. Everyone else stays common. The new rare ranks double generated
HP and use the usual rank loot rules; authored levels stay unchanged.

Individual descriptive height/weight replacements, in whole cm/kg, are:
17500 165/85, 17502 185/85, 17504 185/105, 17506 185/110,
17509 180/100, 17512 198/115 and 17514 175/65. These are estimates based on
the short stocky lookout, tall captain, big cook, muscular bosun/helmsman,
very tall bodyguard and lean second officer. Other humans inherit 175/75.
All remain medium, including the bodyguard; none gains HUGE. In Scribe,
use height and weight only for the individual replacements.

The Crew 17508 is one combined encounter representing many human men.
Keep its authored level 100, common rank, neutral sex and existing buffs.
Its dimensions describe a representative fighter. Keep the original single
mobile and its existing encounter strength. The concubine is human too;
her flattering divine-presence prose supplies no new divine physiology.
One-eye retains his remaining useful eye, hiding and detection. The resting
pirate keeps his authored standing position despite the hammock wording.

Keep all four individual specials: captain spec_warrior, concubine and
Punjak spec_cast_archmage, Arthur spec_cast_psionicist. These are deliberate
roles and are not supplied by human. Existing aggression, passivity,
sentinel/stay-area settings, magical advantages and equipment all remain.

The original 21 mobile-reset rows produce 20 initial inhabitants and
83 carried items. A second 17503 row is skipped by its existing cap of one.
Further resets fill the existing caps to 30 inhabitants, including three
wandering crewmen: one 17503 and two 17517. The author's notes now describe
those caps and Arthur's existing psionic powers accurately. No reset cap,
location, wear slot, door, room flag or room description changes.

Preserve all 55 rooms and 60 objects, the diary/floorboard/cache-key chains
and area XP setting 115. Room 17543 uses a valid inline vnum/name header;
include it when counting rooms. Objects 17510/17511 also equip a mobile in
Sahuagin Market and stay unchanged. There is no matching Scribe source.

Freeport reptiles and citizens
------------------------------

Freeport uses human for thirty mobiles, dwarf for Raedner 29118, lizard
for 29101-29103 and lizardman for Stathog 29134. Human and dwarf retain
their approved defaults. Both new archetypes use existing body species;
no new body part, area field or individual trait override is needed.

The lizard archetype uses quadrupedal_reptile, with anatomical claws and
fangs and a fang-only natural bite. It is small, height 8 cm, weight 1 kg
and total length 46 cm. The length reflects the area's eighteen inches;
height and whole-number mass are estimates. Keep neutral combat/XP
modifiers and ordinary living organs. It adds no special or resistance.
Freeport's three colours keep their individual hiding and timid behaviour.
Their existing tongue pills still provide Sanctuary, Poison and Curse
when consumed. Those loot effects do not add venomous bites or spells.

The lizardman archetype uses humanoid, adding scales, a nonprehensile tail,
claws and fangs. Ordinary unarmed contacts can use claws or fangs at the
existing DD4 attack rate. The internal natural_armour trait supplies -10
armour; MSTAT shows its lizardman origin. Default dimensions are height
200 cm, weight 100 kg and medium size. Other combat/XP modifiers remain
neutral, with no default special or racial resistance package. Creature
family lizardman is internal identity, not an ACT bit or area field.

These are DD4 adaptations informed by the second-edition Monstrous Manual.
Its Lizard Man entry supports the scaled humanoid anatomy, ordinary natural
weapons and adult proportions. The exact armour bonus and dimensions are
our estimates. The Lizard entry concerns giant varieties; it does not give
statistics for Freeport's small local animals. Existing NPC water movement
already handles these creatures. Keep its current rules; do not use the
removable swimming stance as permanent anatomy. Player packages stay as
they are. The earlier giant_lizard archetype is unchanged.

Stathog retains his exceptional knowledge, personality, protected flags,
teacher base 70, every lesson and individual spec_cast_psionicist. His
height 210 and weight 95 replace the lizardman defaults. He remains common,
with medium size; his profession does not make him a boss or a primitive
tribal creature. No intelligence or alignment restriction is inherited.

The laughing child 29109 uses human with height 110, weight 20 and size 0
(small). Apprentice boy 29112 uses height 140 and weight 40, keeping medium
size. Other humans inherit 175/75/medium and Raedner 130/70/small. Use
Scribe height, weight and size for these existing individual replacements.

Mourning 29132 is boss, Oglethorpe 29131 elite, and Blacksail 29130,
Mayor Friendly 29126 and Whitecap 29125 rare. Other mobiles stay common,
including the high-level shops and teacher. Rank affects generated HP
once and uses its normal reward rules; authored levels stay unchanged.
Keep the three individual spec_poison routines and Stathog's psionics.
They are deliberate roles, not defaults supplied by the new archetypes.

Clear raw body 36 to 0 on the three lizards. The body species already
supplies no arms and no speech; copying 36 would XOR those restrictions
off. Other inherited anatomy remains available for targeting and trauma.
All ordinary natural-attack injury checks still apply.

The reset labelled Admiral Blacksail now loads 29130 in room 29172,
with its existing cap of one, cape, signet and devil weed. It previously
named a scruffy pirate whose cap was already full, so Blacksail never
appeared. Preserve the two ordinary pirates and all other placements/caps.
Ceres 29121 loses only the unused practice bit; his working healer and
fame flags remain. Whitecap loses only wiz-invisibility and keeps his
sentinel/lose-fame flags, making his public conversation available.
Raedner and Samantha keep their existing services; their unsupported
repair/identify offer sentences are removed. Heralt remains the tinker.

Keep all seven shop inventories, prices and hours, all 77 rooms and their
flags/exits/doors, all 36 objects, area no_quest and the other reset/gear
records. No aggression, passivity, sentinel, wimpy, source level, alignment
or sex changes are intended. There is no matching Freeport Scribe source.

Kingdom of Juargan creatures
---------------------------

Juargan uses dwarf for 4700-4704, duergar for 4705, wight for Grimdale 4706,
rock_worm for 4707 and ghost for the three spirits 4708. Keep all authored
levels, alignments, sexes, positions and behaviour flags. The miners and
watchful dwarves are adults. Ordinary dwarves use the established anatomy,
defences and training; small stature does not mean untrained childhood.

Juargan 4704 is boss, Grimdale 4706 elite and the rock worm 4707 rare. The
other six definitions remain common. Rank follows their encounter roles;
the old HP dice letters do not select ranks. Normal rank HP applies once.
Juargan's muscular build uses height 130 cm and weight 100 kg. Grimdale has
height 130, weight 50 and small size. The spirits have a 130 cm outline,
small size and unknown mass. They keep their conscious, suffering souls,
Sanctuary and detection affects; their description also calls for flight.

Duergar is a humanoid with dwarf family and inherited dwarf training.
Defaults are height 120 cm, weight 55 kg and small size. It has infrared,
Sneak, magic resistance and immunity to poison, hold and paralysis. Its
sunlight_sensitive trait is -2 under the existing sheltered/daylight rules.
This does not add the tabletop daylight Dexterity or opponent-save changes.
The local colony's hostility and strange behaviour stay in the area; they
are not personality or alignment defaults for every gray dwarf.

The code-only illusion_immunity trait recognises Ventriloquate's deception
without an ordinary saving throw. Future genuinely illusory effects should
consult the same trait. DD4 Colour Spray remains an energy attack, including
the separate breath route. Faerie Fog and invisibility keep their usual
rules. A spell's learning group alone does not establish an illusory effect.
No ACT or RES bit, player package or individual trait field was added.

spec_duergar tries ordinary self-Invisibility on one in eight eligible idle
opportunities. The creature must be awake, free to act, not charmed, held or
dazed, not fighting and not already invisible. The ordinary spell duration,
dispelling and revelation through attacking still apply. It does not keep
refreshing invisibility in combat. Its default single slot is 100 percent.
The archetype adds 5 XP percentage points and this special adds the normal
10, making the approved total +15 rather than counting the power twice.
Other HP, damage, critical and haste modifiers are neutral.

The second-edition Monstrous Manual's Duergar entry informed this profile.
Do not use the adjacent Derro's 30-percent magic resistance for Duergar.
Exact dimensions, XP and DD4 effects are adaptations. True enlargement and
shrinking remain an explicit required follow-up: the current height/weight
affect cases do nothing, and a shared temporary-size mechanic must come
first. Retrofit duergar when it is implemented.

limbless_invertebrate supplies a living head, eyes, heart, brains and guts,
with no arms, legs or speech. rock_worm adds fangs and HUGE, with an ordinary
piercing natural bite at the existing attack rate. It uses Large size,
150 cm body thickness, estimated 800 cm length and estimated 8000 kg mass.
Only the roughly five-foot thickness is stated by the area. There is no
automatic venom, tail sting, stone armour or rock-crossing movement power.
Its neutral archetype XP and spec_swallow's +10 give a total +10 adjustment.

spec_swallow is reusable by other suitable creatures. It tries the existing
Swallow skill on 15 percent of eligible combat opportunities, against the
current opponent, with at most one swallowed victim. Normal safety, injury,
status, success, wait, escape, expiry and removal rules remain. An awake,
free-to-act creature needs a working head and an undamaged mouth. Its default
single slot is 100 percent; the 15-percent attempt rate belongs to the power.

Swallow refuses HUGE targets, for both players and mobiles. Large size
alone is allowed. The skill no longer reads the attacker's race or compares
against an unrelated size value. This applies to manual Swallow and the
existing whale special too. The new special adds no whale flukeslap or talk.

Clear raw body 172 to 0 on worm 4707: the new profile already supplies no
arms, no legs, no speech and HUGE. Clear raw body 320 to 0 on spirits 4708:
the ghost profile already supplies inorganic physiology and no corpse.
Keeping those duplicate masks would XOR the intended properties away.

Keep all 44 original inhabitants, 16 carried items and wear slots, 78 room
flags/exits/doors, 11 objects and every reset/cap/placement. Both royal guards
keep their actual armour; do not create new loot from the other descriptions.
The king retains key 4700, Grimdale retains axe 4706, and the tomb's staff,
shield and coins remain in their original containers/rooms. Digger 4710's
I reset loads room 4707, not mobile 4707. There is no matching Juargan source,
shop, teacher or previous special record. Grimdale and the spirits inherit
undead classification; their tomb already permits undead.

The wooden man uses wooden_automaton (175 cm, 50 kg, Medium). It is mindless,

Dragon Cult creatures
---------------------
Most cult members use human. The draconian knight is a human cult guard;
his dragon-skull helmet does not establish a reptilian body. The priestess
adds individual scales without extra armour or dragon powers. Bob is 183 cm
and 65 kg, matching his six-foot lanky description. The strong fresh zombie
uses zombie with an individual 95 kg weight. Keep every authored behaviour,
including aggression, wimpy and sentinel, and all levels, sexes and alignments.

The knight and Torturer are rare, the High Priestess elite, and the wyrmling
and Lich Priest boss. Others stay common, including office boss Fred and the
high-level candy seller. Rank HP applies once. All rooms, flags, exits, doors,
objects, resets, caps, keys, equipment and candy-shop stock/prices/hours stay.
The initial 23 inhabitants carry 29 items; full caps give 27 inhabitants
and 36 carried items.
There is no matching Scribe source for this area.

black_dragon_hatchling reuses the dragon body and adds flight, infrared,
Detect Invisible/Hidden and acid immunity. Defaults are shoulder height60 cm,
total length 250 cm, weight 70 kg and Medium size, without HUGE. Normal natural
attacks choose claws or bite at the existing rate. The anatomical tail stays,
but the inherited tail attack is removed. The code-only natural_armour trait
is -15. Native spec_breath_acid is 100%; remove a duplicate individual record.
There is no older-dragon fear, magic resistance, tail slap or spell package.
NPC water rules already permit its swimming/water breathing. Other combat
scalars are neutral; archetype XP +10 and normal breath +10 give modifier 120.
These DD4 measurements, armour and XP are estimates informed by the 2e hatchling.

The wyrmling's individual AFF mask omits native Detect Invisible so XOR does
not remove it. Keep its Sanctuary/Protection. Clear its individual mute bit
to allow speech. Older black-dragon powers are for later age profiles.

The shared lich profile is 175 cm, 45 kg, Medium, with infrared and immunity to
hold/paralysis alongside its existing undead defences. It stays conscious,
organless and inorganic, with its soul and capacity for pain. Its existing
fear_aura works independently of special turns, using the normal visible
opponent/save/Flee rules once per opponent per fight.

The internal lich_chill_touch trait gives a 20% chance after an ordinary
unarmed hit actually hurts a living organic target. It needs usable arms
without arm trauma. It adds 1-10 cold damage through ordinary defences; a
surviving target hurt by that cold then saves against paralysis. Failure
causes 2 combat rounds of short HOLD/DAZED paralysis. Existing hold/daze or
paralysis is never stacked/refreshed. The ghoul-only elf exemption does not
apply. Weapons, ranged attacks, named skills, misses and zero-damage hits
cannot trigger it. Existing paralysis cures and the shared contact timer
recover the victim. Removing arm trauma restores the touch. Targeted arm
trauma does not expire naturally.
Regenerate retains its existing harmful effect on NPC undead.
The trait lives only on species/archetypes in code and appears in MSTAT.
Other lich combat scalars stay neutral; its archetype XP modifier is +10.

The Lich Priest uses an explicit 70% spec_lich / 30% spec_cast_cleric configuration,
replacing four overwritten legacy rows. One routine runs per opportunity.
Its existing weighted 15/10 special bonuses round to 14, giving modifier 124
with the archetype bonus. Keep its individual no-corpse bit but allow speech.
No phylactery quest or automatic undead-healing contact is introduced.

Jim uses only spec_fido at 100% and stays living human, as requested. The High
Priestess uses existing spec_cast_priestess at 100%, keeping its normal level
limits: Curse is available at her present level, other spells unlock later.
Its existing XP calculation is unchanged. Ordinary priests keep their
non-casting roles. No new player skill, body part, ACT/RES bit, trait area
field or individual trait override is added. The short-contact paralysis
clock/display names now cover liches as well as ghouls; old ghoul/ghast
durations, saves, elf exception and ordinary long paralysis stay unchanged.

limbless_construct is only a body plan: inorganic, without a physical head,

The blade is 50 cm long, 1 kg and Small, with no standing-height estimate.
It flies without invented wings and slashes with its own body. It has no
added material resistance or vulnerability, held duplicate weapon or new loot.

The internal natural_attack_type trait supplies the blade's ordinary slash
verb (3) when no usable natural attack part is configured. Zero leaves the

bypass, area field, body part, ACT/RES bit or individual trait override.

cave_beast uses a humanoid body shape, not a human identity or giant family.

Walls of Anon creatures
----------------------
Walls of Anon now uses the human, elf, dwarf, spectre and ghost archetypes.
The four wall captains are rare, the knight and battle mage are elite, and
the marshal is a boss. The vile spectre is rare; everyone else stays common.
Rank affects encounter strength, not physical size. The burly worker has an
individual weight of 95 kg; the one-eyed sailor still has a useful eye.

Spectres are Medium spirits, 175 cm tall, with their mass left unspecified.
They inherit cold, charm, hold and paralysis immunity alongside the existing
poison, sleep, drain and ordinary nonmagical-contact immunities. They retain
their minds, souls and capacity for pain, but have no organic blood or corpse.
Their native spec_spectre supplies the existing life drain and phase behaviour.
The Walls spectre uses that special instead of the broad undead caster.

The code-only sunlight_powerless trait stops a spectre's ordinary attacks
and native life drain in direct natural sunlight. It also prevents automatic
ethereal hiding and draws an ethereal spectre back to semi-material form.
An explicit phase of none stays none. Movement, fleeing and the ordinary
incoming-damage rules still work; sunlight itself causes no damage.
Exposure uses the same rules as sunlight sensitivity: a cloudless SUN_LIGHT
day, outdoors, outside both underwater sectors, without dark or no_weather.
Artificial light does not count. Shelter restores normal behaviour.
This is a species/archetype trait in code, shown with its origin in MSTAT;
there is no new area field, Scribe command or player racial ability.

The elf and dwarf inherit infrared rather than cancelling it with duplicate
area flags. Authored human infrared and all other individual buffs stay.
The two spirits' sexes now match their descriptions and existing portraits.

quest exclusion; this does not add the separate priest services role.
The mail gauntlets now advertise hands, matching their existing reset slot.
All room flags, wards, original placements, limits, equipment and keys stay.

script_controller uses the existing mysterious_being body for an abstract
room-script actor. It has no body parts, physical organs, blood, soul, pain,

Arika's Someone keeps its raw WIZINVIS, level 1000, blank keywords, short name,

The local vampire keeps spec_mast_vampire and gains the reviewed vampire

The other staff programs, individual specials, behaviour, flight/sensing/protection

There is no matching Arika Scribe source. The complete Scribe manual and area
guide describe these profiles; existing source/area pairs remain in step.

Speech aliases and Arika food orders
-----------------------------------
Use speech_prog any followed by a list of whole-word aliases when one action
should run once per utterance. In a Scribe mob record, for example:

    mp speech_prog any bread travel
    mpecho The baker offers you one loaf.
    ~

The generated area header is >speech_prog any bread travel~. Saying "bread",
"travel", "buy travel bread" or "bread bread" runs that program once.
Matching is case-insensitive and uses the existing whole-word boundaries.
Other matching programs still run. Keep aliases for the same action together
instead of defining overlapping programs. The new form applies only to speech;
existing word lists and p-phrase triggers retain their behaviour.

Arika's WIZINVIS food controller now has five triggers: menu, wine, waterskin,
bread/travel and vegetable/stew. Each product order runs once, at its usual
price: one gold for a waterskin or bread, two for stew. The menu is free; wine
still produces the chef's refusal, once. All earlier accepted aliases remain.
Insufficient gold refuses the order without an item or charge, and the chef
returns to the kitchen after the interaction. Room rental and staff programs
are separate and retain their behaviour. Someone keeps its WIZINVIS flag,
level, physiology and counter placement. There is no matching Arika source.

guard and Suturb. Lord Kurbistraht uses the established dwarf archetype.
All six remain common. Their existing levels, behaviour, senses, protection
and flight buffs stay. The guard and Suturb keep spec_cast_psionicist at
100%; the other four keep their quest-program roles without extra specials.

The secretary is 155 cm and 65 kg, Kurbistraht 130 cm and 95 kg, Justinian
175 cm and 75 kg, Xiao Tsu 175 cm and 70 kg, the guard 230 cm and 180 kg,
and Suturb 175 cm and 75 kg. These are descriptive measurements. The guard
is an unusually large human with Large size metadata; his body has no HUGE
flag. The others are Medium. Kurbistraht supplies an individual Medium size
because the shared dwarf profile leaves that metadata unspecified.

Kurbistraht inherits the approved dwarf resistance, infrared, trained melee
traits and XP adjustment. His area mask omits duplicate infrared so XOR
keeps the inherited sense. He and the guard are male, matching their prose
and existing portraits. Other sex settings and all portraits stay.

The area is SAFE and NO_QUEST. Keep both settings and every room flag,
exit, reset, object and reward quantity. Xiao Tsu already receives NO_QUEST
from the area; an extra raw flag is unnecessary. Suturb keeps QUESTMASTER
and his ordinary random-quest service. The huge guard still admits visitors
through level 25 and returns higher-level visitors to Kurbistraht's office.

There are 27 programs: two inline and 25 in AGsecret.prg, AGjunior.prg,
AGinterm.prg and AGsenior.prg. Keep those external files with the area.
The secretary now sends levels 26-54 to Justinian and levels 55 onward to
Xiao Tsu. Junior routing is unchanged. The junior second-mission rejection
now correctly refers an over-level visitor to someone less experienced.

Existing mission eligibility and hand-in rules stay, including their older
differences from the briefing ranges. All eight genuine hand-ins retain
their rewards, including the two mithril chokers. No matching Guild Scribe
source exists in the repository, and no parser or shared creature change
is needed for these individual settings.

Aesir Clanhall people and helper

Hildveig carries her existing key and wears chainmail, one spear and round

SOM Headquarters creatures and people
------------------------------------
Manticore 470 uses quadrupedal_mammal / manticore, with four legs, feline
paws and claws, fangs, wings and a spiked tail. It is a living, speaking
creature without hands or arms. Keep its speech: the clan guard warns its
clan over chat. Ordinary claws and bites are fallback attacks, not extra
attacks. The shared profile is Large with HUGE, flight, natural_armour -15
and +5 XP. Its defaults are 183 cm at the shoulder, 457 cm long and 500 kg;
the giant guard overrides those to 210 cm, 550 cm and 700 kg. The 2e entry
informs the anatomy and dimensions; weights and DD4 bonuses are estimates.

Native weapons 471 (tail) and 472 (teeth) keep their existing wield/dual
slots, attack verbs and randomisation. They now have BODY_PART with weapon
value[0] 6 for the tail and 1 for the head. Keep them non-takeable. Do not
add DONOT_RANDOMISE and accidentally activate their old +50 modifiers.
Tail trauma disables the attached tail weapon; targeted strikes can detach
the relevant weapon. Regenerate heals attached parts but does not recreate
an object that was removed. DD4 retains its ordinary bite rules in flight.

The reusable spec_manticore uses the code-only tail_spike_volley trait.
It gets one 25% chance each three-second combat pulse: 1-6 physical piercing
spikes, each doing max(1, level/10) to max(1, level/5) damage before normal
defences. It can fire four volleys per MUD calendar day. It needs a live,
awake combatant, a current opponent in the same room and a usable attached
tail weapon. Hold, daze, tail trauma and detachment prevent firing. Every
spike rechecks both creatures and stops on death, movement or retargeting.
No poison, cross-room shots, ammunition objects or new aggression are added.

Ordinary manticores inherit spec_manticore. This guard keeps its individual
spec_clan_guard at 100%; in combat that routine delegates to the same volley.
The combat hook makes exactly one opportunity per combat pulse, independently
of the four-second mobile update. No other guard gains spikes: the trait is
assigned only in the manticore profile. The +5 XP belongs to that profile;
the special adds no second XP bonus. Day/charge counters stay in NPC memory,
not area files. MSTAT shows the trait value and its archetype origin. Assign
native tail equipment when using this archetype for another area.

Myrithin 471 uses human, 175 cm / 75 kg, and keeps spec_cast_adept and both
clan-only programs. WolfStar 472 uses humanoid / winged_human with wings and
flight, normal living physiology and human family. Shared defaults are
175 cm / 85 kg; his large build overrides them to 200 cm / 110 kg, Medium.
The descriptive angel identity adds no celestial immunities or spellcasting.
Clear only his unused ACT value 16; preserve aggression, wimpy, scavenging,
identify, shop stock, 150/75 profit fields and 0-23 hours. All four mobs stay
common, at their authored levels, sexes and neutral alignments.

Helper 473 uses script_controller, with dimensions unspecified. Omit raw
NO_QUEST so it stays inherited. Keep NOCHARM, WIZINVIS, sentinel, detection
and its program. The Crystal Sword 473 remains a clan object: it is dropped
in healing room 474 before the helper retreats to shared room 537 and
meditates. Do not add NON_CORPOREAL or turn the artefact into a weapon.

Remove only NO_UNDEAD from clan home 472 and healing room 474. Great Hall
471 stays warded, so vampires still burn while walking through it. Keep all
other room flags, exits, door states, key, reset placements/caps, programmes,
stock and clan item references. Correct WolfStar's two spelling errors.
There is no matching SOM Scribe source in the repository.

fangs and anatomical claws, but only fangs supply ordinary natural attacks.
It can speak and has no arms. Default size is Small and body length 10cm;

an ordinary small bat with a voice, not the larger vampire-bat form.

MOC bat (362) keeps its individual fireshield, greeting and spec_repairman.
That special repairs bashed doors, not equipment. Its raw body mask is 0:
adding NO_ARMS again would cancel the inherited restriction through XOR.

magical_spirit uses mysterious_being. It is invisible, inorganic, bodyless,
corpseless and ANALGIC, with speech, a mind and a soul. It is not undead.
Its physical measurements and size are unknown. It has no extra damage
immunity or phasing. Inorganic physiology already prevents blood extraction;
do not infer that every spirit must be soulless or mindless.

transport. These services are not defaults for every magical_spirit. Its

Coven of Bone creatures and services
-----------------------------------
alhoon uses humanoid anatomy with mouth tentacles, a brain and a voice for
its telepathic messages. It is undead and bloodless, retaining its mind,
soul and physical remains. Defaults are 183cm/65kg/Medium. It has RES_MAGIC,
sleep/charm immunity, natural armour -10 and +10% archetype XP. It does not
inherit a human lich's chill touch, fear aura or other specific immunities.

The internal turn_immunity and holy_water_immunity traits express those
particular defences. They are shown by MSTAT, not saved as area flags or
individual overrides. Holy spells and other undead rules still apply.
psionic_casting_pct is 70 for alhoon. spec_alhoon chooses the psionicist
routine 70% of the time and the mage routine 30% when attempting a cast.
It requires a living local opponent, a usable head and brain, and ordinary
combat permissions. Injuries and incapacitation can stop it.

Ythguan (480) keeps spec_clan_guard at 100%. While fighting, the guard uses
the inherited casting trait to delegate to spec_alhoon. Other guards keep
their existing behaviour, including the manticore combat volley. Generic
alhoon inherit spec_alhoon without needing a clan guard or a particular vnum.
Clear Ythguan's raw infravision bit: the profile already supplies it, and a
duplicate raw bit would cancel it through XOR.

Tentacles (481) are a head-attached ITEM_BODY_PART weapon, value[0] = 1,
with their existing suction verb and main-hand slot. They are not a heart
weapon: category 5 means heart. Head trauma or loss of the head disables
head-attached weapons without removing them from inventory. Regenerate
restores an injured attached weapon's use for an eligible target; it does
not recreate a disarmed one. Existing healing rules still apply: Regenerate
harms undead NPCs, while player vampires keep their established exception. Psionic force (480) stays defined for compatibility, but is no longer
equipped as a physical weapon. Keep the tentacles' existing randomisation.

drow uses humanoid anatomy and the elf family. Defaults are 150cm/45kg/Medium,
with infravision, RES_MAGIC and +5% XP. It inherits elf's 90% magical sleep
and charm resistance and ghoul-touch immunity. Its existing sunlight trait
applies -2 accuracy in direct sunlight, without changing its movement or
penalising ordinary torches. This NPC profile does not change player Drow.
Nroba (484) is an individual 145cm/35kg drow with spec_cast_mage. That role
does not make every drow a wizard. Keep her shop and authored behaviour.

Blackbane (481) uses lich and boss rank; Ythguan uses rare. Apply the normal
rank HP multiplier once. The Anatomist (482) is human, 190cm/55kg, and keeps
spec_cast_adept plus clan-only bless and bye. Existing healer/undead-healer
flags add the normal HEAL menu and mend services: 15 gold for 100HP, 35 gold
for 300HP, subject to existing limits and safety. Do not add priest services.
Smittie (485) uses ordinary zombie; helper (483) uses script_controller.
The helper already omits raw NO_QUEST, so keep its raw ACT list as it is.

All rooms keep their flags. Home (490) and healing room (484) remain open to undead.
The staff and chest mentioned in descriptions remain props, not new loot.
Full drow daily innate magic/bright-light rules, tentacle grappling and brain
extraction, and exact tabletop alhoon poison recovery remain later work.

The River teacher and marine supplier

Witch (201) and sailor (202) use human and common rank. They inherit ordinary
living human anatomy and descriptive 175cm/75kg/Medium defaults. The witch
has an individual MobWeight 85 because her description calls her robust.
Her height stays inherited; age alone does not give her a stoop or a penalty.

Keep the witch's PRACTICE, sentinel and NO_QUEST flags. Her individual
spec_cast_psionicist reflects her stated expertise and acts against combat
attackers. It adds no aggression or racial powers to other humans. Preserve
teacher base 15, all nine discipline/magic group limits 75 and psionic base 30.
Those two base records have different purposes. Keep her held flask (202) and
the red book (201) on the cottage floor; the wand remains a described prop.

In an area file, put the < archetype~ rank~ line before the teacher's &
records. Optional MobWeight metadata follows the teaching list. Scribe
already writes those fields in the loader's expected order.

The sailor has no special. Keep his protection and fame flags, good alignment,
shop 120/90, opening hours 0-23 and boat/potion buy types. Preserve canoe (203),
breathe-water potion (204) and inflatable bladder (205) with their original stock
resets. Selling the bladder does not require adding armour to his buy list.

Both ranks stay common. Old-format HP/damage expressions are ignored by the
