water/freezing/SAFE/NO_MOB settings, cottage doors, objects and reset caps. No shared
profile, engine, parser, player skill or lesson-group changes are needed.

Gloomy Forest villagers
-----------------------
The waif child (250) and starving woman (251) use human with common rank.
Their individual measurements reflect their descriptions: 125 cm, 20 kg and
Small for the child; 175 cm, 45 kg and Medium for the woman. These measurements
are descriptive estimates. The existing height, weight and size fields handle
them, and MobSize 0 explicitly means Small. Both keep ordinary living human
anatomy, organs, blood, speech, souls and pain.

Keep spec_fido at 100% on each villager. It devours NPC corpses and spills
their contents safely onto the floor. Player corpses remain intact, and the
special itself grants no healing or nutrition. This grim behaviour belongs
to these starving villagers. Keep their SCAVENGER/STAY_AREA flags, wandering,
levels, alignment, sexes, quest eligibility and six reset placements/caps.

Their raw AFF list is 32768|65536: SNEAK and HIDE. Remove the unexplained
permanent faerie-fire bit (256). In current DD4, that raw bit supplies a Pink
Aura and blocks another faerie-fire cast. HIDE still uses its usual visibility
checks, and the actual spell supplies its armour modifier through a separate
timed effect. Removing the raw bit restores ordinary spell eligibility.

Room 287 gains DARK and uses the FOREST sector to match its description.
Its exits stay the same, as do every other room's flags and sector. The prose
corrections follow the existing paths and fix the repeated mist typo.

Plains of the North creatures
----------------------------
Use human for Aruncus (300), Sorbus (301), both Ofcol citizens (303/304),
Luxan (306) and the innkeeper (308). Aruncus is 190 cm/65 kg; the innkeeper
is 195 cm/120 kg. The other humans inherit 175 cm/75 kg and Medium size.
Their old headless/limbless/mute/corpseless masks are cleared where needed.
Keep Aruncus's evil alignment and individual spec_cast_druid. Sorbus keeps
his blood jar, both shopkeepers keep their Ofcol shops, and everyone keeps
their authored behaviour and original equipment.

giant_worm uses limbless_invertebrate. It has a head, eyes, brain, heart and
guts, no arms/legs/speech, fangs for a normal piercing bite, and HUGE anatomy.
Defaults are 70 cm body thickness, 460 cm length, 1000 kg and Large size.
spec_swallow uses the existing 15% eligible combat check and permits one
swallowed victim. Large targets are allowed; HUGE targets are refused.
Normal safety, trauma, escape and cleanup rules apply. Shudde-M'ell (305)
is individually boss rank, with the usual 7x HP applied once. Its guardian
behaviour, detect-invisible and carried scroll/potion remain. The profile
does not add venom, blood drain, fear, stone armour or special immunities.

rabbit uses quadrupedal_mammal, with paws, claws, tail and ordinary living
organs. Defaults are 25 cm height, 40 cm length, 2 kg and Small size.
DETECT_HIDDEN represents its keen senses. Its incisors are an attached
ITEM_BODY_PART weapon, not fangs: object 313 uses head category 1 and bite
verb 10, without TAKE, randomisation or extra combat affects. Keep one E
reset beside each rabbit M reset in Plains and Haon Dor. Existing head
trauma, targeting, body-part disarming and Regenerate rules apply; lost
teeth are not recreated by Regenerate. Normal equipped-NPC attack rules
still apply. Do not add fleeing or aggression to these passive rabbits.
Raw body 0 inherits no-arms/no-speech cleanly. The original global caps
stay ten in Plains and five in Haon: five rabbits initially, rising to ten
after repeated Plains refills in the current load order.

dragon_hatchling uses dragon anatomy, with claws/bite, wings, scales and
tail. Defaults are 91 cm height, 250 cm length, 70 kg and Medium size,
with flight, infrared, detect-invisible, detect-hidden and natural_armour
-15. It has no default caster, breath weapon or colour-specific immunity.
Ravan's pet (350) keeps its individual mage, Sanctuary, no-speech and
sentinel/WIMPY settings. Raw AFF 524416 (FLYING|SANCTUARY) cancels inherited
flight while leaving inherited detect-invisible intact. Her description
says she cannot yet get airborne. Keep her Ofcol reset commented out.
The name alone does not grant ACT_PET or make her purchasable.

Dimensions are descriptive estimates, guided by the stated fifteen-foot
worm length and three-foot dragon height. The rabbit's keen senses and
worm's Swallow follow the second-edition small-mammal/worm examples, adapted
to existing DD4 rules. All other ranks stay common. Preserve room flags,
exits, doors, ritual clues, existing loot, shop settings and reset caps.

In the Air creatures
--------------------
faerie_dragon uses dragon anatomy with a normal bite, wings, scales, claws
and tail. Its claws and tail are anatomical, not extra attack choices.
Defaults are 20 cm height, 61 cm length, 2 kg and Small size, with flight,
infrared, detect-invisible, detect-hidden, ordinary RES_MAGIC and
natural_armour -15. It can speak and has ordinary living organs, blood,
soul, pain and a corpse. The local two-foot description guides its length;
height and mass are estimates. Do not infer an age or resistance percentage
from its multicoloured appearance or level.

spec_faerie_dragon takes one active action per eligible opportunity. In
combat, 85% delegates to the existing mage routine and 15% tries a little
euphoric breath against the current opponent. The gas uses existing poison
eligibility and mindless/object restrictions, followed by Confusion's one
normal MAGIC|PSYCHIC save. It deals no direct damage, does not stack or
refresh an existing Confusion effect, and uses its normal duration, cures,
penalties and wandering rules. At level 5 that is two normal affect ticks,
-2 hitroll and -1 intelligence/wisdom. The victim can still fight: this is
our DD4 adaptation, not full tabletop pacification.

While idle and visible, the special has a 1-in-8 eligible chance to cast
ordinary self-Invisibility. It respects normal duration, dispelling and
revelation on attack. It does not grant WIZINVIS or greater invisibility.
Awake/action, head, brain, trauma, combat target and safety checks apply.
Keep one inherited special; remove the old individual mage entry on 1000
so it does not replace or duplicate the combined routine.

The local fairy dragon keeps passive STAY_AREA, alignment 500, sex 0 and
its original names and level. Raw AFF 16 keeps its individual detect-magic
while detect-invisible comes from inheritance. Raw body 0 allows speech.

quadrupedal_hybrid is an organic four-legged body for mixed creatures such
as griffins and future hippogriffs. It supplies head, eyes, ears, brain,
heart, guts, four legs, forelegs and feet, with no arms. Archetypes choose
their speech restrictions, wings, outer covering and attacks.

griffin adds feathered wings, feathers, beak, claws, lion tail and hind paws,
no speech, flight, detect-hidden and natural_armour -20. Its ordinary claw
or beak attack uses normal NPC timing, without a guaranteed triple attack.
Defaults are 152 cm shoulder height, 275 cm length, 600 kg and Large size;
it is not HUGE. Keep the local black/golden-maned appearance, aggression,
STAY_AREA, alignment 300, sex 1 and authored level. Raw body 0 preserves
inherited no-arms/no-speech instead of cancelling them. Wild griffins do
not automatically gain mount training, horse-only hunting or player-form
abilities. A trained individual can use the existing mountable flag later.

Both ranks stay common. Racial HP/damage/critical/haste modifiers are neutral,
with +5 racial XP each. The faerie special supplies its usual +10 special
adjustment once. Keep every room, exit, reset placement/cap and the rose's
Dionysus/Wendella gift text. Three fairy dragons and one griffin load first;
ordinary refills grow them to the original caps of ten and three.

Draagdim Countryside creatures
-----------------------------
The nine ordinary humans (700-703 and 705-709) use human, with descriptive
175 cm height, 75 kg weight and Medium size. Half-goblin 704 uses
human_goblin at 150 cm, 50 kg and Medium, retaining infrared, anatomical
fangs and goblin-family matching without a free bite or the full goblin's
sunlight penalty. Small spotted dog 710 uses dog with individual height
35 cm, length 65 cm and weight 12 kg, still Small. Its ordinary piercing
bite and passive behaviour stay.

cattle uses quadrupedal_mammal, with ordinary living organs, four legs,
feet, tail, horns and hooves, no arms or speech. Horns and hooves supply
ordinary pierce/pound attack choices at the existing attack rate. They do
not add attacks, and cattle have no fang bite. Defaults are shoulder height
150 cm, length 250 cm, weight 650 kg and Large size, without BODY_HUGE.
The bull (712) individually replaces these with 170 cm, 280 cm and 1000 kg;
the cow (711) keeps the defaults. Hide supplies natural_armour -5, with
zero racial XP adjustment and no resistance, immunity or special package.
Keep the cow passive and the bull aggressive; there is no stampede rule.
The legacy farm_mammal example remains; goat has been rewritten on
quadrupedal_mammal, as described in Sentinel Pass below.

hill_giant and stone_giant use living, speaking humanoid anatomy and the
giant target family, so existing dwarf giant defence applies. Both are
Large with BODY_HUGE. Hill defaults are 490 cm height and 2050 kg;
stone defaults are 550 cm and 4100 kg. Length is unspecified. The priestess
(715) uses the ordinary hill dimensions. Stone giants are living flesh,
not inorganic constructs, and gain no blanket weapon or magic immunity.
Hill natural_armour is -15 and racial XP +5; stone natural_armour is -30
and racial XP +10. Existing equipment armour and affects still apply.
HP, damage, critical and swiftness modifiers remain neutral for all three
new archetypes. Measurements are descriptive; BODY_HUGE retains its
existing gameplay checks independently of size metadata.

These armour values, weights and XP adjustments are DD4 design choices.
Second-edition giant dimensions guide the rounded heights and masses;
the local nine-foot/ten-foot descriptions are lower bounds. Neither rank
nor sex changes an archetype's dimensions. Only priestess 715 is elite,
with the existing 5x rank HP applied once; the other fifteen remain common.

The giants inherit spec_giant as their one 100% special selection. Once
per eligible combat round, it has a 15% chance to throw one native rock
at the current visible, live opponent in the same room. It cannot open a
fight or throw across rooms. Hill damage is max(1, level / 2) + 2d8, with
at most four rocks per game day; stone damage is max(1, level / 2) + 3d10,
with at most eight. Divide the level by two using integer division. The
15% throw roll is inside the ability, not a spchance 15/85 slot split.

A native rock is a physical, nonmagical crush projectile using ordinary
contact, resistance, phase, safety and death handling. It borrows no
magic or material from the giant's equipped weapon. The thrower must be
awake, corporeal, able to act, have working arms and be free of hold/daze.
Each actual throw spends one rock from its bounded daily supply, including
when caught. Counters live only in NPC memory. No authored object is
created, consumed, unequipped or removed from loot.

Hill giants have a 30% chance and stone giants a 90% chance to catch an
explicitly identified native rock projectile, provided the defender is
awake, capable and has working arms. Catching negates its damage. It gives
no inventory item or automatic return throw. It does not catch generic
blunt damage, melee rock weapon 707, arrows or arbitrary player hurl.
The passive catch trait is separate from active special selection.

Priestess 715 keeps her individual spec_cast_druid at 100%. That replaces
inherited active spec_giant, so she casts instead of throwing rocks. Her
hill anatomy, armour and passive 30% catch remain. Monk 706 instead uses
spec_cast_cleric_helper at 100%: while idle it delegates the existing
vampire-safe adept assistance to players; in combat it delegates the old
cleric routine with no rate dilution. Each opportunity permits one action.
There is no weighted adept/cleric split, paid healer/priest service,
teaching or automatic bread distribution. Other clerics stay unchanged.

spec_giant carries the ordinary +10 special XP adjustment once on 713/714,
separately from their +5/+10 racial adjustments. The thief (703), monk
(706) and priestess (715) keep their existing special XP adjustment once;
do not add a second rock-special bonus for the priestess's passive catch.

Use the existing Scribe fields in a $mob block. For example, these are the
bull's selection and individual dimensions, not a complete source file:

    archetype cattle
    rnk common
    height 170
    length 280
    weight 1000

The exporter writes < cattle~ common~, MobHeight 170, MobLength 280 and
MobWeight 1000. Omit dimensions to inherit the profile, or use inherit;
an explicit zero replaces a dimension with unspecified. Values remain
whole centimetres and kilograms. Do not add units or fractional weights.
For the dog use archetype dog, height 35, length 65 and weight 12.

For the priestess, existing explicit slot syntax replaces the whole active
set and its weights:

    archetype hill_giant
    rnk elite
    sp spec_cast_druid
    sp2 none
    sp3 none
    spchance 100 0 0

For the monk use archetype human with the same slot/percentage pattern,
but sp spec_cast_cleric_helper. A legacy singleton sp spec_cast_druid
or sp spec_cast_cleric_helper also replaces the inherited active set.
Ordinary hill_giant/stone_giant sources omit special fields to inherit
spec_giant; explicitly registering its name does not require repeating it.
Armour, racial XP, rock/catch traits and daily counters belong in the code
profiles, not new Scribe commands, area fields, ACT flags or individual
trait overrides. Player race mappings and skills stay unchanged.
Use MSTAT to inspect resolved traits, their species/archetype origins and
explicit removals, alongside the active special slots and percentages.
The priestess should retain hill armour/catch while showing only druid
casting. MSTAT is an inspection command, not a Scribe trait field.

On dog 710 and cattle 711/712, raw body 0 inherits no-arms/no-speech;
the old raw mask 36 would cancel those defaults through XOR. Omit bf or
use bf none for that zero individual mask. Keep every original ACT/AFF
mask, including NO_QUEST, WIMPY, sentinel, aggression and giant senses.
Keep the thief's role, monk's two loaves and priestess's druid role. The
only prose corrections are the bull's 'takes its job' and the hill giant's
'is at least nine feet tall'. All 54 rooms, flags, exits, doors, well
poison, twelve objects, nineteen carried items and original resets/caps
stay. Peasant 702 remains unspawned. There are 28 initial inhabitants and
30 after refills, with the original cow cap of four.

Troll Den creatures
-------------------

Use troll for the ordinary adults and the young troll, troll_infant for
the speaking baby, den_beast for both furry beasts, and carrion_worm for
the fat worm. These are creature profiles, not occupations. Troll and
troll_infant use humanoid; den_beast uses quadrupedal_mammal; carrion_worm
uses limbless_invertebrate. The last two bodies do not acquire troll
physiology merely because they live in the same den.

Trolls are living, speaking humanoids with infrared, claws and anatomical
fangs. Ordinary claw/bite choices use the normal attack rate, without a
guaranteed extra attack. Natural armour is -15 and racial XP is +10.
HP, damage, critical and swiftness modifiers stay neutral. There is no
general resistance, immunity or vulnerability package. Fire and acid
interrupt regeneration; that does not add incoming damage vulnerability.
Troll-family matching still lets a trained dwarf use giant defence.
Keep the local fur descriptions; they do not require a separate species.

Default troll dimensions are 275 cm height, 225 kg and Large, without
BODY_HUGE. Length is unspecified. The young troll (2801) individually
uses 210 cm, 110 kg and Medium. The baby (2803) uses troll_infant defaults
of 140 cm, 80 kg and Medium, without HUGE. Its ordinary anatomy and native
regeneration match troll, but its active special set is empty and its
internal trait profile explicitly disables rock throwing.

The giant troll (2804) uses 400 cm, 650 kg, Large and individual BODY_HUGE;
the large female (2805) uses 350 cm, 450 kg, Large and individual BODY_HUGE.
The two medium trolls (2802/2808) retain the ordinary adult defaults. The
one-third-size line on 2802 describes bulk: 225 kg is three times the
ordinary human default of 75 kg. It is not an exact height ratio. The
giant's name alone does not make it a separate troll/giant hybrid.

Only 2804 is elite and 2805 rare; the other seven definitions stay common.
The existing rank HP multipliers apply once, 5x and 2x respectively.
Rank does not set dimensions or multiply the native healing amount.
The second-edition Troll entry guides the rounded nine-foot adult height,
claws/bite, infrared, regeneration and occasional stones. All masses,
individual measurements, ranks, numerical bonuses and exact DD4 timings
are design adaptations. Dimensions remain descriptive; BODY_HUGE retains
its separate existing gameplay checks.

Native troll regeneration is passive physiology, independent of active
special slots. While alive, idle or fighting, the troll heals
max(3, level / 5) HP per three-second pulse, using integer division. This
is 3 HP for the den's trolls, with no percentage-of-max-HP or rank scaling.
The first healing begins nine seconds after the first damaging injury;
later ordinary hits do not keep restarting that initial wait.

This single native stream replaces ordinary positive NPC tick healing for
these opted-in profiles. Do not add ACT_REGENERATOR or stack a tick bonus
on top. Existing poison, Prayer Plague and Slow reductions, integer
rounding, ACT_NO_HEAL and the aggravated-damage healing ceiling still
apply. An alive troll does not need working limbs or an awake state for
its passive repair. Player troll regeneration, vulnerabilities, skills
and trait opt-ins remain unchanged.

Actual fire or acid HP damage pauses native regeneration for nine seconds
from the latest damaging event. Pauses refresh rather than accumulate;
there is no banked healing. Misses, immunity and fully blocked damage do
not pause it. This includes fire/acid spells, breaths, damage categories,
direct Fireshield retaliation, elemental traps and actual damaging
Firebrand fire procs. Merely carrying Firebrand, missing with it or being
immune to its fire does not count. Ordinary external healing spells keep
their existing rules; this pause applies to native regeneration.

Every thirty seconds of active regeneration can repair at most one
supported injury category. Fire or acid pauses and resets that injury
recovery progress. Focused repair shares the supported Regenerate injury
logic without repairing aggravated damage, casting the whole spell or
recreating disarmed body-part objects. Death remains final: there is no
postmortem revival, independent severed limb or replacement object.
Shared climbing and food-distraction pursuit rules remain future work.

Ordinary and young trolls inherit spec_giant as their one 100% active
selection. Within that ability, each eligible combat round has a 15%
chance to throw one native rock at the current visible, living opponent
in the same room. Damage is max(1, level / 2) + 1d8, with integer division
and at most two rocks per game day. Trolls have no rock catch. The infant
cannot throw even if an author explicitly selects spec_giant: the native
throw trait is disabled. Passive regeneration is not an active special.

The existing giant ability's working-arm, awake/capable, hold/daze,
corporeal, safety, physical-contact and death rules apply. It cannot open
a fight or throw across rooms. Bounded ammunition is NPC memory, not an
authored object; no item is created, consumed, unequipped or taken from
loot. Its 15% check is inside spec_giant, not spchance 15 0 0. The ordinary
+10 special XP adjustment applies once on the five non-infant trolls,
separately from racial +10. The infant has racial +10 without special XP.

den_beast is a living canine-like quadrupedal mammal, without forcing a
wolf, worg or hell-hound identity. It has tail, paws, anatomical claws and
fangs, but only ordinary piercing fang-bite attacks, no arms and no speech.
Natural armour is -5; racial XP is zero, and HP, damage, critical and
swiftness modifiers are neutral. It adds no special, magical sense,
elemental power or troll regeneration. Both beasts (2800/2807) use
90 cm shoulder height, 180 cm total length, 120 kg and Medium, without
HUGE. Keep authored detect-invisible on both and HIDE on the guardian;
red eyes alone do not establish an additional power. Dog defaults stay.

carrion_worm is a living limbless invertebrate with no distinct external
head or eyes, no arms, legs or speech, and ordinary living organs, blood,
pain and soul. Its profile cancels inherited PART_HEAD/PART_EYE through
XOR and supplies BODY_NO_HEAD/BODY_NO_EYES; absence flags alone would not
cancel the positive anatomy. It is not mindless, bloodless, analgic,
soulless or undead. It has no fang attack, Swallow, venom, HUGE, new sense
or troll regeneration. Ordinary blunt fallback attacks and existing NPC
awareness/visibility rules apply. Defaults are 10 cm body thickness,
150 cm length, 12 kg and Small. Racial XP is zero, and HP, damage,
critical and swiftness modifiers are neutral.

The worm inherits existing spec_fido at 100%. It eats genuine NPC corpses
and spills their contents into the room, without direct HP healing.
It ignores player corpses and scenery containers. Cadaver-container 2800
and bed-container 2801 are scenery, so their belt and ring remain intact.
spec_fido has zero special XP. Preserve the worm's scavenger, aggressive,
wimpy, hide and sneak settings; it lacks STAY_AREA and can wander outside.
Wimpy continues to prevent ordinary aggression against awake players.

Use the existing Scribe fields in a $mob block. For the giant troll,
these optional fields show its selection and individual replacements:

    archetype troll
    rnk elite
    height 400
    weight 650
    size 2
    bf huge

The exporter writes < troll~ elite~, MobHeight 400, MobWeight 650 and
MobSize 2. bf huge supplies the individual raw BODY_HUGE mask (128), not
another size value. Medium is size 1, Large size 2 and Small size 0.
Omit dimensions/size to inherit, or use inherit. Explicit height/length/
weight zero means unspecified; size 0 explicitly means Small. Dimensions
use whole centimetres and kilograms, without units or fractional values.
For the young troll use height 210, weight 110, size 1, with bf none.

For ordinary troll, troll_infant, den_beast and carrion_worm, omit special
fields to inherit each profile's complete active set. A legacy singleton
sp spec_giant or sp spec_fido replaces that set; it is not an extra slot.
Existing explicit replacement syntax for the worm is:

    archetype carrion_worm
    rnk common
    sp spec_fido
    sp2 none
    sp3 none
    spchance 100 0 0

For a deliberate empty active set, existing sp none, sp2 none, sp3 none
and spchance 0 0 0 clear all slots. To explicitly inherit use sp inherit,
sp2 inherit, sp3 inherit and spchance inherit. Do not add a special merely
to make troll regeneration work. Armour, racial XP, native regeneration,
injury recovery, throw eligibility and daily counters are internal code
profile data. There are no new Scribe commands, area fields, ACT bits or
individual trait overrides. Use MSTAT to inspect resolved traits, their
species/archetype origins, explicit removals, dimensions and active slots.

Set the individual raw body mask to zero on 2800/2807 and 2806 so it does
not cancel inherited animal restrictions. Clear the baby's old raw 32:
its WANNA JOIN dialogue explicitly requires speech. Omit bf or use bf none
for a zero individual mask. Keep individual HUGE 128 only on 2804/2805.
Correct the giant's legacy 13e13+130 dice text to 13d13+130; the loader
discards these dice, so this does not add HP beyond the approved rank.

Keep all other ACT/AFF masks, sexes, alignments, levels, fur and local
prose, including SNEAK, SCAVENGER, aggression and the roving beast's actual
SENTINEL. Preserve all five rooms, flags, doors, exits, ambient text and
reset message, all six objects and original reset ordering/caps/placements.
Bedroom 2805 remains DARK/NO_MOB/INDOORS; NO_MOB restricts wandering entry,
not its deliberate resets. Skin 2802 stays equipped on giant 2804, belt
2804 in cadaver-container 2800, ring 2803 in bed-container 2801 and bone
2805 on the floor. There are 18 initial inhabitants and 33 after refills.
There is no matching Scribe source to regenerate for this area.

Illustrations should show canine-like beasts and a carrion worm without
a distinct head, eyes or limbs.


Gnome Village creatures
-----------------------

Use gnome for adult gnomes and gnome_untrained for the child. Both use
humanoid anatomy, infrared, RES_MAGIC, racial XP +5 and descriptive
105 cm / 40 kg / Small defaults. The child uses 70 cm / 12 kg.
RES_POISON is not part of this package.

Gnome training gives +1 melee against goblins and kobolds. Gnome giant
defence gives the attacker -4 melee when it is a gnoll, bugbear, ogre,
ogre mage, troll, giant or titan. These internal traits have separate
target lists from dwarf training. The child explicitly removes both
learned benefits while keeping its physiology. MSTAT shows values,
sources and removals. No ACT markers, area trait fields or new player
abilities are added. Existing matching player races can be targets.
Kobold, gnoll and bugbear are target-family placeholders awaiting full
creature review.

Keep individual scientist mage, chief cleric and hobgoblin king mage
specials. Occupation does not make every gnome a caster. Construction
sensing, animal communication and magical-item malfunction await shared
mechanics; this package does not grant blanket illusion immunity.

Hobgoblins reuse infrared, 200 cm / 100 kg / Medium defaults. Bodyguard
1518 uses 180 cm / 90 kg; king 1519 is 120 kg; cook 1526 is 140 kg.
Trolls reuse native regeneration and bounded rock throws. Small troll
1507 uses 210 cm / 140 kg / Medium; giant troll 1512 uses 400 cm /
650 kg / Large with individual HUGE. Both retain authored no-speech.

Mouse and rat use quadrupedal_mammal with head, organic internals, paws,
claws, tail and fangs, but no arms or speech. Ordinary attacks select
fangs only. Mouse defaults are 3 cm height / 10 cm body length; rat is
8 cm / 20 cm. Whole-kg weights stay unspecified: this does not mean zero
physical mass. Large rat 1510 uses 15 cm / 2 kg / 35 cm length; giant
rat 1511 uses 50 cm / 25 kg / 90 cm length, matching its dog-sized prose.
All remain Small.

The three rats keep authored venom through spec_poison_bite. It requires
usable head/fang anatomy before using existing poison chance, target
selection, saves and physiology checks. It gives no extra ordinary
attack and does not make every rat venomous. Disease awaits shared work.
Black bat 1513 keeps ordinary bat flight/echolocation, individual 10 cm
body length and authored spec_breath_acid_anatomical. This wrapper checks
head presence and head trauma before using the existing breath routine.

Fire bat uses the new elemental_being body, not the old fire_elemental
test profile. It is inorganic, with functional head, eyes, two legs,
wings and fangs, but no arms, speech, heart or corpse. It has flight,
infrared, fire immunity, ordinary piercing bite and racial XP +10.
The area's detect-invisible flag remains individual. Defaults are
30 cm height / 61 cm body length / Small, weight unspecified.
Its inherited spec_breath_fire_anatomical uses the ordinary breath
chance and spell, requiring a usable head. No undead, mindless, soulless
or nonmagic-immunity package is added. Inorganic physiology rules out
blood; ANALGIC supplies pain insensitivity. No Fireshield,
blood-latching, fission or planar reformation is added.

Giant hermit crab uses organic crustacean anatomy: head, eyes/eyestalks,
claw-bearing forelimbs, multiple legs, heart, brains, guts and shell,
without speech. Forelimbs use the existing arm targeting region without
implying human hands. Shell is anatomy; natural armour -20 is separate.
Defaults are 152 cm height / 250 cm body length / 350 kg / Large without
HUGE, racial XP +5. Height follows the five-foot description; mass and
length are estimates.

Native pincer 1525 is an ITEM_BODY_PART weapon: value[0] = 3 (arm),
value[3] = 8 (crush), no TAKE or randomisation, one normal wield reset.
Arm injury disables an attached pincer while inventory lookups still
find it. Regenerate can restore its use while attached, but cannot
recreate a disarmed part. There is no extra attack stream or new target
category. This profile does not select natural claw attacks separately.
The existing NPC wielded-weapon damage and reset XP rules still apply.
NPC water movement already permits this amphibious crab, and NPCs are
outside the drowning check; no temporary SWIM or new water field is needed.

Chief 1505 and king 1519 are elite; giant troll 1512, fire bat 1515 and
bodyguard 1518 are rare; all others are common. Rare gives 2x HP and
elite 5x, once. Rank can affect generated equipment: unchanged object
definitions do not promise identical randomised items. Guard 1516 stays
unspawned.

Clear undefined decimal ACT 16 only on child 1500 and woman 1501,
keeping SCAVENGER. Reconcile inherited anatomy/XOR: zero old rodent,
bat, zombie and crab masks; retain troll no-speech 32 and add HUGE 128
only to giant troll 1512. Zombie 1514 keeps ordinary zombie physiology,
individual 105 cm / 40 kg / Small and male sex matching prose/portrait.
Remove its generic undead caster; the intentional Catacombs zombie
mage remains unchanged.

Carry the scientist's skiff 1514, rat's shield 1505 and large rat's staff
1503 rather than equipping them. Keep loot while removing equipped
benefits and corresponding reset XP. Raise the first guard 1504 reset
limit in room 1519 from 3 to 6, matching other rows. Key 1500 opens the
locked 1536/1537 door. The six-guard ceiling stays, but refills favour
the key/potion/cloak guard in 1519 instead of an ordinary sword guard.
This adds two carried items relative to the former refill arrangement.

Preserve other behaviour, levels, alignment, descriptions, rooms,
doors, flags, maze resets and existing objects. Club 1521 is also given
to Dave the Dealer in Midgaard. Fix chief's ignored 1e12 dice typo to
1d12 and treasurer's old goblin wording to old hobgoblin. There is no
matching Scribe source.

AD&D second edition Monstrous Manual entries for Gnome, Hobgoblin, Bat,
Rat, Small Mammal and Giant Crustacean inform anatomy. Ranks, masses,
DD4 armour/XP and local magical powers are adaptations. Fire bat is the
area's elemental interpretation, not every published fire-bat ability.


Rats Lair creatures
-------------------

Use rat, domestic_cat and human for the ordinary mammals and people.
Snake, wererat, firedrake and green_ooze add the new reviewed profiles.
These are creature identities, not occupations. Their existing area
roles and aggression/passivity flags remain individual.

Snake uses serpentine_reptile: organic head, eyes, heart, organs, tail,
scales and fangs, with no arms, legs or speech. It has a normal fang bite
and natural armour -5; no default venom or heat sight. Defaults are
120 cm body length, 2 kg and Small, height unspecified. Snigger overrides
length to 213 cm and weight to 5 kg, retaining his authored venom,
Sanctuary and Protection. Rat venom stays individual too. Use
spec_poison_bite for these ten authored bites; its XP contribution now
matches the original spec_poison at +5, rather than the generic +10.
Its target selection, chance and venom effects stay unchanged.

Small rat 3801 uses 4 cm height/12 cm body length, ordinary rat 3804 uses
8 cm/20 cm, and arm-length rats 3805/3811 use 20 cm/60 cm/5 kg.
All are Small; tiny whole-kg weights stay unspecified. Sammy uses the
ordinary domestic_cat profile with individual 12 cm shoulder height,
25 cm length and 1 kg, consistent with her under-three-pound description.
Keep the pets' authored level40, protection, Sanctuary and behaviour.

Wererat uses humanoid anatomy with normal arms, hands, organs and speech,
plus rat tail, claws and fangs. Weapons keep ordinary precedence; unarmed
attacks use fangs. Defaults are 150 cm, 55 kg and Medium; natural armour
-5 and racial XP +10. Leader3810 uses180cm/90kg; slavemaster3812 uses
170cm/75kg. The guard's male description and portrait support sex male.

IMM_NONMAGIC and the internal silver_bypasses_nonmagic trait implement
the silver-or-magic weapon defence. Ordinary mundane physical attacks
cannot hurt it; silver and magical weapons deal normal defended damage.
Silver gains no extra vulnerability multiplier. Normal spell categories
still apply. No new ACT bit, area field or player trait opt-in is added.
All five existing wererat venom specials remain local overrides, not
universal racial venom. Do not copy PC werewolf abilities. Shapechanging,
infection, rat control and human-corpse reversion await shared mechanics.

Firedrake uses dragon anatomy with flight, scales, wings, claws, tail and
fangs, without arms or speech. Only fangs supply ordinary natural attacks.
Keep the area's detect-invisible flag. Defaults are65cm height/122cm body
length/25kg/Small, no HUGE, armour-10, fire resistance and racial XP+10.
It has resistance rather than a true dragon's blanket fire immunity.

The inherited spec_firedrake keeps the old fire-breath spell and target
chance with a usable-head check. Its internal fire_breath_daily_casts
trait supplies five successful casts per MUD day. A failed target/head/
action check spends no charge; immunity or a successful target save does
not refund a spell already cast. The full day/month/year controls refill,
not the hour or an area reset. Ordinary attacks continue after exhaustion.
Runtime counters and the re-entry guard never come from area fields.

The internal burning_blood_sides trait is2: a damaging close-range cutting
or piercing hit can splash1d2 fire. A normal fire save negates the reaction.
Fire resistance/immunity, Resist Heat and ordinary protective reductions
apply. Blunt, ranged, missed and zero-damage attacks do not trigger it.
The source must still have usable blood. The reaction is applied inside
the original damage event, so its existing death handling can settle either
or both combatants; it does not create a recursive second attack.

Successful live vampire feeding also exposes the feeder once. A failed
bite, no defended HP loss or no actual blood gain on a metered feed gives
no reaction. Hot blood is gained once before its burn is settled; the
old path remains for ordinary prey. NPC vampire throat attacks use their
existing unmetered feeding path. No Fireshield, free extra attacks,
blood-harvesting/firebomb/coating system or corpse-chemistry system is
added. Existing corpse FEED remains unchanged.

Green ooze is a local living blob, not the tabletop Green Slime
infestation. Its amorphous_organism body has no head, eyes, limbs, heart
or speech, and is bloodless but organic. The archetype is mindless, which
already supplies pain insensitivity. It is not automatically soulless,
inorganic or undead. It has poison/sleep/charm/hold/paralysis immunity,
ordinary blunt pseudopod attacks, ordinary remains and racial XP+5.
It has no armour, weapon/elemental immunity, corrosion, acid proc,
flesh conversion, division, engulfment, flight or Pass Door. Defaults are
30cm maximum thickness/150cm spread length/80kg/Medium, all estimates.

Slaves use ordinary human defaults175cm/75kg/Medium. The explicitly mortal
manifestations also use human: Hatchet185cm/90kg, Icaza180cm/75kg, reflecting
statuesque prose. Keep Hatchet's intentional living undead-caster special,
Icaza's cleric special, level40, NO_QUEST and protection/sight flags.
Their miniguns remain wand objects in HOLD with the original spell/charges.

Only leader3810 is elite; firedrake3809 and slavemaster3812 are rare.
The named pets and mortal manifestations stay common because their
authored level40/protection already defines their strength. Rare HP is2x,
elite5x, once; rank can also affect generated gear.

Clear raw animal36, Snigger60 and ooze63 after inheritance; otherwise XOR
can restore missing parts. Snigger's old missing-heart bit is corrected.
Guard3807 becomes male; Icaza's ignored40e40 dice typo becomes40d40 with
no HP effect. Sammy's broken sentence is corrected without changing intent.

All43 room records/flags/doors/exits,25 existing objects,28 reset inhabitants
and24 carried items remain. Keep wards3838/3839, keys, shrine/portal puzzle,
scenery containers with torch/gem contents, poetry and miniguns. No matching
Scribe source exists. No new body bits, target categories, area fields or
individual trait overrides are needed.

The main references are AD&D second edition Monstrous Manual entries for
Wererat/Lycanthrope, Dragonet/Firedrake, Snake, Small Mammal and the
Ooze/Slime/Jelly groups. Fire-blood timing/save integration, whole-kg masses,
DD4 armour/XP and the local green ooze are deliberate DD4 adaptations.


Drow City creatures
-------------------

Drow City uses the existing humanoid/drow profile for ten definitions,
and humanoid/goblin for the slave. Drow defaults remain 150 cm, 45 kg and
Medium, with infrared, RES_MAGIC, racial XP +5 and the existing elf
sleep, charm and ghoul defences. Their sunlight accuracy penalty stays -2.
The goblin defaults remain 120 cm, 30 kg and Small.

Keep the slave's sentinel/wimpy behaviour and sleeping description. DD4
ignores its old position numbers, so its actual spawn position stays
standing. This conversion preserves that behaviour.

Clear guard 5110's individual infrared bit so XOR leaves inherited
infrared on. Keep its Hide and scout 5111's Hide/Sneak. These four guards
and scouts spawn in dark Thalos forest rooms, where the existing sunlight
test gives no penalty. The Thalos file stays unchanged.

Master 5105 is rare; weapons master 5106 and house matron 5107 are elite;
first-house matron 5108 and yochlol 5109 are bosses. Rank HP applies once:
rare 2x, elite 5x and boss 7x. Keep mage 5103 and clerics 5104/5108;
house matrons 5107 now also use spec_cast_cleric. Masters keep their wands.

Yochlol uses amorphous_organism. Its upper eye/mouth region supports head
targeting, and its eight pseudopods use the existing arms, many-arms and
tentacle tags. It has one eye, no human hands or fingers, no ordinary legs
or heart, and no blood. It is organic, intelligent, ensouled and able to
feel pain. Its reusable profile can speak and makes no corpse. This
particular yochlol stays mute: raw body 96 becomes 32, keeping NO_SPEECH
while inherited NO_CORPSE remains on after XOR.

Its estimated dimensions are 180 cm, 100 kg and Medium. It has neutral
HP, damage, critical and haste modifiers, no natural armour, and racial
XP +10. Ordinary DD4 resistance flags cover magic, cold, fire, electricity
and poison. Its solid form takes ordinary weapon damage.

Mist describes its arrival; it fights in its solid one-eyed column form.
Its eight pseudopods share one native magical body-part WEAPON, object
5115, providing one normal wielded attack stream. Use arm category 3,
pound verb 7, MAGIC, BODY_PART and DONOT_RANDOMISE, with no TAKE. Prototype
damage values 3/4 match the old dagger. Creation still gives weapons the
engine's level-based damage range before DONOT_RANDOMISE is checked;
ordinary NPC attack damage is level-based too. The dagger's hit/damage
bonuses are not copied to the native weapon.

Existing arm injury, disarming and Regenerate rules apply. Regenerate
heals an attached injured weapon; it does not recreate a disarmed object.
Without a usable weapon the yochlol can make ordinary blunt natural
attacks. Dagger 5113 stays unchanged as carried loot. Native body weapons
are cleaned up at death, while the dagger drops normally.

The individual spec_breath_any_anatomical keeps spec_breath_any's random
breath distribution and a single 100% special slot. It requires a usable
head without head trauma, like the other anatomical breath variants.
It has no daily charge limit. Existing users of spec_breath_any keep their
behaviour. Gas-form immunity, shapechanging, engulfment, Domination,
Mind Blank and plane travel await shared demon/form mechanics.

All 56 room flags, exits and doors, all 15 original objects, and all
population placements and caps are preserved. The equipment reset now
carries dagger 5113 and equips the native pseudopods. The green hat stays
carried. Keep authored aggression/passivity, sexes, levels and alignments.
The priestess sentence, ignored yochlol dice typo and two room typos are
corrected. There is no matching Scribe source. Existing area fields and
body/target flags cover the conversion.

The references are the AD&D second edition Monstrous Manual drow entry
and The Drow of the Underdark's yochlol entry (TSR 9326, 1991). Dimensions,
DD4 resistances/XP and the native-weapon mapping are our adaptations.
The portrait should show the approved solid form; the replacement preview
is kept separately from the repository patch.


Holy Grove creatures
--------------------

Holy Grove uses humanoid/human for its six druid definitions, the existing
quadrupedal_mammal/rabbit for its bunny, and serpentine_reptile/snake for
its small snake. Existing profiles and player abilities stay as they are.

The two new profiles, deer and stag, use quadrupedal_mammal. Both have
ordinary organs, blood, senses, pain and remains, with four legs, tail
and hooves. They inherit no arms or speech. Deer is antlerless; stag adds
antlers through the existing PART_HORNS tag. Their ordinary attacks use
blunt hooves, or a choice of antler/pierce and hoof/blunt for stags.
Each selection supplies one normal attack. Existing weapon precedence,
anatomical filters and leg-trauma restrictions apply.

Their armour, HP, damage, critical, haste and racial XP adjustments are
neutral. They have no resistance, immunity, vulnerability or default
aggression, and need no active special or new body/target/trait flag.
Use the clean mammal body. The separate farm_mammal example is not an
animal template; goat now also uses quadrupedal_mammal.

Deer defaults are 100 cm shoulder height, 70 kg, 160 cm length and Medium.
Stag defaults are 130 cm, 180 kg, 210 cm and Medium. Male deer 8905 uses
individual 110 cm/90 kg/180 cm measurements, while the explicitly large
stag 8906 uses the full defaults. These are DD4 estimates. Choosing the
stag profile supplies antlers; changing sex does not rewrite its body.

Humans keep 175 cm/75 kg/Medium. The rabbit keeps its existing 25 cm/2 kg/
40 cm/Small profile and Detect Hidden. The small snake uses 60 cm length,
Small, height unspecified and MobWeight 0. Zero leaves its likely
sub-kilogram weight unknown; it does not make the animal massless.
All dimensions remain descriptive whole-number metadata.

Hierophants 8900/8901 are elite and elders 8902/8903 rare; everyone else
is common. Elite 5x and rare 2x HP apply once. All six authored
spec_cast_cleric assignments stay individual, with their original herb
distributions and equipment. Keep the hierophants' curse detection,
Sanctuary, Protection and sight flags. Age alone does not change physique.

Keep every authored ACT/AFF mask, including STAY_AREA/WIMPY throughout
and the stag/snake AGGRESSIVE+WIMPY combination. Ordinary aggression
usually selects sleeping targets with WIMPY; curse detection has its own
entry path and existing gates. This conversion does not change behaviour.

The snake's harmless name and not-a-cobra joke coexist with an explicit
venom special. Keep its venom as an individual spec_poison_bite, with the
existing usable-head/fang check and original target, chance, effects and
special XP. The shared snake profile remains nonvenomous by default.

Clear raw body 36 on deer/rabbit 8904-8907 so inherited no-arms/no-speech
stays on through XOR. Clear snake 8908's raw 556 after inheriting its
no-arms/no-legs/no-speech, tail, fangs and organs. Inherited PART_TAIL
supplies the old BODY_HAS_TAIL function. Fix the female hierophant's
ignored 20r20+180 typo to 20d20+180 without changing HP, and change the
bunny's sentence from "you wished" to "you wish".

Keep all 20 rooms and their flags, including six NO_UNDEAD rooms, all
14 original objects and every reset/cap. The area has 14 initial
inhabitants and 18 carried items; three refills reach 21 inhabitants
and the same 18 items. Preserve mansion/fog rooms, area music/reset text,
doors, jars, closets, pepper, kernels and the paper's Matrix references.
Keep all herb effects, including mixtures of beneficial spells and poison.
The staffs are wielded weapons and the robes are valid torso armour.
No matching Scribe source or new object is needed.

The references are the AD&D second edition Monstrous Manual Mammal/Wild
Stag and Small Mammal entries. Dimensions and the mapping to DD4's
ordinary attack system are our adaptations.


Desert of Despair creatures
---------------------------

Desert of Despair uses the existing humanoid/human profile for the man,
woman and child, and serpentine_reptile/snake for both venomous snakes.
All five are common. The king-viper name describes the creature; it does
not give it a higher encounter rank or a giant body.

The tall, strong man uses estimated 185 cm height and 85 kg, Medium.
The woman keeps the human defaults of 175 cm/75 kg/Medium. The small child
uses estimated 100 cm/18 kg/Small; level 3 is not being treated as his age.
Height, weight and length are descriptive metadata. Small uses the
existing size field and its usual rules.

The king viper keeps the ordinary snake defaults: 120 cm length, 2 kg,
Small, height unspecified. The small asp uses 60 cm length and MobWeight 0
for unknown sub-kilogram mass, with Small size and height unspecified.
Zero means unknown metadata; the asp is not massless. Keep whole-number
weights and the existing units.

Both snakes' old raw body 60|512 includes NO_HEART as well as no arms,
legs or speech and the old HAS_TAIL bit. Clear that raw mask after adding
the archetype. The inherited serpent body supplies a normal heart and
other organs, head, eyes, scales, tail and fangs, with no arms/legs/speech.
This restores ordinary living anatomy and keeps XOR from cancelling its
inherited restrictions. The existing snake profile supplies a fang attack
and natural armour -5. It adds no default venom, magical senses or
resistances; venom remains an individual choice.

Use spec_poison_bite for both authored venom specials. Its usable-head
and fang checks keep the original target selection, poison effects and
special XP. The man's spec_warrior remains individual. Keep all authored
ACT/AFF masks, levels, alignments and sexes, including his Protection,
sight and Sneak, the woman's scavenging/wimpy behaviour, the child's
wimpy flag, and the snakes' Sneak and AGGRESSIVE/WIMPY combination.
No new racial hostility, active special, trait or player ability is added.

Keep the man's sword 18100 in WIELD16, belt 18105 in WAIST13 and boots
18106 in FEET8. These are valid equipment slots. Keep all seven object
definitions and every population, equipment and treasure reset. The basket
and toy sword remain descriptive details; the unused pocket cloak and key
do not get new resets in this conversion.

Treasure chest 18103 in room 18141 keeps its unusual legacy flags8:
LOCKED is set, but CLOSED and CLOSEABLE are not. Its contents are currently
accessible. Preserve that access and the existing key field/reset rows.
Key 18104 is defined but has no reset found. A working locked-chest puzzle
would need a separate gameplay decision. Gold generation and stacking
follow the existing engine rules.

Keep all 49 room flags, exits and doors, including links to 509/450 and
the oasis trapdoor. There are no NO_UNDEAD rooms here. The area starts
with five inhabitants and three carried items; three refills give twenty
inhabitants and twelve carried items. No matching Scribe source exists.

Correct room 18124's "main street will tents" to "main street with tents",
room 18125's "south is and oasis" to "south is an oasis", and the reset
comment's "Bedoiun child" to "Bedouin child". Keep the woman's wry wording
and cosmetics reference as authored flavour. No gender mismatch or
portrait change is involved.


Temple of Mrinthas creatures
----------------------------

Temple of Mrinthas uses seven humanoid/zombie definitions, human for Dane
and the shopkeeper, and the existing quadrupedal_mammal/rat for its large
rat. The new fish species and eel archetype describe the Fountain Beast;
humanoid/guardian_daemon describes the acid-trunked guardian.

The two sergeant definitions, 13004 and 13011, are rare. Dane 13009 and
the guardian 13012 keep elite. Everyone else is common. MacKurian's high
level and service role do not call for another HP multiplier. Rank applies
to every instance of a definition, once; it is not a body-size setting.

The zombie profile supplies undead physiology and its usual defences.
Ordinary guards/sergeants remain mindless and unable to speak; their moans
and existing spec_guard roles remain. Keep the wandering guard's authored
aggression. MacKurian 13005 and acolyte 13008 cancel inherited AFF_MINDLESS
and BODY_NO_SPEECH through their existing raw AFF/body XOR masks. They can
think, speak and cast, while keeping the other zombie properties. Their
raw body32 removes inherited NO_SPEECH; it does not make another zombie
mute. Do not add the inherited UNDEAD bit again in a raw XOR mask.

Keep MacKurian's spec_cast_adept and the acolyte's spec_cast_cleric. Both
retain good alignment. His old ACT512 is current NO_QUEST, not a working
trainer flag. He has no lesson list, so remove the invitation to practice
rather than invent lessons. TRAIN selects a player's future stat training
without requiring a trainer; PRACTICE needs an actual teacher skill list.
His peace/rest invitation and existing safe adept healing stay.

Dane remains a living human with his authored spec_cast_undead. A special
function is a role, not a test for undead identity. Keep his elite rank,
WIMPY/sentinel behaviour, senses and equipment. The human shopkeeper keeps
spec_cast_mage and his existing shop: types5/9/2/4/8,150/50,hours0-23.

The fish species has head/eyes/heart/brain/guts, fins and tail, no arms,
legs or speech, and intrinsic swimming. It has normal living organic
physiology, blood, pain, soul and remains. NPCs already bypass the
player-only drowning check; there is no new gills flag. Eel adds fangs and
one ordinary piercing bite. It adds no electric discharge, venom, armour,
resistances or racial XP. Generic eel defaults are150cm body length,
5kg,Small, with height unspecified. Fountain Beast13001 individually uses
300cm/20kg/Medium. Clear rawbody60 to0 after inheritance, restoring its
heart and retaining the inherited limb/speech restrictions. Keep its
authored infrared and GLOBE: raw AFF16777216 means GLOBE, not gills.

Rat13006 uses estimated30cm shoulder height,90cm body length and10kg,
Small. Clear rawbody36 to0 after mammal inheritance. Keep spec_fido,
DetectHidden/Sneak and aggression. No venom, shared disease mechanic or
HUGE flag is added. Other rats keep their existing dimensions.

Ordinary zombies use175cm/65kg/Medium, and humans175cm/75kg/Medium. The
guardian's described twelve-foot height becomes366cm;600kg is an estimate.
It is Large and has BODY_HUGE. Height/weight/length are descriptive whole
numbers; Small/Large and HUGE retain their existing gameplay rules.

guardian_daemon is an intelligent organic humanoid with ordinary organs,
speech, blood, pain, soul and remains. Claws supply its ordinary slashing
fallback. Natural armour is-20; it resists MAGIC/NONMAGIC, is immune to
ACID/CHARM/HOLD/SLEEP and has the internal fear_immunity trait. Its racial
XP adjustment is+10, with neutral HP/damage/critical/haste modifiers.
Mundane weapons are resisted, not blocked by a new enchantment threshold.
The local mob keeps flight, Hide/Sneak, sight, Protection and its authored
behaviours/key. Flight alone does not add physical wings. No universal
daemon elemental package or currently non-functional polymorph immunity
is claimed.

spec_guardian_daemon keeps the existing demon caster on80% of eligible
opportunities. The other20% can release one trunk acid spit at a current
opponent: dice(level,4) acid damage, ordinary spell save halves, with
existing acid defences applying. The acid_spit_daily_casts trait supplies
three released attacks per MUD day. A save or acid immunity still uses a
released charge; no target, invalid selection or unusable head uses none.
After the daily supply is spent, the old demon caster handles every
opportunity. A damaged/missing head blocks the spit without silencing its
independent demon casting. Calendar day/month/year changes replenish the
supply. There is no area field or player skill for these native powers.

The spit uses a typed acid-damage path, not the acid-breath spell: it does
not melt containers, etch armour or scale damage with elite HP. MSTAT
shows fear_immunity and acid_spit_daily_casts with their archetype origin.
Fear spell, fear aura and banshee fear use the common supernatural save;
the self-targeted fear case also honours the trait. Unrelated psychic
effects do not become immune just because they use the same damage type.
All older trait profiles and player-race opt-ins remain unchanged.

The area has70rooms,37objects and no NO_UNDEAD rooms. Keep all room flags,
doors/keys, reset placements/caps and treasure. Ready sergeant13011 in
room13064 receives sword13012 instead of spear13003, matching the other
sergeants. Seven rat reset rows still share maximum1; do not turn those
rows into seven live rats. Varying officer/acolyte caps keep their old
refill behaviour. Initial population35 carries111items; three refills
give65inhabitants/211items.

MacKurian's curse staff13030 stays in WIELD16 and fireball book13022 in
HOLD17, with their old staff types/bonuses. The held book already uses the
normal NPC staff-use slot; moving the other staff to HOLD would change
his gear and combat. Preserve both resets rather than rewriting treasure.

13004/13010/13011 use male sex data to match their prose. Portraits were
checked:13004's helmet obscures his face,13010 is skeletal, and13011's
living-man picture needs a separate undead replacement. This conversion
does not publish artwork. Small spelling/grammar fixes and the local
shop comment are corrected; no matching Scribe source exists.


Lea'Non creatures
----------------

swimming provider or special amphibian movement rule.

Human defaults are 175 cm/75 kg/Medium. The slave18207 uses an individual

This beast cancels inherited NO_SPEECH with individual raw body32,

Redferne's Residence: the Grand Knight
------------------------------------

Knight 7900 uses the existing humanoid/human profile. Make him rare,

and Medium size, and use an individual weight of 90 kg for his sturdy
build. These measurements are estimates for description, not stat bonuses.

Keep his level 29, male sex, good alignment 1000, stay-area, NO_QUEST,
PRACTICE, Detect Invisible, Sanctuary and spec_cast_cleric. His lessons
stay at teacher base 30, Riding Techniques 85 and Knowledge 85. Teacher
base is the student's minimum level; it does not set the knight's level.
He keeps ordinary human anatomy and physiology. His behaviour stays as
written, with no new paladin powers or priest services. Tidy the ignored
old HP notation from 20r10+700 to 20d10+700; rank is set separately.

His old weapon reset tried to equip red dragon claw 7216. It is anti-good,
so the good knight dropped it. Give him new local object 7900, a longsword
of the Rose, in the same WIELD slot 16 with the same maximum of 99.

red dragon 7040 keeps its claw and its existing reset.

The sword is an ordinary steel weapon with TAKE/WIELD and
MAGIC/ANTI_EVIL/ANTI_NEUTRAL, so only good characters can equip it.
Its prototype values are 0/4/4/3, using slash instead of claw. Copy the
old weapon's 19/1800/900 line and +5 AC/+5 DAMROLL affects. Positive AC
is an armour penalty. Normal NPC weapon generation and randomisation
still apply, so these values do not promise fixed live damage. There
are no extra powers, special metals or BODY_PART flag. It follows the
ordinary rules for held weapons, arm injuries and disarming.

Keep the eight shared armour pieces, brass key 7911, locked chest 7910
and its money 7298/lightstick 7208, treasure 7909, water barrel 6013,

items with his sword equipped, without leaving a claw on the floor.
The ignored old gold field still gives no live gold. The described rats,
monster pen and absent mansion owner stay scenery. There is no matching
Scribe source and no new player spell or skill.


The Sea of Deception: ordinary sea life and Dahij
-----------------------------------------------

The seven mobs in this area use common rank. Keep their authored levels,
alignments, sexes and behaviour. A whale's size and a questmaster's high
level do not by themselves call for a higher encounter rank.

Use archetype names seagull, dolphin, blue_whale, hammerhead_shark and
small_fish. The new body species are bird and cetacean.

The sailor 18800 and Dahij 18806 use humanoid/human, at 175 cm and 75 kg,
Medium. The sailor starts swimming, as his description says. This uses
the existing removable swimming state; it is not permanent water breathing.

The bird species has a head, eyes, two legs and feet, wings, feathers,
beak, tail and ordinary organs. It has no arms or spoken Common. Flight
belongs to the archetype, so the body can also serve flightless birds.
Seagull 18801 flies and uses one ordinary piercing beak attack. It is
25 cm tall, 45 cm long and 1 kg, Small. Its two herring resets stay in
HOLD slot 17, representing food carried in the beak.

The cetacean species is a mammal body with fins, flukes and ordinary
organs, blood, soul, pain and remains. It has no arms, legs or spoken
Common. Dolphin 18802 is 180 cm long and 90 kg, Medium, with natural
armour -5. Its teeth are anatomical; its ordinary attack uses the tail.
Blue whale 18803 is 24 m long and 100,000 kg, Large with BODY_HUGE and
natural armour -6. It also uses one ordinary blunt tail attack.

The dolphin inherits spec_small_whale and the blue whale inherits
spec_large_whale. Their resolved special remains the same as before.
DD4 deliberately allows the blue whale to swallow people. Keep the
existing selection chances, damage, safety, trauma and swallowing rules,
including the rule allowing Large targets but refusing HUGE targets.
There is no new whale special or change to the existing functions.

Hammerhead shark 18804 and small fish 18805 reuse the fish body. The
shark is 3 m long and 200 kg, Large without BODY_HUGE. It has scales,
fangs, one ordinary piercing bite and natural armour -4. The small fish
is 20 cm long and Small, with scales and no special bite. Its weight is
unspecified rather than a fraction of a kilogram. Other aquatic heights
are also unspecified. Measurements are descriptive estimates; the
dolphin's length and whale's broad size follow the second-edition entries.

Use raw body 0 for these five animals. Repeating the old no-arm, no-leg
or no-speech bits would cancel those inherited properties through XOR.
The whale already inherits BODY_HUGE; do not toggle it off individually.
Swimming uses the existing state and movement rules, with no new habitat
or air-supply simulation. No new resistance, sonar or player powers apply.

The shark head object 18802 remains loot, with its definition unchanged.
Give it to the shark as inventory instead of equipping it in HEAD slot 6.
This removes its helmet armour and +5 HP from the live shark. Keep the
reset maximum of 100 and all other equipment, treasure and reset caps.
The two small-fish rows still share a maximum of one; no extra fish spawn.

Dahij keeps his questmaster role, protections, source level 250, light
3031 and spec_evil_evil_gezhp. This special was written for him and is
not redundant species data. Keep all 17 lessons and the quest recall to
room 18835 for 500 points. His archetype line goes before the teacher
list. He gains no healer or priest service.

Keep all five objects, 47 room flags and sectors, exits and randomisation.
Correct only "keen an eye" to "keep an eye" in room 18800 and "to dark"
to "too dark" in room 18833. There is no matching Scribe source. Initial
and refill populations stay at twelve, with four carried objects, no
NO_UNDEAD rooms, no shops and one teacher. No player skill or spell is new.


Forest: woodland animals, swarms and the medicine man
---------------------------------------------------

Use rabbit for 18000, brown_bear for 18001, mosquito_swarm for 18002,
wasp_swarm for 18003, stag for 18004, badger for 18005, robin for 18006,
human for 18007 and lynx for 18008. The medicine man is rare, with 2x
rank HP applied once. The other eight are common. Keep their authored
levels, alignments, aggression, wandering and wimpy behaviour.

The rabbit and stag keep their existing mammal defaults. Neither has a
reset, so this conversion does not add them to the population. The rabbit
becomes male to agree with "his" in the description. Keep both the rabbit's
and robin's individual spec_breath_lightning: these are authored surprises,
not abilities supplied to every rabbit or bird. The medicine man stays
human, with his existing flight, Sanctuary and spec_breath_any.

A brown bear uses quadrupedal_mammal, with paws, claws, fangs and a tail,
ordinary living organs, natural armour -4, and claw/bite attack choices.
Its default measurements are 150 cm at the shoulder, 280 cm long and
450 kg, Large. This giant Kodiak uses individual 160 cm/330 cm/800 kg
measurements and BODY_HUGE. These are descriptive estimates, not extra
stat bonuses. A large body does not by itself set a higher encounter rank.

The bear's inherited spec_bear has a 15% chance per combat opportunity
to deliver a 2d6 blunt crushing hug. It needs a live opponent in the same
room, usable forelegs and the normal ability to act. The bear takes its
usual violence wait after a hug. The target gains no hold, stun or trauma;
normal safety and damage defences apply. This is our adaptation of the
second-edition bear hug and adds 5 special XP. It does not change how
NPCs die or let a bear keep fighting below zero HP.

Object 18000 remains the bear's equipped claw weapon and loot. Add
ITEM_BODY_PART, set value[0] to 4 for the leg/foreleg region, and use
attack verb 5, claw. Keep HUM, TAKE/WIELD, the other prototype numbers,
+3 hitroll/-10 spell-saving affects and existing level-based generation.
Do not add DONOT_RANDOMISE or a new claw object. Keep its original reset.
Brown bears opt into the internal body_weapon_loot trait so these existing
claws remain loot after death or a successful body-part disarm. Detachment
clears ITEM_BODY_PART and resets value[0] to 0: the claw is an ordinary
held weapon then, rather than a new owner's attached limb. Its damage,
other flags and affects stay intact. Other native body weapons keep their
existing cleanup unless their species/archetype explicitly opts in.
Use raw atk claws (MobAttackParts 8589934592) to cancel the inherited
claw attack through XOR. The resolved fallback is fangs only. While attached,
the claw weapon supplies the claw attack; after it is torn off, the bear
can still bite without immediately regrowing a native claw attack.

Leg trauma alone does not newly disable that attached claw weapon.
The shared body-part weapon eligibility function is unchanged. Existing
injury penalties and the separate chance to tear off a leg-region item
still apply. Regenerate does not recreate a torn-off object. The new hug
requires usable forelegs, so leg trauma prevents that special manoeuvre.

Badger uses ordinary mammal anatomy, claw/bite attacks and natural
armour -6, at 30 cm tall/70 cm long/12 kg, Small. Lynx uses the same basic
body with natural armour -5, infrared, sneak and claw/bite choices, at
55 cm/100 cm/20 kg, Small. Robin reuses bird anatomy, flight and a beak
attack, at 7 cm tall and 10 cm long, Small. Its weight is unspecified;
do not invent a fractional kilogram. All these animals keep ordinary
blood, pain, soul and remains behaviour. The medicine man uses the normal
human measurements, 175 cm and 75 kg, Medium.

The new insect_swarm species represents a living cloud. It has no single
head, eyes, arms, legs or heart to target, no speech and no coherent corpse.
It is bloodless for feeding purposes, but remains organic and living,
with ordinary pain and soul behaviour. It gains no blanket weapon or
mental immunity. Both swarms are Medium; their height, length and mass
are unspecified, and their sex is unspecified because each is a group.

The archetypes supply flight and inherit existing spec_poison. The
ordinary natural_attack_type trait gives mosquitoes bite verb 10 and
wasps sting verb 16, one normal attack at a time without a single head
or fang part. The poison helper uses sting wording for configured sting
attackers; its old bite/palm wording, target selection, venom and saves
stay as before for other creatures. Each swarm keeps the usual 5 special
XP. No separate malaria, blood drain or swarm weapon-immunity system is
added. Keep the existing room's malaria warning as written.

Clear the old raw body 36/61 duplicates so they do not cancel inherited
anatomy through XOR. Clear the robin/swarm raw flight duplicates so flight
stays inherited. The Kodiak's raw BODY_HUGE is an individual addition;
ordinary brown bears do not inherit it. Existing NPC native-attack injury
rules stay in place, separately from the attached-weapon rule above.

Keep all reset rows, caps and placements, all 55 room flags and sectors,
exits and the door reset. The initial population stays seven, with three
carried items; three refills give thirteen, and the eventual cap is fourteen.
Keep the bear claws, medicine man's necklace/gold and ground toe. Keep
short names, keywords and picture paths. Correct only the stag's "scar's",
the lynx's "it seem", and the wasp room's plural grammar. There is no
matching Scribe source, no shop or teacher, and no new player skill/spell.


Haon Dor: woodland mammals, Shargugh and the green dragon
-------------------------------------------------------

Use human for John 6000 and the elder druid 6116, brown_bear for 6002,
rabbit for 6003, deer for 6004, fox for 6005, warg for 6100/6101, wolf for
6102/6103, adult_green_dragon for 6112 and brownie for Shargugh 6115.
These reuse humanoid, quadrupedal_mammal and dragon; no new basic body
species is needed. All ordinary anatomy still comes through inheritance.

John is common, at 183 cm tall and 95 kg, matching his six-foot muscular
description. The bear inherits the ordinary brown-bear measurements and
spec_bear. It does not get the Kodiak's individual HUGE flag or claw item.
The existing leg-body-weapon rule stays unchanged. The rabid rabbit stays
a loaded definition without a reset; this does not add disease spreading.
The fallow deer keeps the antlerless deer profile and female sex. Remove
her stray blind affect, which contradicts her watchful eyes, and unused
ACT value 16. Keep her sentinel and stay-area behaviour.

Fox and wolf use living mammal anatomy with paws, claws, fangs and a tail.
Only the fangs supply their ordinary native attack. Both have detect-hidden
for keen senses and natural armour -3. Fox defaults to 40 cm at the shoulder,
100 cm long and 7 kg, Small; this large fox uses 45 cm/120 cm/10 kg. Wolf
defaults to 80 cm/150 cm/50 kg, Small. There are no added poison, tracking
or disease specials, and the area's existing aggression stays as authored.

Warg is this area's spelling of the second-edition worg. It uses the same
mammal body, at 100 cm/210 cm/120 kg, Medium, with natural armour -4 and
detect-hidden. Its archetype cancels the inherited no-speech bit, allowing
its simple language. It does not automatically become mountable or gain
a new language system. Neither of these wargs is identified as an alpha;
both remain common. Its trait profile shares the wolf profile within the
same basic species, then replaces the armour value. All weights are whole
kilograms; these measurements are descriptive estimates.

The adult green dragon inherits flight, swimming, infrared, detect-invisible
and detect-hidden, plus HUGE and anatomical horns. Its ordinary dragon
claw, bite and tail attack choices remain; horns add no extra attack.
This adult profile measures 400 cm at the shoulder, 3200 cm including its
tail and 8000 kg, Large. Younger green dragons can have separate profiles.
Natural armour is -20, RES_MAGIC represents adult magical resistance, and
poison immunity uses DD4's existing poisonous-gas channel. That immunity
also covers other poisons; there is no new gas-resistance flag.

It inherits spec_breath_gas, so remove the duplicate area special row.
Keep its individual aggression, sentinel, stay-area and famous
flags. The existing fear aura attempts fear once per visible opponent's
fight, using the normal resistance-aware save; this does not frighten idle
bystanders. No new fear function or generic mage spell list is added.
Age-specific woodland spells can be considered separately later.

Dragon 6112 is boss, with 7x normal rank HP applied once. Its existing gas
breath scales with current HP, so that promotion strengthens the breath
as well. This is an approved encounter choice, not an effect of body size.
Keep the potion 6110, crown 6112 and shield 6111 as carried treasure; do
not equip human-sized items on it. Shargugh and the elder druid are rare,
with 2x rank HP once. All other Haon Dor definitions remain common.

A brownie is a living humanoid, with ordinary blood, pain, soul, speech
and remains. Its default height/weight are 61 cm/9 kg, Small. Shargugh's
explicit three-foot description overrides those to 91 cm/18 kg. Hide,
invisibility and detect-hidden represent woodland concealment and keen
senses. Armour -7 represents agility; RES_MAGIC is our broad adaptation
of strong second-edition saving throws, not a claim that the book gives
brownies a percentage magic-resistance score. No blanket charm/sleep
immunity or material vulnerability is added.

The inherited spec_brownie has a one-in-eight opportunity outside combat
to restore missing self-invisibility or protection, one effect at a time.
It responds to an attacker before refreshing defences. During combat it
has a 15% opportunity to use the existing Confusion spell on its legal
opponent. The ordinary spell supplies its save, duration and restrictions;
this is a magical trick, not poison gas. Readiness, head/brain injury,
safety and existing affects are checked. It adds 5 special XP. Repeatable
DD4 tricks are a deliberate adaptation of the book's daily abilities.

Keep Shargugh's individual detect-invisible, sneak, wimpy/scavenging, good
alignment and ring 6114. Remove only unused ACT value 16 and the raw
invisibility duplicate, so XOR does not cancel that inherited defence.
The elder druid remains human at 175 cm/70 kg, with spec_cast_cleric,
his existing detect-invisible/aggression/sentinel/scavenging and morning star.
His unsettling cultist temple is not a new healer or NO_UNDEAD room.

Clear the dragon's duplicate detect-invisible, raw quadruped body 36
duplicates and the dragon's raw HUGE bit:
those properties now come from their templates. The warg's speech
exception belongs in its archetype, rather than in each area entry.
Keep all source levels, alignments, other behaviour bits, names, sex,
picture paths and loot. Correct only the small dragon/brownie grammar
and the potion's repeated "and". The checked deer and Shargugh portraits
agree with their descriptions and are unchanged.

Keep all 71 room flags, exits, doors, traps, ambient data and reset rows.
The initial population from these definitions is 19, with eight carried
items; refills add the second fox for 20. There are also imported Plains
rabbits 309 with teeth 313, and Arachnos spiders 6318/6319 in room 6134.
The four rabbit rows share global population caps with the Plains, so a
fresh full-world load produces two here and three there. Preserve that
arrangement and the shared water barrel 6013 used in Redferne. There is
no matching Scribe source, no new shop/teacher/player spell and no new
area field or ACT bit.


Arachnos
--------

Use web_spider for the young and drone spiders, hunting_spider for the
Wolf Spider, giant_spider for the Bird Spider and phase_spider for the
ethereal spider. Their arachnid body uses many legs and the front of the
body as its head target. Fangs supply the bite. The ordinary web spider
measures 35 cm high and 70 cm long and weighs 15 kg. Give the young spider
its smaller 8 cm, 15 cm and 1 kg individual measurements.

The poisonous spiders in Haon Dor and their Queen Spider use
venomous_spider. Its armour comes from giant_spider and its inherited
spec_poison_bite uses the existing venom, with the mouth and fang checks.
The queen is rare and uses individual measurements of 220 cm high,
350 cm long and 1800 kg, with Large size and HUGE. The phase spider is a
living spider. Its Blink uses the existing level and mana rules.

Use giant_wasp for the Queen Wasp. An insect is a single living body,
with a head, eyes and many legs. The wasp adds wings, flight and the
piercing sting attack type. Its existing poison callback uses the sting
wording. Its armour bonus represents the chitin, and its rare rank
reflects its role in the hive.

Arachnos uses spider_empress. An arachnoid_humanoid has a speaking
humanoid torso, hands and a spider's walking legs. Arachnos inherits her
gas breath from spider_empress and keeps her individual Invisibility
and Detect Invisible. Yevaud uses adult_green_dragon and inherits gas
breath and Detect Invisible. Clear his duplicate Detect Invisible
and HUGE bits so XOR leaves the inherited abilities active.

Yevaud is elite, Arachnos is boss, and the Queen Wasp, Donjonkeeper and
Queen Spider are rare. Rank applies its normal HP multiplier once.
Gas breath uses current HP, so the dragon's and empress's new ranks also
strengthen their breath. The other definitions are common.

The Ki-rin uses the quadrupedal mammal body, with speech, scales, hooves,
a horn and magical flight. She uses normal magic resistance and has
HUGE on the archetype. The quasit uses the humanoid body with horns,
claws and fangs, Blink and regeneration. It has magic and nonmagic
resistance and immunity to fire, cold and electricity. Keep this
individual's alignment and stealth affects.

Both wormkins use the legless serpentine reptile body, magical flight,
tail attacks and gas breath. Use the juvenile's individual 20 cm
height, 90 cm length, 5 kg weight and Small size. Set both the juvenile's
body mask and MobAttackParts to the fangs bit. This cancels the inherited
fangs in both places while keeping the tail attack.

The trapped orc uses orc. The slave, Hermit, Donjonkeeper and Guardian
use human. Keep the young spider male and the drone female to match
their descriptions. Keep the authored footstep keys, loot and reset
caps, including the four spiders placed in Haon Dor room 6134.


Harpy Haven
-----------

Use harpy for both the screaming harpy and its leader. The archetype
adds feathered wings, flight, claws and fangs to the humanoid body.
Keep the two local sonic fight programs at their original chances.
Place the archetype and rank after each program's closing marker.
Clear the area flight bit so it keeps the inherited flight through XOR.
Both leader spawns use rare rank and keep their individual gas breath.
Rare doubles normal HP once, and gas breath scales with current HP.

Air elemental uses the inorganic elemental_being body and adds the
parts and restrictions of a shifting cloud. Flight and pass-door
come from the archetype. Its ordinary attack uses the existing blunt
pound type for a wind strike. Its measurements are unspecified.
Clear the duplicate flight, pass-door and inorganic bits in the area.

Efreeti is a huge humanoid with flight and fire resistance. Keep the
individual no-corpse bit, defensive affects and mage special. Clear
the individual HUGE bit because the archetype supplies it.

Both rocs use the bird body, flight, HUGE, claws and a beak. Use common
rank for both. Keep their original behaviour, and leave the egg in the
female's nest. Clear the body and flight duplicates in the area.

The Wicked Witch uses the living green_hag profile and elite rank.
Keep her existing Sanctuary, detection affects and necromantic special.
The special selects her spells and works on a living caster.


Witch's Tower creature profiles
------------------------------

`black_bear`, `hydra` and `imp` are available as mob archetypes.
Black bears use quadrupedal mammal anatomy, natural armour -3 and the
existing bear hug special. Their defaults are 100 cm shoulder height,
180 cm length and 120 kg, Medium. Give unusually large individuals their
own dimensions, size and HUGE flag rather than enlarging every black bear.

Hydrae use quadrupedal reptile anatomy, multiple heads, fangs and HUGE,
with natural armour -5. Defaults are 250 cm shoulder height, 900 cm length
and 3,000 kg, Large. The archetype supplies neither breath nor head
regrowth. Add a special only when the individual or a reviewed variant
needs it. MANY_HEAD describes anatomy; injuries still use the existing
shared head-trauma state, not a separate counter for each head.

Imps are small winged humanoids with horns, fangs and a tail. They fly,
regenerate, see infrared, and detect good and magic. They have armour -8,
magic/nonmagic resistance and fire/cold/electricity immunity. Defaults are
61 cm and 12 kg, Small. These are DD4 adaptations of the second-edition
creature; familiar bonds, shapechanging and automatic invisibility are
not supplied. Individual casting still comes from the existing specials.

Attached body-part weapons keep their targeting category in weapon
value 0. A tail sting uses category 6 and attack verb 16; POISONED uses
ordinary DD4 venom, not instant death. Keep BODY_PART, omit TAKE for a
non-lootable attached weapon, and use DONOT_RANDOMISE to prevent random
item upgrades. A separate native attack of the same kind can
undo the point of injuring or removing the attached weapon. Cancel that
individual attack through the existing `atk` XOR setting while
keeping the anatomical part. Regenerate heals injuries but does not
recreate a removed weapon. The existing leg-weapon rules are unchanged.

The tower's ghostly nymph, elf and Aiseen reuse `ghost`; a former identity
does not require a duplicate living-body archetype. Keep deliberate
individual casters such as the nymph's druid and Aiseen's demon special.
Use `wraith` for dedicated wraith phase/drain behaviour and `wight` for
a corporeal undead body with ordinary remains. Remove duplicate raw
physiology flags carefully: area body and attack-part fields use XOR.


Valley of the Elves creature profiles
------------------------------------

`valley_elf`, `cooshee` and `antelope` are available as mob archetypes.
Valley elves use humanoid anatomy and elf family identity, with infrared
and descriptive defaults of 175 cm and 60 kg, Medium. Their trait profile
shares the established elf sleep/charm resistance and ghoul-touch immunity.
