This does not change the ordinary elf, drow or player abilities. Keep
occupational casting on each mob rather than making every valley elf a mage.

Cooshees are elven dogs with quadrupedal mammal anatomy, a fang bite,
Sneak and natural armour -5. Defaults are 80 cm shoulder height,
150 cm length and 60 kg, Medium. Their inherited `spec_cooshee` has a 10%
chance per ready combat opportunity to use Trip on a grounded Small/Medium
biped. It needs usable forelegs and paws and respects leg trauma, physical
contact, safety, visibility, flight, mount, water and action restrictions.
It ignores opponents already prone and adds no direct damage. The usual
Trip effect and two-violence-pulse actor cooldown apply. It neither starts
a fight nor overrides the mob's authored wimpy behaviour. The special is
also available individually through the existing `sp` setting.

Antelopes have quadrupedal mammal anatomy, horns, hooves and armour -3.
Defaults are 100 cm shoulder height, 160 cm length and 70 kg, Medium.
They use existing natural horn/hoof attacks; there is no new herd AI.
These animal measurements and masses are descriptive estimates.

The valley's pyrohydra reuses `hydra` with individual `spec_breath_fire`.
It has a natural bite, so it does not need the `atk` XOR cancellation
used by a hydra whose bites are supplied by attached fang weapons.
The guardian daemon keeps its individual `spec_demon` override: the
acid-trunk wrapper belongs to the already-reviewed trunked guardian form.
Use individual specials when the creature's actual form or role differs.

Body and affect masks use XOR. Remove duplicate no-arms/no-speech/Sneak
from a converted cooshee, and duplicate infrared from valley elf overrides.
Keep the individual's aggression, passivity, movement and other affects.
Rank remains an encounter choice: it does not determine body dimensions.


Eerie Woods creature profiles
----------------------------

`bee`, `enchanted_oak`, `leprechaun` and `horse` are available as archetypes.
The plant body is organic, BLOODLESS and ANALGIC, without animal organs,
head, eyes, legs or speech. Mind and soul are separate choices: being a
plant does not automatically make a creature mindless or soulless.

An enchanted oak has arm-like branches, HUGE, fire vulnerability and
ordinary blunt body/branch contact. Its defaults are 1500 cm height,
10,000 kg and Large. It gains no extra natural armour: the Eerie Woods
oak already wears the existing bark armour and holds an earthquake staff.
Keep that staff's spell-level field intact; it is not a weapon targeting
category. These botanical measurements and masses are descriptive estimates.

Bees use insect anatomy, wings, flight, a sting and ordinary poison.
Their default length is 2 cm, with Small as DD4's minimum size category.
Height and whole-kg mass stay unspecified. Do not turn an individual bee
into a giant or a swarm merely because it has a high MUD level.

Leprechauns use humanoid anatomy, Detect Hidden, armour -2 and ordinary
magic resistance. Defaults are 61 cm and 12 kg, Small. Psionic casting
is an individual role, not a racial default. The new `spec_psionic_thief`
keeps the existing psionic caster in combat. While idle it restores
invisibility if needed, otherwise attempts the existing gold theft.
It takes one action per opportunity and respects normal readiness checks.

Horses have quadrupedal mammal anatomy, a tail, hooves and armour -3.
Defaults are 150 cm shoulder height, 230 cm length and 450 kg, Large.
Mountability, flight and breath are individual choices, not horse defaults.
`spec_breath_frost_anatomical` keeps ordinary frost breath's power and
frequency, with the same missing/injured-head gate as the other anatomical
breath wrappers. Both new specials can use the existing `sp` command.

The Eerie Woods wolf cubs use ordinary wolf anatomy with individual
25 cm height, 45 cm length and 4 kg. Their levels are encounter design,
not a reason to give them adult bodies. Preserve authored aggression,
wimpy behaviour, quest restrictions and programs. Remove duplicate
body/affect defaults carefully because the area overrides use XOR.


Little Haven creature profiles
-----------------------------

`polar_bear`, `adult_white_dragon`, `adult_blue_dragon` and `frost_giant`
are available as archetypes. They use existing mammal, dragon and humanoid
bodies. Dimensions remain descriptive estimates, in cm and whole kg.

Polar bears have paws, claws, fangs, forelegs, a short tail, HUGE,
armour -4 and cold resistance. Defaults are 200 cm shoulder height,
400 cm length and 900 kg, Large. The explicitly giant Little Haven bears
use individual 250 cm / 500 cm / 1500 kg overrides. Their existing
`spec_bear` hug has its usual 15% opportunity and deals 3d6 blunt damage.
The internal `bear_hug_dice` trait selects three dice for polar bears;
brown and black bears keep two. MSTAT shows the resolved trait and origin.
It is not an area field or a Scribe command. Injury, safety and readiness
checks still apply; the hug does not hold or stun its target.

Adult white and blue dragons have flight, infrared, enhanced senses,
HUGE, the existing combat fear aura, armour -20 and magic resistance.
White dragons are cold-immune and breathe frost; blue dragons are
electricity-immune and breathe lightning. The blue's horn is anatomical,
not another attack. White defaults are 350 cm shoulder height, 3000 cm
including the tail and 6000 kg; blue defaults are 500 cm / 4000 cm /
12000 kg. Both use Large. No extra age powers or fire vulnerability
are implied. Generic profiles inherit their breath, not mage casting.

`spec_breath_lightning_anatomical` keeps ordinary lightning breath, with
the existing missing/injured-head gate. It is available through `sp`,
like the frost, fire and acid wrappers. Little Haven's two dragons keep
individual 50% mage / 50% appropriate-breath choices. Each opportunity
selects one special; a failed choice does not try another. They carry
their old wand and axe as loot instead of equipping them without arms.

Frost giants have ordinary humanoid organs and speech, HUGE, cold
immunity and armour -15. Defaults are 640 cm / 3600 kg, Large. Their
giant-family identity participates in the existing dwarf/gnome defences.
`spec_giant` gives a 15% eligible combat-pulse throw chance, four rocks
per game day, 2d10 plus the existing level/2 term and a 40% catch chance.
Its normal arm/head/eye, safety, visibility and ammunition checks apply.
This grants no new axe, regeneration or fire vulnerability.

Keep local encounter choices separate: the Snow Queen is a human mage,
her servants remain human, and the area's thief uses `spec_thief`.
The dragons are Elite and the Queen is Boss; ordinary guards and bears
remain Common. Rank applies HP once through the existing system.
When replacing raw body flags, remember XOR: do not cancel inherited
HUGE, NO_ARMS or NO_SPEECH by repeating them in the area override.


Crystalmir Lake creature profiles
--------------------------------

`baaz_draconian`, `barracuda` and `dragon_turtle` use the existing humanoid,
fish and quadrupedal reptile bodies. No new area fields are needed.

A Baaz is a living scaled humanoid with wings, a tail, claws and fangs.
It has ordinary arms, organs, speech, blood, pain and soul, with armour -6
and ordinary magic resistance. Defaults are 168 cm / 75 kg, Medium.
An equipped weapon keeps precedence over its ordinary claw/bite fallback.
Wings do not grant powered flight; actual gliding remains future work.

Its code-only `stone_death` trait makes it turn to stone and crumble at
death. It leaves no biological corpse or death-part drops; normal gear
and coins fall to the floor. This works independently of active specials.
MSTAT shows the trait and origin. Do not mark the living creature undead,
inorganic, bloodless, soulless or analgic. The brief statue interval and
temporary killing-weapon trap are deliberately not implemented here.

Barracudas have ordinary fish anatomy, scales, fangs and a piercing bite,
with armour -4. Defaults are 120 cm length and 10 kg, Small; height is
unspecified. Being a predatory fish does not imply venom or swallowing.

Dragon turtles have four reptile legs, a tail, scales, shell, claws and
fangs, with HUGE and armour -20. Defaults are 350 cm shoulder height,
1200 cm length and 25000 kg, Large. They can speak by default; keep a
local NO_SPEECH override when a particular area has a deliberately mute
turtle. The Crystalmir turtle carries its original wearable shell as
loot, rather than counting that shell's armour twice. Leave the item
unmarked as BODY_PART so existing death cleanup does not destroy it.
There is no automatic flight, fear aura, magical defence or fire immunity.

`spec_breath_steam_anatomical` keeps existing steam breath and its ordinary
opportunities, with the same missing/injured-head gate as other anatomical
breath wrappers. It is available through `sp`. No daily charge limit,
ship-capsizing mechanic or new player spell is added.

`spec_cleric_adept` is a reusable individual role: combat cleric when
engaged, otherwise the existing helpful adept. It takes one action per
opportunity and keeps the adept's harmful-help checks. It does not add
paid healer services or change any teaching groups. In an old area, two
legacy M rows replace each other; use this role when both jobs are wanted.

Goldmoon, Toede and Riverwind have individual dimensions rounded from
their second-edition character cards. Keep ordinary hobgoblin/human
defaults for other creatures unless their own descriptions differ.
Pooh uses `animated_soft_toy`, retaining its individual thief routine;
it is a stuffed toy, not a living bear. Ranks are encounter choices,
not the sourcebook's morale labels, and their HP multipliers apply once.

Use the existing `silver` item flag for explicitly silver gear. It does
not replace alignment restrictions, ordinary weapon flags or attack verbs.
The Crystalmir footman's lance keeps its lance flag and now pierces.


Gremlin Lair creature profiles
-----------------------------

`lair_gremlin` and `mogwai` use the existing humanoid body.
`animated_toy_head` uses head_only. There are no new area fields.

The lair gremlin is our wingless local variant. Adults default to 46 cm
and 6 kg, Small, with normal living organs, speech, blood, pain, soul and
remains. Claws and fangs supply ordinary unarmed attack choices, not extra
attacks; an equipped weapon keeps precedence. Natural armour is -6 and
ordinary magic resistance applies. This is a coarse adaptation, not a
literal 25 percent chance to cancel spells. Unlike the standard 2e creature,
this beginner-area variant can be hurt by ordinary weapons. It has no
racial flight or equipment-malfunction power. Its creature family is not
goblin, so goblin-targeted racial bonuses do not automatically apply.

Keep aggression, passivity, sentinel, stay-area, scavenging and wimpy
choices in the individual area build. King Boo keeps his wimpy behaviour
as an Elite encounter. Jeremiah's imposing description is a joke: he stays
Common and has no fear aura. Boos's uneven legs do not imply a permanent
injury; Boozes's eye-poking does not add a blindness power. Booz retains
his individual spec_fido, and remains a living creature.

Mogwai default to 30 cm and 3 kg, Small, with ordinary living humanoid
anatomy. They gain no new racial resistances, natural weapons or special
powers. Gizmo keeps his Rare rank, individual spec_cast_adept, magical
flight and other authored effects. His flight does not add wings. The
water/midnight warning remains flavour; no cloning or transformation rule
is implemented.

An animated toy head defaults to 15 cm, Small, with weight unspecified:
we do not invent a fractional kilogram or claim that it has no mass.
It has a head, eyes and ears, without arms, legs, heart, brains or guts.
Like the other soft toys it is inorganic, mindless, bloodless, analgic,
soulless, mute and corpseless. It is immune to poison, sleep, charm and
drain, and vulnerable to fire. It has no sharp teeth or bite; ordinary
blunt fallback still works. It remains attackable and drops ordinary loot.
Do not repeat the inherited NO_ARMS/NO_LEGS/NO_HEART flags in an individual
XOR mask, since that would cancel those restrictions.

Little/baby/large/puny gremlins and King Boo use existing individual height
and whole-kilogram weight overrides. Sir Durok reuses human defaults and
becomes Rare; no knight caster special is added. Rank HP applies once.
The Female Gremlin is female, and Boose is male as his room text says.
All existing portraits, room flags, equipment and resets stay unchanged.
The soda can remains the cell key and Xevlin's corpse stays a scenery
container. The repeated journal reset already produces just one page.


Mud School creature profiles
----------------------------

`puma` and `wild_boar` use quadrupedal_mammal; `cobra` uses
serpentine_reptile. Kobold now fills out the existing humanoid placeholder.
There are no new area fields or trait commands.

An adult puma defaults to 75 cm, 60 kg and 200 cm long, Medium. It has a
tail, paws, claws and fangs, with ordinary claw/bite choices and armour -4.
Infrared and sneak use the same coarse cat treatment as the lynx. The
school's young female uses 55 cm, 25 kg and 130 cm long, Small.
Leaping and a coordinated rear-claw rake remain separate future mechanics;
the ordinary attack choices do not create extra attacks.

A cobra defaults to 200 cm long and 3 kg, Small, with height unspecified.
It has normal snake organs, scales, tail and fangs, an ordinary piercing
bite and armour -4. It has no arms, legs or speech. The school specimen is
300 cm long and 6 kg. The profile deliberately inherits no venom special,
and the introductory school cobra must not gain poison. A future builder
can explicitly choose an existing venom special for a different encounter.
Do not silently give it a pit viper's infrared, poison immunity, spitting,
constriction or instant-death venom. The original descriptive prose stays.

Wild boar default to 90 cm, 120 kg and 160 cm long, Medium. Tusks supply
the ordinary gore attack; their hooves and tail are anatomical. Armour is
-3. They gain no extra hoof attack or active special. Fighting below zero
HP needs a safe shared ferocity mechanic and remains deferred.

These three profiles have normal living bodies, blood, pain, soul and
remains, neutral HP/damage/critical/haste modifiers and no racial XP bonus.
They do not decide whether a particular creature is aggressive or passive.

Kobolds default to 91 cm and 15 kg, Small, with scales, small horns and a
non-prehensile tail. They keep ordinary humanoid organs, hands and speech.
Infrared and the existing NPC-only sunlight -1 hit trait apply. Their
kobold family still participates in existing racial target matching. No
player-race behaviour changes. Innate armour stays zero; the source's
AC7(10) listing is not treated as proof of an armoured hide. Horns and tail
do not grant extra natural attacks. The stooped school kobold is 85 cm
and 12 kg. Do not add fire breath, wings or forced anti-gnome aggression.

Keep all sixteen school mobs Common. Staff levels are service choices,
and the gladiator is a novice graduation fight. Preserve all individual
behaviour, effects, source levels and alignments, including quest exclusions.
The school area's NO_QUEST also contributes to loaded ACT flags.

The starved goblin uses 100 cm / 20 kg, the tall unhealthy lizardman
220 cm / 80 kg, and the muscular Quartermaster 175 cm / 90 kg. Scars and
poor health do not add permanent trauma or another HP penalty. The small
giant lizard overrides the generic 457 cm/Large default with 45 cm shoulder
height, 35 kg, 180 cm length and Small. The shared giant_lizard stays as it
was. Dimensions remain descriptive. Use whole kilograms.

Do not repeat inherited missing-limb or NO_SPEECH body flags in the area
XOR mask: those repeated flags would cancel the body restrictions. The
school dog, puma and chained wolf now use raw body zero instead of 36;
the cobra uses zero instead of 44. Other raw body choices stay unchanged.

The manservant is male, matching his prose and portrait, and remains
unplaced without a reset. His four programs and level 300 stay. The Cleric
remains female and keeps adept aid, paid-healer services and fly help. Keep
the Loremaster's 35 teacher rows and individual psionicist special, and the
Quartermaster's shop, healer flag, prices and stock. The gladiator's diploma,
white stone and key remain carried rewards, not newly equipped gear.
All room flags, area school/safe/no_quest settings, XP125 and reset caps
stay. No new NO_UNDEAD rooms are added. The giant-lizard portrait should
show the described red-scaled, blue-tongued quadruped; artwork is separate.


The Shire creature profiles
--------------------------

`halfling` and `halfling_untrained` use humanoid. `pig` uses
quadrupedal_mammal and `chicken` uses bird. No new area fields are needed.

Halflings default to 91 cm and 30 kg, Small, with ordinary living organs,
hands, speech, blood, pain, soul and remains. Ordinary magic/poison
resistance and sneak adapt their second-edition defences. They gain no
innate armour, generic infrared, automatic hide/invisibility or fear bonus.
Tall or stout prose alone does not select a separate halfling subrace.

The code-only bow_accuracy trait gives trained NPC halflings +3 to hit
with an actual bow shot. It does not improve melee, thrown knives, damage
or attack count. PCs keep their existing abilities; their halfling family
identity is recognised for matching only. Slings remain deferred until
there is a supported sling weapon type. No bows or automatic shooting AI
are added to the Shire residents.

Use halfling_untrained for the toddlers and youths. It shares all ordinary
halfling defaults and has a same-body trait parent that explicitly removes
bow_accuracy. No vnum exception, ACT marker or individual trait command is
used. MSTAT reports the trait source and explicit removal.

The NPC shoot command now fires at least one shot below level 30. Its
higher-level counts, normal combat/equipment/safety checks and PC behaviour
stay unchanged. NPC snapshot remains disabled. The racial bonus applies
once to an eligible bow shot, not to every ordinary attack with a bow held.

Pigs default to 75 cm, 120 kg and 130 cm long, Medium, with tail, hooves,
fangs and an ordinary bite. No innate armour or active special is added.
The Shire's particularly fat pig is 200 kg and 150 cm long. Its slow-looking
prose does not add a permanent combat penalty.

Chickens default to 40 cm, 3 kg and 50 cm long, Small. Their bird body has
feathered wings, two legs, feet, beak and normal organs, with no arms or
speech. Ordinary attacks use the beak only. Brief fluttering does not grant
full flying behaviour. Existing egg resets remain; no egg-laying routine.

The cow and bull reuse cattle. The older cow is 140 cm, 550 kg and 240 cm
long; the bull is 165 cm, 900 kg and 280 cm. Both are Large. The cow cancels
inherited horns in both its body and attack XOR masks, leaving hoof-only
attacks. Do not repeat inherited NO_ARMS/NO_SPEECH restrictions, since that
would cancel them. The pig, chicken, bull and horse likewise drop only
their repeated absence flags. Generic cattle/horse defaults stay unchanged.

Keeper/Thain are Elite; Wizard/Prince/Battle master are Rare. Other Shire
mobs remain Common, including the level-50 smith. Rank HP applies once.
Private caster, guard, thief and Keeper poison roles stay individual;
ordinary halflings do not gain venom. The elven warrior and dwarf prince
retain their authored mage specials. The traveller is human; unspecified
village staff are halflings. Individual stature/bulk overrides are descriptive.
The unusually tall dwarf prince explicitly uses Medium instead of the
ordinary dwarf Small size; shared dwarf defaults stay as they are.

Cow is female and pig male, matching their prose; horse sex stays unknown.
The Green Dragon room calls its male innkeeper a host. Shiriff movement
flags stay as authored, while stale non-mobile comments are corrected.
Three dagger reset caps now match the existing first-post cap of nine.
This tidies the authored data; non-shop E resets currently ignore that cap,
and all four authored daggers already equip. No new guard weapon resets are added. Shared bardiche 1113 stays unchanged,
including its Midgaard use; only stale small-sword comments are corrected.
All room flags, mob placements and five shops remain. Shiriff 1132 stays
unplaced. The cloak's ABOUT slot and the rainbow staff's light role are
already correct. Receptionist artwork is handled separately from area data.


New Ofcol and the gold dragon profiles
-------------------------------------

New Ofcol reuses human, cattle, horse, pig and chicken. The new
gold_dragon_kin profile uses humanoid; ancient_gold_dragon uses dragon.
No new area fields, ACT markers or individual trait commands are needed.

Gold dragon kin are a DD4 interpretation of the Keeper's sons and his
draconic knights. They default to 190 cm, 100 kg and Medium, with normal
living humanoid organs, hands, speech, blood, pain, soul and remains.
Scales and fangs add an ordinary bite fallback. Their code-only traits
give -10 natural armour, with ordinary fire and poison resistance.
They gain no innate immunity, flight, fear aura, HUGE or extra attacks.
Their existing equipment keeps its role; the shared Golden Claw is an
ordinary weapon, not a body-part weapon attached to every holder.

Ancient gold dragons default to 700 cm shoulder height, 6000 cm total
length and 40000 kg, Large with HUGE. These are descriptive DD4 estimates,
informed by the second-edition Gold Dragon rather than exact book weights.
Their dragon body supplies ordinary claw, fang and tail attacks. They fly
and swim, see infrared, invisible and hidden creatures, resist magic and
are immune to fire and poison. Their code-only natural armour is -40.
Fear uses the existing independent in-combat aura, not a special slot.

spec_breath_gold_anatomical needs a usable head, like the other anatomical
breaths. It chooses fire or gas equally and uses the existing spell damage,
saves and targeting. It adds no daily pool or extra attack. Gold dragon kin
use it for their single special opportunity. Ancient gold dragons select
70% gold-breath and 30% mage opportunities; a failed choice does not fall
through into another special. These percentages describe opportunities,
not guaranteed successful casts. MSTAT shows the inherited set and traits.
Polymorph, gem luck, summoning, detect-lie and quest powers remain deferred.
Existing player abilities and other dragon profiles stay as they are.

The Keeper's local Sanctuary, Protect, sentinel and scavenger choices stay.
Do not repeat inherited HUGE, infrared or detect-invisible in his raw XOR
masks. His signet ring, Golden Claw and healing-staff Dragon Orb are carried
loot instead of worn equipment. All three remain, but their worn bonuses
cease; the native anatomy and defences supply the intended dragon model.
The shared item definitions and other mobs' equipment are unchanged.

The hen (612), cows (613/614) and horse (615) drop repeated
NO_ARMS/NO_SPEECH XOR bits, so those restrictions remain inherited. Dairy cow 614 cancels horns in both
its body and attack XOR masks, leaving hoof-only attacks. Grazing cow 613
keeps horns/hooves and is female, matching its portrait. Horse 615 remains
mountable and wimpy, with a local height of 130 cm, weight of 250 kg and
length of 200 cm.
Pig 616 uses ordinary pig defaults; hen 612 has no full flying behaviour.

Diana (601) and the Dragonlords (630) are Elite. The captains (602/603),
priestess (628) and Dragonknights (633) are Rare; the Keeper (631) is Boss.
Others remain Common. Rank HP
applies once; current-HP breath damage also rises with the dragon's rank.
Diana keeps NO_EXPERIENCE. All source levels, alignments, legacy positions,
aggression, passivity, wimpy and sentinel choices stay as authored.

The two teacher lists, seven shops and other individual specials stay.
Priestess 628 keeps her supportive adept and teaching role; the hostile
spec_cast_priestess routine is not a substitute. No new paid services are
added. Tracy keeps repairs, the teller keeps banking, and the maid keeps
janitor behaviour. The bard does not gain an arbitrary combat caster.
Marty's son is Jack (610); Jim (604) is the separate blacksmith. The hall
now describes its three reset sons. All 102 room flags/exits, including seven NO_UNDEAD rooms, stay intact.
Portrait corrections are separate from the area-file patch.
For hand-written .are mobs, keep programs first, then archetype/rank,
then teacher rows, then named metadata such as MobWeight. Scribe emits
that order for you.


Ambush, pigeons and environmental hazards
----------------------------------------

Ambush reuses human, goblin, drow, dog and horse. The new pigeon profile
uses bird. The new environmental_hazard body supplies fire_hazard and
smoke_hazard. These choices use the existing archetype field.

Pigeons default to 15 cm high, 30 cm long and Small, with flight, feathers,
wings, a beak and normal living bird anatomy. Their ordinary attack uses
the beak. Weight stays unknown: the whole-kilogram field cannot describe
an ordinary sub-kilogram pigeon. No fractional weights or new active
specials are added. Avoid repeating inherited flight and absence flags.

The hazard body has no head, eyes, arms, legs, heart, speech or corpse.
It is inorganic and mindless, with no blood, pain or soul. Poison, drain,
sleep, charm, hold, paralysis and psychic effects do not affect it. Physical
attacks can still defeat these existing encounters. The profiles do not
add ACT_OBJECT or change the existing targeting rules. Their Medium size
describes the hazard's extent; height, length and weight remain unknown.

Fire hazards use the existing blast attack verb and ordinary attack amount.
The code-only natural_damage_type trait selects fire as their damage kind.
The internal kind is named; MSTAT shows fire and its profile origin. Other
native damage kinds can reuse this mechanism through species/archetype
traits. No ACT marker, vnum exception, area field or individual trait input
is needed. Zero removes the override. Existing weapon attacks, object/skill
categories and player abilities retain their rules. Source magic/material
classification and ordinary contact handling remain in place.

These flames are fire-immune and take the usual 50% extra damage from cold
or water. Smoke is also fire-immune, but keeps its existing ordinary damage
in this chunk. Proper suffocation, including creatures that do not breathe,
remains a separate shared mechanic. There are no extra attacks, fireballs
or active special opportunities. Authored aggression, sentinel, detection
and blank hazard descriptions stay. The old raw body mask 383 was deliberate;
the new body now supplies it through inheritance. The hazards use neutral
sex data. The old fire_elemental is a training example, not a suitable real
fire elemental. Review and fix it before actual use; it is unchanged here.

War dog 4504 is about two feet at the shoulder: 61 cm high, 40 kg and 110 cm
long, Medium. It keeps spec_fido. Horse 4511 keeps its mountable/sentinel
settings, ordinary 150 cm/450 kg/230 cm Large dimensions and both resets.
Dog, horse and pigeon drop repeated NO_ARMS/NO_SPEECH raw XOR bits, so
those restrictions remain inherited. Goblin women 4524 use female data;
mixed children 4525 remain neutral. Collective names and single resets
stay; their dimensions describe a representative individual.

Elite guard 4517 stays Rare. Gruluk 4530 becomes Elite; Nevdara 4527 and
the personal guard 4528 become Rare. Other named captives and guards stay
Common. Rank HP applies once. All levels, alignments, sleep/Curse effects,
legacy positions and authored behaviour remain. No permanent injuries or
daze are added from story prose. Essabella keeps her protective spec_guard;
Nevdara gains no arbitrary caster and the goblin archer gains no shooting AI
or racial accuracy bonus. Shared Omu equipment and all other loot stay.

All 39 room flags/exits, area XP 115, 76 object definitions, traps, keys,
placements and caps are preserved. Only the approved small grammar fixes
change prose. The three hazard portrait replacements are handled separately.


The Circus, lions and elephants
-------------------------------

The Circus uses human for its visitors and staff, including its two short
adult performers. Their stature does not make them fantasy dwarves. The
new lion and elephant archetypes use quadrupedal_mammal; brown_bear, horse
and dog reuse their existing profiles. No new area fields or ACT markers
are needed. Individual measurements remain descriptive centimetres and
whole kilograms, with ordinary existing inheritance and XOR rules.

Lions default to 110 cm at the shoulder, 190 kg and 280 cm total length,
Medium. They have a tail, paws, claws and fangs, with ordinary claw/bite
choices, infrared and -4 natural armour. These choices do not grant extra
automatic attacks. The 2e Lion informs the anatomy; these dimensions and
the uniform armour are DD4 estimates. Shared leap/rear-rake and precise
surprise mechanics remain deferred, as with the puma. Circus lions keep
their authored aggression and alignment, with male data matching the text.

Elephants default to 330 cm at the shoulder, 5500 kg and 650 cm total length,
Large with HUGE. Tusks and forelegs supply piercing and blunt ordinary
attack choices; their feet are not flagged as hooves. Natural armour is -4.
The 2e Elephant informs the anatomy; the exact measurements are DD4 estimates.
There is no five-attack tabletop sequence, extra racial HP/damage multiplier,
fire vulnerability or new mountability. The Circus elephant stays passive
and sentinel. Its one reset row still has cap two, so refills can add a second.

The elephant's spec_elephant has a 15% chance per eligible special opportunity
to strike its current Small or Medium opponent for 2d6 blunt/nonmagic damage.
HUGE targets are excluded. It needs normal NPC action readiness, a present
trunk and a usable head. Head trauma blocks it; arm trauma does not treat the
trunk as a missing human arm. Normal safety, physical contact, phase and
damage defences apply. It uses the ordinary special timing, without a hold,
stun, throw or multi-target routine. Shared fear-of-fire morale and trunk
inventory manipulation remain separate future work.

PART_TRUNK is anatomy, not an ordinary attack-part choice. Scribe accepts
bf trunk through its existing body-mask input, and MSTAT names the part.
It can appear among ordinary death parts. It belongs to the existing head
injury/recovery group: target head and head trauma affect native trunk use,
and Regenerate restores that use when it heals the injury. There is no new
target category, trauma bit, area field, human hand or equipment slot.
The existing guardian_daemon also gains trunk anatomy. Its acid-spit/caster
mix, daily limit and damage stay; it does not gain the elephant's special.

Ivan 4409 and the Owner 4426 become Rare. Rank HP applies once, with normal
rank labels and randomised-loot treatment. The Ringmaster, high-level ticket
clerk and chef stay Common; occupation, name and level alone do not decide
rank. The legacy HP letter in 8r8 does not set a rank and remains untouched.
The short performers are 110 cm/35 kg/Small; children use 110 cm/20 kg/Small.
Ivan is 200 cm/150 kg, the big clown 190 cm/110 kg and the worker 190 cm/105 kg.

The starving bear uses 300 kg instead of the shared 450 kg; its existing hug,
armour and native loot stay. Both underfed horses use 375 kg instead of 450 kg,
without a new starvation penalty. The white horse is an ordinary horse before
a false horn is fitted, not a real unicorn. The guard dog is a rather large
65 cm/40 kg/110 cm, Medium. Existing aggression, detection and mountability
choices stay. Remove repeated NO_ARMS/NO_SPEECH raw bits on the five animals
which already have body 36, so they do not cancel inherited restrictions.

Red clown noses belong on the head, not the body. The assistant's blindfold
also belongs on the head rather than in her hand; its effects and her Sleep
remain. The lion tamer now carries the existing cage key 4436 beside his
wielded whip. All other original objects, affects, keys, locks, reset caps,
placements and room flags stay. The two shops and games remain, along with
the unplaced alternate illusionist, worker and thief. The thief keeps his
special and the chef keeps NO_QUEST. No new helper summon, combat magician,
tamer domination or actual dragon is inferred from the circus scenery.
The four portrait replacements are handled separately from the area patch.


Moria, constrictors and coloured bats
------------------------------------

All 33 Moria definitions stay Common. No rank promotion is part of this
conversion. Reuse human, orc, hobgoblin, kobold, snake, puma, hill_giant,
troll and troll_infant, with the approved individual dimensions. The short
mage 4157 is human; the small dancing troll 4159 uses the juvenile profile.
Existing source levels, alignments, behaviour and effects stay. Brainless,
frothing, fear or sickness in prose does not add new status flags.

Constrictor_snake uses serpentine_reptile, with ordinary fangs, -4 natural
armour, 70 kg, 600 cm and Large. Height remains unspecified and HUGE is not
added. Its spec_constrictor has a 15% chance per eligible special opportunity
to crush its current Small or Medium, non-HUGE opponent. Damage is two dice,
each with ceil(level / 2) sides, with at least one side: 2d6 at levels 11-12,
2d25 at level 50 and 2d50 at level 100. This scales with the actual user's
level, without a vnum check. Physical blunt/nonmagic contact, phase rules,
defences, sanctuary and ordinary native action timing still apply.

The crush needs a usable head and tail; matching trauma blocks it. It adds
no lasting Hold, stun, throw, extra bite or DOT. Player Coil/Constrict and
the old special-NPC permissions are unchanged. Full persistent binding and
escape rules remain a separate design. The new special keeps the old poison
special's +5 XP contribution, without an extra archetype modifier. Python
4000 now uses it instead of poison and keeps its detect-invisible, behaviour,
corpse-shaped container and coins. Garter snake 4001 loses harmful poison;
its existing snake profile gets a local length of 80 cm and weight 0, meaning
unknown rather than a literally massless animal. Other venomous snakes
4053, 4102 and 4158 keep the anatomical poison-bite special. Guardian snake
4102 is 300 cm/15 kg/Medium; ordinary brown snakes keep 120 cm/2 kg/Small.

The new myriapod body reuses existing many-leg, head, eye and organic-organ
flags, without human arms or speech. Centipede adds piercing mouthparts
through the existing fang/bite representation, chitin through husk_shell,
and -1 natural armour. It defaults to 15 cm and Small; height and sub-kilogram
weight stay unknown. These three explicitly harmless, vegetation-foraging
DD4 creatures gain no venom or active special. They keep the ordinary
individual-arthropod physiology used for reviewed wasps and bees; no extra
mindless, analgic, bloodless, soulless or blanket immunity is inferred.

Lightning_bat, fire_breathing_bat, gas_bat, frost_bat and acid_bat use winged_mammal.
They keep normal living bat anatomy, flight and ordinary fang attacks, and
share the existing bat echolocation trait. The estimates are 30 cm body
length, 1 kg and Small; height remains unspecified. Ordinary bat defaults
and vampire bat form dimensions stay. Their matched immunities are
electricity, fire, poison, cold and acid, respectively. These are living
magical bats, not inorganic elementals. No opposite-element vulnerability,
physical/magic immunity, damaging aura or fire shield is added.

Each bat profile supplies its original breath spell through an anatomical
head check. The new spec_breath_gas_anatomical completes the shared wrapper
set. Original spell, selection, chance, damage and special XP mapping stay;
there is no new daily cap or extra attack. The old individual breath rows
are removed so another builder can reuse the whole profile. No individual
trait fields or ACT markers are needed. The 2e Bat, Centipede and Snake
entries inform the anatomy; coloured-bat powers, harmless centipedes and
the exact DD4 measurements/mechanics are deliberate adaptations.

Hill giant 4058 explicitly stands nine feet tall, so use local 274 cm/350 kg,
Large and cancel inherited HUGE through its existing body XOR. Its giant
family, defences and rock rules remain. Mountain lion 4057 uses puma, not
common lion. Both remain unplaced. Remove repeated raw absence masks on
snakes and the centipede/puma/bats so they do not cancel inherited anatomy.

All 121 room flags/exits, 15 objects, original placements/caps, carried-vs-worn
choices and other loot stay. Moria's definitions produce 62 initial and 67
refill inhabitants, carrying 17 then 20 items. Its imported Midgaard thief
3005 still follows the original shared cap and is converted with Midgaard,
not counted again here. Both mage routines, thief invisibility/Sneak and
silver dagger remain. The only text corrections are bares its teeth, there
is, and a reset comment. The two portrait corrections are handled separately.


Shadow Keep, animated armour and shadow fiends
---------------------------------------------

Shadow Keep's 27 definitions use the reviewed human, wraith, skeleton,
zombie, horse, dog, gnome and dwarf profiles, plus giant_electric_eel,
giant_rat, animated_armour and shadow_fiend. The captain stays Elite and
the king stays Boss; all others stay Common. There are no rank promotions.
These profiles append without renumbering earlier entries or adding area
fields, body flags or ACT markers.

Giant_electric_eel uses fish: ordinary head, eyes, heart and other organs,
fins, tail and fang bite, swimming and electrical immunity. Its estimates
are 300 cm length, 30 kg and Medium, with height unspecified and no HUGE.
Spec_electric_eel keeps the old lightning-breath spell, one-in-four victim
selection, damage and +10 special XP. It describes bodily discharge instead
of lightning from a mouth. A missing or injured tail blocks it; Regenerate
repairs the injury. Arm/head trauma does not disable its electrical organs.
Ordinary native readiness, safety and no-fight rules apply. There is no new
radius model, extra attack or one-hour recharge. The old eel stays unchanged.

Giant_rat uses quadrupedal_mammal with rat anatomy, swimming and ordinary
fang attacks. Defaults are 30 cm shoulder height, 8 kg, 60 cm body length
and Small. It has no blanket poison immunity or inherited venom special.
Shadow Keep's rat keeps its authored venom through individual
spec_poison_bite. Shared disease and fire-aversion remain separate work.

Animated_armour uses bipedal_construct: inorganic, mindless, no speech,
blood, soul, pain or corpse, with the existing construct pattern of poison,
sleep, charm and drain immunity. Defaults are 175 cm, 35 kg and Medium.
It gains no extra natural armour, weapon immunity, magic immunity or golem
breath. The two guardians keep individual spec_guard and their worn silver
equipment and axes. Ordinary loot still drops when the construct breaks.

Shadow_fiend is an intelligent creature of darkness, not an undead shadow.
It uses humanoid limbs and senses, claws, fangs and small wings, without
physical heart, brain or guts. It is inorganic and leaves no corpse, but
retains soul, pain and communication. It is not mindless, soulless, analgic
or undead. Defaults are 183 cm and Medium, with mass unspecified. Small
wings do not grant flight. It is immune to fire, cold and electricity and
inherits the existing spec_demon as this DD4 creature's casting routine.

The internal shadow_light_response trait opts an NPC into live lighting
responses. In darkness it gains +2 melee accuracy and -8 natural armour,
and receives 50% damage. Ordinary light gives +1, -4 and 100%. Direct
sunlight or an active Continual Light orb gives +0, +0 and 200%. Natural
sunlight keeps the normal shelter, weather and underwater rules. Spell
orbs count on the ground or worn in the light slot; inactive, deleted or
unequipped inventory orbs do not brighten the room. Other light uses the
normal room-darkness check. No vnum test selects the creature, and PCs do
not inherit this new trait. MSTAT shows its source and current response.

Damage changes once after contact/resistance, before ordinary combat
defences and the existing damage cap. Zero damage stays zero. Light does
not add automatic fleeing, attacks, possession, darkness/fear casting,
special turning or an undead flag. The tabletop light response informs
this adaptation; direct-target Light damage has no matching DD4 spell.

The first undead soldier 16601 is a skeleton. The decaying soldier 16607
is a deliberate zombie caster: use the existing area AFF/body XOR to remove
inherited mindless/no-speech while keeping its individual spec_cast_undead.
The wraith keeps invisibility, flight, muteness and its ordinary claw loot,
and inherits the reviewed wraith phase/drain routine. Its raw corpse flag
is removed so it cannot cancel inherited no-corpse.

The great stallion 16608 uses local 165 cm shoulder height, 600 kg and
260 cm length, Large without HUGE. Its mountable flag stays. Page boy
16617 uses adolescent 155 cm/45 kg. The cook and ambiguous prisoner are
human; the prisoner's rat comparison does not grant lycanthropy. Human
bodyguards and that prisoner retain their authored poison power through
the existing arm-checked spec_poison_palm. The priest keeps mage casting:
his demon book and offensive scroll support that authored occult role.

All source behaviour, effects, sexes, levels and alignments stay. Clear
repeated absence masks on rat/horse/dog, and the eel's erroneous no-heart
mask. Preserve all 98 room flags/exits, 57 objects, keys, original caps,
placements and carried-versus-worn choices. The old soldier caps yield
52 initial inhabitants/125 carried items and 53/127 after refills. The
shared coin 16615 and its seven Omu Central consumers stay unchanged.
The archer's bow is already correctly flagged and wielded. No matching
Shadow Keep source exists; other source/area pairs stay unchanged.


Solace and ordinary birds of prey
--------------------------------

Solace's 57 definitions use human, ghost, rat and bat, plus four new
bird archetypes: macaw, raven, hawk and giant_falcon. These append without
renumbering existing profiles or adding species, area fields or ACT flags.
The Captain is Elite; the Lieutenant and Sergeant are Rare. Other ranks
stay Common. Old HP letters are not rank markers.

All four new birds use the existing bird body, including wings, feathers,
beak, tail, ordinary organs and legs without arms or speech. They add claw
anatomy and flight. The macaw's ordinary attack is its beak; the other
three choose a beak or claw through the normal single-attack rules.
Weapons, targeting, trauma and Regenerate keep their existing behaviour.
These birds gain no extra attacks, venom, armour, resistances, immunities,
casting, mountability or HUGE flag. They retain normal blood, soul and pain.
No new leg-trauma restriction is introduced.

Descriptive defaults are macaw 40 cm standing height, 1 kg and 90 cm
length; raven 25/1/65; hawk 30/1/60; giant_falcon 80/25/150. The first
three are Small and the giant falcon is Medium. These are whole-kilogram
DD4 estimates informed by the second-edition bird entries. Pet levels stay
12, 15, 18 and 21. Timed eye injuries, diving attacks, scouting, surprise
bonuses and mimicry remain separate future work, not hidden extra powers.

The five ghosts use the existing semi-material ghost profile, ordinary
nonmagic immunity and corpse-free physiology. Bar ghosts can speak, and
duplicate raw corpse flags no longer cancel their inherited no-corpse.
Their old broad undead casting is removed. The tired human adventurer
loses an incorrectly assigned undead special; it is not moved onto the
ghost gambler. The Lieutenant keeps the effective cleric routine, with
the earlier overwritten mage row removed. Other authored specials and
Identify, banker and tinker roles stay.

The human child gains ordinary arms and speech by clearing an erroneous
body XOR. Rats, bat and birds stop cancelling their inherited absence
flags. The ghost barkeeper is male and the old lady female, matching their
words and retained portraits. Individual dimensions reflect the reviewed
children, bulky guards, lean clerk and thief, tall patrons and large rat
and bat. The ordinary bat stays Small, 1 kg and 30 cm long; it does not
use the larger vampire transformation. Dimensions remain descriptive.

Tika's shop row now names Tika rather than duplicating Otik. Prices,
opening hours, stock and existing shop roles stay. Matt's ringmail uses
the body wear flag that its reset already expects. The city and depository
keys are carried rather than forced into a hold slot they cannot use.
Their prototype vnums still control the original locks; old value metadata
is unchanged. High Dice and its greet program stay exactly as authored.

All 120 room flags and exits, other 112-object data, encounter caps,
placements, levels, alignments and aggressive/passive choices stay. The
old caps still produce 129 initial and 142 refill inhabitants, with 238
and 244 carried items. Imported Limbo tobacconist Ghelt 81 is unchanged
and is not one of the 57 conversions. The shared robe and Underdark reset
also stay unchanged. No matching Solace source exists; other source/area
pairs remain in sync.


Lemming folk and the Beast
-------------------------

Lemming Caves uses lemming_folk for its nine speaking, tool-using
lemmings and bear_wolf for the Beast. Both append to the archetype list;
existing species and archetype numbers stay the same. There are no new
species, area fields, traits or body flags.

Lemming_folk uses humanoid as its anatomical base: head, eyes, ears,
arms, hands, fingers, two legs, feet and ordinary organs. It adds a short
tail and tooth anatomy through existing PART_FANGS, with an ordinary
nonvenomous fallback bite. Wielded tools and weapons keep precedence.
It retains speech, blood, soul, pain and normal corpses. Defaults are
70 cm standing height, 15 kg and Small; length is unspecified. The small
adult uses 50 cm/8 kg, while the large lemming, carpenter and wounded
lemming use 90 cm/25 kg. These are descriptive DD4 estimates, not child
combat penalties or tabletop statistics. No extra senses, speed, disease,
burrowing, attacks, resistances or immunities are added.

Bear_wolf uses quadrupedal_mammal, adding tail, paws, claws and fangs.
It has four legs and ordinary organs, without arms or speech. Defaults
are 120 cm shoulder height, 300 kg, 240 cm length and Large without HUGE.
Ordinary fallback attacks choose a claw or bite; no extra attack, active
special, hug, venom, disease, lycanthropy or magical defence is granted.
All template HP, damage, critical, haste and XP modifiers are zero.

The Beast keeps its existing Elite rank, senses, aggression and magical
wielded claw treasure 29960. That treasure keeps its flags, claw verb,
damage bonus, TAKE/WIELD and reset. It remains an ordinary loot weapon;
this conversion adds no BODY_PART flag or trauma rule. The nine lemmings
stay Common, including the smith, doctor and mage.

Doctor 29955 uses existing spec_cleric_adept: cleric combat, then adept
player support while idle. ACT_IS_HEALER enables the usual paid services
and prices. Existing undead checks still apply. No priest-service flag
is added. The adept skips NPCs, so the patients remain narrative actors;
their bandages do not create permanent trauma, bleeding or HP penalties.
The smith keeps PRACTICE, teacher base 15 and all three 75-percent lessons.
The mage keeps its casting and robes. Three male pronouns are matched by
male sex fields; the other authored sex values stay.

All authored behaviour stays, including the small lemming's aggressive
and wimpy combination. It normally avoids initiating against awake players.
The nervous miner gains no new wimpy flag. Raw body masks stay zero so
they do not cancel the new inherited anatomy or the Beast's absence flags.
Only the doctor's healer flag is added to individual ACT data.

The conversion fixes up/down exit descriptions in 29951, two text typos
and the north exit description in 29979. All 31 room flags, destinations,
doors and key links stay. All 16 objects, original reset commands, caps,
placements and loot stay. Existing caps still give 24 initial and 32 refill
inhabitants, carrying 12 and 13 objects. Keep area exp_mod 115, key 29964,
the bedding and heroic helm. Pickaxe 29965 is the intentional ITEM_DIGGER
with a fixed-level-12 I reset; weapon pickaxe 29950 remains separate.
There is no matching Lemming Caves source. Other source/area pairs stay
unchanged, and the ten existing portraits are retained.


The Pit, venomous claws and mastodons
------------------------------------

The Pit uses nine humans plus jakyra, bile_demon and mastodon. The three
new archetypes append without renumbering earlier entries. Painkiller
keeps Elite; the Sentinel is Boss as the arena's final champion. Others
stay Common. Human defaults are 175 cm/75 kg/Medium; Turbo uses 165/55.
Myra's female sex matches her wording and retained portrait. The Sentinel,
Valan and Osarr use the approved human interpretations and replacement art.

Jakyra uses quadrupedal_mammal with paws, claws, fangs and tail, four legs
and ordinary organs, without arms or speech. Defaults are 100 cm shoulder
height, 120 kg, 200 cm length and Medium without HUGE. Ordinary attacks
choose claw or bite. Spec_poison_claw is a reusable venomous-claw action:
an awake, ready creature needs usable claws and a living current opponent
in the same room. Normal safety, no-fight, injury and action rules apply.
It keeps the existing one-in-four poison roll and Venom spell's level,
save and duration, with the normal poison-special XP allowance. It adds
no physical damage or venom to ordinary bites. No new trauma rule or
body-part flag is added. The anti-evil axe is carried as loot; its flags
and statistics stay unchanged.

Bile_demon uses humanoid arms, hands, legs and ordinary living organs.
Its spiked head masses use existing horn anatomy and a normal head-based
fallback attack. Defaults are 220 cm, 350 kg and Large without HUGE.
It inherits the existing spec_demon and keeps its glaive. The duplicate
area special is removed, leaving one effective routine and XP allowance.
No blanket demon immunities or new breath attack are granted.

Mastodon uses quadrupedal_mammal with tusks, trunk, tail and four legs,
Large with HUGE. Defaults are 305 cm shoulder height, 6000 kg and 700 cm
length; mass and length are DD4 estimates. The existing elephant trait
profile supplies -4 natural armour. It uses ordinary tusk/foreleg attacks
and spec_elephant, with no automatic mountability or rhino horn. Raw body
36 becomes zero to retain inherited no-arms/no-speech. Its magical wolf-claw
treasure remains an ordinary wielded loot weapon. Template HP, damage,
critical, haste and extra XP scalars are zero for all three new profiles.

The elephant trunk power now asks is_safe about its opponent, matching
ordinary NPC combat. A SAFE room or area can contain legitimate scripted
NPC fights, as in the Pit. Existing protected-target checks still apply.
Readiness, no-fight, current-opponent, same-room, living target, Small or
Medium/non-HUGE target, usable head/trunk and injury checks stay. The
15-percent choice, 2d6 damage, wait, physical contact, resistance and
phasing rules are unchanged. All room and area flags remain intact.

The Official and arms dealer keep acid breath through the existing
head-checked wrapper. Turbo uses poison palm with the same Venom rules.
The Defender keeps executioner; both psionicists retain their casting and
authored flight. All source behaviour and regeneration remain.

Turbo's duplicate M reset is collapsed so her three existing equipment
rows run, with one Turbo in the same room. Their empty ground descriptions
are filled. The Jakyra's axe is carried instead of being dropped by an
incompatible wield reset. These changes keep 12 inhabitants and put 33
items on mobs instead of 29. The single Official/cap and unused ticketing
room stay. Eight greet programs, all 48 room flags/exits/doors and other
reset caps/placements are preserved. Two spectator robes use existing
plain-wearable ITEM_TRASH rather than furniture: ARMOR would automatically
gain AC at creation, so plain wearables preserve their zero-AC behaviour.
The unnamed Sentinel blast, other object stats, shop prices/hours and
unplaced artifacts stay. Text fixes cover the champion, copied Jakyra
description, mastodon, paladin, rack keyword and misleading reset comments.
No matching Pit source exists; other source/area pairs stay unchanged.


Great Eastern Desert creatures and native powers
-----------------------------------------------

These archetypes are available through the existing archetype field:
small_scorpion, purple_worm, adult_brass_dragon, drider, myconid,
dustdigger and camel. The new fungoid and radial_invertebrate entries
are basic body species, not archetypes to put directly on an area mob.
Existing IDs and individual area fields have not changed.

The coral snake 5002 stays harmless: it has an ordinary bite and no
poison special, poisoned body weapon or inherited venom. Its original
harmless description stays. Its 90cm length and 1kg weight are descriptive.
The school cobra's separate harmless exception also stays.

Small scorpions use arachnid anatomy, pincers, a shell and a tail.
Purple worms are living, limbless animals with a gut, mouth and tail.
Both use an attached poisoned body-part weapon for the stinger, not a
new stinger flag or target group. The weapon has ITEM_BODY_PART,
ITEM_POISONED and ITEM_DONOT_RANDOMISE, no TAKE, value[0]=6 for tail
targeting and value[3]=16 for sting. Tail injury/disarming stops it;
ordinary pincers or bite remain available. Regenerate heals an attached
injured weapon, but does not recreate one that has been cut off.
Source weapon dice are 1d4 for 5033 and 2d4 for 5034; DD4's existing weapon
creation still sets live dice from level, before the randomiser flag.
That existing scaling is unchanged. These stingers add the usual +5
Venom allowance once through their archetypes. Purple worm Swallow
adds its own +10 once. Swallow continues to exclude HUGE targets,
not every Large target. Purple worms have -4 natural armour and HUGE.

Adult brass dragons are living, speaking dragons with wings, organs,
blood and a corpse. They have ordinary adult-dragon senses, flight,
fear, magic resistance, fire immunity and -20 natural armour.
spec_breath_brass chooses scorching heat or sleep equally. Heat uses
the existing fire-breath spell; sleep uses the existing Sleep spell,
its save and immunity. It never refreshes an already sleeping target.
The power needs a usable head and a permitted current opponent.
The special adds +10 XP. Existing area aggression and Sanctuary stay.

Driders use arachnoid_humanoid, with a drow upper body and spider legs.
They have infrared, magic resistance, -10 natural armour, the reviewed
drow light penalty and elf sleep/charm/ghoul-touch defences. Their
trait profile supplies these directly: a trait parent is not shared
across different basic body species. Default specials are 70 percent
mage and 30 percent spec_paralysing_bite. The bite needs usable fangs,
does no second physical hit, saves against poison/paralysis and holds
for one tick. It does not refresh an existing hold. The profile's +5
defence allowance and the specials' +10 each are counted once.

Myconids are conscious organic fungi, with limbs and a cap/head region
but no animal eyes, heart, brain, gut or usable blood. They are not
mindless, undead, inorganic, soulless or automatically ANALGIC, and
they leave a physical corpse. spec_myconid_spores has a one-in-four
eligible-pulse chance to cause Confusion for one tick after a poison
save. It uses the ordinary level-based confusion penalties without
stacking, refreshing or confusing mindless/attackable-object targets.
The special adds +5 XP. The local shaman keeps cleric casting with
70 percent cleric/30 percent spores, and releases spores without speech.
Full rapport, reproductive spawning and corpse-animation systems are
not supplied by this archetype.

Dustdiggers have five arms around a central mouth, with no separate
head, eyes, legs or heart. They are living, bloodless radial animals,
not inorganic objects or soulless hazards. They keep physical corpses,
have -6 natural armour and use the existing natural-attack-type trait
for a central bite. spec_engulf uses ordinary Swallow at a 15 percent
eligible-pulse attempt rate, but checks the arms/torso instead of
requiring a head. It keeps one meal, ordinary safety, HUGE exclusion,
escape and digestion. The special adds +10 XP. The area keeps its
authored oasis disguise and Hide; there is no new global mirage system.
The reference is the original AD&D 1e Monster Manual II, p61. A 2e entry
has not been verified; this older reference was an explicit decision.

Camels use quadrupedal_mammal with padded feet, a tail and a biting
mouth. They have -3 natural armour and ordinary bite/foreleg attacks.
spec_camel_spit has a one-in-four eligible-pulse chance to blind a
target with eyes for one tick, with a normal save and no HP damage.
It does not refresh blindness. The special adds +5 XP. This does not
make a camel mountable or implement contagious rabies automatically.

The new combat powers require a living, awake, available NPC and a
permitted current opponent in the same room. Normal player-order
authority, safety and resistance rules still apply. Physical venom,
spores, spit and engulf cannot touch an incorporeal target. No new
player spells, ACT markers, trait fields or area-file fields were added.

In Great Eastern Desert, the brass dragon 5005 and dracolich 5010 are
Boss, nomad leader 5006 is Elite, commander 5007 and shaman 5014 are Rare.
Others are Common. Rank HP applies once. All aggression, Wimpy,
Sentinel, Stay Area, regeneration, fame, levels, alignments and sexes
remain as authored. Body XOR masks now inherit the intended anatomy.
Brass dragon retains her key and scroll. Dracolich uses its normal
power 70 percent/fire breath 30 percent and carries its old mail/halberd
as loot. Two stinger objects/resets are added; other object properties,
population caps/placements, room flags, random exits and key links stay.
There is no matching Scribe source for this area.


Plains of the South creatures
----------------------------

rolling_boulder and carnivorous_grass are available through the existing
archetype field. They append to the table without changing earlier IDs.
No new body species, area fields, flags or individual trait fields are needed.

rolling_boulder uses limbless_construct. It is inorganic, mindless,
bloodless, ANALGIC, soulless and corpseless, without organs or limbs.
It has the same poison/sleep/charm/drain immunities as the other animated
objects, -10 natural armour and an ordinary crushing attack supplied by
the existing natural_attack_type trait. It has no extra attack, physical
immunity or magical-weapon requirement. Its defaults are 150cm high and
long, 4000kg, Large without HUGE. These measurements are descriptive.
Its XP modifier is zero. The four existing rock weapons in this area
are now carried loot; their object properties have not changed.

carnivorous_grass uses plant, retaining an organic, bloodless, ANALGIC
body without animal organs, eyes, arms or speech. Its bulb has a
targetable mouth/head region, many little legs and teeth for an ordinary
bite. The archetype removes the parent's NO_HEAD and NO_LEGS through
the usual XOR fields. It supplies hidden detection and the existing
spec_fido corpse-eating behaviour, with no toxin or entangling power.
Defaults are 60cm high, 80cm long, 10kg and Small. Both area variants
now inherit the same anatomy. The second reset for 18405 uses cap 4,
matching the existing overall cap and restoring its room 18404 placement.

The two crazed rabbits reuse rabbit and attached incisor weapon 313.
Place an E reset for 313 after every rabbit M reset. The existing head
target category, bite verb, injury/disarming/Regenerate rules and
level-based weapon scaling apply. Do not add venom or fangs. Their
authored aggression stays, unlike the passive rabbits in other areas.

The dead King 18404 uses lich and Boss, with the existing rank HP bonus
applied once. He keeps his individual spec_cast_psionicist at 100 percent,
his original stone reset, coin pile and other effects. The stone is
anti-evil, so the King drops it in the barrow as he did before; its
flags and affects stay unchanged. Lich physiology supplies undead
defences, fear and the existing 20 percent
chill contact on qualifying damaging unarmed hits. That trait does
not depend on the default lich special. The contradictory permanent
poison flag is removed. His default dimensions are 175cm/45kg/Medium.

Ben 18407 uses human at 175cm/75kg/Medium and remains Common. His level 300
and protected hunting-service flags are individual area design, not
human defaults. Clear the old NO_ARMS/NO_SPEECH body mask so his arms
and scripted dialogue work. Keep his senses, infinite-light object,
15 trophy programs, greet and two speech programs. Cockatrice wings
now give the intended two 30-QP tokens and existing 55 platinum; the bad
mp0load 280 line is corrected to mpoload 380. Remove the surplus final
endif in the bone-golem program. Other payouts and the 7 percent rename
piece chance stay. All 70 room flags, exits, music and original objects
are preserved. There is no matching Scribe source for this area.


Highlands creatures
------------------

giant_frog, sheep, plesiosaur and giant_leech are available through the
existing archetype field. They use existing body species and append
without renumbering earlier entries. The old fire_elemental example is
now a reviewed profile on elemental_being, at its original ID.

giant_frog uses quadrupedal_amphibian, with a long tongue, swimming,
ordinary organs, four legs, no arms or speech, and -3 natural armour.
Defaults are 60cm high, 110kg, 180cm long and Medium. The Highlands frog
is explicitly over six metres long: it overrides these to 200cm/5000kg/
650cm, Large and HUGE. Its six resets equip mouth weapon 11530 with
ITEM_BODY_PART and ITEM_DONOT_RANDOMISE, no TAKE, head category 1 and
bite verb 10. Existing targeting, injury, disarming, Regenerate and
level-based weapon scaling apply. Its individual spec_swallow keeps
the existing 15 percent attempt, one meal, safety and head gates,
escape/digestion and HUGE-target exclusion. Large targets remain
allowed. The ordinary profile has no automatic Swallow or venom.
Tongue grappling, exceptional jumping and fire avoidance are follow-ups.

sheep uses clean quadrupedal_mammal, with hooves and a tail, normal
organs and an ordinary hoof attack. Defaults are 80cm high, 70kg,
120cm long and Small. No goat demonstration bonuses, vulnerabilities,
poison, scavenging or special function are inherited. The twenty
existing sheep placements and passive wandering stay.

plesiosaur uses quadrupedal_reptile. Fins replace ordinary feet on
its four swimming limbs; it has a tail, organs, blood and a biting
mouth, without arms or speech. It has -3 natural armour, swimming and
ordinary piercing bites. Defaults are 1500cm long, 5000kg, Large/HUGE,
with height unspecified. Nessy's child overrides length to 400cm and
weight to 150kg and cancels HUGE through its individual body XOR.
Both keep their individual spec_breath_any assignments; magical breath
is not a default for every plesiosaur. Keep the mother's Sanctuary,
the child's infrared and both passive Sentinel settings. The child's
sex is female to match her description; no new sleep behaviour is added.

The Holy Slayer uses ghost and keeps Rare, neutral alignment, existing
effects, Holy Basher and holy armour. Existing semi-material, conscious,
corpse-free undead rules apply. His sex is male to match the prose.
The Lord stays Boss and the elite barbarians stay Elite. Other ranks
remain Common. Human defaults are 175cm/75kg/Medium; the short, tough
off-duty guard overrides height/weight to 165cm/80kg. Existing warrior,
mage and cleric roles, bagpipes and four tower keys remain individual.

fire_elemental is conscious, inorganic, bloodless, ANALGIC and
corpse-free, with a soul, flame arms and an eye/head region, but no
legs, physical heart or animal brain/gut. It is not undead or mindless.
Defaults are 300cm/Large, unknown weight, -8 natural armour, neutral
combat scalars and +10 XP. It is immune to fire, ordinary nonmagical
weapons, poison, sleep, drain, hold and paralysis. Cold/water
vulnerability is a DD4 choice. The existing magical/nonmagical
distinction approximates the sourcebook's weapon protection.
Ordinary unarmed contact uses magical fire damage at normal strength;
there is no frost breath, poison special or extra active attack.
magical_natural_attacks is an internal code-only trait, shown in MSTAT.
Only opted-in NPC ordinary unarmed hits use it; equipped weapons,
skills, spells and players keep their existing categories. No new ACT
bit, area field or individual trait override is available. The local
guards keep their authored flight, Fireshield, senses, mute flag,
aggression and Sentinel behaviour. Cross-water rules, object ignition
and broader Fireshield changes remain separate follow-ups.

giant_leech uses limbless_invertebrate with a mouth/head, simple eyes,
ganglia and gut, blood and ordinary pain sensation, but no limbs,
speech or one discrete targetable heart. Defaults are 10cm high, 8kg,
100cm long and Small. Its ordinary mouth bite uses the existing
natural_attack_type trait, without invented fangs or a new weapon.
It inherits the exact existing spec_bloodsucker at 100 percent. The
bogleech's feeding chance, damage and blood interactions, effective
aggression/scavenging/Stay Area, swimming, hiding, sneaking and senses
are unchanged. Its squirming program and death-drop cure-poison paste
are unchanged. No wrapper, attachment system, disease or Drained
condition is added. The feeding special's +5 XP applies once.

All dimensions are descriptive. All 200 room flags/exits/randomisation/
keys, 30 original objects, original reset locations/caps and behaviour
are retained. Six frog-mouth E rows are added. No Highlands source
file exists; the four other source/area pairs remain unchanged.


Cobresh and the Yitrite Mines
----------------------------

ancient_fire_dragon, fiery_demon, hell_hound and acid_hell_hound are
available through the existing archetype field. They append without
renumbering older entries and use existing body species. No new ACT
trait bit, area field or individual trait override is needed.

ancient_fire_dragon uses dragon, with wings, scales, claws, fangs,
a tail and ordinary living organs. It has no arms. Defaults are
700cm at the shoulder, 30000kg, 4500cm long, Large and HUGE. These
are DD4 estimates for an ancient wyrm, not measurements in the area.
It flies, sees infrared/hidden/invisible targets, resists magic and
is immune to fire. Natural armour is -40. Ordinary unarmed attacks
are magical through the existing internal magical_natural_attacks
trait; equipped weapons keep their own contact categories. It has
the existing independent fear aura and spec_breath_any_anatomical
as its sole selected special. This keeps the old breath choices and
probabilities, but needs a usable head. It does not add mage casting,
a daily breath limit, poison immunity or a canonical dragon colour.
The Cobresh Dragon is female and Boss; its rank HP applies once.

fiery_demon uses living humanoid anatomy with wings, claws, fangs
and a barbed tail. Defaults are 240cm/180kg/Large, without HUGE.
It flies, has -10 natural armour and magical ordinary natural
attacks, resists magic/cold and is immune to fire/electricity/poison.
It keeps spec_demon and its existing spell selection. This is a
generic DD4 fiery fiend, not a Balor; complete fire immunity and
the armour value are DD4 choices. No passive Fireshield, regeneration,
telepathy or additional caster is supplied. All24 local encounters
stay Common and keep their authored behaviour, buffs and senses.

hell_hound uses quadrupedal_mammal, with four legs, paws, fangs,
a tail and ordinary organs, without arms or speech. Defaults are
90cm at the shoulder, 65kg, 150cm long and Medium. It has infrared,
fire immunity, -6 natural armour and an ordinary bite. Its sole
special is spec_breath_fire_anatomical, retaining the existing fire
breath and requiring a usable head. No new stealth or probabilistic
detection system is added.

acid_hell_hound is the same-body variant with hooves instead of paws.
Defaults are 100cm/100kg/180cm/Medium. It shares hell_hound's internal
armour trait and adds ordinary acid bite contact through the existing
natural_damage_type trait. This changes one hit's damage kind; it
does not add a second acid hit, poison or a hoof attack. Fire breath
stays fire. The hoofed, acid-salivating variant follows the Cobresh
description rather than claiming to be an ordinary2e hell hound.
Neither profile gains cold vulnerability or magical ordinary contact.

The existing fire_elemental remains on elemental_being, at ID2,
with its reviewed flame physiology, magical fire contact, -8 armour,
immunities, cold/water vulnerabilities and +10 profile XP. The local
eight-foot elemental overrides height to244cm, stays Large with no
HUGE and unknown weight, and keeps its muteness and individual fire
breath through spec_breath_fire_anatomical. A profile without an
active special does not erase this local assignment.

The seventeen dwarves use the existing dwarf profile: RES_MAGIC,
RES_POISON, infrared and trained melee +1 against goblinoids/-4
opponent accuracy against giants. Human Stain and Mazzy stay human.
Erich remains Elite, Guntharson Boss and Ivan Rare; the other nineteen
definitions stay Common. Named councillors are not automatically
promoted. Individual guard/thief/cleric/archmage roles stay, and Bjorn
keeps his free spec_cast_adept assistance rather than paid services.
All original aggression/passivity, StayArea, Sentinel, Wimpy, senses,
buffs, levels, alignments and sexes stay.

Shared dwarf defaults are130cm/70kg/Small. The seventeen Cobresh
dwarves explicitly override size toMedium, following the approved
area table; earlier dwarf profiles and areas stay unchanged.
Individual height/weight estimates follow the prose: Hanson120/65, Olaf130/90, entrance guard
130/120, smith125/95, Friedreich130/90, scout120/55, citizen120/75 and
baker130/100. Stain is190/65 and teenage Mazzy160/50, both Medium.
Dimensions remain descriptive and are not extra rank/combat bonuses.

When an effect becomes inherited, clear its duplicate raw XOR input.
The Dragon keeps HUGE and its senses; dwarves keep infrared; hounds
keep their lack of arms/speech; the elemental keeps no corpse and
its local mute input. MSTAT shows resolved anatomy and trait origins.
Traits are internal and cannot be assigned to individual area mobs.

Keep all39 original objects and every original reset/equipment slot.
The Dragon's Scale/Talon/Eye and the demon's wearable tail remain
ordinary player loot, not BODY_PART objects. Keep the workers'
shackles and their movement penalties, royal gear, keys, forge,
digger, Stain's pipe and triple-Heal whiskey. The bakery's zero
markup is valid: normal base price, not a free shop. All198 rooms,
flags, exits, two NO_UNDEAD wards and area XP40 remain. Only obvious
mobile prose errors are corrected. No matching Scribe source exists;
the four other source/area pairs remain unchanged.


Old Thalos creatures
-------------------

baby_beholder, tame_lamia and stone_golem append at IDs 200-202 using
existing bodies. Earlier IDs, body species, flags and trait IDs stay.
These profiles are available through the existing archetype field.

baby_beholder uses limbless_invertebrate: a living organic orb with
head, eyes/eyestalks, heart, brain and gut, no arms/legs, retained
juvenile muteness and an ordinary fang bite. Flight represents
levitation without wings. Defaults are 45 cm body diameter, 12 kg,
45 cm length and Small, with -2 natural armour and no resistance
package. It inherits the existing spec_cast_mage at 100 percent.
Keep the local good alignment, detect-invisibility and friendly
Sentinel/Scavenger/Wimpy build. Clear the old heartless/inorganic
raw body input; the inherited juvenile mute result stays. Adult
eye rays, anti-magic cones and full adult beholder rules aren't
implemented by this baby profile. Its dimensions and armour are
DD4 estimates, informed by the 2e adult's living anatomy/levitation.

tame_lamia uses centauroid_mammal: a human upper body and four-legged
feline lower body, with paws/claws/tail, ordinary organs, blood,
speech, pain, soul and corpse. Defaults are 150 cm ground-to-head,
140 kg, 210 cm length and Medium, with -7 natural armour. It has
no active special, extra ordinary claw attack, resistance or combat
scalar adjustment. The good level-four ambient pets retain their
collars and behaviour. The twenty-mob cap across twenty-eight reset
locations stays. This tame DD4 variant does not supply the wild
lamia's Wisdom drain, charm/illusion package or follower status.
Those need a separately reviewed wild profile, not an unannounced
power increase for the city's pets.

stone_golem uses bipedal_construct, with head/eyes/ears, arms/hands,
two legs and feet, but no biological organs. It is mindless, mute,
inorganic, bloodless, ANALGIC and without a usable mortal soul;
it is neither undead nor ACT_OBJECT. Physical remains and loot stay.
Defaults are 290 cm, 900 kg, Large without HUGE, -5 natural armour
and a magical ordinary fist/pound attack using existing traits.
It resists magic and is immune to nonmagic, poison, sleep, drain,
charm, hold and paralysis. HP/damage/critical/swiftness scalars are
zero. Its profile adds +10 XP for the passive package; its selected
special has the separate ordinary +10 allowance. The 2e height/mass
inform the rounded dimensions. This DD4 defence package is not full
2e spell immunity or a numeric +2-weapon threshold. Mud/flesh/stone
spell exceptions remain separate future mechanics.

spec_cast_slow is a reusable, silent innate power for the current
opponent. It gets one 50 percent attempt per eligible special
opportunity, with normal action/safety/visibility/magical contact
checks and no stacking. It calls the existing Slow spell, keeping
its saves, Haste/Quicken interactions and ordinary spell outcomes.
It needs no voice, spellbook or brain. It is a probabilistic DD4
adaptation of the golem's sourcebook power, not an alternating-round
timer, unrelated caster package or extra custom damage attack.

Gordon becomes Elite; the other thirty-four definitions stay Common.
Rank HP applies once. Keep his teacher base 25, four knowledge limits
85 and warrior base 30. His actual iron breastplate remains loot;
the prose now says a fine suit of plate rather than full mithril.
High priest 5307 keeps free adept healing, Sanctuary, teacher base
25 and six 85 magik limits. The two druids use spec_cast_druid.
All guard/mage/cleric/janitor/fido roles otherwise stay individual.
All six shops keep their types, prices, hours and stock.

Human defaults are 175 cm/75 kg/Medium. The scholar is 160/50;
big guards 5331/5334 are 190/95. Thaellor inherits dwarf 130/70/Small,
RES_MAGIC/RES_POISON/infrared and the approved +1/-4 training traits.
Doe uses deer 100/70/160/Medium. Male 5323 uses stag 130/180/210;
larger 5324 overrides to 140/220/230, still Medium with no HUGE.
Rabbit uses 25/2/40/Small and existing head-targeted incisors 313.
One E row equips every new spawn from its single reset location.
The snake stays nonvenomous, 60 cm long with explicit unknown weight
zero; clear its obsolete heartless input and keep normal organs.
Fido is a small dog at 30/8/50, with its original scavenging special.
All dimensions remain descriptive estimates/defaults.

The four golem sword resets become carried loot, so fists supply
their ordinary attacks. Keep all Ioun stones in their authored held
slots, every other object/equipment row and both teachers. Existing
deer/rabbit/body XOR duplicates are cleared without cancelling the
intended no-arms/no-speech or construct physiology. All aggression,
passivity, Sentinel, StayArea, Wimpy, base levels, alignments and
sexes stay. No new trait, body species, area field or ACT bit is added.
All 86 rooms, nine NO_UNDEAD wards, 32 original objects and reset
caps/placements remain. No matching source exists; the four other
source/area pairs stay unchanged. Obvious mobile prose errors and
the janitor's blank description are corrected as approved.


Thalos creatures
----------------

beholder, lamia and mimic append at IDs 203-205. MAX_MOB is 206;
earlier profiles keep their indices. No basic species or area field
is added. The internal trait catalogue gains antimagic_gaze_pct.
The ordinary archetype field selects these creatures in Scribe.

beholder uses the living limbless_invertebrate body, with a head,
central eye, eyestalks, fangs, heart, brain and gut, but no arms or
legs. It can speak. Flight represents levitation without wings.
Defaults are 180 cm body diameter/length, 250 kg, Medium, -10 natural
armour, no elemental resistance or blanket magic immunity. An ordinary
fang bite is physical contact; the native rays are magical abilities.

spec_beholder has a 50 percent attempt per eligible opportunity,
choosing one eligible ray: Slow, Fear, Sleep, Cause Serious, normal
NPC-only Charm Person or Unmaking. It skips redundant Slow/Sleep and
illegal charm targets. It needs usable head/eyes and sight, not hands
or speech. Normal saves, resistances, phase, action, safety and player
order rules remain. One opportunity produces one ray, not ten casts.

Unmaking is deliberately named differently from Disintegrate. It deals
level + random(level, 2*level) MAGIC|ENERGY damage, save for half.
It never destroys equipment, invokes automatic execution or suppresses
a normal corpse. A lethal damage hit still uses ordinary death rules.
Telekinesis, petrification and a separate death-ray instant kill are
not supplied by this profile.

antimagic_gaze_pct is a code-only percentage, 25 on beholder. An awake,
uncharmed, able, living source with usable head/eyes can counter its
visible current opponent's magical activation in the same room.
One casting, native special or spell-item use shares one decision,
including multi-spell consumables and multi-target staves. Several
eligible sources use the strongest percentage once. A nested distinct
activation gets its own scope. Pure nonmagical psionics, physical skills
and physical animal specials do not trigger it. Existing buffs/passive
worn effects remain. Death, separation, blindness/head injury or loss
of the eligible combat state stops it immediately. It never sets a
room flag or leaves a persistent cone. This is a focused DD4 adaptation
of brief central-eye pulses, not the complete second-edition cone.

The countered activation follows its normal resource/item use: the
attempt still spends its normal mana, charges or consumable. Invalid
casts and item targets still fail their ordinary checks. No extra
counterspell roll is made for each effect or target of that activation.
Native spell callers use cast_native_spell with the actual function
pointer and SN; run_mob_special supplies their activation scope. An
