innate physical routine must not be routed through this magical check.

lamia uses centauroid_mammal: living organs, human upper body/arms,
four legs, paws, claws and tail; 150 cm ground-to-head, 140 kg,
210 cm length, Medium and -7 natural armour. It adds no ordinary
claw attack or compulsory behaviour. The tame_lamia profile is unchanged.
spec_lamia has a 25 percent attempt, selecting an eligible Wisdom touch,
self Displacement or normal Charm Person. Player charm and protected
NPC targets remain excluded. Displacement retains its normal duration
and non-stacking policy; this is an illusion approximation, not a new
mirror-image engine or sourcebook once-per-day charge system.

The touch uses normal magical/charm and supernatural-control defences.
It needs usable arms and real close contact with a minded target.
Each failed save adds -1 Wisdom, capped at -3 across all lamias, for
four ticks. Effective Wisdom cannot be pushed below 3 by this condition.
Successful additions below the cap refresh its duration; capped or
resisted attempts do not. NPC Wisdom reflects this condition too.
It causes no permanent loss, player obedience, extra damage or Drained.

mimic uses amorphous_organism, with no fixed head/arms/legs but living
brain/gut/heart, distributed sight and pseudopods. It can bleed, feel
pain, have a soul and leave a corpse. It is not ACT_OBJECT, inorganic
or mindless. Default speech can be removed individually, as for chest
5202. Defaults are 90 cm/500 kg/240 cm, Large without HUGE, -3 natural
armour, infrared, acid immunity and existing sunlight accuracy -1.
Its ordinary attack is a blunt pound. Native glue needs no head or voice.

spec_mimic has a 25 percent physical adhesive-grab attempt against its
current close opponent. A normal NONMAGIC|HOLD save prevents it.
Otherwise it applies one tick of Hold, without damage, stacking or
refresh. The effect records its live creature source; movement or death
releases only that grip. It cannot survive logout/reload. Another source's
hold is preserved. Freedom removes the adhesive condition through its
normal cure path. No weapon theft/sticking or solvent command is added.
Creature-sourced affects show (C) in the existing affect origin display.

lamia touch, mimic glue and unmaking ray are internal effect names,
not player-learnable spells or new services. They use spell_null in the
player spell table and do not belong in class training groups. Native
power routines supply their actual actions.

Thalos 5200 is Elite; all eight other definitions stay Common. Rank HP
applies once. The beholder's shield/cloak, mimic lights/robe and four
golem swords become carried loot. Golem armour and both actual seal keys
stay in their original slots. The golem reuses stone_golem and its Slow.
All original base levels, build numbers, alignments, sexes, aggression,
passivity, wandering, Scavenger, Sentinel, Stay Area and Wimpy remain.
The twenty wild lamias keep their daggers and original reset cap/order.

Mimic disguise overrides keep 500 kg: boulder160cm/220cm length;
floor20/450; pottery60/300; planks45/400. The small horned lizard uses
5 cm/unknown whole-kg weight0/20 cm/Small, individual anatomical horns
and the existing ordinary nonvenomous lizard bite. No extra horn attack.
All dimensions remain descriptive DD4 estimates; size and HUGE differ.

All 80 rooms/flags/exits, 39 original objects, imported Drow reset rows,
keys/container chain, original loot, placements and caps are preserved.
There are no NO_UNDEAD rooms, shops, teachers or matching Scribe source
in Thalos. The four existing source/area pairs elsewhere stay unchanged.
The baby beholder and tame lamias in Old Thalos keep their existing powers.


Golem Settlement and awakened golems
-----------------------------------

awakened_golem appends at ID 206. MAX_MOB is 207; all earlier indices
stay unchanged. It uses the existing bipedal_construct body. There are
33 body rows, 207 archetype rows, 43 internal traits and 111 Scribe
special-function names. No area field, body flag or trait ID is added.

These golems are independent people with families, jobs and spellcasting.
They have a head, eyes, ears, arms, hands, fingers, two legs and feet.
They are inorganic and have no heart or blood, but they are minded,
can speak, feel pain, have a soul and leave physical remains. Do not
infer mindlessness, ANALGIC or SOULLESS from inorganic construction.
The shared bipedal_construct body and earlier construct profiles stay
unchanged. Ordinary body-part targeting and injury rules still apply.

Defaults are 240 cm tall, 300 kg and Large, without HUGE. The child
20400 overrides height/weight/size to 160 cm/100 kg/Medium. Tall healer
20405 uses 270 cm/350 kg. Dorak 20413 is human, 195 cm/110 kg/Medium.
Length is unspecified. These whole-number dimensions are descriptive
DD4 estimates, informed by local prose and the second-edition clay
golem's physical scale, rather than an exact standard golem conversion.

The profile has RES_MAGIC, IMM_POISON and IMM_DRAIN, -3 natural armour
and magical natural attacks. Its ordinary unarmed fallback is pound,
verb 7, with blunt contact. This adds no extra attack and does not make
an equipped weapon magical. Profile XP adds 10 percentage points;
HP/damage/crit/swift modifiers stay neutral. There is no inherited
special, Slow, passive regeneration, immunity to nonmagical weapons,
blanket spell immunity or immunity to charm, fear and sleep.

All thirteen golems clear the old individual body mask 304 to zero.
The body now supplies inorganic/no-heart anatomy and allows speech.
Repeating 304 would XOR away the inherited physiology and add muteness.
Do not copy a resolved body mask into an individual XOR field.

spec_cast_construct_adept is a reusable free helper, not a paid healer
service. It needs an awake, able, corporeal NPC with usable head/arms,
speech and no silencing or anti-magic-room restriction. It refuses
combat, including incoming attackers. On a 25 percent opportunity it
casts normal Cure Serious on one other injured, visible, corporeal NPC
whose resolved body species is bipedal_construct. It skips dead, full-HP,
NO_HEAL, fighting/attacked and harmful helpful-spell targets. A native
cast still passes through the existing magical-activation gateway.
It never heals itself or takes two actions in one opportunity.

Otherwise it delegates the existing free PC adept assistance, retaining
the vampire safeguards. This special is selected individually on 20405;
it is not a compulsory awakened_golem ability. Its special XP allowance
is +10, matching the existing adept role. Ordinary adept code stays
unchanged. No ACT_IS_HEALER or paid services are added.

Temple 20415 keeps NO_MOB, INDOORS, SAFE, HEALING and NO_UNDEAD. NO_MOB
blocks ordinary random wandering; this patch does not make the town
visit the healer. Controlled/following NPCs can still be brought there
through existing movement rules. The cleric 20409 uses the existing
spec_cast_cleric_helper: cleric combat casting and idle free PC aid.
All other individual specials and original combat/service roles stay.

Master warrior 20411 is Boss (7x rank HP once); destroyer 20412 is Rare
(2x once); the other twelve definitions remain Common. The destroyer
has two initial inhabitants and can grow to six through its unchanged
reset cap/order. HP letters are not rank markers. Rank supplies no
automatic dimension change.

All original behaviour, base levels/build numbers, alignments, sexes,
weapons, armour, loot and two shops' terms/hours remain. All 33 room
blocks/flags/exits, 25 objects and reset rows/caps/placements stay.
Population/items remain 46/73 initially and 50/77 after refill. Only
the original three of five children receive toys. The area exp_mod 125
stays. The beggar's tattered cloths becomes tattered clothes.
There is no matching Scribe source; four other source/area pairs stay
unchanged. All fourteen existing portraits are retained.


The Keep of the Warlock
-----------------------

The creature catalogue now has 34 body rows and 213 archetype rows. The
new body tripodal_organism appends at 33; six profiles append at 207-212:
four_armed_demon, flesh_golem, grell, crocodile, flind and otyugh. Existing
IDs stay unchanged. The gnoll and ogre family placeholders now have their
reviewed anatomy and dimensions. Neither had a previously converted area
consumer. Player race opt-ins stay unchanged.

PART_3_LEGS uses body bit 50. In Scribe, bf three_legs sets that individual
XOR bit; an otyugh already inherits it, so do not repeat it there. MSTAT,
body displays, HAS_LEGS, leg targeting, trauma, body-part weapons and death
parts recognise it. It adds no new target command, injury or area column.
The new tripod body is organic, with living organs, three legs and feet.
The otyugh adds its central mouth/sensory region, eyestalks and tentacles.
As with the yochlol, the mouth region uses the existing head-targeting
group rather than inventing a humanoid skull. Tentacles use the existing
arm/limb injury group. More arms do not add attacks or weapon slots.

four_armed_demon is an unnamed DD4 demon, not a claim that the local
creature is a particular formal tanar'ri type. It has four arms, claws
and fangs, magical natural contact, -3 natural armour, infrared, fire/cold
resistance and electricity/poison immunity. It keeps spec_demon and +10
profile XP. Local 15902 retains its original flight without adding wings.
Defaults are 244 cm/220 kg/Large. No gate or extra attack stream is added.

flesh_golem uses bipedal_construct, but XORs inorganic OFF. Its assembled
flesh is organic and bloodless, with no functional heart. It is mindless,
mute, ANALGIC and SOULLESS, with physical remains. Defaults are 230 cm/
160 kg/Large, -1 natural armour and magical ordinary pound. It has magic
resistance and immunity to nonmagical damage, poison, sleep, drain, charm,
hold, paralysis, fire, cold and electricity. Profile XP adds 15 points.
It has no compulsory policing role, blanket spell immunity or berserk
creator-control system. The earlier awakened and stone golems stay intact.

Two reusable code-only traits supply its reactions. electricity_healing_pct
is 20: a positive electrical contact restores floor(contact damage*20/100),
minimum 1 HP, capped by effective/aggravated healing limits. NO_HEAL still
blocks healing. fire_cold_slow_ticks is 1: a positive fire/cold contact
causes ordinary non-stacking Slow until the next character update.
An existing Haste or Quicken is cancelled first, as with normal Slow.
Repeated contacts do not refresh an existing Slow. A zero/missed/blocked
attempt supplies no reaction. Each actual pre-immunity packet is handled
once, including folded firebrand/chill and direct retaliation paths.
Fire-shield resistance is honoured for an opted-in construct; unrelated
legacy NPC paths stay as before. These are elemental-contact reactions,
not damage-over-time healing or an excuse to count an immune hit as blood.

grell is a minded, organic floating brain with a beak and ten tentacles.
It uses the existing limbless_invertebrate body with functional tentacles
in the arm injury group. Defaults are 300 cm overall hanging height,
150 kg, 120 cm body diameter/length, Medium, -5 natural armour, flight
and electricity immunity. Existing local detection/infrared supply the
DD4 sensory model. Its normal attack is a physical beak strike.
spec_grell has a 25 percent tentacle-paralysis opportunity against its
current close opponent. Normal poison/paralysis saves and physical-contact
permissions apply. It needs usable tentacles and a poisonable organic
target. It deals no bonus damage, does not stack/refresh Hold and expires
at the next character update. Profile XP is +10, special XP +5. Local
15911 loses the unsupported Pass Door and its old ordinary poison special.

crocodile uses quadrupedal_reptile: 45 cm shoulder height/150 kg/300 cm
length/Large, scales, tail, claws and fangs, swimming, ordinary fang bite
and -5 natural armour. Profile XP is +5. The local fido feeding role stays.
No venom, compulsory extra tail attack or death-roll system is supplied.

gnoll is a living hyena-like humanoid with tail/fangs and normal hands,
230 cm/130 kg/Large. It has no automatic bite or natural armour. The
second-edition AC 5 (10) includes worn armour. flind uses the same gnoll
family but is shorter and broader: 198 cm/140 kg/Medium. Its new internal
melee_accuracy is +1. spec_flind has a 15 percent opportunity to use normal
Disarm while armed. Weapon, injury, visibility, phase, safety, NO_DROP and
body-part protections remain. This DD4 training works with other wielded
weapons too; there is no vnum/name test. Profile XP +5, special XP +5.

ogre is now 290 cm/160 kg/Large with -5 natural armour, +5 profile XP and
armed_melee_accuracy +2. That accuracy applies only with a real eligible
wielded weapon; no bonus is given merely for carrying one. These two
accuracy traits are signed code-only values. They do not change stored
hitroll, base damage or player opt-ins. The existing family matching stays.

otyugh uses tripodal_organism: a living central toothed mouth, eyestalks,
two tentacles and three legs. Defaults are 140 cm/400 kg/210 cm/Large,
infrared, -7 natural armour, ordinary bite and +10 profile XP. spec_otyugh
keeps idle fido corpse feeding, and has a 25 percent combat opportunity
for a physical tentacle grip. Normal NONMAGIC|HOLD save/contact rules apply;
HUGE targets and unusable tentacles are excluded. There is no extra damage,
stacking or refresh. Grip expires on the next update or immediately when
its source moves, dies, separates or logs out. Freedom removes it too.
The new internal tentacle grip condition uses creature-source metadata
and shared grip cleanup, alongside mimic glue. Other sources' holds stay.
Saved live-creature grips cannot survive a later login. Special XP is +5.
Actual infectious disease and immunity to its own disease remain on the
shared disease retrofit list. A poison effect is not silently called disease.

spec_executioner_local retains the existing KILLER/THIEF attack trigger,
with ordinary action/visibility/safety checks, but calls no city guards.
It is individually selected for flesh golem 15906; stock executioners
elsewhere are unchanged. It adds no XP allowance or forced aggression.
The cat and hawk retain their deliberately authored mage specials. Dagar
keeps mage casting; the apprentice keeps archmage casting. The explicitly
mindless zombie servants lose archmage casting. The troll keeps fido while
passive regeneration works; it does not gain the inherited rock special.
Local firedrake uses spec_breath_fire_anatomical to retain its original
unrestricted breath with head-injury checks, rather than the body's limited
daily breath. The war dog keeps its old poison attack via spec_poison_bite;
its super-rabies prose is still the existing poison abstraction. Mimic
15912 inherits the existing glue power and physiology.

Keep Baragar Boss, Alara Elite and the demon Rare. Only foreman 15919 is
newly Rare (2x HP once); other seventeen definitions stay Common. Baragar
is male. The small apprentice uses 155 cm/48 kg; war dog uses 60 cm/35 kg/
100 cm/Small. Other dimensions inherit. All are without HUGE. Sourcebook
physical measurements inform these whole-number descriptive DD4 estimates;
rank does not supply body size, attacks, behaviour or levels.

All authored aggression/passivity, Sentinel, Stay Area, Wimpy, NO_QUEST,
levels/build numbers/damage and alignments remain. All 43 room blocks,
flags/exits/doors, reset rows/caps/placements/equipment and original loot
remain. Population/items are 37/70 initially and 73/148 after refill.
There is no matching source, shop, teacher, program or NO_UNDEAD room.
Four other source/area pairs stay unchanged. Flind bar 15935 becomes a
proper weapon, keeping blunt verb 7, +5 damage affect, flags and reset.
Scroll 15907's lightening breath becomes lightning breath. Deliberately
mixed helpful/harmful potions stay. Only lower cavers/lower caverns twice
and trangular/triangular are corrected in mobile prose.

The registry now has 47 traits, 115 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows.
tentacle grip is an internal spell_null condition, not a learnable spell
or new priest service. Baragar, Alara and grell portraits are regenerated
separately; portrait publication is independent of applying this patch.


Mahn-Tor Keep
-------------

The catalogue now has 219 archetypes, 34 body species, 48 internal traits,
116 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Six new archetypes append at
213-218: treant, beaked_cave_beast, mistling, yeti, ice_worm and minotaur.
Existing IDs, body rows, flags and player-race trait opt-ins stay unchanged.
These profiles use the existing plant, humanoid, mysterious_being and
limbless_invertebrate bodies; the rock toad reuses giant_frog on the
quadrupedal_amphibian body. No area columns or ACT markers are added.

minotaur is an organic humanoid with a bull head, horns, hooves, normal
organs, hands, speech, blood, soul and pain eligibility. It has infrared,
-4 natural armour and ordinary unarmed horn contact; a wielded weapon
keeps precedence. Default dimensions are 230 cm/250 kg/Large, without HUGE.
The 2e illustration also has hooves. Neither horns nor hooves create an
extra ordinary attack or equipment slot. Its profile XP adjustment is +5.

The new code-only armed_melee_damage trait is +2 for minotaurs. It adds
flat damage to an eligible ordinary melee weapon hit, before the normal
damage adjustments. It does not change stored damroll, the percentage
dam_mod field, unarmed attacks, body-part weapons, bows, ranged skills or
spells. It follows the actual primary or secondary melee weapon used.
Like other traits, define it in the code registry and sparse species or
archetype lists. Do not put armed_melee_damage in Scribe or an area file.
There is no player-race opt-in for this trait in this patch.

spec_horn_gore is reusable by a horned NPC. Against its current visible,
legal opponent it has a 15 percent opportunity to deal ordinary nonmagical
piercing damage of 2d max(4, (level+1)/2), using integer division. It takes
one combat-pulse wait. Head/horns, head trauma, action, room/area safety,
contact, phase and resistance checks apply. It adds no hold, stun or
knockdown. The special XP allowance is +5. Giving another creature the
special still requires appropriate anatomy; no name or vnum test exists.
Local mage/cleric/anti-paladin assignments override this inherited special,
so the old caster roles keep their normal casting selection.

treant is an intelligent organic plant. It retains bloodless, no-heart and
ANALGIC plant physiology and soul eligibility, with wooden physical remains.
Its XOR delta enables a functional head/eyes/ears, speech, branch arms,
hands and root legs/feet. Natural armour is -10, fire vulnerability uses
the normal 150 percent damage response, and ordinary branch blows are
nonmagical blunt contact. Defaults are 450 cm/2000 kg/Large/HUGE, with +10
profile XP. The old treant is 500 cm/2500 kg. Both keep Sentinel. Tree
animation and structural damage remain shared-mechanics retrofit work.

beaked_cave_beast is the area's own furry, clawed, bird-beaked hybrid. It
uses the humanoid body, is mute but minded and organic, and has normal
organs, blood, soul, pain eligibility and physical remains. Ordinary
claw/beak attacks and spec_breath_acid_anatomical preserve its authored
combat role. Defaults are 230 cm/250 kg/Large. It gains no assumed
owlbear hug, feathers, invented armour or resistances; profile XP is 0.
Its old detection abilities and aggression stay.

mistling uses mysterious_being for a substantial mist without organs,
anatomical limbs, speech or a physical corpse. It is inorganic, ANALGIC,
minded and non-undead, retaining soul eligibility. Flight permits movement;
local Sneak stays. Fixed height/weight/length are unknown (0); size is
Medium. No arbitrary Sanctuary, Pass Door, phase or nonmagic immunity is
copied from the old animated_mist example. Profile XP is 0.

yeti is an organic humanoid mammal with long clawed arms and anatomical
fangs, ordinary claw attacks and normal organs/remains. It is mute. The
244 cm/136 kg/Large dimensions follow the 2e eight-foot/about300-pound
description. Natural armour is -4; cold resistance is the normal 50 percent
DD4 approximation of protection from normal cold, and fire vulnerability
is 150 percent. Profile XP is +5. Local 2313 keeps its frost breath through
the anatomical wrapper. Surprise-dependent gaze/fright and heat-absorbing
squeeze remain explicit shared-mechanics retrofit candidates, rather than
extra powers stacked on this area's breath.

ice_worm is a separate DD4 creature, not an asserted remorhaz or purple
worm. It has living worm organs, a mouth/fangs, blood and physical remains,
without limbs/speech. Existing mind eligibility stays. Its explicit almost
50-foot length is represented by 1500 cm, estimated thickness150 cm and
12000 kg, Large/HUGE. It has cold resistance, +5 profile XP and anatomical
frost breath in place of the mismatching acid assignment. No poison,
stinger, Swallow, added armour or damage multiplier is introduced.

All34 definitions are converted. Reused ghost2300 retains undead casting
and a voice; wraith2306 keeps muteness and inherits its drain special,
replacing poison. Five ogres use the completed ogre defaults. The barbarian
and adult captive are human. Darkoth is a living minotaur despite the name
spec_cast_undead. Nasturn keeps cleric casting; Tyrgoth, Gorak and Dorgar
keep their original absence of a caster assignment. No teacher, healer or
priest-service flags are added. Minotaur cold discomfort stays meaningful.

Mahn-Tor2333 is Boss. Ogre chieftain2311 and eight guildmasters2325-2332
are Elite. Inner guards2321 and ring-keeper2324 are Rare; other22Common.
Rank HP applies once. Dice letters, sourcebook morale and the word elite
are not rank markers. Dimensions are individual descriptive choices,
not automatic rank bonuses. Amyrok is200cm/160kg/Medium; Gorak280/450,
Mahn-Tor300/500, Belrak230/200 and Dorgar250/200 areLarge. No minotaur
gets HUGE merely for an impressive adjective.

Remove only the approved conflicting legacy body masks and duplicated
inherited AFF bits. Rock toad2303 keeps its cow-sized150cm/500kg/250cm
Large body and gets anatomical horns/spines, without an extra attack.
All original ACT behaviour, levels/build numbers/dice letters/damage,
alignments, sexes and equipment remain. All100 room blocks/flags/exits,
both NO_UNDEAD wards2374/2390, reset caps/order/slots, eight key-ring keys
and door chain stay. Population/items remain70/135 initially and at refill.
No matching source exists; four other source/area pairs stay unchanged.

Crossbow2334 gains ITEM_BOW while keeping its magic flag and all other
stats/affects/reset placement; verb7 pound becomes11 pierce. This enables
normal bow commands for players, without new automatic NPC shooting AI.
Held spiked knuckles2381 use pierce11 instead of slice1, keeping the maul
primary and their held placement. No extra offhand attack is added. The
other92objects, including shared Underdark items2313/2365/2390, stay intact.
Only Its about/long thins and the Old Treant reset comment are corrected.

Maze/scent/surprise equivalents for minotaurs remain shared-mechanics
retrofit work. Existing player races keep their abilities. Portraits2329
and2331 are regenerated separately: minotaur Sumaron and crossbow Belrak.
The other32 portraits stay; image publication is independent of Git.


Dark Wood
---------

The Dark Wood step brought the catalogue to 227 archetypes, 34 body species,
48 internal traits, 117 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Its entries occupy
219-226: mist_hazard, rock_hazard, enchanted_chamber,
adult_crystal_dragon, forest_giant, tiger, decaying_demon and morepork.
Existing IDs, body rows, traits, player opt-ins and area fields stay
unchanged.

The three environmental archetypes use environmental_hazard. These are
scripted scenery: mindless, inorganic, soulless, without creature organs,
speech or corpses. Their inherited immunity covers poison, drain, sleep,
charm, hold, paralysis and psychic effects. Inorganic physiology already
rules out animal blood and pain; no extra marker is needed. There is no
new physical immunity, nonmagic immunity, invulnerability, ACT_OBJECT or
NO_FIGHT flag. Fixed dimensions stay unknown (0), with Medium as a
technical size placeholder. The local five actors use neutral sex 0. Their
original behaviour and scripts remain: mist knocks visitors from the
bridge, river rocks stay aggressive, and the chamber provides helpful
spec_cast_orb and both home-return triggers. A speech-triggered room need
not have a voice.

adult_crystal_dragon is an organic, intelligent speaking dragon with
normal organs, wings, scales, claws, fangs and tail. Its defaults are 300
cm height/5000 kg/2000 cm total length/Large/HUGE. These are descriptive
estimates for this large slender adult, not a length inferred from rank.
It has flight, infrared, detect-invisible and -20 natural armour. Cold and
magic resistance each use DD4's normal 50 percent damage reduction. This
is an explicit game approximation, not the full 2e light/normal-cold
immunity. Local 11203 keeps Boss, Sanctuary, Pass Door, treasure and
attack-on-look. Remove duplicated inherited HUGE/flight bits so XOR does
not cancel them.

spec_breath_crystal replaces the old poisonous gas with a stream of
glowing magical piercing shards against the current visible legal opponent
only. Damage rolls between max(10,current HP)/8+1 and max(10,current
HP)/4, following the existing breath scale. A resistance save halves
damage; Dragon Shield and the existing exotic protection halve it again.
Shared damage resistance, contact/phase and normal combat permissions
apply. A separate save can prevent a short glare blindness after positive
damage. It needs eyes and does not stack or refresh an existing blindness
affect. The affect uses the ordinary blindness ID, -4 hitroll and duration
0, which clears at the next affect update. A blocked hit, death, movement
or lost opponent cannot apply follow-up blindness. Head/head-trauma,
action/wait, visibility, room/area safety and resistance gates apply. It
damages no bystander or inventory, adds no poison/nausea, and does not add
another special-function turn. The special XP allowance is +10. This native
special uses existing damage and blindness machinery; it adds no learnable
spell, skill row or new player help/area spell-list entry. The archetype
profile XP adjustment is 0. Extra casting, specialised gem fear, psionics,
light immunity and age-dependent spell powers remain explicit shared
retrofits.

forest_giant is a DD4 giant-kin variant, not an asserted Voadkyn. It is
an ordinary organic speaking humanoid, family giant, with 450 cm/1500
kg/Large/ HUGE defaults, -5 natural armour and+5 profile XP. It adds no
rock special, polymorph, broad resistance or strength modifier. Existing
dwarf/giant family matching works normally. Local 11206/11207 keep their
different levels, passive wandering, Sneak, entry programs and green
leather vests. The older generic giant and official hill/stone/frost
profiles stay intact.

tiger uses quadrupedal_mammal: normal living organs, four legs, paws,
claws, fangs and tail, no arms or speech. Ordinary attacks choose claw or
bite, without extra attacks. Defaults 100 cm shoulder/220 kg/300 cm full
length/ Large, -4 armour, infrared/Sneak/Swim and 0 profile XP. Keep both
local tigers' aggression, levels, caps and entry programs. A shared
rear-claw rake or surprise system remains later work; neither is silently
simulated here.

decaying_demon is a separate organic DD4 demon, not an undead corpse or a
forced named tanar'ri subtype. It has two arms, normal organs/blood/soul/
pain/remains, horns, claws and fangs. Horn anatomy adds no automatic gore.
Defaults 240 cm/220 kg/Large, -3 armour, magical ordinary claw/bite
contact, fire/cold resistance, electricity/poison immunity and+10 profile
XP. It inherits spec_demon. Rotten skin does not grant a disease proc,
extra limbs, flight or undead physiology. Existing authored alignment
stays.

morepork uses bird anatomy, beak/talon ordinary attacks, flight, infrared,
Sneak, -5 armour and 0 profile XP. Defaults 20 cm standing height/29 cm
full length/ Small. NZDOC gives 29 cm and 175 g for this small owl; mass
stays 0(unspecified) because the current whole-kg metadata cannot express
175 g. Do not enter a fraction or pretend it weighs 1 kg. Preserve its
Sentinel and 30 percent wing drop program. The dropped wing remains the
original magic loot object.

All 15 local definitions are converted. Darkone 11210 is the approved
living human 175 cm/75 kg/Medium caster under magical imprisonment; keep
Elite, spec_cast_undead, Flaming, greeting and death-release/beacon
program. A special's name is not an undead classification. The two actual
vampires reuse vampire 175 cm/75 kg/Medium and its regeneration, defences,
weaknesses and spec_vampire; their generic undead-caster overrides are
removed. Existing Boss dragon/Elite Darkone and thirteen Common ranks
remain, with rank HP applied once. No new rank promotion or blanket
HP/damage multiplier is introduced. All other original sexes and
alignments stay.

Crystal-claw weapon 11206 moves from reset slot 2(right finger) to
16(WIELD). Its portable TAKE flag, weapon type, pierce verb, dice, affects
and loot remain. It is not converted to an attached ITEM_BODY_PART weapon;
actual dragon anatomy is supplied by the template. Other reset
caps/order/rooms/ gear stay. Population/items remain 15/4 initially and
24/6 after three refills. The original 18 objects,
key/chest/cross/prayer/crystaldoor/slippers puzzle, beacon and wing drops
remain. All 96 room flags/exits stay. Only the approved local spelling
corrections and crystal-chamber reset comment change.

No matching Dark Wood .src exists. If another area has a Scribe .src,
patch the source and its .are together, then regenerate and compare the
complete agreed output, including scripts, resets and room flags. The four
other real source exports, including Eakos, must stay unchanged.

All 15 mob portraits and 96 room images were reviewed. Only mob 11204's
fleshy chamber portrait needs replacement; regenerate it as a crystal
interior. Other 14 mob portraits and all room images stay. Image
publication remains separate from Git. No clear, major room-image mismatch
was found.


Sentinel Pass
-------------

The Sentinel Pass step brought the catalogue to 231 archetypes, 34 body
species, 48 internal traits, 119 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Four archetypes
append at IDs 227-230: giant_land_crab, sasquatch, boulder_beast and
sprite. Goat keeps its existing ID 1 but is completely rewritten as a real
animal. There are no new area fields, body species, trait IDs, ACT bits or
player race opt-ins. Existing entry numbers stay stable.

Goat now uses quadrupedal_mammal, with ordinary organs, four legs,
forelegs, horns, hooves and tail, but no arms or speech. Its ordinary
attacks choose a horn pierce or hoof pound. Defaults are 70 cm at the
shoulder, 55 kg, 110 cm long, Medium, with -3 natural armour and zero HP,
damage, critical, swiftness and profile XP adjustments. It has no
elemental vulnerabilities and no default special. The old poison, fido,
scalar, size-100 and language-3 values were development tests; do not copy
them. Language metadata is now unspecified. The separate farm_mammal
example body remains unchanged. Sentinel's male mountain goat is
individually 100 cm high, 100 kg and 150 cm long, still Medium. It
receives the existing spec_horn_gore individually: 15 percent chance, two
dice with max(4,(level+1)/2) sides using integer division, nonmagical
piercing contact and one combat pulse of wait. Its ordinary action, usable
head, horn, trauma, visibility, safety and contact rules still apply. Do
not add a leg-injury restriction to the horn attack. Its passive StayArea
behaviour remains.

giant_land_crab uses crustacean: eyes on stalks, a head region, pincers
represented by arms and claws, many legs, shell, heart and ordinary
organs; no speech. Ordinary pincers crush, with -7 natural armour and +5
profile XP. Defaults are 100 cm to the shell top, 250 kg, 250 cm long,
Large. It has no added swimming, resistance or default special. Local
18501 keeps Sneak and its deliberately authored gas breath through
spec_breath_gas_anatomical, which needs a usable head. That special has
the existing +10 XP allowance. Gas is not a default for future land crabs.

sasquatch is a large, hairy living humanoid with ordinary organs, hands,
feet, fists and speech eligibility. Defaults are 230 cm, 180 kg, Large, -3
natural armour and zero profile XP. There is no added cold immunity, fire
weakness, fear, squeeze or rock throw. This is DD4's Bigfoot reading; an
exact primary 2e Sasquatch entry was not established in the references
checked, which does not prove that none exists. Jim 18502 retains Rare,
passive wandering, his keywords and individual spec_fido.

boulder_beast uses bipedal_construct. It is a mindless stone biped,
inorganic, bloodless, soulless and mute, with no animal organs and no
animal pain, but physical stone remains. Defaults are 250 cm, 1200 kg,
Large, -15 natural armour and +10 profile XP. It resists piercing and
slashing by the usual 50 percent, takes normal blunt damage and is immune
to poison, drain, sleep, charm, hold and paralysis. Ordinary attacks pound
without becoming magical. It has no full weapon or magic immunity and no
golem Slow. It inherits spec_elemental_burst, preserving this area's
authored elemental power. Local 18503 stays passive and keeps StayArea,
Sneak and detection.

spec_elemental_burst preserves the old mixed breath spell distribution:
fire 1/8, lightning 2/8, gas 1/8, acid 1/8, steam 2/8 and frost 1/8. For
non-gas choices, it keeps the old one-in-four sample per eligible
attacker; it does not guarantee a hit on the current opponent. Gas retains
the existing area spell's target rules. Existing damage scales, saves,
resistances, nausea, inventory interactions and steam sound remain. Only
one spell can use a special opportunity. Narration describes an intrinsic
elemental discharge, so this power needs no head or mouth. It still needs
a living, awake NPC able to act, a legal fight, no wait, charm, hold,
daze, noncorporeality, safe room/area, NO_MAGIC area or active
player-order context. Antimagic uses the existing native-spell activation
rules. The special XP allowance is +10. Other breath specials are
unchanged.

The cave slime 18505 reuses green_ooze and receives spec_elemental_burst
as an individual override. This does not give every green ooze elemental
powers. It remains organic, mindless and bloodless, without discrete
animal organs. Existing inference rules prevent pain; soul eligibility and
physical goo remains stay. Its existing status defences apply. Defaults
are 30 cm approximate thickness, 80 kg, 150 cm long, Medium. Keep Sentinel
and StayArea. There is no new acid-touch, item-dissolution, infection or
blanket weapon-immunity mechanic. The retained breath spells still have
their pre-existing item effects.

sprite is a standard woodland sprite on humanoid anatomy, with normal
organs, blood, soul, pain, speech and remains, plus wings and flight.
Defaults are 61 cm, 4 kg, Small, -4 natural armour and zero profile XP. It
detects good and evil but gains no broad magic resistance. Its spec_sprite
has a +5 XP allowance. On 25 percent of eligible idle opportunities it
casts the existing Invis spell on itself, only outside combat and while
not already invisible or affected by Invis. Usable head and brain, head
trauma, ordinary action, NO_MAGIC and antimagic rules apply. It does not
refresh invisibility, add combat casting or change aggression. Ordinary
spell duration and combat stripping remain. The local female sprite stays
neutral, Sentinel, StayArea, Sneak and Hide. Sleep-arrow ointment awaits
proper projectile support; it is not silently replaced by an innate Sleep
spell or a poisoned unarmed attack.

The shadow 18500 uses the existing shadow archetype and keeps its
individual psionicist special, strength-drain physiology, held mortalsbane
stone 18500, and intentionally empty long description. Its unknown
measurements stay unspecified. Its rank becomes Elite as the unique
level-100 artifact guardian: normal rank HP applies once. Sasquatch's
existing Rare rank stays; the other six mobs remain Common. No mob gains
HUGE. The troglodyte 18504 is the approved prehistoric human
interpretation, 175 cm, 75 kg and Medium. Keep its name, troll keyword,
father joke, male sex, aggression, Sneak and fido. The standard reptilian
2e troglodyte is a different reading, not a reason to add scales or stench
here.

All eight local definitions preserve their original ACT behaviour, levels,
HP/damage build letters, alignments, sexes, reset caps, order, placements
and the held stone's slot and affects. Remove duplicated raw body flags so
XOR does not cancel inherited anatomy. All room flags and exits stay. Text
corrections are only hewn, continues and stretches. No matching Sentinel
.src exists. For future source-backed areas, update the .src and .are
together and compare the whole regenerated result, including scripts,
resets, exits and room flags. No new player spell or skill is added, so
player skill lists and spell lists need no new entry.

The shadow portrait is the only proposed replacement: an insubstantial
humanoid holding the smooth grey stone, without unsupported sword or
armour. The other seven portraits and all 73 room images stay. Image
publication remains separate from applying this code patch.


Old Marsh
---------

The Old Marsh step brought the catalogue to 238 archetypes, 34 body
species, 48 internal traits, 121 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. The seven new entries
at IDs 231-237 are swamp_thing, small_beast, will_o_wisp,
death_slaad_humanoid, shambling_mound, marsh_giant and gargoyle. Existing
rows, body species, trait IDs and player race opt-ins stay unchanged. No
area field or player skill is added. All 17 local mobs remain Common; the
Swamp Thing has no rank promotion or extra HP multiplier.

swamp_thing uses plant anatomy, with a head, eyes, ears, arms, legs, hands
and feet restored through its archetype XOR mask. It is conscious,
soul-eligible and able to leave vegetation remains, but has no animal
organs or blood and is insensible to pain. It stays mute. Defaults are 240
cm, 300 kg and Large, with ordinary pounds, -5 natural armour and +5
profile XP. It gains no elemental immunity or spell package. The local
creature keeps its aggressive behaviour and existing lair.

small_beast is a reusable furry quadrupedal mammal rather than a claim
that every small unidentified animal is a particular real species. It has
normal organs, blood, soul, pain and remains, plus paws, claws, fangs and
tail, but no arms or speech. Only its ordinary bite is selected; claw
anatomy grants no extra attack. Defaults are 30 cm at the shoulder, 6 kg,
50 cm long and Small, with -1 natural armour and zero profile XP. The
local beast keeps Sneak, Wimpy and passive wandering.

will_o_wisp uses mysterious_being as a physical starting point. It is a
conscious inorganic floating orb with eyes, no head, arms, legs, heart,
voice or corpse. It has no usable animal blood and is insensible to pain,
but retains soul eligibility. Defaults are 61 cm diameter, mass
unspecified (0), and Small. Both height and length store that diameter;
zero mass means unknown, not massless. It flies, has -18 natural armour,
poison immunity and ordinary RES_MAGIC. The existing natural_damage_type
trait makes its ordinary contact electrical, using the blast verb. It adds
neither a second bolt nor magical-natural-contact classification; physical
weapons still hurt it normally. Profile XP is +10. RES_MAGIC is the usual
DD4 50 percent damage response, not a literal implementation of the
tabletop wisp's spell whitelist. No undead or noncorporeal flag is added.
Keep its existing Sneak, Wimpy, scavenging and passive behaviour.

spec_innate_fade is a reusable short magical fade for creatures whose
power does not require a mouth or brain organ. On 25 percent of eligible
idle opportunities it applies the ordinary Invis effect to itself, with
duration 0 so it clears at the next affect update. It neither stacks nor
refreshes existing invisibility. Fighting or being attacked prevents the
fade, and normal combat stripping still applies. Awake/action/wait, charm,
hold, daze, phase, ordered-player, NO_MAGIC and antimagic rules apply; no
forced luring or travel is added. The special XP allowance is +5. Existing
spec_sprite keeps its own head/brain requirements and full ordinary Invis
duration. A new special does not mean a new player spell.

death_slaad_humanoid is the living humanoid guise of a death slaad. It
uses normal humanoid anatomy, blood, soul, pain, speech and remains by
default. Defaults are 183 cm, 90 kg and Medium, with -14 natural armour,
RES_MAGIC, nonmagic immunity and +10 profile XP. This uses coarse DD4
magic/weapon categories instead of an exact tabletop 70 percent magic
resistance roll or +2 weapon threshold. It grants no frog claws, fangs,
extra attacks, strength bonus, transformation command or additional
summoning system. The local 8313 is explicitly 13 feet tall, so it uses
396 cm, 600 kg, Large and an individual HUGE bit. Keep its individual
no-speech/no-corpse bits, spec_cast_undead repertoire including its
existing Gate, scythe in WIELD and horned helmet on HEAD. A borrowed
caster special does not classify a living creature as undead. The helmet
explains the horns in its portrait; they are not body anatomy.

shambling_mound uses conscious, soul-eligible plant anatomy. It has
grasping arm and leg masses, no separate head, eyes, animal organs or
voice, no animal blood or pain, and physical vegetation remains. Keep its
existing Hide; its profile adds Sneak and Swim. Defaults are 210 cm, 350
kg and Large, with ordinary crush attacks, -10 natural armour and +15
profile XP. It is immune to blunt damage, fire and electricity, and
resists piercing, slashing and cold by 50 percent. Electricity restores 20
percent of contact damage through the existing electricity_healing_pct
trait, capped by the normal effective/aggravated healing limit and NO_HEAL
rules. It never increases permanent HP or dimensions. The shared reaction
now describes restored vitality so it also makes sense for plants;
flesh-golem reaction mechanics remain unchanged. No blanket charm, sleep
or psychic immunity and no mindless flag are added. Cold resistance is a
DD4 approximation of the stronger tabletop save rule.

spec_vine_grip is a short, physical grasp using the existing
source-tracked grip condition. It has a 25 percent chance against the
current legal opponent, followed by a physical Hold save. It needs usable
arms, normal action/contact/visibility/safety permissions, and cannot
affect a HUGE or already held target. A successful grip uses duration 0,
does no HP damage and adds no attack. It clears at the next affect update
or through the ordinary source movement/death and Freedom handling. Other
sources' holds are preserved. Antimagic and NO_MAGIC do not block a
physical grip. The special XP allowance is +5. This is a bounded DD4
adaptation rather than a countdown to suffocation. Exact cold-save
immunity, permanent electrical growth and autonomous limb rebuilding
remain later work; ordinary Regenerate can still repair suitable plant
limbs.

marsh_giant is a DD4 giant variant on humanoid anatomy. Defaults are 450
cm, 1500 kg, Large and HUGE, with -5 natural armour, giant family matching
and +5 profile XP. Its trait profile shares forest_giant's armour entry
internally; only traits are shared by that parent link. It inherits no
rock special or swamp spell. Keep the local stationary aggression,
detection and carried spears. The older generic giant entry is unchanged.

gargoyle uses the existing bipedal_construct stone anatomy while keeping
consciousness, speech and soul eligibility. It is inorganic, without
animal organs, blood or pain, and leaves physical stone remains. It has
wings, flight, claws, fangs and horns; ordinary attacks choose a claw,
bite or horn without adding attacks. Defaults are 183 cm, 180 kg and
Medium, with -5 natural armour, nonmagic/poison immunity, magical natural
contact and +10 profile XP. It has no mindless or undead classification,
blanket spell immunity or added caster special. Remove its duplicate body
mask so the old mute bit does not cancel the intended anatomy.

The other ten definitions reuse reviewed troll, ogre, gnoll, cave_beast,
constrictor_snake, green_hag, wolf and orc entries. The 15-foot swamp
troll uses 457 cm/900 kg/Large/HUGE, the 20-foot hairy beast uses 610
cm/4000 kg/Large/HUGE, and the 10-metre anaconda uses 1000 cm/250
kg/Large/HUGE. The python keeps 600 cm/70 kg/Large without HUGE. Both
snakes lose the inappropriate poison override and inherit the established
level-scaled spec_constrictor. Keep their aggression, Hide, Sneak and
Wimpy. The plump female wolf uses 65 kg while retaining the wolf's 80 cm
height, 150 cm length and Small size. The hag retains spec_thief; the
shaman retains spec_cast_cleric. Do not stack another regeneration flag on
the trolls' existing native regeneration.

One orc shaman now resets at room 8313, By the Monolith, with global cap
1. The obsidian monolith suits his ritual markings. His existing Sentinel,
aggression, StayArea and scavenging remain, and no extra loot is invented.
This reset is appended after all original equipment chains. Every original
reset number stays, particularly the green trolls' mixed 12/2/2 caps.
Initial population becomes 33 instead of 32 and population after three
refills becomes 40 instead of 39. Carried item counts stay 9 initially and
15 after refills. There are no shops, teachers, local programs or
NO_UNDEAD wards. Remove only the unsupported questmaster role bit from
shaman 8319 and gargoyle 8321. Preserve all other authored behaviour,
builds, levels, alignments, sexes, equipment and room flags.

Text corrections are limited to the wolf's broken long-description
sentence and then -> than in room 8316. No matching Old Marsh .src exists.
For a future source-backed area, update .src and .are together and compare
the whole regenerated result, including scripts, resets and room flags.
The four other real source exports, including Eakos, stay exact. The wisp
portrait and southern lake image are regenerated separately from Git; the
other 16 portraits and 17 room images stay unchanged.


Swamp connector
---------------

Swamp (swamp_conn.are) reuses will_o_wisp and crocodile for its two
definitions. The catalogue remains 238 archetypes, 34 body species and 48
traits. Scribe now knows 122 special names; there are still 637 skill
rows. No body, archetype, trait, area field or player skill is added.

Will o' the Wisp 31400 inherits the reviewed mysterious_being/will_o_wisp
profile: a conscious inorganic orb with eyes, no animal blood or organs,
no voice or corpse, no pain, and an eligible soul. It flies, has -18
natural armour, ordinary RES_MAGIC and poison immunity, and makes ordinary
electrical contact with the blast verb. Its diameter is 61 cm (height and
length), its weight is unspecified (0), and its size is Small. Zero weight
means unknown, not massless. Clear the individual Flying, No Speech and No
Corpse duplicates so XOR does not cancel those inherited properties. Keep
its detection, infrared, passive, StayArea and Wimpy settings.

The local spec_wisp_lightning keeps this wisp's authored lightning attack.
It uses one slot at 100 percent. When idle it delegates to the existing
spec_innate_fade: 25 percent of eligible opportunities, ordinary Invis
with duration 0, no stacking or refresh, normal expiry and combat
stripping. During combat it uses the old lightning breath spell and its
HP-based damage and saves, with one-in-four sampling among eligible
attackers. Lightning arcs from the orb; it does not need a mouth, head or
brain organ. Both paths respect action, phase, ordered-player, NO_MAGIC
and antimagic rules; the attack also checks legal targets and room/area
safety. A message that moves or kills either participant or ends the fight
cancels the bolt. Its special XP allowance stays +10, alongside the
inherited profile +10. These are separate existing XP allowances, not two
full special bonuses.

This is an individual override, not a change to every will_o_wisp. The
ordinary Old Marsh wisp 8308 still uses spec_innate_fade alone. A 50/50
split between fading and lightning would halve their opportunities; the
combined special chooses by combat state instead. For Scribe, use:

    archetype will_o_wisp
    sp spec_wisp_lightning

That singleton deliberately replaces the inherited special set. The .are
equivalent is M 31400 spec_wisp_lightning in #SPECIALS. MSTAT shows the
resolved special and 100/0/0 probabilities. No new player spell is added.

Alligator 31401 uses quadrupedal_reptile/crocodile while keeping its name
and description. It is an ordinary living, blooded, soul-eligible animal,
with pain and physical remains, four legs, scales, tail, claws and fang
bite, without arms or human speech. Inherit Swimming, -5 natural armour
and +5 profile XP. Defaults are 45 cm shoulder height, 150 kg, 300 cm long
and Large, without HUGE. Its normal bite uses the authored damage build;
it adds no attack, poison, tail strike, death roll or swallow ability.
Keep Detect Invisible, Detect Hidden, Infrared and Sneak. Keep Aggressive
and Wimpy together: this existing aggression rule skips awake targets.

Both mobs remain Common. Preserve source levels 70/50, original builds,
sexes, alignments and all six M resets. There is one wisp and five
alligators initially and after refills, without carried equipment. No
individual dimension override is needed. Preserve all 50 room flags, exits
and extras, all four objects and affects, the two portal resets and
destinations, area audio and reset messages. Correct only 'on he ground'
to 'on the ground' in the pool cue's long description. No matching .src
exists. Both mob portraits and all 50 room images stay unchanged.


Wyvern's Tower
--------------

The catalogue now has 243 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal
traits, 124 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. The new body is
hexapodal_mammal at index 34. The five archetypes append at 238-242:
killer_frog, giant_flytrap, black_pudding, clay_golem and displacer_beast.
Existing indices, body profiles, trait values and player opt-ins stay. No
ACT bit, area field, player spell or individual trait override is added.

killer_frog uses quadrupedal_amphibian with fangs and front claws. Either
is one ordinary attack candidate; this does not add attacks. Defaults are
30 cm high, 25 kg, 91 cm long and Small, with -2 natural armour and zero
profile XP. The three-foot length and anatomy follow the 2e Frog entry;
height/weight are estimates. It has no swallowing or venom power. The
local stationary aggression, original build and global cap three stay.

giant_flytrap is a DD4 plant inspired by the 2e mantrap's flytrap form. It
is rooted, mindless, bloodless, insensible to pain and soul-eligible, with
vegetation remains. A tooth-edged mouth uses the head region; leaf masses
use the arm region. It has no eyes, animal heart, hands or legs. Defaults
are 300 cm height/spread, 250 kg and Large without HUGE, with -4 natural
armour, ordinary bite, +5 profile XP and spec_vine_grip. The existing 25
percent physical grip adds no HP damage, respects Hold saves and source
cleanup, and uses arm trauma/Regenerate. No fascination pollen,
inescapable prison or automatic acid digestion is implemented.

black_pudding uses amorphous_organism: an organic cell colony, mindless,
without animal blood, organs, voice, pain or a separate head/limbs. It is
soul-eligible and leaves slime remains. Defaults are 60 cm high, 450 kg,
244 cm across and Large, without HUGE. It has -4 armour, ordinary acid
contact with the suction verb, infrared as heat-sensing abstraction and
the usual mindless status immunities. Cold, acid, poison, electricity and
blunt/pierce/slash contacts are immune; fire and energy stay effective.
Physical/electrical immunity approximates the tabletop fission response
without multiplying live mobs. Profile XP is +10.

spec_acid_spray keeps the old acid-breath spell's HP-based damage, saves,
one-in-four attacker sampling and existing equipment corrosion, without
requiring a mouth or head. It uses normal action, legal-target, safety,
phase, ordered-player, NO_MAGIC and antimagic checks, and revalidates
movement/death/fighting after messages. It neither adds an area attack nor
automatic extra corrosion. Its XP allowance is +10. The local pudding
inherits it instead of the old singleton breath entry.

clay_golem uses bipedal_construct, with mindless, soulless, inorganic,
mute, bloodless and pain-insensitive physiology, no animal organs and
physical clay remains. Defaults are 244 cm/272 kg/Large, -3 armour,
magical natural contact and +15 profile XP. Nonmagic/slash/pierce
immunities express the magical-blunt weapon gate; poison, drain, sleep,
charm, hold and paralysis are immune. Ordinary RES_MAGIC is the coarse DD4
spell defence, not the tabletop's exact exceptions. Local1615 keeps the
rod in WIELD. Cursed wounds, berserk control and individual earth- spell
reactions are not part of this adaptation.

spec_golem_haste has a 20 percent eligible combat chance and one use per
MUD day. It applies the ordinary Haste condition with duration 0 so it
expires at the next affect update; it is not an exact three-round timer.
It does not stack/refresh Haste or Quicken, and skips Slow. Normal action,
legal-opponent, safety, phase, ordered-player, NO_MAGIC and antimagic
rules apply. The short effect and daily reservation are set before
messages can trigger another action. A blocked activation spends no use.
The daily fields live only in runtime character state, not area records; a
newly created creature starts with a fresh supply. XP allowance is +5.

hexapodal_mammal is a basic six-legged mammal shape with ordinary living
organs and the existing many-legs bit, without human speech or hands.
displacer_beast adds paws, claws, fangs, tail and two shoulder tentacles
mapped to the ordinary arm region. Defaults are 100 cm at the shoulder,
220 kg, 300 cm long and Large, with -6 armour and +10 profile XP. The 2e
entry supplies its six-legged anatomy and approximate size/mass; height is
an estimate. Only tentacles are selected for ordinary attacks. Claws/teeth
are anatomy, not bonus attacks or a near-death switch.

The existing PART_TENTACLES bit is now permitted in MobAttackParts and
Scribe atk. It uses whip/slash and requires usable arms; arm trauma or
missing arms removes that candidate. Leg trauma alone does not disable it.
Regenerate restores usable attached anatomy through the existing rules. No
new body-part bit, target region or native weapon object is needed. Other
creatures get no tentacle attack unless explicitly selected.

Two new named internal traits belong in the species/archetype registry:
displacement_melee_penalty uses signed hitroll points (displacer -2)
against ordinary incoming melee. It is suppressed in NO_MAGIC areas and
while the existing Displacement condition supplies the spell's defence.
Detection and Dispel do not remove an inherent trait; a future true-
seeing mechanic needs its own explicit bypass. spell_save_bonus_pct uses
signed percentage points (displacer +10) in ordinary spell saves before
the existing 5..95 clamp. It does not modify stored saving_throw or give
RES_MAGIC. MSTAT shows both values and origins. Existing player race
opt-ins remain unchanged, and neither trait is an individual Scribe field.

All 28 area definitions are converted. Rank totals are 20 Common, two
Rare, four Elite and two Boss. Chief1716 keeps his existing Boss rank and
individual spec_cast_undead as a living caster. Gargoyle1604, captain1613
and final spectre1617 become Elite; shrine shaman1605 is Rare; ancient
wyvern1616 is Boss. Unplaced1707/1708 receive Rare/Elite data without
adding resets. Rank HP multiplies once, with no profile HP bonus. All
authored aggressive/passive, Wimpy, Sentinel and other behaviour flags
stay. Clear duplicate anatomy/AFF XOR entries when selecting profiles.

Ancient wyvern1616 keeps its authored speech and breath mix through an
individual no-speech cancellation and spec_breath_any_anatomical. Its
individual dimensions are 300 cm/1200 kg/1400 cm/Large/HUGE. Add the same
native tail stinger594 used in Miden'nir, in WIELD. Its tail region,
poisoned BODY_PART weapon, trauma/disarm/Regenerate and no-TAKE rules
stay. Bracer1615 becomes carried loot instead of HOLD gear; potion1621
stays. Shaman1605's supposed scarlet belt is actually potion1621; move it
from WAIST to inventory. Shared item definitions1615/1704 stay unchanged.

Wight1609 and spectre1617 inherit their specific undead specials. Both
keep local muteness; the wight regains physical remains and the spectre
stays corpseless. The shadow inherits its approved light/strength-drain
mechanics. Gargoyles reuse stone physiology and nonmagic immunity,
including the Common guards. Minotaurs reuse approved training and gore.
Teacher1706 keeps practice, base30 and all seven lessons at85, bow and
potion. Strong/burly/rugged individual weights are descriptive overrides;
elder/female dimensions are not changed merely for age or sex.

Preserve all61room flags/exits/extras, every original mobile reset cap,
order and placement, keys, doors, maze, chest and three unplaced
definitions 1707/1708/1720. Original population stays46 initially/50 after
refills; items become24/25 only because of the one native stinger. Correct
the approved wording and blank ranger-lord description. No matching .src
exists; all four other real source exports remain unchanged. Portraits
stay. Room1607 gets a separately generated descending-ceiling picture;
image publication is separate from Git.


Apocalypse Now
--------------

The catalogue has 245 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal
traits, 124 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. apocalyptic_avatar
and apocalyptic_steed append at indices 243 and 244. Existing indices and
all old profiles, body defaults, traits and player opt-ins stay. There is
no new field, flag, player ability or special-function implementation.

apocalyptic_avatar is a conscious, corporeal humanoid manifestation, not
automatically an undead creature or a member of the human family. Defaults
are 190 cm, 75 kg and Medium, with ordinary humanoid anatomy. Its family
is none. All stat modifiers and profile XP are zero. No resistance,
immunity, special or ghost phase is supplied. Use the individual record
for an avatar's physiology, dimensions and powers.

The four Horsemen are DD4-specific personifications. Their area-file
descriptions guide this adaptation; these are not claimed AD&D avatar
statistics. Famine 5600 is 195 cm/35 kg/Medium. He keeps No Heart, No
Speech, Inorganic and physical remains. Cancel inherited PART_HEART as
well as physical brains/guts so positive anatomy does not defeat No Heart.
He still thinks, has senses and limbs, and remains soul-eligible. No
Speech does not block his existing Starve/Parch fight programs; it only
suppresses spoken incantations. Keep both 10-percent entries and their
order. The normal trigger walker stops at the first successful non-greet
program, rather than firing both automatically. His original mixed breath
uses spec_breath_any_anatomical.

War 5601 is 185 cm/90 kg/Medium and female, with fleshy humanoid anatomy.
She keeps spec_cast_priestess, her sword and body armour. Pestilence 5603
is 190 cm/75 kg/Medium, male and organic; the surrounding insect cloud
does not turn his body into a swarm. He keeps spec_cast_psionicist and his
gloves. Death 5602 is a 200 cm conscious skeletal manifestation, mute and
corpseless as authored. He is inorganic, Bloodless and Analgic, without a
physical heart, brain or guts, but with senses, head, hands and limbs. His
mass is unknown: use explicit weight 0, overriding the avatar's 75 kg
default. Zero means unspecified, not physical weightlessness. He keeps
spec_cast_archmage and his scythe/robe. Death personified remains
non-undead; no skeleton immunities, mindlessness, soullessness or special
holy-healing rules are imported into this avatar.

apocalyptic_steed uses quadrupedal_mammal with an ordinary horse head,
organs, four legs, tail and hooves. It has no arms or speech. Its ordinary
hoof contact adds no attack opportunities. Defaults are 230 cm shoulder
height, 1400 kg, 330 cm long,Large plus BODY_HUGE, RES_FIRE and +5 profile
XP. All other stat modifiers stay zero. These descriptive estimates depict
the enormous coloured horses whose feet and breath are fiery. This is not
an asserted standard nightmare profile. No wings, flight, ethereal phase,
corpse suppression or undead classification is supplied.

Each steed keeps its own single 100-percent special slot: white Death 5608
uses spec_breath_any_anatomical; red War 5609 fire; brown Famine 5610
acid; green Pestilence 5611 gas, all through their existing anatomical
breath wrappers. The payloads, sampling and XP allowances are unchanged,
with the shared usable-head/head-trauma rules. The archetype itself adds
no breath, so there is no accidental second special or probability split.

Starving man 5604 reuses human at 175 cm/45 kg/Medium. Wounded soldier
5606 reuses human at 180 cm/75 kg/Medium and keeps his sword. Starvation,
wounds and violent despair do not imply undead status, mindlessness or
permanent trauma. Rat 5605 reuses giant_rat at 50 cm shoulder height, 25
kg, 100 cm long, Small. It inherits approved living rat anatomy, swimming
and ordinary fang contact, while its authored poison uses
spec_poison_bite. That existing wrapper requires usable head/fangs but
keeps the poison payload and opportunity sampling. Do not silently replace
poison with deity Plague: shared infectious disease remains a separate
deferred design.

Ghost 5607 reuses ghost at 175 cm/unknown mass/Medium. It now inherits
real undead classification, conscious semi-material phase and the
established mundane-weapon barrier. Keep authored Pass Door and No Speech.
Do not confuse Pass Door or ghost phase with the player Non-Corporeal
affect. Clear the duplicate individual No Corpse bit so XOR does not
restore physical remains. Rat and steed raw No Arms/No Speech bits
likewise clear to inherit their existing body defaults correctly.

All four Horsemen keep their explicit Elite ranks and all eight other
definitions stay Common. Rank HP applies once: Elite is 5x, without an
extra avatar HP bonus. High levels or unique names alone do not promote
the steeds. Preserve Scavenger, Aggressive and StayArea on all twelve,
every alignment/sex/level/build, authored detection/infrared, Horsemen
invisibility/sanctuary/sneak/hide and steed invisibility/sneak/hide.

The starving-man reset in room 5610 now has global cap 10, matching the
other four placements. Cap 1 caused that last row to be skipped on a clean
boot. Initial population becomes 28 instead of 27; eventual refill
population stays 48, and the cap does not reserve any particular room.
Carried items remain 11 initially and 16 after refills. Preserve every
other reset/cap/order, all eleven objects/affects, all 55 room flags,
exits/doors/keys and the absence of NO_UNDEAD rooms. Only the approved
Famine hang/hangs and final Horsemen wording corrections accompany it. No
matching .src exists; the four other real source exports stay. All twelve
portraits and 55 room images were inspected and retained.


Purgatory
---------

Purgatory reuses apocalyptic_avatar for the Reaper of the Dead (401) and
ghost for the lost soul (402). No new profile, body, trait, skill, special
or parser feature is added. The catalogue stays at 245 archetypes, 35 body
species, 50 internal traits, 124 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows.
All shared defaults and player opt-ins stay unchanged.

The Reaper is a conscious, physically present manifestation of Death, not
an ordinary reanimated skeleton. He remains non-undead, soul-bearing and
corpseless. His skeleton has a head, senses, hands, arms and legs, but no
physical heart, brain or guts. Individual Inorganic, Bloodless and Analgic
properties describe that body without making him mindless, soulless or
ethereal. Keep the positive organ bits cancelled as well as No Heart: an
inherited PART_HEART would otherwise defeat the negative flag. His
dimensions are an estimated 200 cm height, unknown mass, Medium and no
HUGE. Explicit weight 0 overrides the avatar's 75 kg default; it means
unspecified, not physically weightless.

Clear his individual No Speech to allow ordinary speech, and set male sex
to match the description's he. His existing shout already takes a separate
channel path; this is not a claim that the fight program was broken. His
portrait shows the skeletal Reaper, scythe and book, but does not
establish sex. The description supplies that decision.

Keep Sentinel, No Quest and the individual Unkillable flag, including its
existing bit 63 value. These are service and encounter settings, not a new
racial ability. Both Reaper and lost soul stay Common. The Reaper is not a
ranked combat boss, and Unkillable makes an extra rank HP multiplier
unnecessary. Levels, alignments, generated builds and existing detection,
Sanctuary and Protection affects stay.

The lost soul inherits conscious undead classification, organless
inorganic/corpseless anatomy, semi-material ghost phase and the existing
mundane-weapon barrier. It retains a soul and a mind, and keeps its
individual No Speech, Stay Area, No Quest and unspecified sex. Clear the
duplicate individual No Corpse bit so XOR does not restore remains. Its
human-sized outline is 175 cm, with unknown mass, Medium and no HUGE. No
drain, fear, wail, flight, Pass Door, Analgic or Soulless property is
added. Ghost phase and player Non-Corporeal are separate.

The Reaper keeps spec_cast_adept in the single 100-percent special slot.
Its existing checks skip helpful spells that would harm undead targets. It
still chooses players rather than the wandering NPC souls. Preserve his
shop, profit settings 100/0 and opening hours 0..23. The greeting still
casts Giant Strength, creates the tear potion, gives it to the visitor and
forces that visitor to quaff it. The fight program keeps its original
5-percent shout. No new fee or priest-service role is added.

His gear stays scythe 403 in WIELD, Book of Judgement 402 in HOLD and tear
potion 401 in inventory. All four objects, stats, affects, flags, potion
spells and book charges stay. Initial population is one Reaper and two
lost souls, with three souls after refills; total population is therefore
3 initially and 4 after refills. Carried items stay at 3.

All 27 room records, flags, exits, maze randomization, hints, area safety,
recall and ambience stay. The area has no NO_UNDEAD rooms. Preserve entry
room 401, player-corpse/judgement room 427 and portal object 404 leading
to room 3054. These identifiers are used by player extraction, corpse
placement, executioner pursuit, quest-room exclusion and the portal's
save-after-entry behavior. A mob, room and object sharing a number are
separate records; classification must not renumber them.

The only wording correction is the Reaper's doesn't seems to doesn't seem.
There is no matching .src. All four other real source exports remain
unchanged. Both portraits and all 27 room images were reviewed and
retained; no image generation or publication accompanies this step.


The Magic Garden
----------------

The catalogue now has 249 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal
traits, 124 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. water_drake,
water_spirit, animated_vine and pixie append at indices 245 through 248.
Earlier indices, shared profiles, body defaults and player opt-ins stay.
These additions use existing traits and specials. No new area field, trait
command, body flag or special-function implementation is needed.

water_drake uses quadrupedal_reptile: a living head, senses, heart,
brains, guts, four legs, scales and tail, without humanoid arms or speech.
It adds claws, fangs, swimming and HUGE. Defaults are 250 cm shoulder
height, 2500 kg, 1000 cm long and Large. The ordinary claw/bite fallback
adds no attack opportunities. All stat modifiers and profile XP are zero;
it supplies no armour, resistance, breath or active special. This is DD4's
aquatic dragon relative, not a claim to reproduce a standard AD&D
water-drake stat block. Its measurements are estimates. Drake 11300 keeps
its existing magical flight, without new wings, its Elite rank,
aggression, Sentinel, Stay Area, greeting attack, ambient sounds, both
talons, dragon's prayer, blue scales and loot.

The talons, object 11300, are WEAPON/BODY_PART with value[0] = 4 for the
leg targeting region. The old value 1 incorrectly meant head. value[3]
stays 5, the existing claw verb. Keep both WIELD/DUAL resets, TAKE and
other flags, damage generation and all three bonuses. This does not add a
leg-trauma attack gate: the existing approved targeting and eligibility
rules still apply. WEAPON and LIGHT use value[0] for the region; ARMOUR
uses value[1]. Staff values remain spell/charge data, so do not
reinterpret the marked native prayer or orb as a weapon.

water_spirit uses elemental_being for a conscious, soul-bearing shape made
of river water. It has a pseudo-head, eyes, ears, arms and hands, but no
legs, animal heart, brains or guts. It is inorganic, Bloodless, Analgic,
mute and corpseless, without undead classification, Mindless, Soulless or
a ghost phase. Defaults are 90 cm, unknown mass (weight 0), Small, no HUGE
and swimming. Zero means unspecified, not weightless. It resists ordinary
nonmagical contact and water and is immune to poison. Its existing
natural_attack_type trait is 7 (pound), and natural_damage_type is
NATURAL_DAMAGE_WATER. This changes ordinary unarmed contact, not the held
orb or an extra damage packet. Profile XP is +5 percentage points; all
other stat modifiers stay zero and there is no active special. Sprites
11301-11305 keep authored Flying, Pass Door, sneak, senses and their
watery orbs. The area describes river-water beings, rather than the
biological sea sprites in AD&D.

Carnivorous plants 11306-11310 reuse giant_flytrap. Their seven-foot
description gives individual height 213 cm; weight 90 kg and spread 150 cm
are estimates replacing the shared 300 cm/250 kg/300 cm defaults. They
stay Large without HUGE. The inherited mouth uses head targeting, and
grasping leaf/tendril masses use arm targeting. They have fangs, ordinary
bite, Mindless, organic vegetation remains, Bloodless and Analgic, without
animal eyes, legs, heart or speech. They stay soul-eligible. Natural
armour is -4; profile XP is +5 and the existing vine-grip special
allowance is +5. Preserve aggression, Stay Area, magical flight and all
original detection affects. Do not turn them into rooted Sentinel plants
merely because they lack walking legs.

animated_vine uses plant, with tendrils mapped to PART_ARMS and
PART_TENTACLES. Its archetype XOR cancels the body's No Arms flag, then
adds usable arm anatomy. Defaults are 30 cm rise, 40 kg, 600 cm extended
length, Large and no HUGE; these are descriptive estimates. It is
Mindless, organic, Bloodless and Analgic, but soul-eligible and leaves
vegetation remains. It has no head, eyes, legs, animal heart or speech.
PART_TENTACLES supplies ordinary tentacle-whip attacks. spec_vine_grip
uses its existing 25-percent eligible combat sampling, nonmagic/Hold save,
safety, usable-arm/trauma, HUGE-target and source lifecycle checks. It
adds no separate damage packet. Profile XP and stat modifiers are zero;
the existing special allowance is +5. Vines 11312-11314 keep Sentinel,
Stay Area and magical flight.

Gardener 11311 reuses gnome at 105 cm/40 kg/Small. Keep Wimpy and flight.
It inherits Infrared, RES_MAGIC, +1 trained melee against goblins and
kobolds, and the established -4 opponent penalty against the gnome
giant-defence target families, including gnoll, bugbear, ogre, ogre mage,
troll, giant and titan. The shared profile XP adjustment is +5.

Fairies 11315-11318 and queen 11321 reuse sprite: 61 cm/4 kg/Small,
ordinary living humanoid organs, wings, Flying, Detect Good/Evil and
natural armour -4. The four fairies are female, matching both their
descriptions and portraits. Their wing items still grant flight. Their
individual spec_cast_archmage slots replace the whole inherited special
set at 100 percent. The queen likewise keeps spec_cast_adept, including
its existing checks against harmful help for undead visitors. Do not add
spec_sprite beside those roles or dilute their casting. The queen stays
Common in SAFE, HEALING, NO_RECALL and NO_UNDEAD room 11405. Royal status
alone does not require a combat-rank promotion.

Oaks 11319/11320 reuse enchanted_oak at 1500 cm/10000 kg/Large/HUGE.
Branches supply usable arms and ordinary blunt pound contact. They are
organic, Bloodless and Analgic, without animal head, eyes, legs, heart or
speech, and have fire vulnerability. They remain soul-eligible and leave
wood remains. Keep spec_warrior, magical flight, their rustling and
forced-rest programs, gnarled branches and bark equipment. Objects
11304/11342 also reset in Eerie Woods; their definitions are unchanged.

pixie uses humanoid with wings and Flying, 76 cm/8 kg/Small, no HUGE,
RES_MAGIC and natural armour -5. Profile XP is +5, with the existing
innate-fade special allowance +5; all other stat modifiers are zero.
spec_innate_fade restores invisibility on 25-percent eligible idle checks,
never as persistent combat invisibility. Pixies 11322-11324 keep their
initial invisibility, senses, sexes and greeting pranks. The height and
defence choices adapt the second-edition pixie entry; 8 kg is an estimate.
RES_MAGIC uses DD4's ordinary resistance mechanic, not literal 25-percent
spell negation. No new daily spell list, sleep arrows or special player
ability is supplied.

Leprechaun 11325 reuses leprechaun at 61 cm/12 kg/Small, with Detect
Hidden, RES_MAGIC and natural armour -2. Keep spec_thief, Wimpy, all other
affects and the flying boots. Correct days and knights to days and nights.
Object 11324 now says A pair of buckled shoes has been left here. These
are the only prose changes.

Drake 11300 remains Elite; all other 25 definitions stay Common. Rank HP
applies once. Preserve levels, alignments, generated builds, all authored
movement/aggression/passivity, flight and other affects, every embedded
program, all reset placements/caps/order and equipment slots. There are 26
inhabitants and 73 carried objects at boot and after refills. All 106
rooms, exits and flags stay, including ward 11405. All 45 object
definitions stay except the talon region and shoe wording. No shop,
teacher or matching .src exists. Four other real source exports remain
unchanged. All 26 portraits and 106 room images were reviewed and
retained; no image generation accompanies this patch.


Chapel Catacombs
----------------

The catalogue now has 255 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal

BODY_ANALGIC. Wind is insensible to physical pain. Inorganic already
prevents usable blood, so no redundant Bloodless flag is needed. This also
applies to Harpy Haven's ghostly air elemental 9103; its other defaults
stay.

mi_go uses fungoid for a conscious, organic, Bloodless fungus. It keeps No
Eyes, No Heart and No Speech, without becoming Mindless, Soulless,
Inorganic or Analgic. Its antenna-bearing body has no head. Cancel the
body's positive PART_HEAD, PART_2_LEGS and PART_FEET before adding

claws. Ordinary claw contact and Flying match the description. Defaults

-4 and profile XP is +5 percentage points; other stat modifiers stay zero.

nightgaunt uses humanoid at 250 cm/140 kg/Large/HUGE. Its head, brain,

fear, resistance or active special. Nightgaunt 3407 keeps aggression, Stay

hunting_horror uses limbless_invertebrate for the area's winged, ropy,

with no extra stat/XP modifier, displacement mechanic or active special.
Horror 3408 keeps its existing aggression and senses. Orb 3425 moves from
HOLD to carried inventory: E 1 3425 3 17 becomes G 1 3425 3. It remains
loot with the same reset value, rather than an item held in a nonexistent
hand. Its gold and all object definitions stay.

infernal_servitor is a living humanoid fiend with ordinary organs, senses,
hands, legs, speech, blood, pain, soul and physical remains. Defaults are
198 cm/100 kg/Medium; the six-foot-six height describes Snapper Joe. It
supplies RES_FIRE, RES_COLD, poison immunity and the existing spec_demon.
These are conservative DD4 fiend defences, not a claim to a particular

key, Sentinel, level and alignment.

rock_slug uses limbless_invertebrate: living head, eyes/eye stalks, mouth
and internal organs, without limbs or speech. It keeps ordinary animal
blood/pain/soul rules and physical remains. Defaults are 20 cm high, 40
kg, 120 cm long and Small, all estimates. The existing PART_FANGS attack

djinni uses humanoid at 320 cm/200 kg/Large, without HUGE. The
second-edition ten-and-a-half-foot height, AC4, flight and invisibility
guide this adaptation. Mass is estimated. It has Flying without wings,
natural armour -6 and existing spec_innate_fade: 25-percent eligible idle
invisibility recovery, not persistent combat invisibility or the full
genie spell list. Profile XP is +5 and the special allowance +5; other

3420 keeps its individual No Corpse and legless whirlwind lower body.
Cancel inherited PART_2_LEGS and PART_FEET as well as setting No Legs.
Keep its evil alignment, speech, printed name and portrait path.

Reuse skeleton for 3400, zombie for 3401/3416, ghoul for 3402, wraith for

The Catacombs
-------------

The catalogue has 259 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal
traits, 124 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. The four appended
profiles are 255 blood_reaper, 256 ratfolk, 257 neo_otyugh and 258
ethereal_servitor. Earlier indices stay. No new body species, registry
trait, flag, special, area field or individual trait override is
introduced. The mummy profile is refined for its first actual converted
area consumer.

blood_reaper uses humanoid: a living, conscious human-derived race whose
blood diet does not make it undead. It keeps normal organs, blood, pain,
soul, speech and remains. Anatomical claws/fangs supply ordinary claw/bite
contact; existing spec_poison_bite preserves the area's venom with mouth
and head checks. Defaults are 175 cm, 75 kg and Medium. There is no added
resistance, vulnerability, regeneration, gaze, drain or invented feeding
power. Stat/profile XP modifiers are zero; the existing poison special
contributes +5 percentage points. Blood Reaper 13300 keeps its behaviour
and senses. Its long line now says Blood Reaper rather than Flesh Reaper.

ratfolk uses humanoid for naturally ratlike people, rather than presumed
were-rats. It has arms/hands, two legs, tail and anatomical claws/fangs,
with only ordinary fang bite selected. Defaults are 152 cm, 45 kg and
Medium: the area supplies five-foot height, while thin-body weight is an
estimate. No lycanthropy, disease power, silver exception, weapon immunity
