active special is supplied. Rat-man 13311 keeps its individual senses,
sneak/hide, aggression and Wimpy. Its sex becomes male to match 'He' in
the description; the portrait is compatible.

neo_otyugh uses tripodal_organism and the same reviewed anatomy as otyugh:
three legs, central toothed mouth, tentacles and eye stalks, ordinary
living organs/blood/pain/soul/remains, conscious but without speech. It
has Infrared, a PART_FANGS bite selector and existing spec_otyugh.
Defaults are 160 cm high, 650 kg, 244 cm long and Large, without HUGE. The

contributes +5. There is no added body-shield/damage-redirection power.
Shared infectious disease remains deferred. Neo-Otyugh 13323 keeps
authored nonaggression, even though its long description mentions
attacking. Add neo-otyugh as a keyword while keeping Neo and Otyugh.

ethereal_servitor uses humanoid for a conscious, soul-bearing, non-undead
magical apparition. It has a humanoid head/eyes/arms/hands/legs outline,
but no biological heart, brain or guts. Cancel the positive PART_HEART,
PART_BRAINS and PART_GUTS inherited bits as well as setting BODY_NO_HEART.
It is Inorganic, Analgic, mute and corpseless; Inorganic already rules out
usable blood, so a duplicate Bloodless flag is unnecessary. Invisible and
Flying are defaults. Height is 175 cm, mass is unknown (0), and size is
Medium. Zero mass means unspecified, not physical weightlessness. There is
no ghost phase, Pass Door, mundane-weapon barrier, immunity or active
special. Stat/XP modifiers are zero. Ethereal Servant 13320 keeps its
individual 100-percent cleric override, senses/sneak/hide/aggression and
deliberately empty long/full descriptions.

mummy remains a conscious, soul-bearing corporeal humanoid with normal
organs and remains. It now defaults to 175 cm/45 kg/Medium, Bloodless and
Analgic, with natural armour -7. Its resistances are Dark plus Blunt,
Pierce and Slash; the physical resistances halve qualifying damage.
Immunities are Poison, Sleep, Charm, Drain, Cold, Hold and Paralysis. Holy
and Fire vulnerabilities remain. This is a scaled DD4 adaptation of the
second-edition mummy's weapon protection, not blanket nonmagical immunity.
Ordinary weapons still affect it through physical resistance; ordinary
fire still exploits its fire vulnerability. No Mindless, Soulless,
Inorganic, No Heart, No Corpse or extra fear-aura flag is added. Existing
spec_mummy supplies scheduled fear and positive weaponless contact mummy
rot, with its existing sampling, resistance and lifecycle checks. Do not
duplicate that fear in another special slot. Profile XP is zero; the
special contributes +15. Guardian 13306 has individual 190 cm/60 kg
measurements for its unusually large human frame and inherits spec_mummy
instead of generic poison.

Reuse human for 13301-13305, 13314, 13315, 13317-13319, 13321, 13322 and

The Great Pyramid
-----------------

The catalogue has 268 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal
traits, 125 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Nine profiles append

skills or individual trait commands. All HP/damage/critical/haste
modifiers remain zero. Ranks and individual dimensions still belong to
encounter design.

asp uses serpentine_reptile, with ordinary living head/eyes/mouth/fangs,
scales/tail/organs/blood/pain/soul/remains and no arms, legs or speech.

animated_carpet uses limbless_construct: no head, eyes, heart, arms or

sandman uses elemental_being at240cm/250kg/Large, with length unspecified.

head/eyes/ears/arms/hands/fingers/two legs/feet do not add biological

caryatid_column uses bipedal_construct for a head/eyes/ears/arms/hands/
fingers/two legs/feet stone humanoid without biological organs. It is

-5; mass is estimated. RES_NONMAGIC halves ordinary weapon damage;
RES_MAGIC is the chosen DD4 adaptation of its save protection. Immunities
are Poison, Sleep, Drain, Charm, Hold and Paralysis. Profile XP is+5;
other stat modifiers zero. No stone-golem Slow, weapon shattering or
stone-transform instant kill is added.

working eyes. Individual240cm/1200kg/Large dimensions describe the
composite; it is not HUGE. Many-part flags describe anatomy, not four
extra attacks, extra wear slots or four weapon drops. Each of the two
original mobs still wields its one ordinary stone scimitar. Keep all reset
caps/order and the second guardian's key exactly, including the author's
intentional reload dependency when only one guardian has been killed.

spec_terrifying_roar is reusable by any suitable mobile, without vnum
checks. When selected it attempts20percent of eligible opportunities. Only
the current opponent is affected. It needs an awake, active NPC, a usable
head without Head Trauma, an unchanged living same-room opponent, normal
combat permissions and a room without Cone of Silence. Source silence is
respected. A human language, eyes or a visible target is not required.
Mindless and ACT_OBJECT victims are excluded. Normal supernatural Fear
saving uses RES_SONIC|RES_PSYCHIC, including current fear protections and
werewolf defences. A successful save has no effect; failure attempts
normal fleeing, which still obeys movement and escape restrictions.

The roar deals no direct HP damage, adds no persistent condition and does
not change deaf/chat settings. It has no three-stage daily counter or
permanent deafness. Messages and saves can run other actions, so recheck
source/victim death identity, room, current opponent, action/head/silence
and permissions before continuing. No bystander is targeted. It adds+5
percentage points through the existing weighted special XP calculation.
Androsphinx30percent selection gives6percent eligible roar attempts; Great
Sphinx20percent gives4percent. These are attempts before saves and normal
flee restrictions, not guaranteed successful escapes.

reuses the reviewed175cm/45kg/Medium mummy, native fear/rot and complete
physiology/defences. Keep Infrared/Wimpy/nonaggression. Ramses2617 uses

Ancalador
---------

The catalogue has 273 archetypes, 35 basic body species, 50 internal
traits, 126 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Five profiles append
at 268-272: undead_riding_beetle, green_slime, crystal_spider, umber_hulk
and ochre_jelly. All 268 earlier resolved profiles and all 35 bodies stay
unchanged. These creatures use existing internal traits, not new ACT
flags, area fields or individual trait commands. Their
HP/damage/critical/haste modifiers are zero. Existing Rare/Elite/Boss HP
multipliers apply once.

undead_riding_beetle is an insect with six legs, head, eyes, shell and
biting mandibles represented by fangs. Cancel inherited heart/brain/guts
through XOR; add No Heart, Bloodless, Analgic and Soulless. No Arms/No
Speech come from insect. It is Undead, Mountable and Mindless, with
physical remains and no flight or casting. Estimated defaults are 120 cm
high/350 kg/300 cm long/Large. Natural armour is-7, Dark is resisted, Holy
is vulnerable, and Cold/Poison/Drain/Sleep/Hold/ Paralysis are immune.
Profile XP is 0. Preserve local Sanctuary/Sentinel and cap 2: one
initially and a second on refill.

green_slime is an organic amorphous_organism: organless, without head,
eyes, limbs or speech, Mindless/Bloodless/Analgic, with physical residue.
Defaults are 20 cm high/40 kg/100 cm long/Small. Existing
natural_attack_type 7 and natural_damage_type acid change ordinary contact
without another hit. Acid/Poison/Sleep/Charm/Hold/Paralysis are immune;
Fire/Cold work normally. No instant transformation, Cure Disease kill
hook, item destruction or full weapon immunity is added. Profile XP is+5.
Existing green_ooze is unchanged.

crystal_spider is a conscious living-crystal arachnid: eight legs, head,
eyes, fanged mandibles and shell, but no arms/heart/brain/guts. It is
Inorganic, Bloodless, Analgic and unable to speak; it is not automatically
Mindless or Soulless. It leaves crystal remains. Green defaults are 90
cm/350 kg/200 cm/Large; yellow individual replacements are 65 cm/180
kg/150 cm/Medium. Natural armour-5, Piercing/Slash resistance, Poison
immunity and Sonic vulnerability apply. Both inherit spec_poison_bite.
Profile XP is+5, plus ordinary weighted special XP. Keep their original
behaviours: green Wimpy/passive, yellow Aggressive. Their glow stays
descriptive. Move glove 16816 and arsenal buckler 16812 to carried loot,
retaining all seven knife resets inside the buckler. The current reset
engine loads only one copy of a vnum into a container, so actual contents
stay at one knife. Worn item bonuses no longer apply to the spider.

umber_hulk is an intelligent, speaking humanoid-shaped biped with ordinary
organs, two arms/two legs, scales, claws, fanged mandibles and Infrared.
Four eyes use existing Eye anatomy, not a new body bit or area field.
Defaults are 244 cm/750 kg/Large/unknown length; height follows 2e, mass
is estimated. Natural armour is-8. It inherits spec_confusing_gaze rather
than gas breath. Profile XP is 0; the special adds+5 through ordinary
weighted special XP. No additional attacks, burrowing exits or cave-ins
are added.

spec_confusing_gaze attempts its power 20 percent of eligible selected
opportunities, against the current fighting opponent only. Source must be
awake/able to act/corporeal, with head/eyes and no Blind/Eye Trauma/Head
Trauma. The viewer must be awake, have usable eyes, not be blind or
suffering Eye Trauma, and be able to see the source. An invisible target
remains eligible if it can see the source. A source hidden from the viewer
cannot affect it. Silence and Cone of Silence do not stop the gaze.
Ordinary safety, ordered- player, mindlessness/object exclusions,
resistance and antimagic still apply.

The gaze uses the existing Confusion condition and resistance-aware save
once. Failure gives hitroll-((level/5)+1),
Intelligence/Wisdom-((level/15)+1), for URANGE(1,level/30,3)ticks. All
three affect records share the duration. Existing Confusion is never
stacked/refreshed. No HP damage or bystander effect occurs. Recheck death
identity, room, opponent, action/anatomy/sight/permissions after messages
and saves, since they can trigger another action. Antimagic is checked
once through native spell activation. This callback adds no player spell;
the ordinary Confusion spell is unchanged.

ochre_jelly is an organic amorphous_organism with organless
Mindless/Bloodless/ Analgic anatomy, no speech and physical residue.
Defaults 40 cm/150 kg/180 cm/ Medium. Contact becomes acidic through
existing traits. Electricity/Acid/ Poison/Sleep/Charm/Hold/Paralysis are
immune. No splitting/population mechanic is added. Existing
spec_acid_spray retains the old acid-breath payload and sampling without
inventing a mouth. Keep Sanctuary/Aggressive and all undigested loot.
Profile XP is+5 plus ordinary weighted special XP.

Putrid zombie 16800 adds its described claws and MobAttackParts claw
selection. Asp 16801 reuses asp and its existing venom; clear old No Heart
and duplicate limb/speech/tail XOR. Morticia 16802 is a living human

Keep Bloodless/Analgic/Soulless, undead protections and remains.
Intelligence does not automatically restore a soul. Lazarus 175 cm/65

The Dragon Tower
----------------

The catalogue now has 279 archetypes, 35 body species, 50 internal traits,
126 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Six profiles append at IDs
273-278: draconian, undead_draconian, adult_black_dragon,
ancient_green_dragon, phase_dragon and tiamat_avatar. All 273 earlier
profiles and all body species stay unchanged. There are no new area
fields, trait commands, registry traits, special callbacks or player
skills.

draconian is the Tower's living humanoid dragonfolk. It has ordinary
organs, arms, hands and legs, plus scales, wings, tail, horns, claws and
fangs. It speaks, bleeds, feels pain, has a mind and soul, and leaves a
corpse. Natural armour is -4; claws and fangs supply ordinary natural
attacks. Defaults are 190 cm high, 90 kg and Medium; length is
unspecified. Wings alone do not grant flight. There is no inherited
breath, casting, broad resistance or Baaz stone death. Use baaz_draconian
only for that distinct creature, not as a shortcut for every dragonfolk
inhabitant.

undead_draconian uses the same humanoid anatomy at 190 cm/80 kg/Medium.
Its scales, wings, tail, horns, claws, fangs and physical remains persist.
It is Undead, Mindless, No Speech, Bloodless, Analgic and Soulless.
Ordinary zombie organs remain anatomically present. It resists Dark, is
vulnerable to Holy and is immune to Cold, Poison, Drain, Sleep, Hold and
Paralysis. Natural armour is -4. There is no inherited flight, breath or
casting. The local zombie 2240 loses generic undead casting and becomes
male to match its description. Its aggression, senses and reset caps
remain.

adult_black_dragon is 350 cm high/5000 kg/2500 cm long/Large with HUGE. It
has natural armour -16, Magic resistance, Acid immunity and anatomical
acid breath. ancient_green_dragon is 600 cm/18000 kg/4000 cm/Large with
HUGE, natural armour -25, magical natural attacks, Magic resistance,
Poison immunity and anatomical gas breath. Both speak and have ordinary
living dragon anatomy, horns, fear aura, flight, swimming and established
dragon senses. The older green is a new profile; adult_green_dragon stays
unchanged. Colour defences and dragon anatomy follow the 2e references;
these whole-number heights and masses are DD4 estimates.

phase_dragon is a small living dragon: 25 cm high/8 kg/100 cm long/Small,
natural armour -4, flight and dragon senses, with anatomical gas breath.
Its existing ACT_BLINK flag grants ordinary defensive Blink. The NPC base
chance is min(30, max(1, level/2)), adjusted by twice the level difference
and clamped to 1..66. A successful roll needs 5 mana and spends it. Blink
does not grant permanent noncorporeality, Pass Door or movement
exceptions. The local phase dragon remains mute, Wimpy, Aggressive,
Scavenger and Stay Area. This flag represents an existing active ability,
not another racial-bonus marker.

tiamat_avatar is a living, corporeal, killable five-headed dragon. Its
Many Heads anatomy cancels the inherited single Head through XOR. It has
ordinary eyes, horns, wings, claws, fangs, tail, organs, blood, pain, soul
and remains. Defaults are 1200 cm high/80000 kg/6500 cm long/Large with
HUGE. Natural armour is -40, natural attacks are magical, Magic is
resisted, and Fire/Cold/Electricity/Acid/Poison are immune. Fear aura,
flight and dragon senses apply. The existing mixed anatomical breath
selects one of its eight payloads per invocation. It does not make five
attacks at once or confer a full deity spell list. Profile XP is +10. All
six profiles have zero HP/damage/critical/haste modifiers; the other five
have XP +0. Existing special XP and individual rank HP multipliers still
apply.

The hatchling 2200 reuses dragon_hatchling with individual replacements 40
cm/12 kg/120 cm/Small, preserving its mute mixed-breath role. A red
portrait does not grant Fire immunity. The King 2205 uses individual 250
cm/220 kg/Large without HUGE; other dragonfolk use the adult defaults.
Human master 2202 keeps authored flight and melee combat, without an
invented lich or mage identity. Human mage 2203, Tiamat priest 2204, royal
mixed breath and slave/fool thievery retain their individual roles. Red
2221 keeps fire-only anatomical breath. Hydra 2226 retains its authored
venom through an individual spec_poison_bite override; shared hydra stays
nonvenomous. No behaviour or moral alignment follows from a job title.

Ranks are individual: Boss 2220/2221/2222/2223/2225, Elite 2202/2205/2243,
Rare 2203/2204, and Common for the other nine definitions. Black 2222 was
already Boss and stays so. Rare/Elite/Boss multiply HP by 2/5/7 once.
Legacy HP dice letters are not rank markers and remain unchanged. All
authored aggression/passivity, Sentinel, Scavenger, Stay Area, Wimpy,
Sanctuary, Invisibility, senses and magical flight are retained through
resolved XOR.

Move priest 2204 from library 2214 to sanctum 2215, and Tiamat 2220 from
mountaintop 2243 into her described final lair 2231 behind the platinum
door. Both stone-door exits between nursery 2205 and hall 2223 use the
existing stone key 2237 instead of missing 2297. Give that key to King
2205 and set its destination value to 2223. Preserve both door flags.
Hydra staff 2244, Tiamat orb 2298, red staff 2245, black leggings 2280 and
white scale mail 2246 become carried loot. Red helm 2275 and green brooch
2261 stay equipped. The Old Marsh use of spear 2200 stays unchanged. All
other reset counts, caps, order, items and room flags remain. The new key
adds one carried object: 49 initially and 58 after three refill resets;

Room and mob names stay unchanged. Minor prose fixes repair missing words,

Media filenames must follow the actual dd-gui lookup, not the image

humanoid_gargantua is a living humanoid in the giant combat family. It has

Poison/Plague quarters healing, Slow halves it, NO_HEAL stops it and the
aggravated healing ceiling applies. Ordinary positive NPC tick healing is
replaced, so it cannot stack. There is no stored catch-up healing or
automatic trauma repair. The sourcebook's regeneration informs this
bounded DD4 timing; no extra trample hit or forced rank is added.

rakshasa is a living flesh-encased spirit, with humanoid arms/hands/legs,
ordinary organs and animal-featured claw/fang anatomy. It keeps blood,
pain, mind, soul and physical remains. Defaults are 183 cm/125 kg/Medium,
length unspecified. Natural armour is -14, natural attacks are magical,

than importing tabletop spell-circle immunity or blessed-bolt instant
death. Its default caster is spec_cast_mage; local 13610 keeps an explicit
100 percent spec_demon override, Flying and its adult female human
disguise. No new disguise subsystem or automatic elemental immunity is
added. Profile XP is +10. Keep displayed Rakasasha and its old keyword;
add the correct rakshasa keyword and repair the species spelling in its
description. Small key 13634 moves from the finger slot to inventory,
retaining the loot.

black_phasebeast is a conscious mysterious_being: no head, eyes, limbs,
organs, speech or corpse; Inorganic/Bloodless/Analgic, with mind and soul
retained. It is not automatically Undead, Soulless or noncorporeal. All
three measurements remain unspecified; size is Medium. It has Poison
immunity, hovering flight and the existing ACT_BLINK defensive ability,
whose successful use costs 5 mana. Preserve local invisibility, hiding,
sneaking, senses, Sentinel and aggression. No Pass Door or room-ward
exception is added. Profile XP is +5, plus ordinary special XP.

spec_gas_cloud is a reusable mouthless release of the old gas-breath spell
payload. It is selected once per eligible fighting opportunity, without
the acid spray's one-in-four target sampling. Keep the original room-wide

The full area, resolved prototype and fresh/live mob values must agree.

Giant red roc 13616 reuses roc with individual 600 cm/3000 kg/1800 cm/
Large/HUGE, retaining authored fire breath through the anatomical wrapper.
Red plumage does not add Fire immunity. Shared roc defaults stay
unchanged. All authored Aggressive/passive, Sentinel, Stay Area, Wimpy,
Scavenger, Sanctuary, Invisibility, senses and flight exceptions stay.
Anatomical wrappers keep ordinary head/trauma restrictions and special
frequency.

Ranks: Kiqir 13609 remains Elite with 5x HP once; Rakasasha 13610 becomes
Rare with 2x HP once as the inner-bedroom deceiver and key guardian. Other
16 definitions stay Common. No rank or racial HP modifier follows from
size, level, sourcebook rarity or legacy HP dice notation.

Keep all original reset counts/order/caps/placements: 19 inhabitants and
22 carried items initially and after three refill resets. Eleven later
guard/servant/maid rows remain suppressed at their existing caps. Only the
small key's equipment slot changes. All 36 object definitions/stats
remain. Make the study return door 13637 east use key 13604, matching
13635 west; make laboratory return door 13639 south use 13602, matching
13636 north. Preserve door flags/reset states and approach-side key
requirements, the crypt's keyless return, magical one-way routes and all
52 room flags.

Repair obvious spelling and broken words without changing displayed mob
names. Remove only the accidental trailing space from room 13631's title,
making the existing image 13631_the_corridor.png match the GUI. Keep the
period in The Staircase. (13633); its exact GUI filename is
13633_the_staircase..png. Supply an unchanged copy under that name. All 70
current portraits/room images were reviewed; no regeneration is needed.
Media publication is separate from the source patch. There is no matching
.src; all four other real source exports stay unchanged, including Eakos.


Hel's Tower
-----------

The current catalogue has 296 archetypes (MAX_MOB), 37 body species
(MAX_SPECIES), 50 internal traits, 130 Scribe special names and 637 skill
rows, including the existing reserved rows where applicable. Two bodies
append: cephalopod 35 and living_appendage 36. Ten profiles append at
286-295 in this order: pteranodon, psi_beast, fire_giant, shark,
water_elemental, earth_elemental, humanoid_avatar, giant_squid_tentacle,
giant_squid and soul_gem. All 286 earlier profiles, 35 earlier bodies and
player opt-ins stay unchanged. There are no new area fields, trait names,
body-part categories, player skills or individual trait commands.

All ten new profiles have zero HP, damage, critical, haste and profile XP
modifiers. Ordinary special XP still applies: spec_tentacle_grip +5,
spec_squid +5 and spec_fire_surge +10. These allowances are not new rank
or HP multipliers.

Dimensions below are descriptive centimetres and whole kilograms. They do
not replace combat size or imply HUGE. Unspecified zero is not a claim
that a creature has no mass. Second-edition entries inform the griffin,
pteranodon, Fire Giant, hag, elementals and giant squid; most masses,
natural armour values and the combat adaptations are agreed DD4 design
estimates. Psi-beast, avatar, Gem and hazard interpretations follow the
actual area prose and roles. No measurement follows from rank alone.

Griffon 13500 reuses griffin/quadrupedal_hybrid: 152 cm height, 275 cm
length, 600 kg, Large without HUGE. Keep inherited wings, feathers,
claws/beak attacks, flight and Detect Hidden, plus individual spec_fido.
Its existing natural armour remains -20; it is not replaced with -7.

pteranodon 13501 uses winged_reptile: living reptilian organs, wings,
flight, beak anatomy and ordinary beak contact. Its archetype XOR cancels
inherited scales for the described leathery covering, and adds no fangs or
venom. Defaults are 120 cm height, 250 cm body length, 30 kg, Large,
natural armour -3. The reference's roughly nine-metre wingspan is not its
body length; do not put that wingspan in MobLength or Scribe length.

psi_beast 13502 is the described psychic monkey, using humanoid anatomy
with clawed hands, fangs, tail and No Speech. Ordinary claw/bite contact,
natural armour -3 and spec_cast_psionicist belong to its profile. Defaults
are 100 cm height, 150 cm length, 70 kg, Medium. Keep individual Hide and
Sneak and all authored behaviour. Psionic casting gives no automatic
psychic immunity and adds no extra ordinary attack stream.

Flamewing 13503 reuses fire_bat/elemental_being at 30 cm height, 61 cm
length, Small, with mass unspecified. Keep the current fiery-bat anatomy,
fang attack, fire immunity, flight, Infrared and anatomical fire ability.
Its authored faerie-fire glow stays. It is not an ordinary mammalian bat.

fire_giant 13504 is a living humanoid in the giant combat family, with
HUGE, Large, natural armour -5 and Fire resistance. Defaults are 549 cm
height and 3400 kg, length unspecified. The second-edition 18-foot and
7500-pound measurements inform those values; Fire resistance is a DD4
adaptation of its source defences. The profile selects spec_giant. This
individual explicitly uses spec_fido/spec_giant at 50/50 to retain corpse
feeding. Its separate rock combat pulse still attempts at 15 percent,
using 2d10 plus the existing level term, four daily throws and 50 percent
catching. Special selection does not halve that separate rock pulse.
Preserve the massive club, scavenging, aggression and Sentinel role.

shark uses fish anatomy, Swim, fins, tail, fangs, an ordinary piercing
bite and natural armour -4. Shared defaults are 300 cm length, 200 kg,
Large, height unspecified, without HUGE. Massive local shark 13505
overrides length to 600 cm and weight to 1500 kg and adds HUGE. Preserve
spec_fido, stealth and Detect Invisible/Detect Hidden. Remove only its
five obsolete teeth 13519 equipment resets: that invisible no-take item
was worn at the neck, not wielded. Keep the unused object definition and
all unrelated loot. The native fangs now supply the actual bite.

Death Hag 13506 reuses living green_hag at 165 cm height, 55 kg, Medium,
length unspecified, with existing claw anatomy/contact, Magic resistance
and natural armour -12. The individual adds anatomical fangs and bite. Her
title and spec_cast_undead do not make her Undead. Keep her mind, soul,
intentional hostile spell list and ring 13509. Green hag is the agreed
closest reusable interpretation, not a subtype named by the prose.

Fire Elemental 13507 reuses the rebuilt fire_elemental physiology, fire
contact and defences at 300 cm height, Large, other measurements unknown.
Keep local No Speech/Hide/Sneak and its individual
spec_breath_fire_anatomical. That wrapper retains the authored attack with
ordinary head/trauma eligibility; older fire elementals gain no new
breath.

water_elemental 13508 is thinking water using elemental_being. It has
material head, eyes and arms, no ordinary legs or biological heart,
brain or guts, and no corpse. It is inorganic, bloodless and ANALGIC, with
mind and soul retained, Swim, natural armour -8 and magical blunt natural
contact. Defaults are 300 cm height, 500 kg, Large, length unknown. It is
immune to Nonmagic, Poison, Sleep, Drain, Hold and Paralysis, with no
invented Cold immunity or electrical vulnerability. Keep its local
muteness, Invisibility, Sneak and authored frost attack through
spec_breath_frost_anatomical. The estimated mass describes a substantial
mobile volume of water, not a new density simulation.

Air Elemental 13509 keeps the existing conscious, organless cloud body,
flight, Pass Door, Nonmagic resistance and natural armour -8. Only this
individual receives descriptive height 300 cm, Large, with mass and length
unknown. Preserve Invisibility/Sneak and its authored poison-gas ability
through spec_gas_cloud; the cloud does not need imaginary jaws. Do not
upgrade older air elementals to Nonmagic immunity or add whirlwind kills.

earth_elemental 13510 is a thinking rocky humanoid using elemental_being,
with material head, eyes, arms, hands and legs, no biological heart,
brain or guts, no corpse, and inorganic/bloodless/ANALGIC physiology. Mind
and soul remain. It has natural armour -8, magical blunt contact and the
same Nonmagic/Poison/Sleep/Drain/Hold/Paralysis immunities as Water.
Defaults are 300 cm height, 1500 kg, Large, length unknown. Keep local
muteness, Hide/Sneak and authored magical flight. Ordinary shared earth
elementals do not acquire flight from this individual exception.

Hel 13511 uses humanoid_avatar: an ordinary living, minded, soul-bearing
humanoid manifestation, not automatically Undead or invulnerable. Defaults
are 180 cm height, 70 kg, Medium, length unspecified. Her actual sex is
female 2, agreeing with room 13581 and the retained female portrait. Keep
her existing Rare rank, psionic casting, Sanctuary, flight, senses and
peaceful Sentinel behaviour, plus all five carried items. Her silver key
13517 still opens Mrinthas 13073 down to 13100; tower entry 13074 north to
13500 and recall 13073 stay intact.

living_appendage is a minimal living arm/tentacle body: no head, eyes,
legs, heart, speech or independent corpse. It remains organic, has blood,
feels pain and keeps mind and soul. Do not add ACT_OBJECT, Mindless or
Soulless. giant_squid_tentacle 13512 uses this body, with ordinary
tentacle contact and spec_tentacle_grip. Defaults are 600 cm length, 100
kg, Large and HUGE, height unspecified. HUGE represents attachment to an
enormous owner. Existing arm targeting/trauma and Regenerate apply; no new
stinger, target category or injury is introduced. The independently reset
entities stay independent: no automatic owner linkage, summons or
despawning. Keep authored aggression, Sentinel, Wimpy and senses; sex
becomes neutral 0.

cephalopod is a reusable living body with head, eyes, normal organs and
multiple arms/tentacles, without ordinary legs or speech. giant_squid adds
fins/beak, Swim, natural armour -7 and ordinary beak/tentacle attacks. Its
defaults are 1830 cm length, 2000 kg, Large and HUGE, height unknown. The
second-edition 60-foot example informs length; mass is a DD4 estimate.
Leviathan 13513 uses this profile and spec_squid, retaining Hide, Detect
Invisible, scavenging, Sentinel and aggression. Multiple arms do not add
weapon slots or extra attack streams. Its rank stays Common.

Ordinary beaks use piercing contact, while tentacles use the existing
slashing whip contact. Head loss removes beak eligibility; unusable arms
or arm trauma remove tentacle eligibility. Regenerate uses the existing
injury repair rules. These are choices within the ordinary attack, not
additional strikes or an override of a valid wielded weapon.

spec_tentacle_grip reuses the existing source-tracked tentacle grip
condition and vine-grip rules with tentacle messages. It has a 25 percent
attempt against an eligible close opponent and the ordinary NONMAGIC|HOLD
save. Usable arms are required; arm trauma, HUGE targets, existing holds,
unsafe rooms/areas and ordered contexts prevent it. The hold does not
stack, refresh or add crushing damage. It expires on the next ordinary
character update; existing cures, separation, source movement/death and
logout cleanup release it without removing another source's hold.
spec_squid uses that same grip during combat and spec_fido corpse feeding
while idle. Both new specials add ordinary special XP +5 each; neither
creates a learnable player skill or a new condition.

The shared grasping_limb_grip helper rechecks source/target identity,
room, opponent, available limbs and permissions after saves and messages.
spec_vine_grip keeps its existing vine wording and rules. Corpse feeding
also rechecks actor/corpse state after messages. These guards preserve the
existing outcome when triggers move, kill or alter an actor or corpse;
they are not extra attacks, damage or new corpse-feeding rewards.

The Fire 13514 reuses fire_hazard/environmental_hazard, an impersonal
Mindless/Soulless/organless danger with fire immunity, Cold/Water
vulnerability and ordinary fire contact. Height, length and mass remain
unknown; size stays Medium. Preserve level 100, Invisibility, aggression
and Sentinel. Its individual spec_fire_surge preserves the old
single-target Fire Breath payload and attack-selection sampling while
describing a surge of fire instead of jaws. It needs no mouth/head and
adds ordinary special XP +10. This does not add ACT_OBJECT,
invulnerability or NO_FIGHT, and does not change other fire_hazard users.

Fire surge retains the old one-in-four sampling of each eligible attacker,
not a guaranteed hit on the current opponent. Normal native action,
ordered-player, room/area safety and antimagic restrictions still apply.
It rechecks actors, room, opponent and permissions after messages before
calling the same Fire Breath spell. It adds no room-wide fire attack.

soul_gem 13515 uses limbless_construct for a conscious floating, speaking
gem: no anatomical organs, head, eyes, limbs or corpse; inorganic,
bloodless and ANALGIC. The profile cancels inherited No Speech and keeps
mind and soul. Never add ACT_OBJECT: has_soul rejects it, contradicting
the trapped souls. Neither Mindless nor Soulless is added. Estimated
defaults are 20 cm height, 20 cm length, 10 kg, Small; sex is neutral 0.
Keep 100 percent spec_cast_orb, its healing/support and existing vampire
safeguards, Sanctuary, senses, protection and NO_QUEST. Keep _Mad_jammer
and add soul/gem/orb keywords. Its original Orb's Blast 13511 is a magical
projection, not an anatomical weapon; do not invent arms to justify it.
Move its E reset immediately after the first successful Gem M and remove
only the final redundant Gem M. Preserve the Gem's cap/location. Its
restored defence if attacked does not replace orb healing.

Only Hel remains Rare (2x HP once); the other fifteen definitions are
Common, including Leviathan, the Fire Giant and the level-100 hazard.
Names, levels, HP dice letters, size and sourcebook rarity do not promote
rank. Racial HP/damage/critical/haste modifiers are not rank multipliers.
Keep every authored alignment, level, aggression/passivity, Sentinel, Stay
Area, Wimpy, scavenging and other deliberate individual outcomes.

Recompute individual flag masks against resolved species/profile defaults:
template = species XOR archetype; individual mask = template XOR desired
final flags. Remove obsolete duplicates rather than repeating inherited
flight, Detect Hidden, No Arms or No Speech and cancelling them. Keep the
approved new anatomy/physiology while preserving local exceptions.
Dimensions replace independently; ordinary special slots and their full
selection vector use their own override rules, never XOR. In particular,
Fire Giant explicitly keeps fido/giant 50/50, while the Gem keeps orb 100.
Traits remain code-only species/profile data, with no area syntax added.

Keep all 84 room flags/doors/exits, 20 object definitions and the
quest-key chain. Preserve original reset counts/caps/placements apart from
the five obsolete shark equipment rows and redundant final Gem M described
above. Do not fill every apparent shark, tentacle or elemental placement:
the existing caps deliberately remain. The old world has 19 initial mobs
and 26 after three refills, carrying eight objects; the repaired Gem
projection adds one carried item without adding a mob. Psi-beasts grow
from four to eight and flamewings from one to four. There is no matching
.src; the four other real source/area exports, including Eakos, stay
unchanged.

Correct flys to flies, the flamewing sentence, an Water Elemental to a
Water Elemental, The Hel to Hel, the missing shark period and ring 13509's
broken ground text to A shadowy ring rests here. Keep displayed room
names. Exact dd-gui lookup preserves punctuation: supply unchanged copies
custom_rooms/13554_falling_upwards!.png and
custom_rooms/13555_splash!!.png from the old underscore filenames,
retaining those aliases. The Water grammar correction also needs
mobs/13508_a_water_elemental.png copied from
mobs/13508_an_water_elemental.png. Do not remove punctuation with a
general slug rule. Approved separate portrait work makes Leviathan a
long-mantled squid with fins/feeding tentacles and The Fire an impersonal
fire hazard; keep Hel's female portrait and the other thirteen. Media
delivery/publication is separate from applying the source patch; these

Elemental Canyon
----------------

This revision has 312 archetypes, 37 body species, 50 internal traits, 131
Scribe special names and 637 skill rows, including reserved rows. Sixteen
profiles append at IDs 296-311; all earlier IDs stay unchanged. There are
no new body species, area fields, player skills or trait names. Traits
remain internal species/archetype data. They cannot be set on an
individual mob through a new area or Scribe command.

The new profiles are cyclops, mixed_elemental, lightning_elemental,
element_bound_humanoid, water_mote, fire_mote, ice_mote, stone_mote,
lightning_mote, acid_elemental, lava_elemental, mud_elemental,
mist_elemental, energy_mote, dust_elemental and air_eddy. An archetype
describes the creature; its encounter rank and job remain individual.

cyclops is a living humanoid in the giant family. It has normal organs,
blood, pain, mind and soul, with Large size, HUGE and natural armour -8.
Its 610 cm height follows the second-edition true cyclops, not the much
smaller cyclopskin. Its 4000 kg mass is a DD4 estimate. Existing eye
anatomy handles its single eye without a new body flag or target category.
Canyon's cyclops 9202 keeps cleric casting and Sanctuary. It does not gain
Detect Invisible or an additional boulder special: the vendor's existing
invisibility elixir must still provide a route past it.

mixed_elemental uses elemental_being for conscious magical matter. It has
material head, eyes, arms, hands and legs, without biological heart, brain
or guts. It is inorganic, bloodless and ANALGIC, leaves no ordinary
corpse, and retains mind and soul. Do not add Mindless, Soulless or
ACT_OBJECT merely because a creature is made of an element. Defaults are
175 cm height, Medium, with mass and length unspecified. It resists
Nonmagic rather than being immune, so ordinary weapons still work at the
existing resistance reduction. Poison, Sleep and Drain do not work on this
physiology. Its one ordinary natural contact is magical Energy; there are
no extra strikes, racial damage/HP bonuses or natural armour bonus. It
does not automatically fly or pass through doors.

Canyon's tiny, small, medium, large and HUGE forms override the shared
height/size as needed: 30 cm/Small, 100 cm/Small, 175 cm/Medium, 300
cm/Large and 600 cm/Large with HUGE. The Rare guardian stays at the 175
cm/Medium default. Rank does not enlarge its body. Definition 9237 is
loaded but has no reset; conversion does not invent a live placement.

The Earth, Fire, Air and Water Rulers reuse the established profiles. Keep
their existing distinctions: Air has Nonmagic resistance, while Earth,
Fire and Water have their established immunity. Their authored
cleric/fire/gas/acid roles remain individual; Water still releases acid,
not an invented frost attack. Sanctuary and all deliberate behaviour stay.
No ruler receives an extra room attack from descriptive prose.

lightning_elemental is conscious electrical matter using elemental_being.
It has material head, eyes and arms, no ordinary legs or biological
organs, no blood or corpse, and inorganic/ANALGIC physiology with mind and
soul retained. It flies and has natural armour -8 and magical electrical
natural contact. It is immune to Electricity, Nonmagic, Poison, Sleep,
Drain, Hold and Paralysis. Defaults are 300 cm height, Large; length and
mass remain unspecified. Described dazzling light and noise do not
automatically add blindness or deafness attacks.

The twelve smaller material profiles also use elemental_being, keep mind
and soul, and have inorganic/bloodless/ANALGIC physiology without
biological organs or ordinary corpses. They have Nonmagic resistance and
Poison/Sleep/Drain immunity, without the full-sized armour bonus. Do not
invent human limbs for a puddle, snowflake, spark or dust cloud. The lava
beast and mud monster can form material heads and limbs. Material anatomy
is separate from biological organs. The existing target skill still
refuses inorganic NPCs, even when they have material heads or arms. Do not
remove that flesh-and-blood restriction as part of classification; the
living bound humanoids remain ordinary targets.

Natural contact follows the material: water_mote uses Water; fire_mote and
lava_elemental use Fire; ice_mote uses Cold; lightning_mote uses
Electricity; acid_elemental uses Acid; energy_mote uses Energy. Stone,
mud, mist, dust and air eddies use Blunt. These are the contact types of
one ordinary magical natural attack, not extra attacks or overrides of a
valid wielded weapon. Fire/lava are Fire-immune and vulnerable to Cold and
Water; ice is Cold-immune and vulnerable to Fire; electricity and acid are
immune to their own type. No extra chemical cross-immunities, equipment
destruction, engulfing or damage aura is inferred. Water and mud can Swim.
Snowflake, spark, mist, particle, dust and eddy can fly; ordinary flame,
pebble and lava remain grounded.

Dimensions are descriptive centimetres and whole kilograms. Unknown zero
means unspecified, not physically massless. The tiny particle's
measurements stay unspecified rather than adding fractional units or
pretending it is one centimetre across. Its "too small to see" prose does
not add actual Invisibility. Other minor-form measurements and most masses
are agreed DD4 estimates, not sourcebook measurements.

element_bound_humanoid is a living host, rather than a full elemental. Use
ordinary humanoid organs, blood, pain, mind, soul and corpse; defaults are
175 cm height, 75 kg, Medium, length unspecified. Blue Flame, Magneto, Ice
Bandit, Rock Monster and Hurricane use this profile. Their exact ancestry
is not identified as human. Existing MobResists overrides supply Fire,
Electricity, Cold, Pierce+Slash and Nonmagic resistance respectively;
Hurricane also gains local magical flight. Their old powers, weapons,
local muteness and authored behaviour stay. Blue Flame and Magneto lose
their old No Corpse flags. Ice Bandit's 100 kg and Rock Monster's 225 kg
estimates include the enclosing shell. Do not turn binding prose into
permanent Hold, a rescue quest, an automatic elemental touch, undeath or a
new creature released on death.

The climber, magician, Rainbow Cult warrior, illusionist, alchemist and
vendor reuse human. Occupation or magical training does not make a person
elemental. The baby rainbow dragon reuses dragon_hatchling, with 91 cm
height, 250 cm length, 70 kg and Medium size, normal dragon anatomy/flight
and its individual anatomical mixed breath. It gains no adult prism flash
or HUGE. The little imp reuses imp at 61 cm/12 kg, Small, including
established wings/flight/regeneration/defences. Its individual spec_thief
makes its described stealing real; keep its scavenging, Hide, Sneak and
Wimpy. Shared imp behaviour is unchanged.

spec_lightning_discharge is a combat-only release of the existing
Lightning Breath payload for forms that need no imaginary mouth. It
retains the old one-in-four sampling of each eligible attacker; it is not
a guaranteed strike on the current opponent. It adds no idle Invisibility,
unlike the existing wisp special. Ordinary native-action, ordered-player,
safe-room/area and antimagic restrictions apply, with state checks after
messages before the spell runs. Ordinary special XP applies; this is not a
learnable player spell or extra attack stream.

Existing mouthless fire, gas and acid releases keep the corresponding old
spell effects. The mixed forms use spec_elemental_burst with the old
distribution: Fire 1/8, Lightning 2/8, Gas 1/8, Acid 1/8, Steam 2/8 and
Frost 1/8. Post-message guards preserve safety if a trigger changes the
actors or room, without adding a second cast or changing the older
Sentinel Pass cave slime. Dust and eddy keep the normal thief chances and
coin accounting, with discovery wording that does not invent hands.

Five rulers are Elite (5x HP once); cyclops and guardian are Rare (2x HP
once); the other thirty definitions stay Common. HP dice letters, level
130 on the vendor and the word HUGE are not rank markers. Preserve all
original levels, alignment, aggression/passivity, Sentinel, Stay Area,
Wimpy, scavenging and other deliberate behaviour. Recompute area flag
masks against species XOR archetype, so inherited traits are not
accidentally cancelled by repeated bits. Individual dimensions are
absolute replacements; specials and their selection vector are not XOR.

Seven actual sex fields become male 1: cyclops 9202, rulers 9208-9212 and
illusionist 9233. Their pronouns and existing portraits agree. Vendor 9238
loses only undefined ACT value 16. Preserve his valid LOSE_FAME flag,
level 130, shop 105/15, all-day hours, mage defence, lantern and original
stock. Do not add No Quest, No Experience or a healer role. Existing
invisibility elixir 9231 remains unchanged.

Correct the alchemist's wand/staff resets from WIELD to HOLD. This really
enables NPC zap/brandish and charge consumption in combat; the existing
equipment XP modifier drops by five per corrected instance. Move the
second baby dragon's ice staff to carried inventory, preserving the drop
without inventing arms or enabling staff use. Its ordinary claw/fang
selection becomes available and its equipment XP modifier drops by ten.
Preserve spells, initial charges, object counts and caps; charges may then
be spent through normal combat. Keep existing valid held magic items on
minor forms: current zap/brandish has no arm or speech gate. Moving the
invalid held scroll to inventory also removes its five-point equipment XP
modifier. No compensating combat bonus is added for these corrections.

Move invalid worn dust, water jugs and the scroll to inventory; move the
lava lamp to LIGHT. Earth ring 9201 changes from Furniture to Treasure
while retaining its wearable flags and Intelligence/Wisdom bonuses; do not
introduce armour randomisation or change bracelet 9212, also equipped in
Underdark. Remove only the unsupported Sword of Illusions stat-card line;
do not invent missing quest treasure.

Keep displayed "an eddie" as the author's Fast Eddie wordplay; add eddy as
a keyword and fix ordinary "eddie current" prose. Minor grammar
corrections preserve identities and displayed room names. Keep all 55 room
flags/sectors/exits, magical water/air walkways, original mob
placements/caps and unrelated loot. There is no matching Canyon .src; the
four existing real source exports, including Eakos, stay unchanged.

Current dd-gui preserves punctuation. Room 9204's final period means its
exact filename is
custom_rooms/9204_a_blind_curve_on_the_mountain_path..png. Supply
unchanged pixels under that name and keep the old alias. Approved separate
portrait replacements depict Magneto as one humanoid inside an electrical
field and the imp with its established wings. Generation and live
publication are separate from applying this source patch.


Cyclops Caves
-------------

The catalogue now contains 316 archetypes, 37 body species, 51 internal
traits, 131 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows, including reserved
rows where applicable. Four profiles append at indices 312-315:
chimera_goat_head, chimera_lion_head, chimera_dragon_head and
modron_secundus. All earlier profiles and bodies remain unchanged. There
is no new body, special function, spell, skill or area field. One internal
trait appends at ID 50; all previous trait IDs 0-49 remain unchanged.
Scribe gains only the four archetype names, not a trait command.

Twenty-five local cyclopes reuse cyclops. The shared true-cyclops defaults
remain 610 cm height, 4000 kg, Large, HUGE and natural armour -8, with
living humanoid organs and giant-family matching. The earlier Canyon
cyclops keeps those defaults. This area describes smaller cyclopes, so the
ordinary adults use individual 305 cm height and 320 kg, still Large. The
local override cancels inherited HUGE on every cyclops except Xoric. Large
size and HUGE are separate properties; rank does not supply either.

The two nine-foot guards, 14005 and 14006, use 274 cm and 227 kg. The
father, 14013, uses 260 cm and 300 kg; child 14015 uses 150 cm, 70 kg and
Medium size. The broad construction supervisor, 14020, uses 335 cm and 700
kg; the muscular smith, 14032, uses 305 cm and 450 kg. The smaller thief,
14038, uses 229 cm and 150 kg. Xoric alone keeps HUGE, with 457 cm height
and 1100 kg. Length stays unspecified on these humanoids. The explicit
feet/pounds in the area inform the guard and king conversions; other
masses and stature refinements are DD4 estimates. All dimensions remain
descriptive whole-number centimetres/kilograms.

The three chimera heads reuse existing head_only. That body supplies a
head, eyes and ears, with No Arms, No Legs and No Heart. It does not make
a creature inorganic, mindless, bloodless, pain-insensitive or soulless.
Those artificial properties belong to preserved_head and
animated_toy_head, which are not used here. The living heads retain blood,
pain, mind, soul and speech eligibility. No Heart means no heart in this
local head body; the blood helper does not require a heart to recognise
organic blood. Add normal brain anatomy without inventing chest organs or
humanoid limbs.

chimera_goat_head has horn anatomy and an ordinary horn attack, natural
armour -4, 50 cm height, 75 cm length, 25 kg and Small size.
chimera_lion_head has fangs and an ordinary bite, armour -5, 60 cm height,
75 cm length, 35 kg and Small size. chimera_dragon_head has scales/fangs
and an ordinary bite, armour -8, 75 cm height, 100 cm length, 50 kg and
Medium size. None is HUGE. These measurements are DD4 estimates for heads,
not the sourcebook's five-foot shoulder height for a whole chimera. Armour
adapts the source's regional ACs through the established 10-AC rule.

The second-edition chimera is one living, low-intelligence hybrid with
limited speech. Its goat horns and lion/dragon bites inform these heads;
low intelligence does not mean AFF_MINDLESS. Keep the area's three
independent reset encounters, one of each in room 14034, with their
original caps, levels, sexes, alignments and aggression/passivity. They
gain no extra attack stream, owner link, fourth body mob, shared HP or
linked death. Each head has No Corpse, so it drops its existing treasure
without leaving an invented whole-creature corpse. Mind and soul remain
intact.

Keep goat horn 14026 at HEAD, lion mane 14025 at NECK_1 and dragon tooth
14027 at WIELD. The tooth remains its original ordinary piercing weapon,
not a newly flagged ITEM_BODY_PART. Valid wielded weapons take precedence
over natural contact, so the equipped tooth still supplies the dragon
head's ordinary hit. Do not invent arms for it, move its reset, or claim
that head trauma disables this ordinary weapon. The anatomical breath
wrapper also has its existing head-trauma gate. Regenerate follows the
existing injury rules; it does not recreate an owner body or duplicate the
tooth.

The new internal trait head_trauma_blocks_attacks defaults to 0 and is
enabled with value 1 only on these three new head profiles. With head
trauma, their head-dependent natural attack parts are excluded from the
usable attack mask. This supplies the agreed injury gate without changing
ordinary bites, horns, tusks or beaks on the earlier 312 profiles. It does
not remove stored anatomy, affect other natural parts or suppress a valid
ordinary wielded weapon. Healing head trauma restores native eligibility
through the existing rules. MSTAT identifies the trait through the generic
registry and its normal value/origin displays. It remains code-only
species/archetype data: no per-mob area field or Scribe command is added.

The dragon head keeps its authored mixed breath through individual 100
percent spec_breath_any_anatomical. This retains Fire 1/8, Lightning 2/8,
Gas 1/8, Acid 1/8, Steam 2/8 and Frost 1/8. Gas keeps its existing
room-wide payload; a single selected special is not a promise that every
payload has one target. The wrapper needs a usable head and rejects head
trauma. It adds no daily limit, petrification, prismatic flash or extra
cast. The sourcebook's fire-only breath does not overwrite this deliberate
DD4 encounter. Goat and lion gain no new active specials or roar proc.

modron_secundus uses humanoid, not a mindless construct profile. Defaults
are estimated 300 cm height, 150 kg and Large size, with length
unspecified and no HUGE. Ordinary head/eyes/ears, brain,
arms/hands/fingers, legs/feet, heart and guts remain, with blood, pain,
mind, soul and speech. The original second-edition Planescape text does
not specify every organ or establish inorganic or soulless matter. These
are conservative DD4 physiology choices. No Corpse represents the source's
disintegration on death; belongings still drop normally. Corpselessness
does not require removing mind or soul.

Secundus has natural armour -16, adapting source AC -6 with the usual
10-AC mapping. Standard Magic, Cold, Fire and Acid resistance is used,
with Sleep, Charm, Hold, Drain and Psychic immunity and the existing
fear_immunity trait set to 1. These are agreed DD4 translations. Standard
Magic resistance is not copied 90 percent immunity. The elemental flags
give a stronger reduction than the source's small per-die adjustment.
Psychic immunity maps the hierarchs' protection from psionic attacks; it
does not make this highly intelligent creature Mindless or grant it
psionic powers. Secundus uses existing defence traits; no individual trait
syntax is added for him.

Local Secundus 14033 keeps level 42, alignment -1000, neutral sex,
aggression, Sentinel, Stay Area, Sanctuary, Detect Invisible/Detect Hidden
and a single 100 percent mage special. Its viceroy identity and the note
to Primus support the Modron classification; canonical planar alignment
does not replace the area's evil envoy role. No extra priest slot, stun
lock, teleport, flight, Pass Door, summoning, invulnerability or No Fight
is added. All four new profiles have zero HP, damage, critical, haste and
profile XP modifiers. Ordinary special XP and individual ranks still
apply.

The torture victim, human prisoner, psionic prisoner and fanatic priest
reuse human. The victim uses 175 cm and 55 kg; the other three use 175 cm
and 75 kg. The dwarven prisoner uses dwarf with individual 122 cm height,
70 kg and Small size; the elf mage prisoner uses elf at 150 cm and 45 kg,
Medium. Their living ancestry and existing training/defences are distinct
from their prison roles. The minotaur envoy reuses minotaur at 230 cm and
250 kg, Large, without HUGE. No additional caster role is inferred for the
elf prisoner merely from the word mage in his description.

Leia reuses humanoid_avatar at 180 cm and 70 kg, Medium. Keep her female
sex, good alignment, Rare rank, flight/senses/Sanctuary/protection, quest
and practice flags, individual spec_cast_adept and the existing lessons:
teacher base 50 and Astral Vortex 100. Her warded chamber 14135 stays
NO_UNDEAD. A divine title does not add invulnerability or a paid healer
role. Incidental adept aid does not set ACT_IS_HEALER; the existing
helpful-spell safeguards for undead and vampires remain in force.

The smith keeps individual spec_repairman, his hammer, apron/gloves and
all loot. That routine fixes bashed doors and a valid reciprocal exit; it
does not repair player equipment or enable a new Tinker service. Keep the
furnace hazard and both workshop doors. All original cleric, mage,
psionic, archmage and assassin roles remain individual rather than being
copied into every cyclops. Xoric keeps spec_assassin and his unshielded
authored state, matching the prisoner's clue about magical shielding. Do
not give him Sanctuary merely because he is the king.

The thief's accidental raw body value 1000 is replaced with the intended
living, tailless cyclops anatomy and explicit HUGE cancellation. That
number was body data, not a monetary reward; actual gold stays zero. Add
individual spec_thief to fulfil his stated profession, retaining dual
weapons, all existing senses/stealth/flaming, No Charm and Rare rank.
Psionic 14023 changes from sex 2 to male 1, agreeing with his pronouns and
existing male portrait. The cook stays female 2; all other sexes stay.

Five definitions are Elite: Battlemaster 14002, elite guard 14009, cleric
14021, minotaur 14030 and priest 14034. Five are Rare: Beastmaster 14001,
Leia 14012, mage 14022, Secundus 14033 and thief 14038. Xoric 14011
remains Boss; the other 26 are Common. Apply the existing HP multiplier
once: Rare 2x, Elite 5x, Boss 7x. HP dice letters, body size and family
ties do not supply a rank or an extra racial HP multiplier.

Recompute individual flag masks against species XOR archetype defaults. In
particular, remove repeated head No Arms/No Legs/No Heart bits rather than
cancelling the restrictions. Cancel inherited HUGE on the smaller local
cyclopes while leaving the shared profile and earlier Canyon mob
unchanged. Preserve all deliberate aggression/passivity, Sentinel, Stay
Area, Wimpy, No Quest, No Charm, levels, alignments and local senses.
Dimensions replace independently; special names and their complete
selection vector are not XOR masks. Traits remain internal profile data.

Guard 14006 still spawns standing and remains aggressive. Correct its
description and room 14012 to a drowsy but watchful guard rather than
inventing a sleeping-spawn mechanism. Elite guard 14009 holds the actual
dull staff, so the description no longer calls it a yellow wand. Keep
equipment, item use and the original limited spell/item opportunities.

Both throne-door sides, 14108 west and 14109 east, now use existing
obsidian key 14030, replacing the mismatched key values. The direct route
therefore opens once that key is obtained; the secret passage remains.
This is an agreed design repair, not proof of the author's original
intent. Preserve locked reset states and door modes, the rusty-key/chest/
obsidian-key chain, Xoric's light orb 14021 and the statue gate. The orb
remains valid in LIGHT and opens its route by object identity; it is not
retyped as an ordinary key. All original object stats and wear slots stay.

Room 14105 describes Secundus as a modron rather than a demon. Other minor
spelling and grammar repairs preserve identities and intended encounters.
Keep all 131 room flags/sectors/exits apart from the two approved key
values, all 77 objects, original reset counts/caps/order, 55 initial
inhabitants and 77 after refill, and 125 initial carried items rising to
162. There is no matching .src. The four other real source exports,
including exact Eakos regeneration, remain unchanged.

Exact GUI names preserve punctuation. Trim only room 14087's accidental
trailing title space and retain its period; supply unchanged pixels as
custom_rooms/14087_the_palace_corridor..png. Room 14091 becomes An
apparent dead end, with custom_rooms/14091_an_apparent_dead_end.png. Keep
the old image aliases. The latter avoids a literal question mark that
ordinary Windows cache filenames cannot represent.

Approved separate artwork replacements cover the female cyclops cook
14037, cyclops family room 14027, unfinished wooden bridge room 14069 and
underground river/cliff rooms 14072, 14073, 14076 and 14077. Preserve the
unfinished gap and underground setting. Keep the male psionic and three
head portraits. Media generation and live publication are separate from
applying this source patch; these notes make no publication claim.


Olympus
-------

The catalogue now has 319 archetypes, 37 body species, 51 internal traits,
131 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows, including reserved rows where
applicable. Three profiles append at indices 316-318: golden_handmaid,
chimera and pegasus. All earlier profiles, bodies and trait IDs remain
unchanged. Scribe gains only these three archetype names. There is no new
body, trait, flag, special function, player ability or area-file format.

The Olympian figures use the existing humanoid_avatar, retaining living
humanoid organs, blood, pain, mind, soul, speech and ordinary remains. A
divine name does not automatically confer Undead physiology, gigantic
dimensions, invulnerability or extra priest services. All authored magical
flags stay, including Invisibility, flight, senses, Sanctuary, Protection
and Hermes's Sneak. Preserve aggression/passivity, Sentinel, scavenging
and the other individual behaviours as well. Deliberate magical flight
does not require inventing anatomical wings on a humanoid manifestation.

Prometheus 902 is the captive Titan, represented by humanoid_avatar rather
than the unrelated giant-monster titan archetype. His description now says
This captive Titan rather than This mortal. That corrects identity without
changing his body size, imprisonment, fire special or encounter role.
Hephaestus's lameness remains descriptive; it does not add a permanent
injury or new movement penalty. His description says golden maidens rather
than promising exactly three: the existing resets produce two initially
and four after refill, and those counts stay unchanged.

Avatar dimensions are individual descriptive estimates. Zeus uses 190 cm
height and 100 kg; Prometheus 180 cm and 75 kg; Hermes 175 cm and 65 kg;
Poseidon 190 cm and 95 kg; Apollo and Athena 180 cm and 75 kg; Ares 190 cm
and 110 kg; Hephaestus 175 cm and 90 kg; Odin 210 cm and 110 kg; Ra 185 cm
and 80 kg; Hera 175 cm and 70 kg. Odin is Large; the others are Medium.
All have unspecified length and none is HUGE. Rank does not enlarge them.

golden_handmaid uses bipedal_construct for an intelligent, speaking golden
servant. It has head, eyes, ears, arms, hands, fingers, two legs and feet,
but no biological heart or fleshy organs. It is inorganic, bloodless and
explicitly ANALGIC, retaining a mind and soul. Do not infer Mindless or
Soulless from its material. It leaves no ordinary corpse; its original
carried gold nugget and other loot still drop. Its estimated 170 cm
height, 1250 kg mass and Medium size describe a human-shaped solid-gold
body, without HUGE. The mass does not create a new mineable resource or
saleable corpse. Length remains unspecified.

The handmaid has natural armour -4 and Poison, Sleep and Drain immunity.
It gains no blanket Magic or Nonmagic immunity, new charm protection, fire
resistance or racial guard occupation. Local handmaid 909 retains
individual 100 percent spec_guard. Its mind, soul and voice remain real
gameplay properties despite pain-insensitivity and lack of organic blood.
The existing target skill rejects inorganic NPCs even when they have
material limbs; this conversion does not remove that restriction. This
archetype is an agreed DD4 interpretation of the area, not a claimed
Monstrous Manual entry for golden servants.

chimera uses quadrupedal_mammal for one complete living hybrid. It has
ordinary organs, blood, pain, mind, soul and remains, with multiple heads,
wings, tail, goat horns/rear hooves, lion paws/claws and dragon
scales/fangs. It can fly and retains limited speech by cancelling the
body's No Speech default. Natural armour is -5, an averaged DD4
adaptation. Defaults are 150 cm shoulder height, 300 cm length, 400 kg and
Large without HUGE. The height approximates the second-edition five-foot
shoulder measurement; length and mass are estimates. No elemental immunity
follows from breathing fire, and low intelligence does not mean
AFF_MINDLESS.

Its ordinary natural attack selects claws, fangs or horns through the
existing combat rules, once per normal attack opportunity. Rear-hoof
anatomy adds no extra attack selector, six-hit stream or automatic kick.
The existing head_trauma_blocks_attacks trait is enabled with value 1:
head trauma disables its head-dependent natural parts, while usable claws
remain. The existing single head injury also blocks its anatomical fire
breath. There is no new per-head injury system; Regenerate repairs
supported trauma through the ordinary rules. This extends the existing
opt-in trait to the full chimera without changing its ID or earlier
consumers.

The shared chimera selects spec_breath_fire_anatomical at 100 percent,
retaining the original Olympus fire payload and opportunity frequency. It
does not acquire the mixed breath of Cyclops Caves' independent dragon
head. Conversely, those three earlier head entities remain independent,
with their own anatomy, corpselessness, armour and authored mixed breath.
Do not combine their HP or introduce owner links when adding this full
body.

pegasus is an organic quadrupedal mammal with normal horse organs, blood,
pain, mind, soul and remains, plus tail, hooves and feathered wings. It
flies, cannot speak, uses the existing ordinary hoof attack and has
natural armour -4 with Detect Good and Detect Evil. Defaults are 170 cm
shoulder height, 260 cm length, 700 kg and Large without HUGE. The source
has differing rounded shoulder measurements; 170 cm is the agreed
descriptive choice. The weight rounds the source's over-1500-pound
description; length is a DD4 estimate. These are ordinary pegasus
defaults, not Greater Pegasus magic resistance or a new diving/rear-kick
attack.

Local pegasus 924 loses its accidental Inorganic override and keeps its
authored neutral alignment, Mount status and existing rider rules. No new
good-only rider restriction, saddle system or player movement ability is
added. Recompute its body mask rather than retaining the old raw value
800. The inherited wings, flight, tail, hooves and organic physiology must
survive the species/profile/individual XOR layers.

The warhound 913 reuses dog, with individual 85 cm shoulder height, 150 cm
length, 70 kg and Large size. Keep its ordinary fang bite and the intended
silence/no-arms outcome. Roc 922 reuses the established bird profile at
250 cm height, 450 cm length, 650 kg and Large with HUGE. Keep its native
claws/beak and flight; remove the old spec_poison because neither this
description nor the agreed ordinary roc supplies venom. Only the roc is
HUGE among this area's 26 definitions. Shared dog and roc defaults and
their earlier encounters stay unchanged.

The soldier, apprentice musician, serving boy, Julius, alchemist, Clive,
Samuel, citizen, cook and jailer reuse human. Their height/weight
estimates are respectively 180 cm/85 kg, 175/70, 140/35, 175/95, 170/60,
195/110, 195/110, 175/75, 175/110 and 180/90. The serving boy is Small and
the other nine are Medium, with length unspecified and no HUGE. The cook
remains male and gains the normal human arms and voice by clearing raw
body 36. All actual levels, alignments and sexes remain unchanged across
the area. Jailer 926 is human, not an inferred ogre from the old portrait.

Zeus 901 and warhound 913 receive the existing generic ACT_FEAR_AURA
individually. This is not a new racial marker, dog-wide default, avatar
power or new ACT bit. Existing visible-opponent rules allow one fear save
per fight at source level plus one, subject to the normal cap. Existing
Magic/Psychic, fear-immunity, werewolf, mindless and blindness protections
still apply; failure attempts ordinary flee. There is no idle harassment,
guaranteed escape, damage or extra special slot. Other authored effects,
including flaming or invisibility where present, keep their actual
meaning.

Preserve each authored divine role as one 100 percent selection: Zeus and
Hera use cleric casting; Prometheus fire; Poseidon and the jailer mage;
Apollo and Athena adept aid; Hephaestus lightning; Odin and Ra mixed
breath. The handmaid retains guard. No shop/healer/caster role is inferred
merely from a profession or divine title. Julius remains the sole
shopkeeper; other stores intentionally refuse players. The apprentice
keeps Practice. Unarmed Clive may select his carried bow or axe through
existing NPC AI; do not add an equipment reset to turn that conditional
behavior into a guaranteed armed spawn. All three new profiles have zero
HP, damage, critical, haste and profile XP modifiers; normal special XP
still applies.

Zeus is Boss. Poseidon, Ares, Odin, Ra and Hera are Elite. Hermes, Apollo,
Athena, Hephaestus and weapons master Clive are Rare. The other fifteen,
including captive Prometheus, chimera, pegasus and handmaid, stay Common.
The existing rank HP multiplier applies once: Rare 2x, Elite 5x and Boss
7x. Neither sourcebook rarity nor profession, level or an HP dice letter
supplies an additional rank, body-size change or racial combat multiplier.

Spear object 903 changes from Armour to Weapon, retaining its authored
build values 0/5/5/2, affects, extra/wear flags and Ares's WIELD reset.
This enables normal weapon generation and stabbing contact; it is a real
combat correction, not just a display label. Fan 915 remains cheap Trash
and gains HOLD alongside TAKE. Lute 916 becomes Instrument 36, with
TAKE|HOLD wear mask 16385. Its existing HOLD resets stay, so the original
lutes can be held and used under the existing song rules. This gives no
new song skill, bard special or automatic musical ability to an NPC.

Correct object 920's orange wording to an orange/An orange, and the cook's
as is to as if. Preserve media-visible names, including Bed Room. Room and
reset bytes are unchanged: all 47 room flags, sectors, exits, locks,
placements, caps, reset order and the four NO_UNDEAD wards at 910, 931,
948 and 951 remain. Keep all 28 object definitions and unrelated loot. The
world retains 41 initial inhabitants with 61 carried objects and 59
inhabitants with 75 objects after three refills. There are no teachers,
programs or matching .src. The four real source exports elsewhere,
including exact Eakos regeneration, remain unchanged.

As each profile is assigned, recompute the individual XOR mask from the
intended final result. Repeating inherited No Arms/No Speech or flight can
cancel the desired properties. In particular, clear the cook's accidental
limb/speech restrictions, keep the dog's silence and preserve the roc's
flight/no-arms result. Scalars such as dimensions use replacement rules;
special names and their complete selection vector are not XOR masks.
Traits remain internal species/archetype data, not individual Scribe
fields.

All 26 portrait names and 47 room-image names already match the current
GUI rules; no filename copy or room-title change is needed. The only
approved artwork replacement is the human male jailer 926, matching the
accepted identity. All other portraits, room images and sex fields stay.
Image generation and publication are separate from applying this source
patch; these builder notes do not claim live publication has occurred.


Ghost Town
----------

The current catalogue contains 334 archetypes, 37 body species, 51
internal traits, 135 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. The fifteen
profiles append at indices 319-333, in order: demon, revenant_spirit,
haunt, crypt_thing, lurker_above, succubus, gray_ooze, nightmare,
retriever, cambion, orcus_avatar, water_weird, kampflut,
animated_evil_tree and tree_spirit. No basic species, trait ID, body-part
flag, player skill or area-file field is added. Earlier indices and player
trait opt-ins stay unchanged.

The new specials are spec_lurker_wrap, spec_water_weird_wrap,
spec_succubus_fascination and spec_retriever_rays. They use the existing
special-selection mechanism; a 100 percent slot chooses the routine, not a
guaranteed successful action. Their attempts and saves are separate. All
fifteen new profiles have zero HP, damage, critical, haste and profile XP
modifiers. Normal engine special XP still applies. Rank multipliers remain
individual encounter decisions and apply once.

Species supplies a basic body, the archetype supplies the reviewed
creature, and the local mobile supplies its exceptions. Recompute all
individual XOR masks against the resolved template. Old raw values such as
36, 63, 96, 160, 180, 191 and 314 cannot simply be pasted back after
adding inheritance. Dimensions are scalar replacements, and special
selection vectors are not XOR masks. Traits stay internal to species and
archetypes: there is no new Scribe trait or individual trait override.

The generic demon profile is a living, speaking humanoid with normal
organs, blood, pain, mind and soul. It resists Fire and Cold and is immune
to Electricity and Poison. Its sole role is spec_demon. Lesser demon 8806
uses 180 cm/90 kg/Medium; greater 8810 uses individual 240 cm/250 kg/Large
and retains its authored magical flight. An unnamed demon does not become
a Balor or Marilith and gains no invented wings, tail or extra limbs. It
does not inherit the unrelated fiery_demon's anatomy or fire powers.

revenant_spirit and haunt use humanoid-shaped ghost physiology and the
existing semi-material phasing/contact rules. They retain minds and souls
without animal organs, biological blood or ordinary corpses. Local silence
and other authored effects stay. Their apparent height is 175 cm, Medium,
with mass unspecified. The priest-spirit 8807 follows its explicit local
description and portrait rather than the usual book corpse-host. It has
turn_immunity 1 and holy_water_immunity 1. Haunt 8808 has turn immunity.
Neither gains a new drain, possession, permanent Dexterity loss or
killer-terror attack against strangers. Keep their authored passivity.

crypt_thing is a minded, speaking skeletal guardian with claws and normal
skeletal remains, without animal organs/blood. It is inorganic and ANALGIC
but not Mindless or Soulless. It has natural armour-7,
Nonmagic/Charm/Hold/Sleep/Poison/Drain immunity and turn_immunity 1. Its
183 cm/12 kg/Medium defaults follow a six-foot skeleton with an estimated
mass. Preserve passive/sentinel behaviour and evil alignment. The book's
scattering teleport is omitted, keeping this area's routing intact.

lurker_above is a living flat manta/carpet, without a separate head, eyes,
limbs, tail or wings. Internal gas sacs provide flight. It retains
internal organs, blood, pain, an animal mind and soul. Natural armour is
-4, and ordinary contact is blunt. It has no Trapper heat/cold immunity.
Its 30 cm height is thickness;610 cm length represents its approximately
twenty-foot span. Mass 300 kg is an estimate. It is Large and HUGE.

The lurker and water weird use body wrapping against their current
non-HUGE combat opponent. Each routine has a 25 percent attempt, a normal
Hold save and at most one combat pulse of source-owned Hold. It cannot
stack or refresh an existing hold. Moving either participant, ending
combat, death/extraction or curing the hold releases the ownership. No
extra damage, forced movement, lethal suffocation or drowning timer is
added. The physical lurker and supernatural water weird share cleanup
while retaining their appropriate antimagic and contact restrictions.
Head/eye/arm/tail anatomy is not required for a whole-body wrap. The
transient wrap and its own Hold bit are not saved with a player; other
independent Hold providers and persistent effects still save normally.

succubus is a living, speaking winged humanoid with claws and fang
anatomy. Defaults 183 cm/65 kg/Medium; natural armour-10; Fire,
Electricity and Poison immunity; Cold, Magic and Nonmagic resistance. Its
ordinary attack selects claws, without an extra fang attack stream.
Nonmagic resistance is a softened adaptation of the book weapon barrier,
and ordinary Magic resistance is not literal 30 percent spell rejection.

spec_succubus_fascination has a 25 percent attempt against the current
opponent. Normal Charm/Psychic saves, immunity and werewolf protections
apply. Failure adds one combat pulse of wait, not followers or control.
Usable eyes/head and normal safety/antimagic checks apply. As with the
existing vampire-gaze policy, an invisible combat opponent is not rejected
just because the source cannot see that target. It does not harass idle
characters or add a kiss/life-drain package. Ordinary Charm's player
restriction and disabled Domination remain unchanged.

gray_ooze is organic, headless, eyeless, limbless, bloodless and ANALGIC,
with an animal mind and soul. Defaults 20 cm thickness/244 cm length/120
kg, Medium. It has natural armour-2, Fire/Cold/Poison immunity and
ordinary Magic resistance. Existing traits give acid ordinary contact, not
ranged acid spray. Weapons and electricity are not made immune; an
electric spell may still include Magic in its normal resistance
calculation. There is no automatic equipment destruction, splitting or
psionics.

nightmare is a living horse-shaped fiend, with ordinary organs, blood,
pain, mind, soul and remains, plus hooves, tail, source-specific wings and
anatomical fangs. It cannot speak. Defaults 183 cm shoulder height, 270 cm
length/700 kg/Large, without HUGE. Armour-14 and existing magical Fire
ordinary-contact traits apply to its hoof attacks. Fangs do not add an
ordinary attack selector. Retain authored flight and Mountable; no new
rider-alignment restriction, general fire immunity, smoke cloud or
gas/fire breath is inferred. The local winged variant is deliberate.

retriever is a living construct, not a mindless inorganic golem. Keep
organs/blood/pain, mind and soul. It has four walking legs and four bladed
forelimbs, a usable head/gem eyes and mandibles. Cancel inherited
NoArms/ManyLegs and use the existing four-leg/many-arm/claw anatomy,
without inventing fingers, a scorpion tail or stinger. It cannot speak.
Defaults 366 cm height/450 cm length/2000 kg/Large,HUGE; armour-8;
ordinary claws and the existing generic fear aura. Normal living-target
effects, including Charm/Sleep, remain possible; no blanket golem or
mundane- weapon immunity is added. Source non-Mountable stays despite the
word steed. Weapon-category-dependent regeneration is deferred.

spec_retriever_rays has a 25 percent attempt and at least three combat
pulses between attempts. One current-opponent ray is Fire, Cold, Lightning
or stiffening, with equal selection. Elemental raw damage starts at
level/2 before normal saves and resistance. Stiffening gives Slow until
the next tick, without refreshing an existing Slow; it is not permanent
petrification. Usable eyes/head and appropriate visibility, safety and
antimagic checks apply. It does not use the unrelated full beholder power
list or attack bystanders.

cambion is a living humanoid half-fiend, with scales, anatomical fangs,
normal organs/blood/pain/mind/soul and speech. Defaults 198 cm/100
kg/Medium; armour-4; Electricity/Poison immunity and Fire/Cold/Magic
resistance. The local scout retains Hide, senses, Scavenger, WIMPY and no
caster special. Conditional book wizard abilities do not make every scout
a wizard. No unsupported wings, tail or noble subtype is added.

orcus_avatar represents the described local twenty-foot corpulent humanoid
manifestation, with horns/hooves and humanoid arms/hands. It is living,
minded and souled, with voice and normal organs/blood/pain. Being Prince
of the Undead does not itself set ACT_UNDEAD. Defaults 610 cm and
estimated 3000 kg/Large,HUGE. Preserve Boss, psionicist 100, Sentinel,
WIMPY and nonaggression. Exact second-edition Orcus statistics were not
verified, so no new god-power immunity/instant-kill/summoning package is
invented. There are no new ordinary horn/hoof attack selectors.

water_weird is a thinking liquid elemental without separate head, eyes,
limbs, animal organs, blood or speech. It is inorganic and ANALGIC,
retaining mind and soul. Defaults 91 cm raised height/305 cm length/250
kg, Large without HUGE; armour-6; Fire/Pierce/Slash resistance and
Poison/Drain immunity. Existing traits give water ordinary contact and the
saved body wrap described above. Keep its well's existing Town terrain.
Cold-slowing, purify-water destruction, automatic reformation and forced
drowning are deferred rather than approximated by unrelated flags or
terrain changes.

kampflut is an organic hungry oak with tentacle branches, walking roots
and a toothed maw. It has head/arms/legs but no eyes, animal heart or
guts/blood. It is ANALGIC with mind and soul, without speech. Defaults 600
cm/4000 kg/Large,HUGE; armour-5 and Fire vulnerability. Ordinary
fang/tentacle attacks use the existing attack rules and existing
head-trauma opt-in. spec_tentacle_grip keeps its own 25 percent attempt.
Preserve neutral alignment and nonaggression. No exact second-edition
kampflut entry was verified; this is the DD 4 description, using treant
only as an anatomy/bark/fire analogy. Walking roots support its authored
wandering rather than adding a new rooted movement restriction.

animated_evil_tree is an organic animated plant with branch arms and
walking roots/legs, without animal blood/organs; ANALGIC, minded and
souled, without speech. Defaults 450 cm/2000 kg/Large,HUGE; armour-5, Fire
