vulnerability and ordinary branch-pound contact. Clear the old Inorganic
outcome. The local tree 8825 changes alignment 0 to-400 because its prose
explicitly calls it evil, while keeping nonaggression.

tree_spirit is a nonundead feminine wood-and-leaf humanoid manifestation
matching the portrait. It has head, senses, hands/fingers, branch arms and
root legs, mind/soul/speech, but no animal organs/blood or normal corpse.
It is ANALGIC. Apparent height 175 cm/Medium; mass unspecified. Keep
original Regenerator, invisibility, senses and spec_cast_adept 100. Local
8826 gains ACT_IS_HEALER so its promised paid healing works. Refuge 8892
already has NoMob|Safe|Healing|NoUndead: preserve every flag. Its
safe-rest protection needs no new guard special. It does not acquire
additional priest or undead-healer services from its creature identity.

The existing death_knight development placeholder is rebuilt for its first
actual area use:185 cm/140 kg/Medium, without HUGE; natural armour -10,
ordinary Magic resistance, turn_immunity 1 and the existing generic fear
aura. Keep Dark resistance, Holy vulnerability and Poison/Sleep/ Drain
immunity; add Charm/Hold immunity. It is corporeal undead with
head/limbs/voice, mind/soul and normal remains, without physiological
heart/guts/blood. It is not Mindless, Inorganic or NoCorpse. Keep the
existing spec_death_knight 100, without a new spell catalogue, reflection
or inferred fear immunity. Ordinary Magic resistance is not literal 75
percent spell rejection. Local 8827 keeps its original Rare rank.

The normal vampire profile gains PART_FANGS anatomy only; its ordinary
attack selector, specials and player abilities stay unchanged. Ten earlier
converted dependants have no raw fang override that cancels it. Keep their
other individual exceptions. This change does not add fangs to an
unrelated player form or change vampire-bat dimensions.

Reuse established spectre/wraith/wight/ghost/lich/dracolich/zombie/rat/
spider/plant/slime mechanics for the remaining mobiles. Shambling 8812
uses a corporeal zombie interpretation; its generic spirit paragraph is
also used by the corporeal wight. Preserve spec_cast_undead 100 and cancel
inherited Mindless individually, as in earlier intelligent zombies.
Rotting corpse 8818 remains the normal mindless zombie.

Keep all authored singleton roles: vampire brothers 8804 master-vampire,
Baron 8834 ordinary vampire, lich 8805 archmage, demons demon, Master 8819
mage, Orcus 8831 psionicist and tree spirit adept. Dragon 8811 retains its
gas breath through spec_breath_gas_anatomical 100, rather than silently
switching to generic 70/30 dracolich/mixed breath. Its shared passive
defences/anatomy/fear still inherit. Venomous spider 8814 changes generic
Poison to existing anatomical Poison Bite. Spider 8833 keeps no venom
special, WIMPY and nonaggression. Xantharus 8817 remains human and gains
individual mage 100 to match his explicitly described mage role.

The giant rat and both spiders explicitly stand ten feet high: individual
305 cm height, with respective 600 cm/3000 kg and 500 cm/1000 kg estimated
length/mass. They are Large and HUGE. Shambling mound 8820 explicitly
stands twelve feet high:366 cm/1500 kg/Large,HUGE. Dracolich 8811 uses
estimated 300 cm height/1200 cm length/1500 kg/Large,HUGE. Black pudding
8822 keeps its individual HUGE in addition to the established Large
defaults. All other dimensions follow the resolved profiles or documented
individual estimates. Measurements remain descriptive whole units;
unspecified mass does not mean zero physical mass and rank does not
determine size.

Only two new rank promotions occur: lich 8805 Rare and dragon 8811 Elite.
Preserve Master 8819 Elite, knight 8827 Rare, Orcus 8831 Boss and Baron
8834 Elite. The remaining 29 definitions stay Common. Rank HP multipliers
apply once: Rare 2x,Elite 5x,Boss 7x. HP dice letters and book rarity are
not ranks.

Chest 8806 uses the Master's actual key 8820 instead of missing object
8120. Container flags change 8 to 13, retaining Locked and adding
Closeable and Closed without Pickproof. This matters because item
retrieval tests the Closed flag. Preserve the hoard 8805 contents/reset
and original loot. Unholy Symbol 8817 remains its permanent Light item;
its equipment reset changes HOLD 17 to LIGHT 0. Holy Symbol 8818 stays its
authored magical light, without an unrelated holy-focus/priest-service
redesign.

Correct sex 0 to male 1 for 8804,8805,8807,8808,8827,8831 and 8834,
matching explicit local prose and inspected portraits. Keep every other
sex, all levels and all alignments except evil tree 8825. Correct the
lich's he as returned and the lurker's abberration typos, without renaming
media subjects. Preserve aggression/passivity, WIMPY, Sentinel, movement,
magical senses and all other authored behaviour. Keep intentional local
NoSpeech/NoCorpse outcomes on spirits, wight and Baron. Tree healer 8826
is the explicitly supported speech correction.

All 100 room flags/sectors/exits/door routing and ward 8892 are unchanged.
Keep all placements, reset caps/order and original objects except the
approved chest fields and one Light equipment slot. Initial population is
44 with 23 carried objects; three refills produce 62/23. There is no
matching .src, shop, teacher or mob program. The four real Scribe exports
elsewhere, including exact Eakos regeneration, remain unchanged.

All 35 portrait and 100 room-image filenames match current dd-gui rules.
Regenerate only lurker 8815 as a flat limbless ceiling manta/carpet and
retriever 8829 with four walking legs/four bladed forelimbs and no
scorpion tail/stinger. Keep the priest-spirit revenant and feminine tree
spirit portraits. Image generation into staging and live publication are
separate from applying this source patch.


The Astral Plane
----------------

The current catalogue has 341 archetypes, 37 body species, 51 internal
traits, 135 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. The seven new
profiles use existing bodies. There are no new area fields, body-part
flags, trait IDs, player skills or special-function names. Earlier
profiles and player trait choices stay unchanged.

The seven profiles are astral_manifestation, githyanki, githzerai,
night_hag, invisible_stalker, mind_flayer and small_red_dragon. Use the
compiled catalogue for their stable indices. Body species describes
anatomy, the archetype supplies the creature's defaults, and the local
definition supplies its encounter and exceptions. Keep traits internal to
species/archetypes. All seven profiles have zero HP, damage, critical,
haste and profile XP modifiers; ordinary engine special XP still applies.

astral_manifestation is a featureless solid magical humanoid. It keeps
arms, hands, legs and a head shape, mind and soul, without biological
organs, blood, physical eyes, speech or corpse. It is Inorganic and
ANALGIC. Apparent height is 180 cm, Medium; mass is unspecified. No golem,
undead or weapon-immunity package is inferred. Local guardian 1900 keeps
its authored magical perception, sanctuary and protection. Ordinary
visibility logic does not require physical eyes, although blindness and
other applicable restrictions still work.

githyanki is a living humanoid with ordinary organs, blood, pain, mind,
soul and voice. It has Infrared and anatomical fangs, without an ordinary
bite selector. Defaults 175 cm/65 kg/Medium. Do not infer innate armour,
Magic resistance or Psychic resistance from psionic ability. Local thief
and mage roles remain individual. Hunter 1905 is 170/60; the two gish
1910/1911 are 165/60. Knight 1908 and protector 1909 are explicitly a foot
taller:205/90/Large, without HUGE. Other githyanki use 175/65.

githzerai is a living humanoid without assumed fangs. Defaults 175 cm/70
kg/ Medium, Infrared and ordinary Magic resistance. Ordinary RES_MAGIC is
our bounded adaptation of book magic resistance, not literal percentage
spell rejection or a new Psychic/control immunity. Prisoner 1916 keeps his
authored evil alignment and passive behaviour.

night_hag is a living speaking fiend with claws/fangs, natural armour-10,
Magic/Nonmagic resistance, Fire/Cold/Charm/Sleep immunity and the existing
fear_immunity trait. Defaults 152 cm/50 kg/Medium. Its ordinary claw/fang
selectors use normal attack opportunities and applicable trauma gates.
Nonmagic resistance deliberately softens the book's weapon barrier; no new
silver/cold-iron/+3 weapon threshold, dream attack, permanent soul theft
or disease subsystem is added. Local 1902 keeps mage 100 and its authored
NoCorpse exception.

invisible_stalker is a thinking, souled air vortex. Its body has no animal
organs, blood, corpse, wings or ordinary speech. It is Inorganic and
ANALGIC, with natural armour-7, ordinary Magic resistance, Invisible,
Flying and ordinary blunt contact. Apparent height 244 cm/Large; mass and
length unspecified. It gains neither PassDoor nor Nonmagic immunity.

mind_flayer is a living warm-blooded humanoid, with hands and four facial
tentacles. It retains ordinary organs, blood, pain, mind, soul and voice.
Defaults 183 cm/65 kg/Medium, natural armour-5, ordinary Magic resistance
and Infrared. Facial tentacles are anatomical only: an arm-gated tentacle
grip is not a mouth attack. Do not add a beak, brain extraction or broad
psionic/control immunity. Tortured prisoner 1917 keeps mage 100 and its
low authored level; hunched posture does not create permanent trauma. The
undead alhoon is a different creature.

small_red_dragon has living dragon anatomy, wings, tail, claws/fangs,
natural armour-10 and Fire immunity. Defaults 150 cm shoulder height, 610
cm total length, estimated 500 kg/Large,HUGE. The area gives twenty feet
in length; exact age is not asserted. No adult fear, Magic resistance,
missile immunity or adult tail attack is added. Ordinary claw/fang attacks
use existing trauma rules. Local 1918 cancels inherited Flying because it
is chained, retaining wings and Sentinel/nonaggression. This is not a new
hard binding, summoning or teleport restriction.

The nightmare 1901 reuses the approved nightmare profile and cancels wings
individually while retaining magical flight, fire hoof contact, Mountable
and existing 183 cm shoulder/270 cm length/700 kg/Large defaults. Ghost
Town's explicitly winged nightmare stays unchanged. Ordinary fang attacks
are not added by the anatomical fang bit.

Soulless wanderer 1904 reuses human with individual Soulless and Mindless.
Keep its living organs and blood; do not add Undead, Inorganic, Bloodless
or ANALGIC. The existing can_experience_pain rule already rejects Mindless
creatures, so pain-based effects remain ineffective through that inference
even without an explicit ANALGIC flag. These properties stay independent:
soul absence alone does not remove a mind. Its 175 cm/65 kg measurements
are a gaunt-adult estimate.

The lich-queen 1913 reuses lich,175 cm/45 kg/Medium, with her authored
female identity. Lord Gith 1914 and ghost 1915 reuse ghost, apparent 175
cm/ Medium with mass unspecified. Lord Gith is the local male dead
founder, not a rewrite of another setting's character. He keeps undead
casting and gains the existing fear aura. Ghost 1915 remains silent but
minded: its envy, intention and authored casting are not cancelled by the
phrase destroyed mind. No new drain or possession power is added.

Blast 1919 reuses fire_hazard/environmental_hazard: nonbiological,
Mindless/Soulless and Inorganic, without organs, blood or corpse. Its
existing Mindless state makes it insensible to pain without an explicit
ANALGIC flag. Fire ordinary contact/immunity and Cold/Water vulnerability
inherit. Its measurements stay unspecified, Medium. Use existing
spec_fire_surge at 100 percent, not the unreviewed fire_elemental
development example. The surge calls the same fire-breath spell and
attacker sampling as legacy breath, with flame messaging rather than an
open maw. Existing native readiness, action-validity, safety and antimagic
checks apply. It needs neither a physical head nor speech;100 percent
selection is not a guaranteed activation. Damage and item-burning rules
are unchanged.

Repeated legacy M special records are replacements, not additions. An M
record clears the vector before assigning its routine. Queen 1913's mage
was therefore overwritten by undead casting; dragon 1918's fire breath was
overwritten by mage casting. Use the existing special slots and chance
vector to preserve both roles: queen mage 50/undead 50, dragon
anatomical-fire 50/mage 50. The equal split is an approved design choice,
not a recovered historical probability. There are no extra actions. The
queen's individual vector intentionally replaces shared spec_lich.

Raw body 6000 on guardian 1907 and 3000 on gish 1910 contradict their
living humanoid descriptions. Recompute local XOR masks against the
resolved profile, restoring normal organs, limbs, speech and remains. Do
not copy these raw numbers into a templated mob or claim they were old
gold amounts: their historical origin was not established. Likewise,
preserve deliberate local mute/corpseless and wing/flight exceptions by
computing the final resolved result rather than assuming zero means no
body.

Ranks are individual: Lord Gith 1914 Boss, queen 1913 Elite, knight 1908
and protector 1909 Rare; the other 16 definitions Common. HP dice letters
are not rank markers. Keep authored build rolls; ordinary rank multipliers
apply once. Rank does not determine dimensions. Unknown mass is not zero
physical mass, and all numeric measurements remain descriptive whole
units.

Keep all sexes, levels, alignments, aggression/passivity, WIMPY, Sentinel,
StayArea and other authored behaviour. Keep original objects, equipment,
placements, caps and reset order. Initial population 62 carries 88 items;
three refills produce 75/108. All 80 room flags, sectors, exits, maze
randomisation, unlocked prison doors and the one-way locked gateway
remain. Preserve NO_UNDEAD 1905/1906. Queen's black pentagram 1924 is the
correct key; pouch 1925 is a money object, not a broken container. Silvery
weapon prose does not by itself mean ITEM_SILVER.

Only narrow prose corrections are made to gish 1910, ghost 1915, rooms
1906/ 1977 and flaming stone 1913. Mob short names and room titles stay,
keeping media filenames stable. No matching Astral Scribe source exists.
All 100 portrait/room filenames match current dd-gui. Repaint only fire
blast 1919 as impersonal furnace flames, without a monster face/body.
Staging image generation and live publication are separate from this
source patch.


The Sewer
---------

The catalogue now has 350 archetypes, 38 body species, 51 internal traits,
137 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. The new body is
multipedal_reptile, index 37. It represents a living reptile with more
than four legs, using the existing ManyLegs flag. An eight-legged basilisk
is neither an arachnid nor a quadruped. No new body-part flag or area
field is needed. All earlier indices and player trait choices stay
unchanged.

Nine profiles append at 341-349: giant_ground_beetle, giant_maggot,
homunculus, chreffn, sea_hag, guardian_naga, basilisk, lemure and ettin.
They have zero HP, damage, critical, haste and profile XP modifiers. The
two new routines also have explicit special XP bonuses of zero, rather
than falling through to the legacy default. Rank remains an individual
encounter decision and its HP multiplier applies once.

Use species for the basic body, the archetype for the creature's shared
defaults, and the individual mobile for local differences. Recompute every
XOR operand against the final inherited flags. Old raw 36/52/60/63 are not
complete modern anatomy definitions. A zero local mask means inherit
unchanged, not no organs. Dimensions are scalar replacements; local length
0 can deliberately clear an inherited length to unspecified. Traits stay
internal to species and archetypes, with no individual trait field or
Scribe trait command.

giant_ground_beetle uses insect with six walking legs, mandibles,
head/eyes/normal organs, mind and soul. Its fang selector represents
mandibular contact, with the existing head-trauma gate. Natural armour-4;
40 cm height/100 cm length/15 kg/Small are local design estimates. It
gains no rhinoceros horn, venom or extra special.

giant_maggot uses limbless_invertebrate with a reduced head/mouth and
normal living organs, but no eyes, arms, legs or speech. Keep blood, mind,
soul and pain sensitivity.25 cm height/100 cm length/8 kg/Small are
estimates. Ordinary contact stays; there is no new poison, swallow or
immunity package. Eyeless follows the local authored NoEyes setting.

homunculus is a living manufactured humanoid, not an inorganic golem. It
has wings/fangs/hands, normal organs, blood, pain, mind and soul, without
speech. Height 46 cm rounds the local 18in;2 kg/Small are estimates. It
flies, has natural armour-4 and ordinary fang attacks with the existing
head-trauma gate. No creator binding, owner field, shared death damage,
resurrection or generic construct immunities are added.

spec_homunculus_sleep_bite has a 25percent attempt against its current
combat opponent. It needs usable head/configured fangs and a reachable,
poison-susceptible body. Normal Poison/Sleep saves, immunities and
inferred eligibility apply. It adds no HP damage. Failure applies the
existing Sleep condition with duration 0: the venom's restriction expires
at the next ordinary affect tick, after which normal waking is possible.
It never refreshes existing Sleep. Normal forced sleep, dismount/fall,
combat stopping, waking and damage rules remain. Physical venom needs
neither speech nor sight and can work in a no-magic area; it is not an
extra spell cast. No long-duration helplessness or new disease is added.

chreffn uses a conservative living humanoid body under copper-like
covering, with organs/blood/pain/mind/soul/voice. Natural armour-2 and 150
cm/50 kg/Medium are design choices. The local identity is not securely
identified in a monster book. Copper colouring or armour does not
establish a solid-metal golem, absent soul or elemental immunity. No new
claw attack selector or special is inferred from the portrait.

sea_hag is a living speaking aquatic humanoid with claws and the local
fang/horn anatomy.165 cm/55 kg/Medium are estimates. Natural armour-3,
ordinary Magic resistance, Swim and the existing fear aura are bounded
adaptations. Ordinary claws follow general hag anatomy; fang/horn bits
alone add no extra bite/gore. Local 7041 keeps its authored
spec_cast_undead 100 but gains no ACT_UNDEAD. No instant-death glance,
multiday paralysis, Strength drain, covey or night-hag immunity package.

guardian_naga uses serpentine_reptile, cancelling inherited NoSpeech for
the speaking human-headed serpent. It keeps living head/eyes/organs,
blood/mind/soul, scales/tail/fangs, without arms or legs. Length 610 cm
rounds 20ft; raised height 100 cm/180 kg/Large,HUGE are
estimates/adaptations. Natural armour-7; no automatic Magic or Poison
immunity. Its two roles are cleric 50/poison-bite 50, using existing
slots/chances rather than overwriting either role. This is not an extra
action or guaranteed activation. Preserve kindly Sentinel and local
sex/alignment/Infrared. No ranged venom spit, persistent coil timer or
invented spellcasting arms.

basilisk uses multipedal_reptile with eight legs, normal living reptilian
organs, eyes/scales/tail, NoArms/NoSpeech and ordinary fang attacks with
the existing head-trauma gate. Natural armour-6. Length 213 cm rounds 7ft;
height 45 cm/90 kg/Medium are estimates. It is not HUGE. The identified
ordinary basilisk does not inherit greater-basilisk poison or random
dragon breath. Local 7043 replaces spec_breath_any with the new gaze.

spec_basilisk_stiffening_gaze has a 25percent attempt against one current
fleshy/organic combat opponent. Both actors need usable physical eyes;
source blindness/head/eye trauma and target blindness/eye trauma prevent
it. The viewer must see the source's eyes, while an invisible viewer is
not rejected merely because the source cannot see it. Normal Magic/Hold
saves/resistance, antimagic and safety apply. Failure adds ordinary Slow
with duration 0 and-1Dexterity until the next affect tick. It cannot stack
or refresh existing Slow. Normal Slow cures remain. No damage, permanent
petrification, item conversion, stat loss, idle victims or instant death.
Mindlessness or ANALGIC alone does not protect against physical
stiffening.

Both new routines reuse the existing bounded-action identity/generation
and reentry guards. Messages, saves, combat changes, movement, death,
extraction and ordered-player context must not redirect an old attempt
onto a replacement actor or opponent. Neither adds an extra normal attack
stream, and neither needs ordinary speech. The physical bite and
supernatural gaze keep their different contact/magic restrictions.

lemure is organic devil flesh with a usable face/eyes and clawed arms,
without a normal heart, walking legs or speech. It is Mindless but still
souled, and is neither undead nor inorganic. Keep blood; no explicit
Bloodless or ANALGIC is added. Existing Mindless inference nevertheless
makes pain effects ineffective. Natural armour-3, Fire/Poison immunity,
Cold resistance and ordinary Regenerator are adaptations.152 cm follows
five feet;75 kg/Medium is estimated. No regeneration from ashes, holy-only
permanent death, general devil spell list or extra attacks.

ettin is a living speaking two-headed humanoid giant with normal
organs/blood/pain/mind/soul and the giant family. It has Infrared, natural
armour-7 and HUGE.396 cm rounds 13ft;1100 kg/Large are estimates. Both
Jones 7045 and Herald 7046 use the same adult defaults. Poor clothes or a
drooping head do not establish a juvenile or permanent trauma. ManyHead
still uses the single existing head injury group; it adds no attack,
independent head health, surprise immunity or rock throwing. Keep Jones
aggressive and Herald passive.

Mudmonsters 7000/7013 reuse mud_elemental with individual 250 cm/600 kg/
Large,HUGE, NoSpeech and unspecified length 0. Keep minded/souled mud,
Bloodless/Analgic/Inorganic/NoCorpse, existing magical blunt contact,
Nonmagic resistance and Poison/Sleep/Drain immunity. This identifies mud
substance, not a forced planar biography.7000 stays passive and 7013
aggressive; both keep Sneak. Do not substitute a plant shambling mound.

Bat 7001 keeps 10 cm length with mass unspecified; spider 7003 uses 2 cm
height/8 cm length/unknown mass instead of web_spider's larger defaults.
Neither gains venom. Rat 7002 reuses giant_rat 30 cm/8 kg/60 cm/Small,
keeping its ring 7190 reset. Wanderer 7007 stays a thin human 175 cm/55
kg/Medium. Local morkoth 7011 reuses ratfolk 152 cm/45 kg/Medium with
NoSpeech: its human-rat shadow appearance is not an aquatic
beaked/tentacled morkoth, undead spirit, incorporeality or new hypnotic
power. Preserve aggression and WIMPY. Names alone never supply anatomy or
a spell catalogue.

Red dragon 7040 reuses small_red_dragon 150 cm shoulder/610 cm total/500
kg/ Large,HUGE, with Fire immunity, armour-10, ordinary claws/fangs and
anatomical fire 100. Those measurements are explicit reuse-first design
estimates, not recovered Sewer facts or an exact age. Keep its wings/
flight and original fire-only role. Do not copy Astral 1918's local Flying
cancellation or 50/50mage exception, or infer adult fear/Magic
resistance/tail attacks/Cold vulnerability from level or rank.

The only shared profile change is small_red_dragon's existing
BODY_WEAPON_LOOT trait opt-in. It lets a TAKE-able attached weapon detach
into ordinary loot on death or dismemberment. Sewer claw 7216 gains
ITEM_BODY_PART with weapon value[0]=4 for the foreleg. Preserve
TAKE/MAGIC/anti-good,4d 4/base fields/bonuses and current randomization;
do not add DONOT_RANDOMISE. Foreleg category 4 deliberately keeps the
existing leg-trauma rules; no new leg restriction is introduced. Astral
1918 has no attached body weapon, so its actual behaviour stays.

Keep armour 7304-7310 as ordinary equipment and loot: current body-part
armour extraction would destroy those drops.7307 is explicitly crafted to
resemble a skull, and the leggings/shield/guards are equipment. Correct
7306 from Furniture 12 to Armor 9. Swap only the two incorrect resets:
rear claws 7309 belong in Feet 8, arm guards 7310 in Arms 10. Orb 7303
stays an external held staff. Weapon/light body regions use value[0];
armour would use value[1], never overwrite armour value[0]AC.

Mindflayers 7200/7201/7202 reuse the living profile and mage 100 roles.
Explicit 7ft/6ft 6/6ft heights become 213/198/183 cm;85/75/65 kg are
estimates. Their tentacle-themed rods/maces are ordinary weapons, not
their own facial tentacles. No new brain extraction or forced psionicist
role. Wererat 7203 reuses the reviewed profile with individual 122 cm/35
kg/Small, male 1 from his in the long description. Preserve its armour-5,
Nonmagic immunity/silver bypass andXP+10; no disease transmission,
shapeshift or shared rebuild. Gigantic rat 7204 has explicit 305 cm total
length, estimated 80 cm/100 kg/Large,HUGE. Keep existing venom on snake,
wererat and huge rat through spec_poison_bite 100 and normal anatomy
checks, without adding poison to unrelated ordinary animals.

Ranks: dragon 7040 Boss, master mindflayer 7200 Elite, senior 7201 Rare;
the other 21 definitions Common. HP dice letters and book morale labels
are not ranks. Keep original builds and apply rank HP once. Dimensions are
descriptive whole units; unspecified mass does not mean zero mass. Rank,
poor equipment and level alone never establish age or body size.

All 177 room records/flags/sectors/exits/doors remain byte-identical, with
no new NO_UNDEAD flag. All placements/caps/order/loot remain except the
two corrected equipment slots. Population 57 carries 32 items initially;
refills produce 94/32. Keep all authored aggression/passivity, WIMPY,
Sentinel, StayArea, levels, alignments and senses. Only 7203 changes sex;
all other sexes remain. Narrow look-description grammar changes affect
7040/7042/7043/7044/7046 without changing names or long descriptions. No
matching .src exists. Shared Redferne armour 7217-7224/lights/money and
all other real-source exports remain unchanged.

All 201 media images exist. Exact GUI lookup matches all 24mob names and
158of 177rooms.19 room images need unchanged-byte copies restoring hyphens
or final periods in their basenames. A title ending in a period
legitimately produces a filename ending..png. Keep all authored room
titles and old public files. Regenerate only maggot 7005 as eyeless and
limbless, and basilisk 7043 with four distinct pairs of legs. Media files
are packaged separately from source; staging and live publication are
separate steps. No GUI code or image-tool configuration change is made.


The Plane of Nirvana
-------------------

The twelve Nirvana creatures now use reusable modron archetypes. Tridrone
uses tripodal_organism; quadrone, pentadrone, decaton, nonaton and octon use
the new geometric_being body. Septon through Primus use humanoid. The new
body is a living core with eyes and internal organs, without a separate
head. Its profiles supply the appropriate arms, tentacles and legs. It
keeps blood, pain, a mind, a soul and speech. Headless does not mean
brainless or mute. Metallic artwork does not establish inorganic anatomy.

New archetypes are modron_tridrone, modron_quadrone, modron_pentadrone,
modron_decaton, modron_nonaton, modron_octon, modron_septon, modron_hexton,
modron_quinton, modron_tertian and primus_manifestation. Existing
modron_secundus is reused. Extra limbs do not multiply attack streams.
The archetype catalogue has 361 rows and the body catalogue has 39 rows;
append new rows without moving existing entries. The trait registry has
54 entries and Scribe recognises 138 special-function names.

All modrons leave no corpse: their bodies disintegrate, but their carried
equipment still drops. Cold, Fire and Acid are resisted; Sleep, Charm,
Hold and Drain are immune. Fear immunity and the existing illusion trait
apply. The latter currently protects against Ventriloquism, not every
effect which someone might call an illusion. modron_secundus gains only
this illusion trait; its existing measurements and other properties stay.

The three lower forms do not gain Magic resistance or blanket Psychic
immunity. Hierarchs, from decaton upwards, have ordinary Magic resistance
and Psychic immunity. These are DD4 adaptations of the second-edition
Modron entry, not a new percentage-based spell rejection system. Primus is
a fightable local manifestation with its existing mage role and loot.

Natural armour by archetype is tridrone -5, quadrone -6, pentadrone -7,
decaton -8, nonaton -10, octon -9, septon -11, hexton -12, quinton -13,
tertian -15, secundus -16 and Primus -20. Nonaton and octon intentionally
do not follow a smooth sequence. Original area armour/build fields stay.
No enchanted-weapon threshold or Nonmagic immunity is added.

Quadrone and pentadrone gain Infrared. Decaton floats on gas; octon flies
and swims through its collar; septon swims; hexton and quinton have wings
and flight; Primus flies. The ordinary quadrone is wingless. Pentadrone
has no permanent flight. Existing Secundus stays grounded without
Infrared or HUGE. Tertian and Primus have HUGE in addition to Large.

Hexton has two hands and four claw-tipped tentacles. Tentacles provide
ordinary slash contact and obey arm trauma; claw anatomy does not add the
generic claw selector. Tertian's mace-tail uses the existing blunt tail
attack and tail-trauma gate. Regenerate restores injured eligibility.
Quinton's prehensile tail is not a mace-tail weapon.

spec_paralysing_gas is a physical combat emission. Pentadrone supplies
paralysing_gas_chance=25, paralysing_gas_daily_uses=5 and
paralysing_gas_cooldown=5 through its code-only trait list. These are not
Scribe commands, area fields or individual mob overrides. A new creature
can reuse the routine by supplying these traits in its archetype and
selecting the special through the normal active-special system. Assigning
the special alone does not invent a supply or chance.

Chance and daily uses accept 0..100; zero disables the power. Cooldown is
measured in combat pulses, clamped to 1..100 when the power is active.
The approved pentadrone limit is five releases per game calendar day and
five combat rounds between releases. The runtime character owns its used
count and cooldown. A new day, month or year replenishes the daily supply;
the cooldown still runs. These counters are transient and are not saved
into area files or player files.

Gas targets only the current corporeal, poison-susceptible opponent.
Poison, Paralysis and Hold immunity/save rules apply. A failed save adds
the existing paralysis condition until the next ordinary affect tick,
without damage, stacking or refreshing an existing Hold. Freedom,
Regenerate and ordinary expiry apply. Physical gas needs neither a head
nor speech and can work in NO_MAGIC. Combat safety, actor identity and
movement/death/combat-change guards still apply. Limits are reserved before
messages or saves can cause a nested action. A resisted release spends a
charge; an ineligible attempt does not.

Decaton, nonaton and octon gain ordinary cleric casting. Existing tridrone
thief, septon/hexton/quinton cleric and tertian/secundus/Primus mage roles
remain. Local aggression, passivity, levels, alignments, Scavenger,
Sentinel and Stay Area choices remain area design. More arms, titles and
HP dice letters do not establish encounter rank.

Nirvana's Tertian 8109 is Rare, Secundus 8110 is Elite and Primus 8111 is
Boss. The other nine are Common. Local masculine prose is reflected by
sex male on 8105, 8107, 8110 and 8111; other definitions remain neuter.
Ranks and sex remain individual choices, not archetype-wide assumptions.

Whole-number height/weight estimates are listed in the current catalogue.
Tertian's height follows the authored twelve feet; Secundus retains its
approved 300 cm/150 kg. Measurements are descriptive, and unspecified
length is not a claim of zero physical length. No fractional weights or
new area columns are introduced. The sixty room flags, routes, doors,
resets and all five Primus equipment pieces are preserved. There is no
matching Nirvana Scribe source. The two corrected room titles need media
filenames generated from their corrected text, preserving hyphens as the
current dd-gui Lua does.


Yggdrasil
---------

Yggdrasil's fifteen definitions use fourteen new archetypes and the
existing frost_giant. All basic bodies already exist. The catalogue has
375 archetypes, 39 bodies, 60 internal traits and 140 Scribe special names.
Keep old indices stable; no new area fields or individual trait overrides
are introduced. Use archetype names in Scribe and let the body/archetype
resolver supply inherited values before applying the area's XOR masks.

New archetypes are hollyphant, planetar, lesser_shedu, dretch, chasme,
babau, yagnoloth, kostchtchie_avatar, nabassu, cloud_dragon, solar, wemic,
gorgimera and giant_squirrel. The catalogue lists their current dimensions,
defences and body choices. These are corporeal creatures with organs,
blood, pain, minds, souls and ordinary remains. Celestial or fiendish
identity does not imply Undead, Mindless, Inorganic, Soulless or NoCorpse.

The shared hollyphant is a small winged elephant, 45 cm high, 30 kg and
61 cm long. Yggdrasil deliberately keeps its authored large variant:
150 cm high, 500 kg, 240 cm long and Large without HUGE. These are absolute
MobHeight/MobWeight/MobLength/MobSize replacements, not XOR operations.
Wings, flight, trunk and tusks belong to the archetype; NoArms and NoSpeech
remain. NoSpeech does not prevent trumpeting. The carried hollyphant tusk
9908 remains a wand, not a detachable body-part weapon.

Planetar and Solar have wings, flight and ordinary ACT_REGENERATOR
regeneration. Planetar resists Magic/Fire and is immune to Cold,
Electricity, Poison, Drain and Charm; Acid remains effective. Solar resists
Magic and is immune to Cold, Electricity, Acid, Poison, Drain, Charm and
Hold, and has Infrared. Planetar retains its local mage role, Solar its
cleric role. Their physical flesh, pain and souls remain.

lesser_shedu uses a human head on a winged equine body, with hooves,
NoArms and speech. It inherits the existing psionic caster; psionic
abilities do not automatically grant Psychic resistance. Wemic is a
human upper torso and arms over a four-legged lion body with paws,
foreclaws and tail. Its local thief role remains an area choice.

Dretch, Chasme, Babau, Kostchtchie and Nabassu resist Magic/Fire/Cold and
are immune to Electricity/Poison. Babau's protective slime becomes
Slash/Pierce resistance, without corroding carried weapons. Its skeletal
appearance is living leathery flesh. Chasme has a human head/proboscis,
two grasping arms, four walking legs and fly wings. Nabassu has leathery
wings, claws and fangs; the profile does not create a feeding/maturation
system. Dretch keeps the authored Wimpy and Scavenger behaviour.

Yagnodemon 9906 uses yagnoloth while retaining its old displayed name and
keywords, with yagnoloth added as a keyword. Its unequal arms remain
descriptive. It resists Magic, is immune to Acid/Fire/Poison and vulnerable
to Cold; it does not inherit the tanar'ri Electricity immunity. The local
spec_demon is a casting role, not a species classifier. Its polearm stays.

Kostchtchie's fightable avatar uses the book's 28-foot stature, 853 cm,
with an estimated 8000 kg mass, Large and HUGE. It joins the giant combat
family without inheriting frost giant rocks. Keep its demon caster,
resting position and cold-iron hammer. The listed conservative defences
do not import a full divine immunity package.

Cloud Dragon remains a physical winged dragon. Its cold breath uses
spec_breath_frost_anatomical and Cold immunity. Its age is unspecified;
no age-based fear, Magic resistance or extra tail attack is inferred.
There is no permanent NonCorporeal or PassDoor cloud form. The old raw
NoArms bit is cleared so it does not cancel the dragon body's NoArms.

Ratatosk remains the local giant squirrel courier, 100 cm high, 80 kg and
240 cm long, Medium without HUGE. It retains NoArms/NoSpeech, its senses
and Sanctuary, with ordinary paws/claws/tail. This does not turn it into
the separate humanoid gliding squirrel-folk or grant powered flight.
Clear the duplicated raw NoArms/NoSpeech mask rather than cancelling the
new inherited defaults. Hollyphant needs the same XOR cleanup.

Hollyphant uses cleric 70 / holy trumpet 30. spec_holy_trumpet has a 25%
attempt chance when selected, three releases per game calendar day and
a five-combat-round cooldown. It deals max(1, level/2) Magic/Holy damage
to the current opponent; a successful save halves it. It requires a usable
head and trunk. Magical Silence, applicable silence restrictions, cone of
silence, NO_MAGIC and antimagic block it. NoSpeech alone does not. There
is no area damage, deafness or extra action. An ineligible or antimagic-
blocked attempt spends no daily use; an actual resisted release does.

The trumpet's parameters are code-only holy_trumpet_chance,
holy_trumpet_daily_uses and holy_trumpet_cooldown. Chance/daily uses accept
0..100, with zero disabling the routine. Cooldown accepts 0..100 and is
clamped to 1..100 for an active power. Daily supply resets when the game
day/month/year changes; the cooldown continues. Counters are runtime-only.

Chasme uses demon 70 / lulling drone 30. spec_lulling_drone makes a 25%
attempt when selected, with a five-combat-round cooldown. It applies
ordinary magical Sleep until the next affect tick, without damage,
stacking or refreshing. Normal saves, immunity, cures, wake restrictions
and damage wake-up apply. It targets the current awake opponent and needs
a usable head and fly wings. Silence/cone of silence/NO_MAGIC/antimagic
and normal combat safety still apply. No conversational language is needed.
The communication-channel deaf setting is not physical hearing immunity.
Code-only lulling_drone_chance and lulling_drone_cooldown follow the same
chance/cooldown limits as the trumpet; there is no daily supply limit.

Gorgimera has lion, red-dragon and gorgon heads, lion forequarters, gorgon
hindquarters and a winged dragon body. ManyHead is anatomical: this does
not create independent head HP or extra attack streams. Whole-head trauma
uses the current shared head-injury model. Its fire 50 / gas 50 vector
selects one action. Fire uses the existing anatomical wrapper. Gas reuses
spec_paralysing_gas, with a 25% attempt when selected, two releases daily
and a five-round cooldown. The gas is brief physical paralysis with
existing Poison/Paralysis/Hold protections, next-tick expiry and
Freedom/Regenerate cures; it does not transform anyone into stone.

New code-only paralysing_gas_requires_head is a 0/1 opt-in. Gorgimera sets
1, so missing head or head trauma blocks its gas. Default 0 preserves the
headless Pentadrone and its original 25% / five daily uses / five-round
cooldown. The physical gas still works in NO_MAGIC. Different creatures
own separate supplies and counters. This is a trait, not a Scribe command.

Kostchtchie 9907 is Boss, Solar 9910 Elite, Planetar 9901 and Ratatosk 9914
Rare; the other eleven stay Common. Original HP/build fields remain,
with the normal rank multiplier applied once. Existing frost_giant 142
stays unchanged: 640 cm/3600 kg/Large/HUGE, Cold immunity, natural armour
-15 and its existing rock throw/catch routines. Shared elephant, chimera,
ratfolk and earlier planar profiles keep their approved behaviour.

Planetar 9901, Babau 9905 and Ratatosk 9914 use male to match their authored
he/his references and compatible portraits. Other sexes stay. Every
original aggression/passivity/Scavenger/Wimpy/Sentinel/StayArea choice,
alignment, placement/cap/order, object/loot and room flag/exit/door is
preserved. There is no matching Yggdrasil .src. Room titles 9902/9917/9918
correct Yggrasil to Yggdrasil and need the matching GUI-derived media names.
The corresponding room pictures retain their pixels.


Undersea
--------

The current catalogue contains 389 archetypes, 39 basic body species,
60 internal traits, 140 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Fourteen
profiles append at 375-388; no basic body, trait ID, active special or
player-race rule changes. All 375 earlier profiles, including Dolphin,
remain unchanged. New HP/damage/critical/haste/profile-XP modifiers are
zero. Natural armour uses existing code-only traits; it does not replace
the area's authored AC/build values. Existing special XP rules remain.

New names in index order: megalodon, vampire_squid, spider_crab,
sea_anemone, anglerfish, sea_lion, sea_snake, cuttlefish, jellyfish,
goblin_shark, porcupinefish, giant_lobster, giant_octopus and sahuagin.
Both dolphins reuse dolphin 99 with individual absolute dimensions.

All fourteen new profiles have Swim, no resistance/vulnerability/immunity
package and no active special by default. Swim is not water breathing.
Ordinary marine identity adds no Cold resistance, magical attack, Undead,
Inorganic, Soulless, NoCorpse or pain immunity. Retain the area's existing
caster, contact-poison, warrior, fido and whale roles individually.
Sahuagin's fishlike anatomy does not change player race 19.

Measurements below are height cm / whole kg / length cm, with 0 meaning
unspecified. All are agreed descriptive DD4 estimates except the authored
Megalodon's 20-metre length. Neither rank nor level supplies dimensions.
Crab/lobster length excludes extended limbs; jelly length includes trailing
tentacles. Combat size and HUGE are independent explicit settings.

Vnum   Archetype          H / kg / L          Size          Armour
27000  megalodon             0 / 50000 / 2000  Large+HUGE   -5
27001  vampire_squid         0 /     1 /   30  Small        0
27002  spider_crab          40 /    15 /   60  Medium       0
27003  sea_anemone          50 /    20 /  100  Medium       0
27004  anglerfish            0 /    10 /   80  Small        -2
27005  dolphin               0 /   100 /  180  Medium       -5
27006  dolphin               0 /   300 /  300  Large        -5
27007  sea_lion             80 /   200 /  220  Large        -3

27017  giant_octopus         0 / 10000 / 2000  Large+HUGE   -3
27018  sahuagin            183 /    90 /    0  Medium       -5

Megalodon stays Common: the user explicitly rejected a promotion. Kraken
keeps its existing Elite rank; every other Undersea creature is Common.
Rank HP multipliers still apply once, independently of profile modifiers.

Fish use the existing fish body with head, eyes, brains, heart, guts,
fins and tail, without arms, legs or ordinary speech. Megalodon, Anglerfish
and Goblin Shark add fangs with an ordinary bite. Goblin Shark is not a
goblinoid. Porcupinefish has beaklike tooth plates and descriptive sharp
scales; only its beak is selected for a native ordinary attack. There is
no automatic spine retaliation, venomous bite or added poison special.

Vampire Squid, Cuttlefish and Giant Octopus use cephalopod: head, eyes,
brain, heart, guts and many tentacled arms, without legs or speech. All
keep usable blood, mind, pain and soul. Multiple biological hearts remain
one ordinary heart-trauma group. Vampire Squid and Cuttlefish have fins;
Giant Octopus does not inherit squid fins. Native tentacle/beak selection
uses existing arm/head trauma eligibility and adds no attack stream.
These new head attacks opt in to head_trauma_blocks_attacks, as do the
new fang/beak profiles. Regenerate uses existing healing behaviour.

Vampire Squid is a living detritus feeder, not undead or blood-draining.
Its escape echoes describe glowing mucus rather than black ink; preserve
all six direction/peace/sneak/hide sequences, their 2% chances, and the
separate 7% visibility program. Cuttlefish retains hide/visibility scripts.
The local Kraken is an enormous octopus. It gains no squid-Kraken spell
list, extra ink poison, swallowing or new constrictor/grip special. Keep
both existing poisoned tentacle weapons and the original attack programs.

Spider Crab and Giant Lobster use crustacean: stalked eyes, brain, heart,
guts, shell, grasping claw arms and many walking legs, without speech.
Their haemolymph counts as usable blood; retain mind, pain and soul.
Claws provide ordinary fallback contact rather than additional attacks.
Spider Crab has natural armour 0 because equipped carapace 27002 already
provides AC -20. Do not add a second shell bonus. Giant Lobster's -6 is
a separate agreed value. Its shared claw object 27014 and all five Omu
Central uses remain intact; no shared item/source rewrite is required.

Sea Anemone and Jellyfish use radial_invertebrate: tentacled arms and a
digestive cavity, without a separate head, eyes, central brain, biological
heart or walking legs. They are organic and Bloodless, with mind, pain,
soul and remains retained. Do not set Mindless, Analgic or Soulless simply
because there is no central brain. Existing can_experience_pain also
rejects Mindless, independently of the Analgic bit; this chunk sets neither.

Both radial profiles reuse natural_attack_type 16. The existing contact
poison routine then says "sting" instead of "bite" where that individual
actually has poison. This passive trait supplies no poison, paralysis,
save change or new active action. Healthy native tentacle selection still
uses the ordinary whip contact; the trait's ordinary sting fallback is
used only when no eligible natural selector remains. Existing contact
poison itself has no head/arm prerequisite, and is not newly limb-gated.

Keep the authored pairs distinct: sea snake 27008 has no poison, 27009
has its original poison special; jelly 27011 has contact poison, 27012
has its original paralysis fight program; porcupinefish 27014 is harmless,
27015 has its original contact-poison role. A shared profile never gives
all members the active power. Headless/NoSpeech does not invalidate the
existing jelly fight-program spell; its ordinary casting gates remain.
The approved movement correction removes Pass Door only from Cuttlefish
27010 and Jellyfish 27011/27012, preserving all other effects and scripts.

Sea Lion is the described real pinniped. It reuses quadrupedal_mammal
with four flipper limbs, fins, head, ears, ordinary organs and fang bite;
there are no human grasping arms/hands or fantasy lion/fish hybrid powers.
Sea Snake reuses serpentine_reptile with scales, fangs and paddle-tail
anatomy. Both keep ordinary blood, mind, pain and soul and NoSpeech.

Keep both dolphins' original programmes, keywords, chances, kiss, follow
and reunion commands exactly: mutual following is the intended behaviour.
Baby 27005 remains Wimpy, 100 kg/180 cm/Medium, and explicitly suppresses
the inherited active whale special. Mother 27006 keeps spec_small_whale,
300 kg/300 cm/Large without HUGE. These are individual overrides; the
shared Dolphin remains 90 kg/180 cm/Medium and natural armour -5.

Sahuagin is a speaking, minded, souled humanoid with scales, webbed limbs,
fins, tail, claws/fangs and ordinary brain/heart/blood. Keep spec_warrior
and the actual race-19 dialogue. The approved area-script repair uses
the actual interpreter's flat early-exit guard and independent sex and
direction checks: preserve Brother/Sister/Clutchmate dialogue, the five
sequential 20% movement choices, their order and break behaviour. No new
interpreter rule, race value or global quest behaviour is introduced.

Attached weapons retain precedence over native fallback. Keep their
BODY_PART/poison/no-take flags, existing head/arm categories, trauma,
disarming, Regenerate and loot behaviour. More limbs do not generate
additional simultaneous attacks. BODY_PART staves retain staff charges
and spell levels; never reinterpret their values as weapon-region fields.

The user explicitly keeps conch 27004 as ITEM_CORPSE_NPC, byte-identical;
do not relabel or convert it to treasure. Potion 27020 adds Breathe Water
in a spare spell slot while retaining Armor and level 50. All other
objects, placements/caps/order, wear slots, G-versus-E choices, original
ACT behaviour, builds/alignments/sexes and 101 room flags/routes remain.
There is no matching Undersea source; unrelated real sources stay intact.

Within an existing $mob block, for example:

    archetype porcupinefish
    rnk common
    height 0
    weight 2
    length 50
    size 0

Set its individual role separately if needed. Do not copy the poisonous
partner's special onto a harmless variant. Use the existing explicit
empty-special override for the baby Dolphin; omitting a special means
inheritance, not suppression. Traits remain code-only lists with no area
field, individual override or Scribe trait command.

Current verified conversion progress is 95/153 areas (62.1%) and
1552/4131 mob definitions (37.6%). Undersea adds 19 definitions; after
its merge is verified, totals become 96/153 (62.7%) and 1571/4131 (38.0%).


The Abyss
---------

The current catalogue contains 402 archetypes, 39 basic body species,
60 internal traits, 141 Scribe special names and 637 skill rows. Thirteen
profiles append at 389-401, using eight existing body groups. All earlier
389 profiles, 39 bodies, 60 trait IDs and player opt-ins stay unchanged.
New profile HP/damage/critical/haste/XP modifiers are zero. Natural armour
uses existing internal traits without replacing the authored area AC.

New names in index order: photophore, mesopelag, malacost, pycnogonid,
anthozoa, scyph, siph, erenna, copepod, abralia, veranyi, calimedusae and
cyclothone. These are the area's fictional creatures, not a compulsory
classification as real marine species. A familiar scientific-sounding
name never overrides the actual description or authored role.

This is an AIRBORNE realm: the source explicitly describes airy, inky
gloom and impossible flight. All thirteen profiles have magical Flying,
without invented wings or automatic Swim. Fish and cephalopod bodies
normally supply Swim, so these archetypes XOR that inherited bit off and
add Flying. The area must remove duplicate raw Flying bits to avoid
cancelling it again. Flight is preserved, not exchanged for swimming.
All ACT behaviour remains individual: do not add generic aggression,
Sentinel, Wimpy, Mountable or NoCharm through the shared profiles.

Measurements are height cm / whole kg / length cm. Zero is unspecified.
All numeric dimensions are agreed DD4 design estimates; the explicit
Pycnogonid two-metre leg span is a separate descriptive measurement,
not a replacement for body length. Rank does not determine dimensions.
Only Veranyi has HUGE, preserving the original exception. Long tentacles
do not automatically give Abralia or Calimedusae an additional HUGE flag.

Vnum  Profile        H / kg / L           Size       Armour  Rank
7500  photophore      80 /   30 /   80  Medium       0     common
7501  mesopelag        0 /  200 /  300  Large       -4     common
7502  malacost        50 /   30 /  100  Medium       0     common
7503  pycnogonid      65 /   75 /  150  Medium      -6     common
7504  anthozoa       200 /  150 /  300  Large        0     common
7505  scyph           80 /  200 /  200  Large        0     common
7506  siph           200 /   20 /  300  Large        0     common
7507  erenna         150 /  200 /  600  Large        0     common
7508  copepod         40 /   25 /  150  Medium      -3     common
7509  abralia          0 /  500 /  900  Large       -7     common
7510  veranyi          0 / 2000 /  800  Large+HUGE  -2     rare
7511  calimedusae      0 /  300 /  600  Large       -2     rare
7512  cyclothone       0 /  300 /  700  Large       -1     elite

Veranyi 7510 and Calimedusae 7511 are Rare encounters in their individual
lairs. Cyclothone 7512 keeps its existing Elite rank. The other ten remain
Common, including the passive mount. Existing rank HP multipliers still
apply exactly once, separately from zero profile HP modifiers.

Photophore uses amorphous_organism: an organic Bloodless blob without a
separate head, eyes, limbs, brain or heart. NoCorpse preserves its existing
death-jelly program rather than adding a second ordinary corpse. Retain
individual Blind and NoCharm. NoCharm does not mean Mindless: Photophore
keeps mind, soul and pain eligibility despite its unconventional anatomy.
There is no new acid contact, splitting power or ooze immunity package.

Mesopelag is a living shark-like fish: head/eyes, brain/heart/gut, fins,
tail and ordinary fang bite, without arms, legs or ordinary speech. Keep
its local senses. Its -4 armour follows the existing Shark comparison;
no shared Shark change or additional active role is needed.

Malacost is a dog-sized woodlouse/slater-like crustacean with many walking
legs, ordinary organs/blood, segmented shell and unstalked eyes. Its XOR
removes inherited grasping arms and eyestalks; it gains no pincers. Do not
retain an old NoLegs exception that would contradict those walking legs.
Natural armour is 0 because its existing equipped plates already supply
AC -15. Keep Wimpy, Protection and the death-curl program. Shared plates
7501 and their external Sahuagin Market equipment use remain unchanged.

Pycnogonid is the explicit fictional venomous, toothed flying spider,
not a forced reconstruction of real sea-spider mouthparts. Arachnid gives
eight legs, head/eyes and ordinary brain/heart/gut/blood; fangs supply the
ordinary bite. Natural armour is -6. Existing spec_poison_bite preserves
the old poison payload and rolls, adding the existing usable head/fang
and head-trauma checks. Its ordinary native fangs also opt into the
existing head_trauma_blocks_attacks trait. Poison and poison_bite retain
their existing +5 special-XP allowance; no new venom status is created.

Anthozoa uses radial_invertebrate with grasping tentacles and a digestive
cavity, without separate head/eyes/central brain/heart or walking legs.
It is organic and Bloodless, but retains mind, soul, pain and ordinary
remains. Restore its arm anatomy for the attached region-3 tentacle
weapon. Keep Sentinel/aggression and its nonpoison role; resemblance to
an anemone does not grant a new poison special.

Scyph uses limbless_invertebrate with the local headless/eyeless form,
internal brain-ganglia, heart, gut and usable blood. Its single muscular
foot is represented by clearing NoLegs and adding Feet, with no numbered
leg flags. Existing HAS_LEGS then permits the existing leg target group
and weapon region 4 without inventing two legs or a new body species.
Keep the original Blind, Hold and Sentinel as the authored stationary
limpet encounter. This conversion does not add a global leg-trauma
weapon gate or promise that leg trauma disables every region-4 weapon.
Its equipped shell already supplies AC -35, so natural armour remains 0.

Siph is an organic algal plant colony: no central head, eyes, limbs,
brain, heart or gut, Bloodless and NoCorpse. Plant Analgic makes it
insensible to pain; this is independent of its retained mind and soul.
Do not add Mindless, Soulless, Inorganic, new resistances or poison merely
because it is a colony. Keep fido, Wimpy, PassDoor and the dispersal/drop
program. It has no invented grasping limb or added native damage power.

Erenna is an explicit magical-jelly exception: its death program leaves
a slimy heart, so its radial body cancels NoHeart and adds a real
targetable Heart. It remains Bloodless, without a central brain, and
retains mind, pain and soul. Gut and tentacle Arms remain; there is no
Tail. Existing stinger weapon 7507 changes only its target region from
tail 6 to arm 3. Keep the poison/weapon properties, PassDoor, NoCorpse
and exact heart-drop program. There is no new stinger part or trauma.

Copepod follows the described giant shrimp: shell, stalked eyes,
head/brain/heart/gut, many paddling legs, feeding appendages and tail.
Its ordinary living blood, mind, pain and soul remain. Natural armour
is -3. The name does not force a microscopic animal or supply large
claws, venom or a new special attack absent from the local description.

Abralia and Calimedusae use cephalopod with head/eyes, brain/hearts/gut,
usable blood, tentacled arms, beak and fins, without walking legs or
ordinary speech. Their beaks and tentacles use existing natural attack
selection and head/arm trauma; more arms never add simultaneous streams.
New beak profiles opt into the existing head-trauma trait. The wielded
Calimedusae beak retains ordinary attached-weapon precedence and rules.

Abralia stays nonaggressive, Mountable and NoCorpse, with its blue-ink
death program unchanged. Do not import Giant Squid's grip/fido special.
The approved new reset loads one Abralia, cap 1, in room 7550 at the
stronger end of the maze; preserve all original reset placements/caps.
Calimedusae remains a magical hungry cuttlefish, without an invented
spell catalogue, extra poison or new grip.

Veranyi is a gigantic living fish with ordinary organs, fangs, fins,
tail and the existing HUGE exception. Its lure is descriptive and its
original equipment remains; no Charm/gaze is added. New
spec_bloodsucker_bite is a narrow anatomical wrapper: require a usable
head, no head trauma and usable native fangs, then invoke the unchanged
spec_bloodsucker. The old routine's target, rolls, hit/drain behaviour
and messages remain. Its XP exception is +5, equal to old bloodsucker;
this is not a new profile-XP premium or a global bloodsucker rewrite.

Cyclothone is an eel-like living fish with head/eyes, brain/heart/gut,
fins, tail and teeth. It explicitly gains Electricity immunity as the
approved gameplay choice; no other new resistance or immunity is added.
Preserve the complete weighted, level-gated spec_cast_electric selection,
including all existing non-electric spell choices, rather than replacing
it with a mild real-eel discharge. Keep Elite, Sanctuary, aggression and
Sentinel. Ordinary NoSpeech does not make the existing native caster
ineligible; its existing communication guards remain authoritative.

All thirteen profiles preserve organic bodies and souls. None adds
Mindless or Undead. Photophore, Anthozoa and Erenna remain pain-capable;
Siph alone receives plant Analgic. Preserve existing NoCorpse specifically
on 7500, 7506, 7507 and 7509, alongside their exact death-program drops.
Corpse choice, blood availability, organ targeting, mind and pain are
independent properties rather than one bundled "invertebrate" package.

All old builds, alignments, sexes, ACT behaviour, room flags/sectors/
routes/randomisation, equipment and loot remain except the approved
Abralia placement and Erenna weapon-region correction. All 60 rooms stay
airborne as authored; no NO_UNDEAD rollout or underwater conversion is
implied. No matching Abyss source exists; unrelated real .src exports
remain unchanged. Only room 7553 needs an unchanged-image filename alias;
there is no portrait repaint or gameplay dependence on new artwork.

Within a complete $mob block, the ordinary authoring selection is:

    archetype veranyi
    rnk rare

This inherits its body, dimensions and anatomical bloodsucker role.
Keep authored behaviour and equipment in the individual definition. In
other uses, ordinary sp/sp2/spchance overrides remain available. No new
area field, trait command, individual trait override or body-part target
category is introduced. MSTAT continues to show resolved trait origins.

Current merged completion is 96/153 areas (62.7%) and 1571/4131 mobile
definitions (38.0%). The Abyss adds 13 definitions; after its merge is
verified, totals become 97/153 (63.4%) and 1584/4131 (38.3%).
